# Phase 4 Labs I Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
> Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Hands-on practice that works offline before November 14: the lab contract and the Sort engine with its 11 core labs first (a shippable cut on its own), then the Configure and Simulate engines with their 8 core labs, the daily incident and the lightning round, with rule R11 switched on so CI replays every shipped lab's stored solution and wrong paths, plus the Phase 3 leftovers (`coreLabs` for Hands On and Lab Complete, the "Lightning spam" anti-grind row, and the incident and lab entries of the extras line).

**Architecture:** Each lab engine is a pure reducer in `src/labs/<engine>/model.ts` behind one contract (`init`, `reduce`, `check`, `hint`), registered in `src/labs/engines.ts` with the actions that settle a board before any check and a test that it is complete, and its zod spec and action schemas live in `schemas/lab.ts`, so the validator, the build and CI replay labs with the same code the app runs, while zod stays out of the app.
Lab files under `content/labs/` ship inside the module chunks without their solutions; the lab screen replays the actions kept in one kv draft, checks goals on demand, records `lab.done` on the first all-pass Check, and runs 2 to 3 counted debrief questions in a new `debrief` mode.
Everything else is still derived from the one event log: `derive` gains lab completions and incident offers, `deriveMemory` pays lab, incident and lightning XP, qualifies lab and incident days, counts Hands On per complete module and turns `review.now` into a due card, so the storage schema stays at version 1 while `ENGINE_VERSION` moves to 5 once, for the whole phase.
Content adds two agent fan-outs run with the Workflow tool in their own worktrees: lab writers with a fresh critic per lab and an R11 replay gate, and incident writers whose steps go through the Phase 3 check rounds with `--pool incident`.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24.21.0, pnpm 12.8.1, TypeScript 7.0.2, Vite 8.3.2, Preact 10.29.8 with @preact/signals 2.11.3, zod 4.6.5 (classic for content and lab schemas, `zod/mini` for events and backups), ts-fsrs 5.4.2 (imported only by `src/engine/srs.ts`), marked 18.0.14, yaml 2.9.1, Vitest 5.0.3, fast-check 4.10.2, Playwright 1.63.0 with @axe-core/playwright 4.13.0, Biome 2.5.15, IndexedDB, the Cache Storage and Service Worker APIs; no new dependencies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-aws-cpe-study-site-design.md`, sections 1.2, 2.3, 2.6, 2.7, 2.9 (R1, R2, R4 to R9, R11), 2.10, 3.1, 3.2, 3.5, 3.6, 3.10, 3.11, 4.1, 4.5, 4.6, 5 (5.1 and 5.2), 7.2 to 7.6, 8, 9 and 10.1 (Phase 4 row) and 10.2.
**Target:** Saturday, October 31, 2026 (0.6.0), with the Sort cut (Tasks 1 to 9, the wave S labs and the Task 11 gate) released as 0.5.0 by Saturday, October 24; go/no-go on Saturday, November 7, after which nothing but fixes ships; exam on Saturday, November 14, 2026.

## Conventions for every task

- Run every command from the repository root, `/Users/danielchahine/Programs/AWS Cloud Practitioner Essentials` (or your worktree of it), where `node --version` must print `v24.21.0`; in a fresh shell start with `eval "$(fnm env)"; fnm use`, since `tests/unit/toolchain.test.ts` fails under any other Node.
- Three timing tests already on `master` (`tests/regressions/2026-10-10-exam-date-replay-cost.test.ts`, `tests/property/exam-date-fidelity.property.test.ts` and `tests/unit/forms.test.ts`) can pass Vitest's 5 s timeout while other agents load the machine; when one fails in a full run, rerun it alone before treating it as a failure, and report it.
- Never write the em dash character (U+2014) anywhere, and keep one sentence per line in every Markdown file outside code blocks and tables (rule R8 checks both).
- Commit messages are plain imperative sentences with a short body; never add a `Co-Authored-By` line (Dani's global instructions forbid agent co-author lines).
- Run `pnpm lint:fix` before each commit so Biome formats the code; the formatter has the last word.
- The pre-commit hook runs `pnpm check`, so a commit fails while any check fails; fix the cause rather than bypassing the hook, and never use `--no-verify`.
- Stage files by path, never with `git add -A` or `git add .`.
- Never hand-edit generated files: `content/fingerprints/*` comes only from `pnpm content:fingerprint`, `content/exam/quotas.yaml` and `content/exam/forms.yaml` never change in this phase, the `verified` and `review` blocks of questions and incident steps come only from `pnpm content:stamp`, and every `*.snapshot.json` and journey `.jsonl` under `tests/fixtures/` only from its test helper (delete it and rerun the test to regenerate it).
- Course text stays outside the repo in `~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/`; no file, fixture, test, agent prompt or agent output in the repo may contain it, and test fixtures use made-up bakery text.
- Lab text (titles, briefs, goals, hints, wrong-path mistakes, card, field and parameter labels, debrief lines) and incident text are original, set in the cafe, and grounded by citing the source lesson (`notes` on a lab, `note` and `evidence` on an incident step), never copied; R7 checks both.
- Labs, debriefs, incidents and lightning draw only from the live practice pool, never the exam pool; lab and incident writers never read `content/exam/`, the course source text, `content/fingerprints/` or AWS exam questions.
- Dani must never see exam-pool item text: plans, tool output, test output, agent returns, logs and commit bodies name exam items by id and count only.
- Prices in labs are "lab credits", a made-up unit, and any figure the notes do not state is called a lab value; never show a real AWS price.
- Agent results and check batches live under `.results/` (git-ignored) and are never committed.
- Code under `src/engine/` and every `.ts` file under `src/labs/` is pure: it imports no Preact, signals, `src/app`, `src/ui`, `src/store` or zod code, touches no DOM, storage, `crypto` or `globalThis`, and takes the current moment, the UTC offset and any seed as arguments; Biome enforces the boundary.
- Raw question, lab and concept ids appear only in the runner's report panel and in tool output, never elsewhere in the UI (spec 2.8).
- UI copy in this plan is exact: tests match it word for word, so change it only together with its test.
- Every bug found while building gets `tests/regressions/YYYY-MM-DD-<slug>.test.ts`, written and failing before the fix (spec 9).
- The initial JS bundle measures 49.4 KB of its 50 KB budget at HEAD 56f2c1a in 4 scripts; Task 6 makes the Help sheet lazy and Task 7's `vite.config.ts` ships every statically imported module as one initial chunk (48.1 KB in 1 script on the prototype), which leaves about 1.9 KB for Tasks 8, 16, 17, 18 and 19 (about 1.4 KB together on the prototypes).
- Every task that touches code in the initial bundle runs `pnpm build` and states the measured size and headroom in its report, stops and reports rather than raising the budget when the `budgets:` line passes 50.0 KB, and loads new screens and rarely used helpers through `lazyScreen` or `import()`.

- Task 2 needs Task 1; Task 3 needs Task 2; Task 4 needs Tasks 1 and 2.
- Task 7 needs Tasks 1, 4, 5 and 6; Task 8 needs Tasks 4 to 7; Task 9 needs Tasks 7 and 8.
- Task 10 starts wave S with the Workflow tool once Tasks 1 to 3 are on `master` and merges `master` into its branch before its Commit phase; wave CS branches from `master` once Task 12 is there (the two Configure writers start at once) and merges `master` again when Task 13 lands (the six Simulate writers start then); the two waves merge into `master` independently.
- Task 11 (the Sort cut gate, 0.5.0) needs Tasks 1 to 9 and the wave S merge.

- Tasks 1 to 11 (wave S and its gate) are the Sort cut: they ship on their own as 0.5.0 if the rest runs late (spec 10.2).

## Task 1: Lab ids, lab schemas, the lab contract and the Sort model

**Files:**
- Create: `schemas/lab.ts`, `src/labs/contract.ts`, `src/labs/engines.ts`, `src/labs/sort/model.ts`, `tests/fixtures/labs.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-schema.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-sort.test.ts`, `tests/property/labs.property.test.ts`
- Modify: `schemas/ids.ts`, `biome.json`
- Test: `tests/unit/lab-schema.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-sort.test.ts`, `tests/property/labs.property.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `schemas/ids.ts` stays zod-free and gains `LAB_ENGINES`, `LabEngineId`, `LAB_ID` and `INCIDENT_ID`, so the engine registry, the app and the content scripts share one engine list.
- `schemas/lab.ts` is the only home of lab zod schemas: the `LabFile` envelope, the Sort spec and actions, and `LAB_SCHEMAS[engine] = { spec, action }`, which only grows when Tasks 12 and 13 add Configure and Simulate.
- The contract has no `schema` member (a deliberate deviation from spec 5.1, recorded by Task 11): models import the spec and action types with `import type`, so zod never reaches the app bundle.
- `LabFile` adds `title`, `notes`, `status` (default `live`) and each wrong path's `mistake` to the spec example, and its refinement ties the id's `mNN` to `module`, keeps goal ids unique, makes every `failsGoal` name a goal, gives every goal a wrong path and keeps debrief qids unique.
- The engine spec, the solution and the wrong-path actions are `unknown` in the envelope; R11 (Task 2) parses them with `LAB_SCHEMAS`, so a bad spec reports with the engine's own messages.
- A Sort card names its `bucket`, up to two allowed alternatives in `also` (default `[]`) and its `goal`; buckets only, no ordered slots (a deliberate deviation from spec 5.1, since no Phase 4 or Phase 5 Sort lab orders anything).
- `SortState` holds the spec's card and bucket ids beside `placed`, so `reduce` can ignore unknown ids without the spec, as the contract's `reduce(state, action)` signature requires.
- `reduce` returns the very same state object for any action it cannot apply (unknown ids, unknown `t`, a non-object, a no-op), which the property test and the screen's "nothing changed" checks rely on.
- `check` returns one result per goal in the order goals first appear on cards, with the message `<right> of <total> right`, and `hint` returns one sentence counting the goal's cards still in the tray and in a wrong bucket.
- `ENGINES[engine] = { engine, settle, complete }`: Sort settles with no actions and is complete when no card is left in the tray; `checkLab` applies `settle` and then `check`, and every check in the app and in R11 goes through it.
- `engineOf(id)` uses `Object.hasOwn`, so an id such as `toString` never finds an inherited member.
- Biome's purity override extends to every `src/labs/**/*.ts` file (not the `.tsx` views) and also bans `zod` and `zod/mini` there.
- `tests/fixtures/labs.ts` holds the one worked Sort example of this phase: `bakeryLab()`, a made-up bakery lab with 2 buckets, 4 cards, 2 goals, a solution, 2 wrong paths and the debrief qids `q-b4k7m2` and `q-d9p4r6` (the second fixture question arrives in Task 2).
- Covers P4-006 (lab schema half), P4-008, P4-022 (Sort half), P4-023, P4-037, P4-040, P4-041, P4-042, P4-046, P4-048 and P4-053 (Sort).

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the bakery lab to the fixtures**

Create `tests/fixtures/labs.ts`:

```ts
import { mkdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { stringify } from 'yaml';
import type { LabFile } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';

/** The fixture tree's one lab file. */
export const FIXTURE_LAB = 'content/labs/01-baking-basics/sort-the-bakes.yaml';

/** A made-up bakery Sort lab for the pipeline tests; each call returns a fresh copy. */
export function bakeryLab(over: Partial<LabFile> = {}): LabFile {
  return {
    id: 'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes',
    module: 1,
    title: 'Sort the bakes',
    engine: 'sort',
    tier: 'core',
    status: 'live',
    rev: 1,
    concepts: ['shared-kitchen', 'per-minute-rental'],
    notes: ['m01/what-a-bakery-cloud-is'],
    minutes: 4,
    brief:
      'The harbour branch runs short of oven space on Saturdays and pays for cabinets it barely uses. Sort each need under the bakery cloud idea that answers it.',
    spec: {
      buckets: [
        { id: 'shared-kitchen', label: 'Shared kitchen' },
        { id: 'per-minute-rental', label: 'Per-minute rental', detail: 'Pay only while it is in use' },
      ],
      cards: [
        {
          id: 'saturday-ovens',
          text: 'Two extra ovens for the long Saturday queue',
          bucket: 'shared-kitchen',
          also: [],
          goal: 'g1',
        },
        {
          id: 'harbour-trays',
          text: 'Extra harbour trays baked away from the branch',
          bucket: 'shared-kitchen',
          also: [],
          goal: 'g1',
        },
        {
          id: 'forty-minutes',
          text: 'Forty minutes of rising time for the rye dough',
          bucket: 'per-minute-rental',
          also: [],
          goal: 'g2',
        },
        {
          id: 'empty-overnight',
          text: 'A cabinet nobody needs between closing and dawn',
          bucket: 'per-minute-rental',
          also: [],
          goal: 'g2',
        },
      ],
    },
    goals: [
      {
        id: 'g1',
        label: 'Oven needs sit under Shared kitchen',
        hint: 'Ask whether the branch borrows ovens it does not own while its queue is long.',
      },
      {
        id: 'g2',
        label: 'Cabinet needs sit under Per-minute rental',
        hint: 'Ask whether the branch pays only for the minutes the dough is inside.',
      },
    ],
    solution: [
      { t: 'place', card: 'saturday-ovens', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' },
      { t: 'place', card: 'harbour-trays', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' },
      { t: 'place', card: 'forty-minutes', bucket: 'per-minute-rental' },
      { t: 'place', card: 'empty-overnight', bucket: 'per-minute-rental' },
    ],
    wrongPaths: [
      {
        actions: [
          { t: 'place', card: 'saturday-ovens', bucket: 'per-minute-rental' },
          { t: 'place', card: 'harbour-trays', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' },
          { t: 'place', card: 'forty-minutes', bucket: 'per-minute-rental' },
          { t: 'place', card: 'empty-overnight', bucket: 'per-minute-rental' },
        ],
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        mistake: 'Treats borrowed ovens as a cabinet billed by the minute',
      },
      {
        actions: [
          { t: 'place', card: 'saturday-ovens', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' },
          { t: 'place', card: 'harbour-trays', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' },
          { t: 'place', card: 'forty-minutes', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' },
          { t: 'place', card: 'empty-overnight', bucket: 'per-minute-rental' },
        ],
        failsGoal: 'g2',
        mistake: 'Treats a rented cabinet as an oven borrowed from the kitchen',
      },
    ],
    debrief: {
      lines: [
        'Borrowing central ovens covers a long queue without owning ovens that sit idle on quiet days.',
        'A cabinet billed by the minute costs nothing once it goes back, so empty hours are free.',
      ],
      questions: ['q-b4k7m2', 'q-d9p4r6'],
    },
    ...over,
  };
}

/** Writes a lab as YAML into a fixture root. */
export function writeLab(root: string, lab: unknown, file = FIXTURE_LAB): void {
  const path = join(root, file);
  mkdirSync(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(path, stringify(lab));
}

/** Deletes the fixture lab, for tests that retire or rewrite the questions its debrief names. */
export function dropFixtureLab(root: string): void {
  rmSync(join(root, FIXTURE_LAB));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/unit/lab-schema.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { LAB_SCHEMAS, LabFile, SortSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { bakeryLab } from '../fixtures/labs.ts';

/** Each issue as `path: message`, for exact assertions on the custom messages. */
const issues = (result: {
  success: boolean;
  error?: { issues: { path: PropertyKey[]; message: string }[] };
}) => (result.error?.issues ?? []).map((i) => `${i.path.map(String).join('.')}: ${i.message}`);
const paths = (result: { error?: { issues: { path: PropertyKey[] }[] } }) =>
  (result.error?.issues ?? []).map((i) => i.path.map(String).join('.'));

const spec = () => bakeryLab().spec as SortSpec;

describe('lab file schema (spec 5.1)', () => {
  it('accepts the bakery lab and defaults status to live', () => {
    const { status: _, ...noStatus } = bakeryLab();
    const parsed = LabFile.safeParse(noStatus);
    expect(issues(parsed)).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsed.data?.status).toBe('live');
  });

  it('rejects an id whose module number differs from module', () => {
    expect(issues(LabFile.safeParse(bakeryLab({ module: 2 })))).toEqual([
      'id: must start with lab-m02- to match module 2',
    ]);
  });

  it('rejects a wrong path whose failsGoal names no goal, and a goal with no wrong path', () => {
    const [first, second] = bakeryLab().wrongPaths;
    if (first === undefined || second === undefined) throw new Error('the bakery lab has two wrong paths');
    expect(
      issues(LabFile.safeParse(bakeryLab({ wrongPaths: [first, { ...second, failsGoal: 'g7' }] }))),
    ).toEqual(['wrongPaths.1.failsGoal: names no goal: g7', 'goals.1: goal g2 has no wrong path']);
  });

  it('rejects one or four debrief questions, and a question listed twice', () => {
    const debrief = (questions: string[]) => bakeryLab({ debrief: { ...bakeryLab().debrief, questions } });
    expect(paths(LabFile.safeParse(debrief(['q-b4k7m2'])))).toEqual(['debrief.questions']);
    expect(paths(LabFile.safeParse(debrief(['q-b4k7m2', 'q-d9p4r6', 'q-c8n3p5', 'q-7k2m9p'])))).toEqual([
      'debrief.questions',
    ]);
    expect(issues(LabFile.safeParse(debrief(['q-b4k7m2', 'q-b4k7m2'])))).toEqual([
      'debrief.questions.1: q-b4k7m2 is listed twice',
    ]);
  });

  it('rejects an engine that does not exist', () => {
    expect(paths(LabFile.safeParse({ ...bakeryLab(), engine: 'place' }))).toEqual(['engine']);
  });
});

describe('Sort spec schema (spec 5.1)', () => {
  it('accepts the bakery spec and its solution actions', () => {
    const sort = LAB_SCHEMAS.sort;
    if (sort === undefined) throw new Error('Sort ships in Phase 4');
    expect(sort.spec.safeParse(spec()).success).toBe(true);
    expect(bakeryLab().solution.every((a) => sort.action.safeParse(a).success)).toBe(true);
    expect(sort.action.safeParse({ t: 'drag', card: 'saturday-ovens' }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it('defaults also to an empty list', () => {
    const s = spec();
    const cards = s.cards.map(({ also: _, ...card }) => card);
    expect(SortSpec.parse({ ...s, cards }).cards.every((c) => c.also.length === 0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects a card whose bucket is unknown, an also that repeats the bucket, and duplicate card ids', () => {
    const s = spec();
    const [a, b, c, d] = s.cards;
    if (a === undefined || b === undefined || c === undefined || d === undefined)
      throw new Error('four cards');
    expect(issues(SortSpec.safeParse({ ...s, cards: [{ ...a, bucket: 'bread-van' }, b, c, d] }))).toEqual([
      'cards.0.bucket: names no bucket: bread-van',
    ]);
    expect(
      issues(SortSpec.safeParse({ ...s, cards: [{ ...a, also: ['shared-kitchen'] }, b, c, d] })),
    ).toEqual(["cards.0.also.0: repeats the card's bucket shared-kitchen"]);
    expect(issues(SortSpec.safeParse({ ...s, cards: [a, { ...b, id: a.id }, c, d] }))).toEqual([
      'cards.1.id: card saturday-ovens is used twice',
    ]);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/lab-sort.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { SortAction, SortSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { allPass, replayLab } from '../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import { checkLab, ENGINES, engineOf } from '../../src/labs/engines.ts';
import { SORT, sortComplete } from '../../src/labs/sort/model.ts';
import { bakeryLab } from '../fixtures/labs.ts';

const LAB = bakeryLab();
const SPEC = LAB.spec as SortSpec;
const SOLUTION = LAB.solution as SortAction[];
const place = (card: string, bucket: string): SortAction => ({ t: 'place', card, bucket });
const play = (...actions: SortAction[]) => replayLab(SORT, SPEC, actions);
const messages = (actions: SortAction[], spec: SortSpec = SPEC) =>
  SORT.check(spec, replayLab(SORT, spec, actions)).map(
    (r) => `${r.goalId} ${r.pass ? 'pass' : 'fail'} ${r.message}`,
  );

describe('Sort checker (spec 5.1, 9)', () => {
  it('fails every goal on the starting board', () => {
    expect(messages([])).toEqual(['g1 fail 0 of 2 right', 'g2 fail 0 of 2 right']);
  });

  it('passes every goal with the solution', () => {
    expect(messages(SOLUTION)).toEqual(['g1 pass 2 of 2 right', 'g2 pass 2 of 2 right']);
    expect(allPass(SORT.check(SPEC, play(...SOLUTION)))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('counts an allowed alternative bucket as right', () => {
    const cards = SPEC.cards.map((c) =>
      c.id === 'harbour-trays' ? { ...c, also: ['per-minute-rental'] } : c,
    );
    const spec: SortSpec = { ...SPEC, cards };
    const moved = SOLUTION.map((a) =>
      a.t === 'place' && a.card === 'harbour-trays' ? place('harbour-trays', 'per-minute-rental') : a,
    );
    expect(messages(moved, spec)).toEqual(['g1 pass 2 of 2 right', 'g2 pass 2 of 2 right']);
  });

  it('gives 1 of 2 right for a card in the wrong bucket', () => {
    expect(messages([...SOLUTION, place('saturday-ovens', 'per-minute-rental')])).toEqual([
      'g1 fail 1 of 2 right',
      'g2 pass 2 of 2 right',
    ]);
  });

  it('takes a card back with unplace and clears the board with reset', () => {
    expect(play(...SOLUTION, { t: 'unplace', card: 'forty-minutes' }).placed).toEqual({
      'saturday-ovens': 'shared-kitchen',
      'harbour-trays': 'shared-kitchen',
      'empty-overnight': 'per-minute-rental',
    });
    expect(play(...SOLUTION, { t: 'reset' })).toEqual(SORT.init(SPEC));
  });

  it('returns the same state object for an unknown card, bucket or action', () => {
    const state = play(place('saturday-ovens', 'shared-kitchen'));
    const junk = [
      place('rye-loaf', 'shared-kitchen'),
      place('saturday-ovens', 'bread-van'),
      { t: 'unplace', card: 'rye-loaf' },
      { t: 'drag', card: 'saturday-ovens' },
      null,
      'place',
      [],
    ] as unknown as SortAction[];
    for (const action of junk) expect(SORT.reduce(state, action)).toBe(state);
  });

  it('is complete only once no card is left in the tray', () => {
    expect(sortComplete(SPEC, play(...SOLUTION.slice(0, 3)))).toBe(false);
    expect(sortComplete(SPEC, play(...SOLUTION))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('checks through the registry with no settle actions, exactly as check does', () => {
    const entry = engineOf('sort');
    expect(entry).toBe(ENGINES.sort);
    expect(engineOf('place')).toBeNull();
    expect(engineOf('toString')).toBeNull();
    if (entry === null) throw new Error('Sort ships in Phase 4');
    expect(entry.settle).toEqual([]);
    const state = play(place('saturday-ovens', 'shared-kitchen'));
    expect(checkLab(entry, SPEC, state)).toEqual(SORT.check(SPEC, state));
    expect(entry.complete(SPEC, play(...SOLUTION))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('says what is left for a goal in one sentence', () => {
    expect(SORT.hint(SPEC, play(), 'g1')).toBe('2 cards still in the tray.');
    expect(SORT.hint(SPEC, play(place('saturday-ovens', 'per-minute-rental')), 'g1')).toBe(
      '1 card still in the tray and 1 card in the wrong bucket.',
    );
    expect(
      SORT.hint(
        SPEC,
        play(place('saturday-ovens', 'per-minute-rental'), place('harbour-trays', 'per-minute-rental')),
        'g1',
      ),
    ).toBe('2 cards in the wrong bucket.');
    expect(SORT.hint(SPEC, play(...SOLUTION), 'g2')).toBe('Every card of this goal is in place.');
  });
});
```

Create `tests/property/labs.property.test.ts`:

```ts
import fc from 'fast-check';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { SortSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { toPlain } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { replayLab } from '../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import { SORT } from '../../src/labs/sort/model.ts';
import { bakeryLab } from '../fixtures/labs.ts';

/** Lab reducers (spec 5.1, 9): any action sequence, known ids or not, never throws, replays the same and checks purely. */
const RUNS = { seed: 20_261_031, numRuns: 200 };

const SORT_SPEC = bakeryLab().spec as SortSpec;
const cardArb = fc.constantFrom(...SORT_SPEC.cards.map((c) => c.id), 'rye-loaf', '');
const bucketArb = fc.constantFrom(...SORT_SPEC.buckets.map((b) => b.id), 'bread-van');
const sortActionArb: fc.Arbitrary<unknown> = fc.oneof(
  fc.record({ t: fc.constant('place'), card: cardArb, bucket: bucketArb }),
  fc.record({ t: fc.constant('unplace'), card: cardArb }),
  fc.constant({ t: 'reset' }),
  fc.anything(),
);

describe('Sort reducer properties', () => {
  it('never throws, replays to equal states and checks as a pure function of the state', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.array(sortActionArb, { maxLength: 40 }), (actions) => {
        const once = replayLab(SORT, SORT_SPEC, actions as never[]);
        const twice = replayLab(SORT, SORT_SPEC, actions as never[]);
        expect(toPlain(once)).toEqual(toPlain(twice));
        const before = toPlain(once);
        const first = SORT.check(SORT_SPEC, once);
        expect(SORT.check(SORT_SPEC, once)).toEqual(first);
        expect(toPlain(once)).toEqual(before);
        expect(first.map((r) => r.goalId)).toEqual(['g1', 'g2']);
      }),
      RUNS,
    );
  });
});
```

Apply this change to `schemas/ids.ts`:

```diff
diff --git a/schemas/ids.ts b/schemas/ids.ts
--- a/schemas/ids.ts
+++ b/schemas/ids.ts
@@ -27,3 +27,13 @@ export const BADGE_IDS = [
   'architect',
 ] as const;
 export type BadgeIdValue = (typeof BADGE_IDS)[number];
+
+/** The lab engines Phase 4 ships (spec 5.1); Place, Trace and Investigate arrive in Phase 5. */
+export const LAB_ENGINES = ['sort', 'configure', 'simulate'] as const;
+export type LabEngineId = (typeof LAB_ENGINES)[number];
+
+/** A lab id: `lab-mNN-<slug>`, where NN is the lab's module (spec 1.2). */
+export const LAB_ID = /^lab-m(0[1-9]|1[0-3])-[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
+
+/** An incident id: `inc-NNN` (spec 1.2). */
+export const INCIDENT_ID = /^inc-\d{3}$/;
```

Create `schemas/lab.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { ConceptId, ModuleNumber, NoteId, Slug } from './common.ts';
import { LAB_ENGINES, LAB_ID, type LabEngineId } from './ids.ts';
import { Qid } from './question.ts';

/**
 * Lab files (spec 5.1): the envelope every engine shares, and each engine's spec and actions. This is the only home of
 * lab zod schemas; `src/labs` models import the inferred types with `import type`, so zod never reaches the app bundle.
 */
export const LabId = z.string().regex(LAB_ID, 'must look like lab-m03-pick-the-compute');
export const GoalId = z.string().regex(/^g[1-9]$/, 'must look like g1');

export const LabGoal = z.strictObject({
  id: GoalId,
  label: z.string().min(8).max(80),
  hint: z.string().min(20).max(240),
});

/** A wrong path: actions that model one learner mistake, the goal they must fail, and that mistake in a few words. */
export const WrongPath = z.strictObject({
  actions: z.array(z.unknown()).min(1),
  failsGoal: GoalId,
  mistake: z.string().min(10).max(120),
});

export const Debrief = z.strictObject({
  lines: z.array(z.string().min(20).max(240)).min(2).max(4),
  questions: z.array(Qid).min(2).max(3),
});

export const LabFile = z
  .strictObject({
    id: LabId,
    module: ModuleNumber,
    title: z.string().min(3).max(60),
    engine: z.enum(LAB_ENGINES),
    tier: z.enum(['core', 'stretch']),
    status: z.enum(['live', 'retired']).default('live'),
    rev: z.int().min(1),
    concepts: z.array(ConceptId).min(1).max(12),
    notes: z.array(NoteId).min(1).max(6),
    minutes: z.int().min(2).max(20),
    brief: z.string().min(40).max(500),
    spec: z.unknown(),
    goals: z.array(LabGoal).min(1).max(5),
    solution: z.array(z.unknown()).min(1),
    wrongPaths: z.array(WrongPath).min(1),
    debrief: Debrief,
  })
  .superRefine((lab, ctx) => {
    const nn = String(lab.module).padStart(2, '0');
    if (!lab.id.startsWith(`lab-m${nn}-`)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: 'custom',
        path: ['id'],
        message: `must start with lab-m${nn}- to match module ${lab.module}`,
      });
    }
    const goals = new Set<string>();
    for (const [i, goal] of lab.goals.entries()) {
      if (goals.has(goal.id))
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['goals', i, 'id'], message: `goal ${goal.id} is used twice` });
      goals.add(goal.id);
    }
    for (const [j, path] of lab.wrongPaths.entries()) {
      if (!goals.has(path.failsGoal)) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['wrongPaths', j, 'failsGoal'],
          message: `names no goal: ${path.failsGoal}`,
        });
      }
    }
    for (const [i, goal] of lab.goals.entries()) {
      if (!lab.wrongPaths.some((p) => p.failsGoal === goal.id)) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['goals', i], message: `goal ${goal.id} has no wrong path` });
      }
    }
    const qids = new Set<string>();
    for (const [k, qid] of lab.debrief.questions.entries()) {
      if (qids.has(qid))
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['debrief', 'questions', k],
          message: `${qid} is listed twice`,
        });
      qids.add(qid);
    }
  });
export type LabFile = z.infer<typeof LabFile>;

/** Sort (spec 5.1): cards go into buckets; each card names its bucket, up to two allowed alternatives and its goal. */
export const SortSpec = z
  .strictObject({
    buckets: z
      .array(
        z.strictObject({
          id: Slug,
          label: z.string().min(2).max(40),
          detail: z.string().max(80).optional(),
        }),
      )
      .min(2)
      .max(10),
    cards: z
      .array(
        z.strictObject({
          id: Slug,
          text: z.string().min(3).max(120),
          bucket: Slug,
          also: z.array(Slug).max(2).default([]),
          goal: GoalId,
        }),
      )
      .min(4)
      .max(16),
  })
  .superRefine((spec, ctx) => {
    const buckets = new Set<string>();
    for (const [i, bucket] of spec.buckets.entries()) {
      if (buckets.has(bucket.id))
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['buckets', i, 'id'],
          message: `bucket ${bucket.id} is used twice`,
        });
      buckets.add(bucket.id);
    }
    const cards = new Set<string>();
    for (const [i, card] of spec.cards.entries()) {
      if (cards.has(card.id))
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['cards', i, 'id'], message: `card ${card.id} is used twice` });
      cards.add(card.id);
      if (!buckets.has(card.bucket)) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['cards', i, 'bucket'],
          message: `names no bucket: ${card.bucket}`,
        });
      }
      const seen = new Set<string>();
      for (const [k, alt] of card.also.entries()) {
        const where = ['cards', i, 'also', k];
        if (!buckets.has(alt))
          ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: where, message: `names no bucket: ${alt}` });
        else if (alt === card.bucket)
          ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: where, message: `repeats the card's bucket ${alt}` });
        else if (seen.has(alt)) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: where, message: `lists ${alt} twice` });
        seen.add(alt);
      }
    }
  });
export const SortAction = z.discriminatedUnion('t', [
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('place'), card: Slug, bucket: Slug }),
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('unplace'), card: Slug }),
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('reset') }),
]);
export type SortSpec = z.infer<typeof SortSpec>;
export type SortAction = z.infer<typeof SortAction>;

/** Each shipped engine's spec and action schemas; R11 and the build parse with these, the app never imports them. */
export const LAB_SCHEMAS: {
  readonly [E in LabEngineId]?: { readonly spec: z.ZodType; readonly action: z.ZodType };
} = {
  sort: { spec: SortSpec, action: SortAction },
};
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the contract, the Sort model and the engine registry**

Create `src/labs/contract.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The lab contract (spec 5.1): every engine is a pure reducer the UI drives only by dispatching actions. The zod spec
 * and action schemas live in `schemas/lab.ts` (a deliberate deviation: zod stays out of the app bundle).
 */
export interface GoalResult {
  goalId: string;
  pass: boolean;
  message: string;
}

export interface LabEngine<Spec, State, Action> {
  init(spec: Spec): State;
  /** Pure; a malformed or unknown action returns `state` itself. */
  reduce(state: State, action: Action): State;
  /** One result per goal id the spec names, in first-mention order. */
  check(spec: Spec, state: State): GoalResult[];
  hint(spec: Spec, state: State, goalId: string): string;
}

/** The state after `actions`, from the engine's starting state. */
export function replayLab<Spec, State, Action>(
  engine: LabEngine<Spec, State, Action>,
  spec: Spec,
  actions: readonly Action[],
): State {
  return actions.reduce((state, action) => engine.reduce(state, action), engine.init(spec));
}

/** True when there is at least one result and every one passes. */
export function allPass(results: readonly GoalResult[]): boolean {
  return results.length > 0 && results.every((r) => r.pass);
}
```

Create `src/labs/sort/model.ts`:

```ts
import type { SortAction, SortSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import type { GoalResult, LabEngine } from '../contract.ts';

/** A Sort board: the card and bucket ids of its spec, and where each placed card sits; a card not in `placed` is in the tray. */
export interface SortState {
  readonly cards: readonly string[];
  readonly buckets: readonly string[];
  readonly placed: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
}

type Card = SortSpec['cards'][number];

const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);

const placedIn = (state: SortState, card: string): string | undefined =>
  Object.hasOwn(state.placed, card) ? state.placed[card] : undefined;

const isRight = (card: Card, bucket: string | undefined): boolean =>
  bucket !== undefined && (bucket === card.bucket || card.also.includes(bucket));

/** Goal ids in the order they first appear on cards. */
function goalIds(spec: SortSpec): string[] {
  return [...new Set(spec.cards.map((c) => c.goal))];
}

const plural = (n: number, what: string): string => `${n} card${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} ${what}`;

export const SORT: LabEngine<SortSpec, SortState, SortAction> = {
  init(spec) {
    return { cards: spec.cards.map((c) => c.id), buckets: spec.buckets.map((b) => b.id), placed: {} };
  },

  reduce(state, action) {
    const a: unknown = action;
    if (!isRecord(a)) return state;
    if (a.t === 'place') {
      const { card, bucket } = a;
      if (typeof card !== 'string' || typeof bucket !== 'string') return state;
      if (!state.cards.includes(card) || !state.buckets.includes(bucket)) return state;
      if (placedIn(state, card) === bucket) return state;
      return { ...state, placed: { ...state.placed, [card]: bucket } };
    }
    if (a.t === 'unplace') {
      const { card } = a;
      if (typeof card !== 'string' || placedIn(state, card) === undefined) return state;
      const placed = { ...state.placed };
      delete placed[card];
      return { ...state, placed };
    }
    if (a.t === 'reset') return Object.keys(state.placed).length === 0 ? state : { ...state, placed: {} };
    return state;
  },

  check(spec, state) {
    return goalIds(spec).map((goalId): GoalResult => {
      const cards = spec.cards.filter((c) => c.goal === goalId);
      const right = cards.filter((c) => isRight(c, placedIn(state, c.id))).length;
      return { goalId, pass: right === cards.length, message: `${right} of ${cards.length} right` };
    });
  },

  hint(spec, state, goalId) {
    const cards = spec.cards.filter((c) => c.goal === goalId);
    const tray = cards.filter((c) => placedIn(state, c.id) === undefined).length;
    const wrong = cards.filter((c) => {
      const bucket = placedIn(state, c.id);
      return bucket !== undefined && !isRight(c, bucket);
    }).length;
    const parts = [
      ...(tray > 0 ? [plural(tray, 'still in the tray')] : []),
      ...(wrong > 0 ? [plural(wrong, 'in the wrong bucket')] : []),
    ];
    if (parts.length === 0) return 'Every card of this goal is in place.';
    const sentence = parts.join(' and ');
    return `${sentence.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${sentence.slice(1)}.`;
  },
};

/** A Sort board is finished when no card is left in the tray. */
export function sortComplete(spec: SortSpec, state: SortState): boolean {
  return spec.cards.every((c) => placedIn(state, c.id) !== undefined);
}
```

Create `src/labs/engines.ts`:

```ts
import type { LabEngineId } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import type { SortSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import type { GoalResult, LabEngine } from './contract.ts';
import { SORT, type SortState, sortComplete } from './sort/model.ts';

export type AnyLabEngine = LabEngine<unknown, unknown, unknown>;

/**
 * One engine as the lab screen and R11 use it: `settle` is the actions applied before any check (Simulate runs its
 * model; Sort and Configure need none), and `complete` says the board is finished, so a wrong path is judged by its
 * mistake and not by an unfinished board.
 */
export interface EngineEntry {
  readonly engine: AnyLabEngine;
  readonly settle: readonly unknown[];
  readonly complete: (spec: unknown, state: unknown) => boolean;
}

export const ENGINES: Readonly<Partial<Record<LabEngineId, EngineEntry>>> = {
  sort: {
    engine: SORT as unknown as AnyLabEngine,
    settle: [],
    complete: (spec, state) => sortComplete(spec as SortSpec, state as SortState),
  },
};

/** The registry entry of an engine id, or null when this build does not ship that engine. */
export function engineOf(id: string): EngineEntry | null {
  return Object.hasOwn(ENGINES, id) ? (ENGINES[id as LabEngineId] ?? null) : null;
}

/** Checks a board the one way the screen and R11 do: the `settle` actions first, then the engine's check. */
export function checkLab(entry: EngineEntry, spec: unknown, state: unknown): GoalResult[] {
  const settled = entry.settle.reduce((s: unknown, action) => entry.engine.reduce(s, action), state);
  return entry.engine.check(spec, settled);
}
```

## Task 2: Load labs and switch on R11, with R2, R6 and R7 for labs

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/labs.ts`, `tests/unit/r11.test.ts`, `tests/content/labs-replay.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad/r11-solution-misses-goal.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r11-wrong-path-passes.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r11-wrong-path-unfinished.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r11-bad-spec.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r11-retired-plan-name.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/labs/01-baking-basics/sort-the-bakes.yaml`
- Modify: `scripts/content/lib/context.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/style.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/index.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r02-ids.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r06-references.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r07-originality.ts`, `content/validate.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/validate.yaml`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/questions/01-baking-basics/01-what-a-bakery-cloud-is.yaml`
- Test: `tests/unit/r11.test.ts`, `tests/content/labs-replay.test.ts`, `tests/unit/context.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r02.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r06.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r07.test.ts`, `tests/unit/vite-plugin.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad.test.ts` (unchanged, now covering R11), and the exact expectations the second live fixture question changes in `tests/unit/build.test.ts`, `tests/unit/coverage.test.ts`, `tests/unit/draft.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r10.test.ts` and `tests/unit/style.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `loadContext` loads `content/labs/NN-<module-dir>/<file-slug>.yaml` into `Context.labs` (`LabEntry { path; module; slug; lab }`, file order); schema errors are R1 findings as for questions, and a lab whose `module` differs from its folder, whose id is not `lab-mNN-<file slug>`, or any other file under `content/labs/` is an R1 finding too and never joins `labs`.
- `labProblems(entry)` in `scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts` is the one replay used by R11, the draft check (Task 3) and the replay test: it parses the spec and every action with `LAB_SCHEMAS`, checks that the file's goals match the spec's, and judges the start (`init`), the solution and each wrong path with `checkLab`, so Simulate's implicit `run` will happen before every check exactly as on the screen.
- A wrong path must first finish the board (`complete` after `settle`), so a Sort wrong path fails because of its mistake and never only because a card is still in the tray.
- A Sort start can never meet every goal (every goal owns a card and every card starts in the tray), so the "starting state" message has no YAML fixture; `tests/unit/r11.test.ts` proves it, and that every check runs after `settle`, with engine stubs injected through `vi.mock`.
- R11 skips retired labs and reports nothing for a tree with no labs; each message starts with the lab id, and the retired Support plan check runs `OLD_PLANS` over `labTexts(lab)` through the new `labStyleProblems`.
- R2 claims every lab id beside concepts, aliases, missions and questions; a lab in HEAD that is gone is an R2 finding (labs have no drafts, so every committed lab has shipped), and a committed lab edited without a higher `rev` gives `lab <id> changed since HEAD, so it needs rev <n+1>`, comparing the parsed files without `rev`.
- R6 checks each non-retired lab: its concepts resolve (alias included) and are not `tested: false`, its notes exist, and each debrief question is a live practice item on one of the lab's concepts, named by id only, so an exam-pool id never shows its text.
- R7 gives every `labTexts(lab)` string of a live lab the same 9-word run check as question text, a deliberate extension of spec R7 because the task rule requires original lab text.
- R11 switches on now, before any real lab exists, because a rule once on stays on (spec 2.9); the fixture tree gains one passing Sort lab (the Task 1 bakery lab as YAML) and a second live question, `q-d9p4r6` on `per-minute-rental`, so the lab's debrief has two live practice questions and the baseline runs R11 for real.
- The second live question changes exact counts in tests that later tasks own (build, coverage, draft, r10, style and context); Task 2 updates all of them, and the commit body names them.
- `tests/content/labs-replay.test.ts` replays every real lab as its own named test, so CI names the lab that broke; while `content/labs/` is empty it runs a placeholder test instead.
- Covers P4-002 (the switch), P4-003, P4-004, P4-005, P4-006, P4-007, P4-011, P4-012, P4-014, P4-015, P4-017, P4-018, P4-020, P4-035 (labs), P4-037, P4-039 and P4-041 (the rev rule).

```yaml
id: lab-m01-sort-the-bakes

solution:
```

Write the five R11 bad fixtures from the bakery lab with this one-off script, saved as `.results/r11-fixtures.ts` (git-ignored), so each differs from the passing lab by exactly one mistake:

```ts
// One-off: writes the five R11 bad fixtures from the bakery lab, so each differs from it by one mistake.
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { stringify } from 'yaml';
import { bakeryLab } from '../tests/fixtures/labs.ts';

const lab = bakeryLab();
const spec = lab.spec as { cards: { text: string; bucket: string }[] };
const [ovens, cabinet] = lab.wrongPaths;
if (ovens === undefined || cabinet === undefined) throw new Error('the bakery lab has two wrong paths');
const cards = (change: (card: { text: string; bucket: string }, i: number) => object) => ({
  ...lab,
  spec: { ...spec, cards: spec.cards.map(change) },
});
const write = (name: string, why: string, value: unknown): void =>
  writeFileSync(
    `tests/content/bad/${name}`,
    `# fixture-target: content/labs/01-baking-basics/sort-the-bakes.yaml\n# ${why}\n${stringify(value)}`,
  );

write(
  'r11-solution-misses-goal.yaml',
  'The solution never places the empty-overnight card, so it leaves g2 unmet.',
  { ...lab, solution: lab.solution.slice(0, 3) },
);
write(
  'r11-wrong-path-passes.yaml',
  'The second wrong path is the solution, so it meets g2, the goal it should fail.',
  { ...lab, wrongPaths: [ovens, { ...cabinet, actions: lab.solution }] },
);
write(
  'r11-wrong-path-unfinished.yaml',
  'The first wrong path places three cards right and leaves one in the tray, so it fails only by being unfinished.',
  { ...lab, wrongPaths: [{ ...ovens, actions: lab.solution.slice(1) }, cabinet] },
);
write(
  'r11-bad-spec.yaml',
  'The first card names a bucket the spec does not have.',
  cards((c, i) => (i === 0 ? { ...c, bucket: 'bread-van' } : c)),
);
write(
  'r11-retired-plan-name.yaml',
  'A card names Business Support without the plus, a plan the exam no longer uses.',
  cards((c, i) => (i === 3 ? { ...c, text: 'Night help from Business Support for the van' } : c)),
);
```

Create `tests/unit/r11.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { SortAction, SortSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import { r11 } from '../../scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts';
import { replayLab } from '../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import type { AnyLabEngine, EngineEntry } from '../../src/labs/engines.ts';
import { SORT, type SortState } from '../../src/labs/sort/model.ts';
import { FIXTURE_TODAY, makeFixtureRoot } from '../fixtures/content-root.ts';
import { bakeryLab, dropFixtureLab, writeLab } from '../fixtures/labs.ts';

/** The engine `engineOf` hands R11: the real registry while `entry` is undefined, else this stub (null: none). */
const stub = vi.hoisted(() => ({ entry: undefined as EngineEntry | null | undefined }));
vi.mock('../../src/labs/engines.ts', async (importOriginal) => {
  const real = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/labs/engines.ts')>();
  return { ...real, engineOf: (id: string) => (stub.entry === undefined ? real.engineOf(id) : stub.entry) };
});
afterEach(() => {
  stub.entry = undefined;
});

const LAB = bakeryLab();
const SPEC = LAB.spec as SortSpec;
const SOLUTION = LAB.solution as SortAction[];
const [WRONG_OVENS, WRONG_CABINET] = LAB.wrongPaths;
if (WRONG_OVENS === undefined || WRONG_CABINET === undefined)
  throw new Error('the bakery lab has two wrong paths');

/** R11 messages after `edit` changes the fixture tree (by default: nothing). */
function r11Of(edit: (root: string) => void = () => {}): string[] {
  const root = makeFixtureRoot();
  edit(root);
  return r11.run(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY })).map((f) => f.message);
}
const withLab = (lab: unknown) => (root: string) => writeLab(root, lab);
const withCards = (cards: SortSpec['cards']) => withLab({ ...LAB, spec: { ...SPEC, cards } });

describe('R11 labs replay (spec 2.9)', () => {
  it('passes the fixture lab, a retired lab whose solution misses, and a tree with no labs', () => {
    expect(r11Of()).toEqual([]);
    expect(r11Of(withLab(bakeryLab({ status: 'retired', solution: SOLUTION.slice(0, 1) })))).toEqual([]);
    expect(r11Of(dropFixtureLab)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reports an engine this build does not ship', () => {
    stub.entry = null;
    expect(r11Of()).toEqual(['lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: engine sort is not available yet']);
  });

  it('reports a spec or an action that breaks the engine schemas', () => {
    expect(r11Of(withCards(SPEC.cards.map((c, i) => (i === 0 ? { ...c, bucket: 'bread-van' } : c))))).toEqual(
      ['lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: spec: cards.0.bucket: names no bucket: bread-van'],
    );
    const badCard: SortAction = { t: 'place', card: 'Saturday Ovens', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' };
    expect(r11Of(withLab({ ...LAB, solution: [badCard, ...SOLUTION.slice(1)] }))).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: solution[0].card: must be kebab-case',
    ]);
    const drag = { t: 'drag', card: 'forty-minutes' };
    const wrongPaths = [WRONG_OVENS, { ...WRONG_CABINET, actions: [...WRONG_CABINET.actions, drag] }];
    expect(r11Of(withLab({ ...LAB, wrongPaths }))).toEqual([
      "lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: wrongPaths[1].actions[4].t: Invalid discriminator value. Expected 'place' | 'unplace' | 'reset'",
    ]);
  });

  it('reports goals that do not match the spec', () => {
    const goals = [
      ...LAB.goals,
      { id: 'g3', label: 'Every loaf is labelled', hint: 'Ask which card belongs to this goal.' },
    ];
    const wrongPaths = [...LAB.wrongPaths, { ...WRONG_OVENS, failsGoal: 'g3' }];
    expect(r11Of(withLab({ ...LAB, goals, wrongPaths }))).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: goals do not match the spec: g3',
    ]);
  });

  it('reports a solution that leaves a goal unmet', () => {
    expect(r11Of(withLab({ ...LAB, solution: SOLUTION.slice(0, 3) }))).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: the solution leaves g2 unmet: 1 of 2 right',
    ]);
  });

  it('reports a wrong path that leaves the board unfinished, and one that meets the goal it should fail', () => {
    const unfinished = { ...WRONG_OVENS, actions: SOLUTION.slice(1) };
    const passing = { ...WRONG_CABINET, actions: SOLUTION };
    expect(r11Of(withLab({ ...LAB, wrongPaths: [unfinished, passing] }))).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: wrongPaths[0] (Treats borrowed ovens as a cabinet billed by the minute) leaves the board unfinished',
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: wrongPaths[1] (Treats a rented cabinet as an oven borrowed from the kitchen) meets g2, which it should fail',
    ]);
  });

  it('reports a retired Support plan name in lab text and passes the current one', () => {
    const named = (text: string) => withCards(SPEC.cards.map((c, i) => (i === 3 ? { ...c, text } : c)));
    expect(r11Of(named('Night help from Business Support for the van'))).toEqual([
      "lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: retired Support plan name in 'Night help from Business Support for the van'",
    ]);
    expect(r11Of(named('Night help from AWS Business Support+ for the van'))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reports a start that already meets every goal', () => {
    const solvedAtStart = { ...SORT, init: (spec: SortSpec) => replayLab(SORT, spec, SOLUTION) };
    stub.entry = { engine: solvedAtStart as unknown as AnyLabEngine, settle: [], complete: () => true };
    expect(r11Of()).toEqual(['lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: the starting state already meets every goal']);
  });

  it('checks the start, the solution and every wrong path after the settle actions', () => {
    const settleSolves = {
      ...SORT,
      reduce: (state: SortState, action: SortAction | { t: 'settle' }): SortState =>
        action.t === 'settle'
          ? SOLUTION.reduce((s, a) => SORT.reduce(s, a), state)
          : SORT.reduce(state, action),
    };
    stub.entry = {
      engine: settleSolves as unknown as AnyLabEngine,
      settle: [{ t: 'settle' }],
      complete: () => true,
    };
    expect(r11Of()).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: the starting state already meets every goal',
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: wrongPaths[0] (Treats borrowed ovens as a cabinet billed by the minute) meets g1, which it should fail',
      'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes: wrongPaths[1] (Treats a rented cabinet as an oven borrowed from the kitchen) meets g2, which it should fail',
    ]);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/content/labs-replay.test.ts`:

```ts
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { LABS_DIR, loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import { labProblems } from '../../scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts';

/** Every shipped lab replays its solution and wrong paths, one named test per lab (spec 9, the Phase 4 exit). */
const ROOT = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
const ctx = loadContext(ROOT);

describe('the real labs', () => {
  it('finds every lab file', () => {
    const files = ctx.files.filter((path) => path.startsWith(`${LABS_DIR}/`) && path.endsWith('.yaml'));
    expect(ctx.labs.map((e) => e.path)).toEqual(files);
  });

  if (ctx.labs.length === 0) {
    it('has no labs yet', () => {
      expect(ctx.labs).toEqual([]);
    });
  } else {
    it.each(ctx.labs.map((e) => [e.lab.id, e] as const))(
      '%s replays its solution and wrong paths',
      (_, e) => {
        expect(labProblems(e)).toEqual([]);
      },
    );
  }
});
```

```diff
+  /** R2 findings with `heads` as the HEAD lab files, after `edit` changes the working tree. */
+  function withLabHead(heads: Record<string, string>, edit: (root: string) => void = () => {}): string[] {
+    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
+    edit(root);
+    const ctx = loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY });
+    const withHeadFiles: Context = {
+      ...ctx,
+      headPaths: (prefix) => Object.keys(heads).filter((path) => path.startsWith(prefix)),
+      head: (path) => heads[path] ?? null,
+    };
+    return runRules(withHeadFiles, ['R2']).findings.map((f) => `${f.file}  ${f.message}`);
+  }

+    ]);
+  });
+

+    const old = stringify(bakeryLab({ id: 'lab-m01-old-bakes' }));
+    expect(withLabHead({ [FIXTURE_LAB]: stringify(bakeryLab()), [OLD_LAB]: old })).toEqual([
+      `${OLD_LAB}  lab lab-m01-old-bakes was in HEAD and is gone; retire it instead`,
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('needs a higher rev for any edit to a lab of HEAD, and nothing for an unchanged lab', () => {
+    const head = { [FIXTURE_LAB]: stringify(bakeryLab()) };
+    expect(withLabHead(head)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(withLabHead(head, (root) => writeLab(root, bakeryLab({ title: 'Sort the loaves' })))).toEqual([
+      `${FIXTURE_LAB}  lab lab-m01-sort-the-bakes changed since HEAD, so it needs rev 2`,
+    ]);
+    expect(
+      withLabHead(head, (root) => writeLab(root, bakeryLab({ title: 'Sort the loaves', rev: 2 }))),
+    ).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the tests and see them fail**

```bash
pnpm vitest run tests/unit/r11.test.ts tests/unit/r02.test.ts tests/unit/r06.test.ts tests/unit/r07.test.ts tests/unit/context.test.ts tests/unit/vite-plugin.test.ts tests/content
```

Expected: `Test Files  8 failed (8)` and `Tests  21 failed | 71 passed (92)`.
`tests/unit/r11.test.ts` and `tests/content/labs-replay.test.ts` fail to load `scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts`; `bad.test.ts` fails "cover every implemented rule" because R11 has fixtures but no rule, and each `r11-*.yaml` fixture fires nothing.
The new R2, R6, R7 and context lab tests, and the R6 question rows that now also expect lab lines, fail because nothing loads or checks labs yet.
In `vite-plugin.test.ts` the new R11 build test and the three existing build and dev-server tests fail with `R11 is switched on but not implemented yet`, because Step 1 already switched R11 on in the fixture `validate.yaml`.

Create `scripts/content/lib/labs.ts`:

```ts
import type { LabEngineId } from '../../../schemas/ids.ts';
import { type LabFile, SortSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';

/** Each engine's visible spec strings, from a spec that parses; [] for one that does not (R11 reports that). */
export const SPEC_TEXTS: { readonly [E in LabEngineId]?: (spec: unknown) => string[] } = {
  sort(spec) {
    const parsed = SortSpec.safeParse(spec);
    if (!parsed.success) return [];
    return [
      ...parsed.data.buckets.flatMap((b) => [b.label, ...(b.detail === undefined ? [] : [b.detail])]),
      ...parsed.data.cards.map((c) => c.text),
    ];
  },
};

/** Every string of a lab that Dani reads or a writer wrote: R7 runs its course check on them and R11 its name check. */
export function labTexts(lab: LabFile): string[] {
  return [
    lab.title,
    lab.brief,
    ...lab.goals.flatMap((g) => [g.label, g.hint]),
    ...lab.wrongPaths.map((p) => p.mistake),
    ...lab.debrief.lines,
    ...(SPEC_TEXTS[lab.engine]?.(lab.spec) ?? []),
  ];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add R11 and switch it on**

Create `scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts`:

```ts
import { LAB_SCHEMAS } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { allPass, replayLab } from '../../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import { checkLab, engineOf } from '../../../src/labs/engines.ts';
import type { Finding, LabEntry } from '../lib/context.ts';
import { labTexts } from '../lib/labs.ts';
import { labStyleProblems } from '../lib/style.ts';
import { finding, type Rule } from './rule.ts';

type Issue = { readonly path: readonly PropertyKey[]; readonly message: string };
const where = (issue: Issue): string => issue.path.map(String).join('.');

/**
 * The R11 problems of one live lab, each prefixed with its id: the spec and every action parse with the engine's
 * schemas, the goals match the spec, the start fails a goal, the solution meets every goal, and each wrong path
 * finishes the board and fails its named goal, all judged the way the lab screen checks (`checkLab`, after `settle`).
 * Used by R11, the draft check and the replay test.
 */
export function labProblems(entry: LabEntry): string[] {
  const { lab } = entry;
  if (lab.status !== 'live') return [];
  const say = (message: string): string => `${lab.id}: ${message}`;
  const style = labStyleProblems(labTexts(lab)).map(say);
  const schemas = LAB_SCHEMAS[lab.engine];
  const engine = engineOf(lab.engine);
  if (schemas === undefined || engine === null)
    return [say(`engine ${lab.engine} is not available yet`), ...style];

  const problems: string[] = [];
  const spec = schemas.spec.safeParse(lab.spec);
  if (!spec.success)
    for (const issue of spec.error.issues) problems.push(say(`spec: ${where(issue)}: ${issue.message}`));
  const actions = (list: readonly unknown[], label: string): unknown[] =>
    list.map((action, i) => {
      const parsed = schemas.action.safeParse(action);
      if (parsed.success) return parsed.data;
      for (const issue of parsed.error.issues) {
        const path = where(issue);
        problems.push(say(`${label}[${i}]${path === '' ? '' : `.${path}`}: ${issue.message}`));
      }
      return action;
    });
  const solution = actions(lab.solution, 'solution');
  const wrongPaths = lab.wrongPaths.map((p, j) => ({
    ...p,
    actions: actions(p.actions, `wrongPaths[${j}].actions`),
  }));
  if (!spec.success || problems.length > 0) return [...problems, ...style];

  const start = checkLab(engine, spec.data, engine.engine.init(spec.data));
  const inFile = lab.goals.map((g) => g.id);
  const inSpec = start.map((r) => r.goalId);
  const differ = [
    ...inFile.filter((id) => !inSpec.includes(id)),
    ...inSpec.filter((id) => !inFile.includes(id)),
  ];
  if (differ.length > 0) return [say(`goals do not match the spec: ${differ.sort().join(', ')}`), ...style];

  if (allPass(start)) problems.push(say('the starting state already meets every goal'));
  const solved = checkLab(engine, spec.data, replayLab(engine.engine, spec.data, solution));
  for (const r of solved)
    if (!r.pass) problems.push(say(`the solution leaves ${r.goalId} unmet: ${r.message}`));
  for (const [j, path] of wrongPaths.entries()) {
    const state = replayLab(engine.engine, spec.data, path.actions);
    const settled = engine.settle.reduce((s: unknown, action) => engine.engine.reduce(s, action), state);
    if (!engine.complete(spec.data, settled)) {
      problems.push(say(`wrongPaths[${j}] (${path.mistake}) leaves the board unfinished`));
      continue;
    }
    const result = checkLab(engine, spec.data, state).find((r) => r.goalId === path.failsGoal);
    if (result?.pass === true) {
      problems.push(say(`wrongPaths[${j}] (${path.mistake}) meets ${path.failsGoal}, which it should fail`));
    }
  }
  return [...problems, ...style];
}

/** R11 Labs replay (spec 2.9): every live lab replays its solution and wrong paths with the code the app runs. */
export const r11: Rule = {
  id: 'R11',
  title: 'Labs replay',
  run(ctx) {
    const out: Finding[] = [];
    for (const entry of ctx.labs)
      for (const message of labProblems(entry)) out.push(finding('R11', entry.path, message));
    return out;
  },
};
```

```diff
+/** A lab file's parsed YAML as a record, or null when it is missing, does not parse or is not a mapping. */
+function labRecord(yamlText: string | null): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+  if (yamlText === null) return null;
+  try {
+    const data: unknown = parseYaml(yamlText);
+    return typeof data === 'object' && data !== null && !Array.isArray(data)
+      ? (data as Record<string, unknown>)
+      : null;
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+/** A lab record without its `rev`, as text, so two versions compare on everything else. */
+function withoutRev(lab: Record<string, unknown>): string {
+  const { rev: _, ...rest } = lab;
+  return JSON.stringify(rest);
+}
+
 /** The forms of a forms.yaml text that pass the schema; [] when it is missing or does not parse. */
 function formsIn(yamlText: string | null): Forms['forms'] {
   if (yamlText === null) return [];

  * disappears; a live or retired question in HEAD stays, and a live question whose answer changes takes a key-fix
- * version; a form frozen in HEAD keeps its qids, their order and `frozen: true` (spec 4.3).
+ * version; a lab in HEAD stays, and any edit to it raises its `rev`; a form frozen in HEAD keeps its qids, their
+ * order and `frozen: true` (spec 4.3, 5.1).
  */
 export const r02: Rule = {
   id: 'R2',
@@ -121,6 +142,7 @@ export const r02: Rule = {

     for (const entry of ctx.questions) claim(entry.q.id, 'question', entry.path);
+    for (const entry of ctx.labs) claim(entry.lab.id, 'lab', entry.path);
 
     // A working tree that does not parse is R1's finding; comparing it with HEAD would only bury that one error.
     const before = conceptIdsIn(ctx.head(CONCEPTS_FILE));
@@ -185,6 +207,32 @@ export const r02: Rule = {
       }
     }
 
+    // Labs have no drafts: every lab in HEAD has shipped, so it stays (retired in place) and each edit raises its rev.
+    // A lab file that does not parse is R1's finding, not a lab that is gone.
+    const labIds = new Set<string>();
+    const unreadableLabs = new Set<string>();
+    for (const path of ctx.files) {
+      if (!path.startsWith(`${LABS_DIR}/`) || !path.endsWith('.yaml')) continue;
+      const lab = labRecord(ctx.read(path));
+      if (lab === null) unreadableLabs.add(path);
+      else for (const id of strings(lab.id)) labIds.add(id);
+    }
+    for (const path of ctx.headPaths(`${LABS_DIR}/`)) {
+      const before = labRecord(ctx.head(path));
+      const id = before === null ? undefined : strings(before.id)[0];
+      if (before === null || id === undefined || unreadableLabs.has(path)) continue;
+      if (!labIds.has(id)) {
+        out.push(finding('R2', path, `lab ${id} was in HEAD and is gone; retire it instead`));
+        continue;
+      }
+      const after = labRecord(ctx.read(path));
+      const was = typeof before.rev === 'number' ? before.rev : 0;
+      const now = typeof after?.rev === 'number' ? after.rev : 0;
+      if (after !== null && withoutRev(after) !== withoutRev(before) && now <= was) {
+        out.push(finding('R2', path, `lab ${id} changed since HEAD, so it needs rev ${was + 1}`));
+      }
+    }
+
     // A forms.yaml that does not parse is R1's finding; a missing one breaks every form frozen in HEAD.
     if (ctx.forms !== null || ctx.read(FORMS_FILE) === null) {
       const now = new Map((ctx.forms?.forms ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f] as const));
```

```diff
+
+    // Labs: concepts and notes resolve, and the debrief asks live practice questions on the lab's own concepts,
+    // named by id only so no exam item text can reach a finding (spec 5.1).
+    const resolve = (id: string): string =>
+      ctx.concepts.find((c) => c.id === id || (c.aliases ?? []).includes(id))?.id ?? id;
+    for (const { path, lab } of ctx.labs) {
+      if (lab.status === 'retired') continue;
+      const report = (message: string): void => {
+        out.push(finding('R6', path, `${lab.id}: ${message}`));
+      };
+      for (const id of lab.concepts) {
+        const concept = ctx.concepts.find((c) => c.id === id || (c.aliases ?? []).includes(id));
+        if (concept === undefined) report(`concept '${id}' is not registered`);
+        else if (concept.tested === false) report(`concept '${id}' is marked tested: false`);
+      }
+      for (const id of lab.notes) if (!lessons.has(id)) report(`note ${id} is not a note`);
+      const concepts = new Set(lab.concepts.map(resolve));
+      for (const qid of lab.debrief.questions) {
+        const entry = ctx.questions.find(
+          (e) => e.q.id === qid && e.q.pool === 'practice' && e.q.status === 'live',
+        );
+        if (entry === undefined) report(`debrief question ${qid} is not a live practice question`);
+        else if (!concepts.has(resolve(entry.q.concept))) {
+          report(`debrief question ${qid} is on ${entry.q.concept}, which the lab does not list`);
+        }
+      }
+    }
     return out;
   },
 };
```

## Task 3: The lab writer tooling and prompts

**Files:**
- Create: `agents/lab-writer.md`, `agents/lab-critic.md`
- Modify: `scripts/content/lib/draft.ts`, `scripts/content/validate.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/report.ts`, `scripts/content/report.ts`, `content/STYLE.md`
- Test: `tests/unit/draft.test.ts`, `tests/unit/coverage.test.ts`, `tests/unit/agents.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `labDraftCheck(ctx, file)` runs R1, R2, R6, R7, R8 and R11 over the context and keeps the findings on that one file, formatted with `formatFinding`; `pnpm content:validate --draft <file>` sends any path under `content/labs/` to it and prints `draft check <file>: 1 lab, <p> problems`.
- A lab has no draft status, so the draft check runs the rules as they are, and a lab that fails its schema counts as `0 labs` with its R1 lines.
- The CLI reads the repository root, so the tests assert `labDraftCheck`'s `{ labs, problems }`, which is exactly what the CLI prints.
- `labTargets(ctx)` lists every module in order with its folder name (`dir`, taken from its `resources/` folder, the same names as `content/questions/`), the lab ids already written, and each tested concept homed in it with its live practice question ids and types, never an exam item or a draft; `pnpm content:report --lab-targets` prints it as JSON for the lab writers.
- `report(ctx)` gains `Labs: <n> live (<c> core, <s> stretch); sort <a>, configure <b>, simulate <d>` after the `questions:` line, and one `mNN labs: <k>` line per module with a live lab before `concepts.md drift:`.
- `agents/lab-writer.md` (version 1) writes one module's labs, one file per lab, in its own folder, with a complete Sort example in the bakery world (the Task 1 fixture lab), the note id rule, the `minutes` rule, the content rules, the revise mode, the `--draft` loop and a JSON return; Tasks 12 and 13 add the Configure and Simulate sections.
- `agents/lab-critic.md` (version 1) judges one lab file and never edits: grounding, wrong paths, alternatives, originality, style, current names and the debrief, returning `{ lab, approved, issues }`.
- `content/STYLE.md` gains `## Labs` at its end with the H3s `Brief`, `Cards and buckets`, `Goals and hints`, `Wrong paths`, `Debrief`, `Lab credits and lab values` and `Grounding`; Tasks 12 and 13 add their H3s inside it, and Task 14 appends `## Incidents` after it.
- Covers P4-019 (labs), P4-025 (lab-writer), P4-026, P4-027 (labs), P4-036, P4-038 and P4-051 (style).

Apply this change to `scripts/content/lib/draft.ts`:

```diff
diff --git a/scripts/content/lib/draft.ts b/scripts/content/lib/draft.ts
--- a/scripts/content/lib/draft.ts
+++ b/scripts/content/lib/draft.ts
@@ -1,14 +1,17 @@
-/** The `--draft <file>` and `--demote` modes of pnpm content:validate (spec 2.7, 2.9 and 8.4). */
+/** The `--draft <file>` and `--demote` modes of pnpm content:validate (spec 2.7, 2.9, 5.1 and 8.4). */
 import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
 import { join } from 'node:path';
 import { MAX_PER_CONCEPT } from '../../../src/engine/quotas.ts';
 import { formatFinding, isExamFile, redact, redactMessage } from '../rules/index.ts';
 import { r01 } from '../rules/r01-schema.ts';
+import { r02 } from '../rules/r02-ids.ts';
 import { r03 } from '../rules/r03-answer-counts.ts';
 import { r04 } from '../rules/r04-explanations.ts';
 import { r05 } from '../rules/r05-evidence.ts';
 import { r06 } from '../rules/r06-references.ts';
 import { r07 } from '../rules/r07-originality.ts';
+import { r08 } from '../rules/r08-style.ts';
+import { r11 } from '../rules/r11-labs.ts';
 import type { Rule } from '../rules/rule.ts';
 import type { Context, LessonNote, QuestionEntry } from './context.ts';
 import { examConceptCounts } from './coverage.ts';
@@ -69,6 +72,16 @@ export function draftCheck(ctx: Context, file: string): { questions: number; pro
   return { questions: entries.length, problems };
 }
 
+/** The rules a lab draft must pass, in id order: schema, ids, references, originality, style and the replay. */
+const LAB_DRAFT_RULES: readonly Rule[] = [r01, r02, r06, r07, r08, r11];
+
+/** Every problem `--draft` reports for one lab file: the findings of the lab rules on that file, R11 included. */
+export function labDraftCheck(ctx: Context, file: string): { labs: number; problems: string[] } {
+  const findings = LAB_DRAFT_RULES.flatMap((rule) => rule.run(ctx)).filter((f) => f.file === file);
+  findings.sort((a, b) => Number(a.rule.slice(1)) - Number(b.rule.slice(1)) || (a.line ?? 0) - (b.line ?? 0));
+  return { labs: ctx.labs.filter((e) => e.path === file).length, problems: findings.map(formatFinding) };
+}
+
 /** Sets every live question that fails R5 back to draft in its file, and returns one line per question it demoted. */
 export function demote(ctx: Context): string[] {
   const failing = new Set(r05.run(ctx).map((f) => `${f.file}:${f.line ?? 0}`));
```

```diff
+/**
+ * `--lab-targets`: for each module, in order, its folder name, the lab ids already written, and each tested concept
+ * homed in it with the ids and types of its live practice questions (debrief candidates; never exam items).
+ */
+export function labTargets(ctx: Context): LabTarget[] {
+  const aliasOf = new Map<string, string>();
+  for (const c of ctx.concepts) for (const alias of c.aliases ?? []) aliasOf.set(alias, c.id);
+  const live = ctx.questions.filter((e) => e.q.pool === 'practice' && e.q.status === 'live');
+  const out: LabTarget[] = [];
+  for (const readme of ctx.notes.filter((n) => n.kind === 'module')) {
+    const m = readme.module;
+    const prefix = `m${pad2(m)}/`;
+    out.push({
+      module: m,
+      dir: readme.path.split('/')[1] ?? '',
+      labs: ctx.labs.filter((e) => e.module === m).map((e) => e.lab.id),
+      concepts: ctx.concepts
+        .filter((c) => c.tested !== false && c.home.startsWith(prefix))
+        .map((c) => ({
+          id: c.id,
+          name: c.name,
+          note: c.home,
+          tasks: [...c.tasks],
+          practice: live
+            .filter((e) => (aliasOf.get(e.q.concept) ?? e.q.concept) === c.id)
+            .map((e) => ({ id: e.q.id, type: e.q.type })),
+        })),
+    });
+  }
+  return out;
+}
```
