- [ ] **Step 5: Add the Labs section of the style guide and the two prompts**

Apply this change to `content/STYLE.md`:

```diff
diff --git a/content/STYLE.md b/content/STYLE.md
--- a/content/STYLE.md
+++ b/content/STYLE.md
@@ -109,3 +109,55 @@ Exam-pool items live in `content/exam/pool/NN-<module-slug>.yaml` and fill the m
 
 - Good: `A regional clinic stores scanned patient forms that staff open often in the first month and rarely after that.`
 - Bad: `The cafe's loyalty app stores receipts.` sets an exam item in the cafe.
+
+## Labs
+
+These rules hold for every lab in `content/labs/` (spec 5), written by the lab writer and judged by the lab critic.
+`pnpm content:validate --draft <file>` checks the schema, the references, originality, retired plan names and the R11 replay.
+Lab text is plain text: no Markdown and no HTML.
+
+### Brief
+
+The brief sets one cafe situation in 1 to 3 sentences and says what to do.
+
+- Good: `The cafe's back office runs on servers in a cupboard, and the owner wants to know who looks after what. Sort each job to the side that owns it.`
+- Bad: `Sort these AWS services.` sets no situation.
+
+### Cards and buckets
+
+Card text names the need, never the answer.
+
+- Good: `Nightly copies of the order history that must survive a lost building`.
+- Bad: `Back up with Amazon S3` gives the bucket away.
+- Give each card one right bucket, and list in `also` any other bucket a careful reader could defend.
+
+### Goals and hints
+
+A goal label says what a finished goal looks like, and its hint teaches the idea without naming a placement.
+
+- Good hint: `Ask who touches the item: the cafe's staff, or the people who run the data center.`
+- Bad hint: `The patching card goes under AWS.`
+
+### Wrong paths
+
+Each wrong path models one mistake a learner really makes, named in its `mistake`, and finishes the board.
+
+- Good mistake: `Thinks AWS patches the guest operating system of an EC2 instance`.
+- Bad mistake: `Random placement`.
+
+### Debrief
+
+The 2 to 4 debrief lines state what the lab showed, one idea per line, and the debrief asks 2 or 3 live practice questions on the lab's concepts.
+
+### Lab credits and lab values
+
+Prices appear only as lab credits, a made-up unit, and never as dollars or real AWS prices.
+Any figure the cited notes do not state is called a lab value.
+
+- Good: `Each server costs 4 lab credits an hour (a lab value).`
+- Bad: `Each server costs $0.0416 an hour.`
+
+### Grounding
+
+Every card placement, goal, hint and debrief line rests on a line of a note the lab lists in `notes`.
+Support plans appear only by their four current names: Basic Support, AWS Business Support+, AWS Enterprise Support and AWS Unified Operations.
```

Create `agents/lab-writer.md`:

````markdown
---
name: lab-writer
version: 1
---

# Lab writer

You write the core labs of one module, one YAML file per lab, into that module's folder under `content/labs/` (spec 5).
A fresh lab critic then judges each file, and R11 replays it, so every lab you write must pass `pnpm content:validate --draft` before you return.
This version of the prompt covers the Sort engine; the Configure and Simulate sections arrive with their engines.

## Inputs

- Your module's entry from `pnpm --silent content:report --lab-targets`, given in your task: `module`, the folder name `dir`, the lab ids already written (`labs`), and each tested concept homed in the module with its `name`, home `note`, `tasks` and its live practice questions (`practice`, ids and types only).
- The row of spec 5.2 for each lab you write, given in your task: the lab title, its engine and what it teaches.
- `content/STYLE.md`, and its `## Labs` section above all; read it in full before you write.
- `src/labs/contract.ts`, the engine's `src/labs/<engine>/model.ts`, and the lab shapes in `schemas/lab.ts`, so you know exactly how a board is checked.
- The notes you cite, under `resources/NN-<module-slug>/`, and any other lesson note a card needs.
- `content/concepts.yaml` and `resources/glossary.md`, read only, to pick concept ids and check names.
- Any practice file under `content/questions/`, in any module, read only to look up the ids and stems of the debrief questions you pick.

Never read `~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/`, the course source text, `content/fingerprints/`, anything under `content/exam/`, AWS exam questions or `.results/checks/keys.json`.
Your labs must come from the notes in your own words, so nothing can echo the course or the exam.

## The file

A lab lives in `content/labs/<dir>/<file-slug>.yaml`, and its `id` is `lab-mNN-<file-slug>`, where `NN` is your two-digit module number.
A note id is `mNN/` plus the lesson file name without its `NN-` prefix and `.md`, so `resources/02-compute-in-the-cloud/01-introduction-to-amazon-ec2.md` is `m02/introduction-to-amazon-ec2`.
A lab may cite a note that homes none of its concepts, as long as a card or a goal rests on a line of it.

This complete example is a Sort lab in the made-up bakery world of the test fixtures; your labs are set in the cafe instead.

```yaml
id: lab-m01-sort-the-bakes
module: 1
title: Sort the bakes
engine: sort
tier: core
status: live
rev: 1
concepts: [shared-kitchen, per-minute-rental]
notes: [m01/what-a-bakery-cloud-is]
minutes: 4
brief: >-
  The harbour branch runs short of oven space on Saturdays and pays for cabinets it barely uses.
  Sort each need under the bakery cloud idea that answers it.
spec:
  buckets:
    - id: shared-kitchen
      label: Shared kitchen
    - id: per-minute-rental
      label: Per-minute rental
      detail: Pay only while it is in use
  cards:
    - id: saturday-ovens
      text: Two extra ovens for the long Saturday queue
      bucket: shared-kitchen
      goal: g1
    - id: harbour-trays
      text: Extra harbour trays baked away from the branch
      bucket: shared-kitchen
      goal: g1
    - id: forty-minutes
      text: Forty minutes of rising time for the rye dough
      bucket: per-minute-rental
      goal: g2
    - id: empty-overnight
      text: A cabinet nobody needs between closing and dawn
      bucket: per-minute-rental
      goal: g2
goals:
  - id: g1
    label: Oven needs sit under Shared kitchen
    hint: Ask whether the branch borrows ovens it does not own while its queue is long.
  - id: g2
    label: Cabinet needs sit under Per-minute rental
    hint: Ask whether the branch pays only for the minutes the dough is inside.
solution:
  - { t: place, card: saturday-ovens, bucket: shared-kitchen }
  - { t: place, card: harbour-trays, bucket: shared-kitchen }
  - { t: place, card: forty-minutes, bucket: per-minute-rental }
  - { t: place, card: empty-overnight, bucket: per-minute-rental }
wrongPaths:
  - mistake: Treats borrowed ovens as a cabinet billed by the minute
    failsGoal: g1
    actions:
      - { t: place, card: saturday-ovens, bucket: per-minute-rental }
      - { t: place, card: harbour-trays, bucket: shared-kitchen }
      - { t: place, card: forty-minutes, bucket: per-minute-rental }
      - { t: place, card: empty-overnight, bucket: per-minute-rental }
  - mistake: Treats a rented cabinet as an oven borrowed from the kitchen
    failsGoal: g2
    actions:
      - { t: place, card: saturday-ovens, bucket: shared-kitchen }
      - { t: place, card: harbour-trays, bucket: shared-kitchen }
      - { t: place, card: forty-minutes, bucket: shared-kitchen }
      - { t: place, card: empty-overnight, bucket: per-minute-rental }
debrief:
  lines:
    - Borrowing central ovens covers a long queue without owning ovens that sit idle on quiet days.
    - A cabinet billed by the minute costs nothing once it goes back, so empty hours are free.
  questions: [q-b4k7m2, q-d9p4r6]
```

- A Sort card names its right `bucket`, up to two other buckets it may also sit in (`also`), and the `goal` it counts toward; every goal owns at least one card.
- A goal passes when every one of its cards sits in its bucket or in one of its `also` buckets.
- `solution` and each wrong path's `actions` are Sort actions: `{ t: place, card, bucket }`, `{ t: unplace, card }` and `{ t: reset }`.
- A wrong path places every card, so the board is finished, and fails its `failsGoal` because of the one mistake it models.

## Rules

- Write only your module's folder `content/labs/<dir>/`, one file per lab, with `tier: core`, `rev: 1` and `status: live`.
- Set `minutes` for a Sort lab to 2 plus one per 3 cards, rounded up, so 4 cards take 4 minutes and 9 cards take 5.
- Write original text set in the cafe, in your own words, and never course wording.
- Rest every card placement, goal, hint and debrief line on a line of a note you list in `notes`.
- Use the exam's current names from the STYLE section "Current names", and name a Support plan only by its four current names.
- Write a number only when a cited note states it, and call any other figure a lab value.
- Give every goal at least one wrong path, and make each one model a real learner mistake named in `mistake`.
- Pick 2 or 3 debrief questions from the `practice` ids of the lab's own concepts, preferring `single`, and list them in `debrief.questions`.
- List every concept a card or a debrief question rests on in `concepts`, by its registered id.

## Checking your file

Run `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>` for each file and fix every problem it prints, until its last line ends with `0 problems`.
It checks the schema, the ids, the concepts, notes and debrief questions, originality against the course, the style rules and the R11 replay of your solution and wrong paths.

## Revising

When your task says to revise, it lists the critic's issues for one file.
Fix those issues only, keep the lab's `id`, and keep `rev: 1` until the lab is first committed.
Loop on `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>` until it ends with `0 problems` again.

## Return

Return only this JSON: `{ "module": <n>, "files": [<paths>], "labs": [<ids>], "problems": [<anything you could not fix>] }`.
````

Create `agents/lab-critic.md`:

```markdown
---
name: lab-critic
version: 1
---

# Lab critic

You judge one lab file against its notes and `content/STYLE.md`, and you never edit any file (spec 5).
The lab writer revises the lab from the issues you return, so name each one precisely and say how to fix it.

## Inputs

- The lab file path, given in your task, and the spec 5.2 row it implements.
- `content/STYLE.md`, and its `## Labs` section above all.
- `src/labs/contract.ts`, the engine's `src/labs/<engine>/model.ts` and the lab shapes in `schemas/lab.ts`.
- Every note the lab lists in `notes`, under `resources/`, plus `content/concepts.yaml` and `resources/glossary.md`.
- For each debrief question id, its entry in the practice file under `content/questions/` that holds it, read for its id, concept and stem only.
- The output of `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>`, run from the repository root; read its R7 and R11 lines.

Never read `~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/`, the course source text, `content/fingerprints/`, anything under `content/exam/`, AWS exam questions or `.results/checks/keys.json`.

## What to check

- Grounding: for every card placement, goal, hint and debrief line, name the note line it rests on; anything with no such line is an issue.
- Wrong paths: each wrong path models a mistake a learner would really make, its `mistake` says which, and it fails its `failsGoal` for that reason.
- Alternatives: no card could defensibly sit in a second bucket unless that bucket is in its `also` list.
- Originality: the text is the writer's own words, set in the cafe, and the `--draft` run shows no R7 line.
- Style: the brief, cards, goals, hints and debrief follow the STYLE `## Labs` rules, and the `--draft` run shows no R11 line.
- Current names: every service and every Support plan carries the exam's current name.
- Debrief: the 2 or 3 questions test what the lab just practised, on the lab's own concepts.

## Return

Return only this JSON: `{ "lab": "<id>", "approved": <true or false>, "issues": [{ "kind": "grounding" | "wrongPath" | "alternatives" | "originality" | "style" | "debrief", "where": "<field or card id>", "fix": "<what to change>" }] }`.
Approve only with no issues.
```

## Task 4: Labs in the module chunks, the index and search

**Files:**
- Modify: `schemas/chunks.ts`, `scripts/content/build.ts`, `tests/fixtures/index.ts`, `src/app/screens/Palette.tsx`, `src/app/search.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/build.test.ts`, `tests/unit/build-real.test.ts` (unchanged, still holding every module chunk within 120 KB gzipped), `tests/unit/leakage.test.ts`, `tests/unit/search.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `ModuleChunk` gains `labs: LabView[]` and `ContentIndex` gains `labs: LabMeta[]`, so Home, the module page and the badges read lab metadata without loading a chunk, and the lab screen gets each lab from its module chunk (spec 2.10); no new chunk files, so the 13-chunk budget check and the service worker precache need no change.
- The build ships live labs only, ordered by module, then core before stretch, then id; `spec` is the zod-parsed engine spec with its defaults applied, and `solution`, `wrongPaths` and each `mistake` never ship.
- Each cited `NoteId` resolves to its lesson's route and title for the "Read in notes" links, and each debrief qid resolves to a `QuestionView` from the live practice questions only, with the same helper the module questions use.
- Any other debrief qid (missing, draft, retired, an exam-pool item, and in Task 14 an incident step) makes the build throw `<lab>: debrief question <qid> is not a live practice question; run pnpm content:validate`, naming the qid only, so no exam item can ever reach a chunk.
- `credits` is true for a Simulate lab or when any lab string mentions lab credits, so the screen labels prices (spec 5).
- `ConceptMeta.labs` lists the live lab ids naming a concept (alias-resolved) and is left out when empty, to keep the startup index small.
- Each lab gets one search entry after every other entry, `{ kind: 'lab', title, detail: 'Module N lab', route: '#/m/NN/lab/<id>', text }`, whose text is the brief plus the concept names; `Palette.tsx` labels it `Lab`.
- `SearchEntry['kind']` gaining `lab` also needs a weight in `src/app/search.ts` (a `Record` over the kinds); labs tie with missions at the bottom of the kind order, which only breaks ties between equal matches.
- `emptyIndexFields()` gains `labs: []`, so every hand-built `ContentIndex` in the tests stays valid.
- The initial JS measures 49.4 KB of its 50 KB budget with Tasks 1 to 4 applied, the same as without this task's two app edits (under 0.05 KB together), so 0.6 KB of headroom is left, not the 1.3 KB the header assumed.
- Covers P4-032, P4-033 (labs), P4-034 (labs), P4-087 (the debrief selector), P4-109 (search) and P4-135.

- Modify: `schemas/events.ts`, `src/engine/replay.ts`, `src/engine/memory.ts`, `src/engine/badges.ts`, `src/engine/next.ts`, `src/engine/xp.ts` (doc comment only), `src/app/runner.ts` (one label), `src/app/progress.ts` (two empty refs), `src/app/memory.ts` (one call site), `src/app/home.ts` (the `lab` branch of `extraLink`), `tests/fixtures/memory.ts`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/content.ts`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts`, `tests/property/arbitraries.ts`, `tests/property/grind.property.test.ts`, `tests/property/replay.property.test.ts`
- Regenerate: `tests/fixtures/backups/v1.snapshot.json`, `v1-exam.snapshot.json`, `v1-memory.snapshot.json`; write once: `tests/fixtures/backups/v1-labs.snapshot.json`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/hands-on-week.jsonl`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/hands-on-week.snapshot.json` (the three existing journey snapshots must come out unchanged)
- Test: `tests/unit/events-schema.test.ts`, `tests/unit/replay.test.ts`, `tests/unit/memory.test.ts`, `tests/unit/badges.test.ts`, `tests/unit/next.test.ts`, `tests/unit/streak.test.ts`, `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts`, `tests/unit/runner.test.ts`, `tests/unit/home.test.ts`, `tests/unit/journeys.test.ts`, `tests/unit/backups.test.ts`, `tests/property/grind.property.test.ts`, `tests/property/replay.property.test.ts`

Design notes:
- This task never reads `ContentIndex.labs`, so it starts at once beside Tasks 1 and 6: `ContentRefs` gains `labs` and `incidents`, and `src/app/progress.ts` sets both to empty sets until Task 8 fills `labs` from `index.labs` and Task 17 fills `incidents` from `index.incidents`.
- Until Task 8 lands, every `lab.done` counts in `unknownEvents`, which no screen of the Sort cut shows before Task 8 ships with it.
- `MODES` gains `debrief` last, an additive enum value, so every older backup stays valid and `SCHEMA_VERSION` stays 1; `COUNTING_MODES` gains it, and `MODE_LABELS.debrief` is `Lab debrief`.
- `derive` keeps the first `lab.done` of each lab id in `Progress.labsDone` as a `LabDone` (`at`, `day`, `rev`, `hints`, `eventId`), never replaced or revoked by a later completion or a higher `rev` (spec 8.4); the lab screen reads the day for its done line and the event id for its badge line.
- `applyLab` in `deriveMemory` now returns early for a lab already done, so only the first completion pays `XP.lab` plus `XP.labNoHints` when `hints` is 0, sets `completion` (the day qualifies) and can grant a badge.
- Hands On counts a module only when it lists at least `CORE_LABS_PER_MODULE` (2) core labs and all are done, so the five modules that ship one core lab in Phase 4 never count early; Lab Complete keeps `all.length >= CORE_LABS`.
- The effort bonus stays as built: `firstOfDay` pays it in every counting mode, so a first debrief answer of the day on a due concept earns it like a review answer, and a test pins that (P4-062).
- `weakestLab` picks, among modules whose lessons are all read and which have an undone lab, the lowest mastery (a missing mastery reads as 0), ties to the lower module, and returns that module's first undone lab in the order given; `extras` pushes the boss, the mock, then the lab, and cuts to `MAX_EXTRAS` (2).
- `src/app/memory.ts` passes `lab: null` until Task 8 computes the lab extra from the index.
- Brief gap resolved here: adding the `lab` member to `Extra` breaks the exhaustive `extraLink` in `src/app/home.ts`, so this task adds its `lab` branch (`Lab: <title>`, `#/m/NN/lab/<id>`) with an optional third parameter `labs` (`{ id, title }[]`, default `[]`) and one test in `tests/unit/home.test.ts`; Task 8 then replaces `extraLink` with its `(extra, idx: ExtraContent)` form and `Home.tsx` passes `idx`, as the brief says, and replaces this test with its own.
- `ENGINE_VERSION` becomes 5 here, once for the whole phase; the doc line names only lab XP, debriefs and Hands On, and Tasks 16 and 18 extend it without changing the number.
- Goldens: the three backup snapshots gain only `"labsDone": []`; the new `v1-labs.json` backup holds a first completion, a debrief session and a rerun at a higher `rev`, and the new `hands-on-week` journey covers first completions with and without hints, a rerun, debriefs, a day whose only completion is a lab and a day with only a rerun; the three existing journey snapshots do not change because `MemoryState` gains no field.
- `JOURNEY_CONTENT.coreLabs` now lists two labs for each of Modules 1 to 3; no existing journey holds a `lab.done`, so their snapshots stay as they are.
- The anti-grind audit gains `Re-running a lab and its debrief`: a rerun pays nothing and a day with only a rerun does not qualify, while its debrief answers follow the counted-answer rules unchanged.
- The replay cost suite in `tests/property/grind.property.test.ts` gains a case that adds a `lab.done` to each of the 100 grind days: it stays under the same 1,000 ms bound and pays each of the six journey labs once (P4-128).
- Measured on the prototype: the initial JS is 49.4 KB at HEAD (the brief's 48.7 KB is stale) and 49.6 KB after this task, under the 50 KB budget; Task 7 restores the headroom.
- Covers P4-044, P4-045, P4-047, P4-054, P4-058, P4-061 (debrief), P4-062, P4-063, P4-065, P4-066 (labs), P4-067, P4-068, P4-070, P4-071, P4-072, P4-074 (debrief), P4-075, P4-076, P4-077 (labs), P4-078 (labs), P4-101 (lab), P4-102 (lab), P4-103, P4-127, P4-128, P4-129 and P4-131 (labs).

```diff
+/** A lab completion: the lab id, the hints opened during the run and the lab's `rev`. */

+  return { t: 'lab.done', lab, rev, hints };
```

In `tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts`, add `Journey.lab`, `Journey.debrief` and the `handsOnWeek` builder:

```diff
diff --git a/tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts b/tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts
index 4a65e6c..14cbdac 100644
--- a/tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts
+++ b/tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts
@@ -35,6 +35,17 @@ class Journey {
     return this;
   }
 
+  /** A lab completion with the hints opened during the run, at `rev` 1. */
+  lab(at: string, id: string, hints: number): this {
+    this.push(new Date(at), { t: 'lab.done', lab: id, rev: 1, hints });
+    return this;
+  }
+
+  /** A lab's debrief: a `debrief` session scoped to the lab, one answer per concept, then the submit. */
+  debrief(at: string, labId: string, concepts: readonly string[], script: Script = {}): this {
+    return this.session(at, 'debrief', labId, concepts, script);
+  }
+
   examDate(at: string, examDate: string | null): this {
     this.push(new Date(at), { t: 'settings', patch: { examDate } });
     return this;
@@ -260,3 +271,35 @@ export function comebackTenDays(): StudyEvent[] {
   j.session('2026-10-27T09:00:00.000Z', 'review', 'review', all.slice(0, 10), { variant: 'b' }, true);
   return j.events();
 }
+
+/**
+ * Five days from Monday, October 19: Modules 1 to 3 read and checked, then each module's two core labs, each
+ * followed by its debrief over two concepts; one lab with a hint, a rerun of a done lab on day two, a day whose
+ * only completion is a lab (October 22) and a day of a rerun alone (October 23), which does not qualify.
+ */
+export function handsOnWeek(): StudyEvent[] {
+  const j = new Journey();
+  for (let module = 1; module <= 3; module++) {
+    const day = addDays('2026-10-19', module - 1);
+    j.read(`${day}T08:00:00.000Z`, lessonNote(module, 1));
+    j.read(`${day}T08:20:00.000Z`, lessonNote(module, 2));
+    j.session(`${day}T08:40:00.000Z`, 'check', lessonNote(module, 1), lessonConcepts(module, 1));
+  }
+  j.lab('2026-10-19T10:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m01-a', 0);
+  j.debrief('2026-10-19T10:01:00.000Z', 'lab-m01-a', [conceptId(1, 1), conceptId(1, 6)], {
+    wrong: [conceptId(1, 6)],
+  });
+  j.lab('2026-10-20T10:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m01-b', 1);
+  j.debrief('2026-10-20T10:01:00.000Z', 'lab-m01-b', [conceptId(1, 2), conceptId(1, 7)], {
+    notSure: [conceptId(1, 7)],
+  });
+  j.lab('2026-10-20T11:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m01-a', 0);
+  j.debrief('2026-10-20T11:01:00.000Z', 'lab-m01-a', [conceptId(1, 1), conceptId(1, 6)]);
+  j.lab('2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m02-a', 0);
+  j.debrief('2026-10-21T10:01:00.000Z', 'lab-m02-a', [conceptId(2, 1), conceptId(2, 6)]);
+  j.lab('2026-10-21T11:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m02-b', 0);
+  j.debrief('2026-10-21T11:01:00.000Z', 'lab-m02-b', [conceptId(2, 2), conceptId(2, 7)]);
+  j.lab('2026-10-22T10:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m03-a', 2);
+  j.lab('2026-10-23T10:00:00.000Z', 'lab-m02-a', 0);
+  return j.events();
+}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests and see them fail**

```bash
pnpm vitest run tests/unit/memory.test.ts tests/unit/badges.test.ts tests/unit/next.test.ts tests/unit/replay.test.ts tests/unit/runner.test.ts tests/unit/events-schema.test.ts tests/unit/streak.test.ts tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts tests/unit/home.test.ts
```

## Task 6: The lab route, lab keys, lab drafts and Help rows

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/lab-draft.ts`, `src/app/lab-keys.ts`, `src/app/shortcuts.ts`, `src/app/screens/Lab.tsx` (stub), `tests/unit/lab-draft.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-keys.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/router.ts`, `src/app/nav.ts`, `src/app/keys.ts`, `src/app/keyboard.ts`, `src/app/state.ts`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Help.tsx`, `src/store/kv.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/router.test.ts`, `tests/unit/nav.test.ts`, `tests/unit/keys.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-keys.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-draft.test.ts`, `tests/unit/backup.test.ts`, `tests/unit/migrations.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `#/m/NN/lab/<id>` parses only when the path's `NN` equals the id's `mNN`, so a lab is never shown under another module; anything else stays `not-found`, and Escape goes up to the module page.
- `labDrafts` is one kv record, `Record<lab id, { rev, actions, hints }>`, merged by the newer `updatedAt` on import like every kv key (spec 8.1); `labDraftsOf` drops malformed entries and never throws, and `labDraftOf` returns null for a draft started on another `rev`, whose actions may not fit the edited lab.
- `labKey` is pure like `runnerKey` and takes the same `RunnerKeyInput`; `select` marks a focused select or number field, where only `Enter` is the lab's, and `onControl` marks a focused button, link or summary other than a card or a bucket target, where `Enter` is left to the native action (Hint, Reset, Keep working, Read in notes).
- `LabKeyState.engine` is the literal union `'sort' | 'configure' | 'simulate'`, not Task 1's `LabEngineId`, so this task depends on nothing.
- `labHolding` lives in `src/app/state.ts`, `keyCommand` yields Escape while it is true, and `arrive()` clears it on every route change; the lab screen sets it in the same call that picks a card up, because a key can arrive before an effect would run.
- `gAt` moves from the listener closure to module scope in `keyboard.ts`, and `gPending(at)` answers for the keydown at `at` whichever window listener sees it first: the global listener stores whether a `g` was pending for the keydown it handled, and any later reader of the same keydown gets that answer.
- Brief gap resolved here: `SHORTCUTS` is module-private in `Help.tsx`, which Vitest cannot import (its `state.ts` import reads `location` at load, and the test environment is `node`), so the rows move to a new pure `src/app/shortcuts.ts` that exports `SHORTCUTS` and its `Shortcut` row type; `Help.tsx` imports them, and `tests/unit/keys.test.ts` matches the eight lab rows word for word.
- The Help sheet becomes lazy like the Progress overlay and is preloaded once the store is ready; `ProgressLoadError` becomes `sheetLoadError(sheet, title)`, so a Help sheet whose code cannot load is still a closable dialog that gives the keys back.
- The stub `Lab` renders from the route alone (the trail and `<h1>Lab</h1>`) and never reads `idx.labs`, which Task 4 adds; Task 7 replaces it.
- Measured on the prototype: the initial JS is 49.9 KB of 50.0 KB in 6 scripts after Tasks 5 and 6 (Help 1.0 KB and the stub 0.3 KB gzipped load on demand); with Help left in the initial bundle it would be 50.4 KB, over budget, so the lazy Help is required, and Task 7 then folds the initial scripts back into one.
- Covers P4-055, P4-107 (route), P4-110, P4-111, P4-112 and P4-132.

Create `tests/unit/lab-draft.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { type LabDraft, labDraftOf, labDraftsOf, withDraft, withHint } from '../../src/app/lab-draft.ts';

const LAB = 'lab-m01-sort-the-bakes';
const DRAFT: LabDraft = {
  rev: 2,
  actions: [{ t: 'place', card: 'rye', bucket: 'shared-kitchen' }],
  hints: ['g1'],
};

describe('labDraftsOf', () => {
  it('reads well-formed drafts and copies them', () => {
    const value = { [LAB]: DRAFT };
    const drafts = labDraftsOf(value);
    expect(drafts).toEqual(value);
    expect(drafts[LAB]).not.toBe(DRAFT);
  });

  it('gives {} for a value that is not a record, and never throws', () => {
    for (const value of [undefined, null, 3, 'x', [DRAFT], true]) expect(labDraftsOf(value)).toEqual({});
  });

  it('drops each malformed entry and keeps the rest', () => {
    const value = {
      [LAB]: DRAFT,
      'lab-m01-no-rev': { actions: [], hints: [] },
      'lab-m01-rev-0': { rev: 0, actions: [], hints: [] },
      'lab-m01-half-rev': { rev: 1.5, actions: [], hints: [] },
      'lab-m01-actions': { rev: 1, actions: 'place', hints: [] },
      'lab-m01-hints': { rev: 1, actions: [], hints: [1] },
      'lab-m01-null': null,
    };
    expect(labDraftsOf(value)).toEqual({ [LAB]: DRAFT });
  });
});

describe('labDraftOf', () => {
  it('finds the draft of the lab at its rev, and null when absent or the rev changed', () => {
    const drafts = { [LAB]: DRAFT };
    expect(labDraftOf(drafts, LAB, 2)).toEqual(DRAFT);
    expect(labDraftOf(drafts, LAB, 3)).toBeNull();
    expect(labDraftOf(drafts, 'lab-m02-other', 2)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('withDraft and withHint', () => {
  it('sets a draft, replaces it, and removes it with null', () => {
    const one = withDraft({}, LAB, DRAFT);
    expect(one).toEqual({ [LAB]: DRAFT });
    const other = withDraft(one, 'lab-m02-other', { rev: 1, actions: [], hints: [] });
    expect(Object.keys(other)).toEqual([LAB, 'lab-m02-other']);
    expect(withDraft(other, LAB, null)).toEqual({ 'lab-m02-other': { rev: 1, actions: [], hints: [] } });
    expect(withDraft({}, LAB, null)).toEqual({});
  });

  it('adds an opened hint once', () => {
    expect(withHint(DRAFT, 'g2').hints).toEqual(['g1', 'g2']);
    expect(withHint(DRAFT, 'g1')).toBe(DRAFT);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/lab-keys.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { type LabKeyState, labKey } from '../../src/app/lab-keys.ts';
import type { RunnerKeyInput } from '../../src/app/runner-keys.ts';

const key = (k: string, more: Partial<RunnerKeyInput> = {}): RunnerKeyInput => ({
  key: k,
  meta: false,
  ctrl: false,
  alt: false,
  shift: false,
  typing: false,
  control: false,
  at: 10_000,
  ...more,
});
const state = (more: Partial<LabKeyState> = {}): LabKeyState => ({
  engine: 'sort',
  holding: false,
  bucketCount: 3,
  inItems: false,
  onControl: false,
  gPending: false,
  confirmOpen: false,
  done: false,
  ...more,
});

describe('labKey', () => {
  it('checks the goals with Enter, but leaves Enter to a focused button, link or summary', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('Enter'), state())).toEqual({ kind: 'check' });
    expect(labKey(key('Enter'), state({ onControl: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('Enter', { control: true }), state({ inItems: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'check' });
  });

  it('shows a hint with h and asks to reset with r, in either case', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('h'), state())).toEqual({ kind: 'hint' });
    expect(labKey(key('H', { shift: true }), state())).toEqual({ kind: 'hint' });
    expect(labKey(key('r'), state())).toEqual({ kind: 'reset' });
    expect(labKey(key('R', { shift: true }), state({ engine: 'simulate' }))).toEqual({ kind: 'reset' });
  });

  it('leaves h and r to the g h and g r sequences while a g is pending', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('h'), state({ gPending: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('r'), state({ gPending: true }))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('places a held card with a digit, 0 being the tenth bucket, only in a bucket that exists', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('1'), state({ holding: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'place', bucket: 1 });
    expect(labKey(key('3'), state({ holding: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'place', bucket: 3 });
    expect(labKey(key('4'), state({ holding: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('0'), state({ holding: true, bucketCount: 10 }))).toEqual({
      kind: 'place',
      bucket: 10,
    });
    expect(labKey(key('0'), state({ holding: true, bucketCount: 9 }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('1'), state())).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sends a held card back with Backspace and puts it down with Escape', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('Backspace'), state({ holding: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'unplace' });
    expect(labKey(key('Escape'), state({ holding: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'drop' });
    expect(labKey(key('Backspace'), state())).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('Escape'), state())).toBeNull();
  });

  it('moves within a region with the arrow keys only from one of its items', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('ArrowDown'), state({ inItems: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'move', delta: 1 });
    expect(labKey(key('ArrowRight'), state({ inItems: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'move', delta: 1 });
    expect(labKey(key('ArrowUp'), state({ inItems: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'move', delta: -1 });
    expect(labKey(key('ArrowLeft'), state({ inItems: true }))).toEqual({ kind: 'move', delta: -1 });
    expect(labKey(key('ArrowDown'), state())).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps the Sort keys to Sort labs', () => {
    for (const engine of ['configure', 'simulate'] as const) {
      expect(labKey(key('1'), state({ engine, holding: true }))).toBeNull();
      expect(labKey(key('Backspace'), state({ engine, holding: true }))).toBeNull();
      expect(labKey(key('ArrowDown'), state({ engine, inItems: true }))).toBeNull();
      expect(labKey(key('Enter'), state({ engine }))).toEqual({ kind: 'check' });
    }
  });

  it('takes only Enter from a focused select or number field, and nothing from a text field', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('Enter', { typing: true, select: true }), state({ engine: 'configure' }))).toEqual({
      kind: 'check',
    });
    expect(labKey(key('h', { typing: true, select: true }), state())).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('1', { typing: true, select: true }), state({ holding: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('Enter', { typing: true }), state())).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('r', { typing: true }), state())).toBeNull();
  });

  it('does nothing with a modifier, while the reset panel asks, or once the lab is done', () => {
    expect(labKey(key('h', { meta: true }), state())).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('Enter', { ctrl: true }), state())).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('1', { alt: true }), state({ holding: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('Enter'), state({ confirmOpen: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('r'), state({ confirmOpen: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('Enter'), state({ done: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(labKey(key('h'), state({ done: true }))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Create `src/app/lab-draft.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Unfinished labs (spec 8.1): one kv record, `labDrafts`, maps each lab id to the lab `rev` it was started on, the
 * actions dispatched so far and the goals whose hint was opened. Only actions are kept, never a state: the screen
 * replays them through the engine's reducer, so the reducer stays the one source of truth.
 */
export interface LabDraft {
  rev: number;
  actions: unknown[];
  hints: string[];
}

export type LabDrafts = Readonly<Record<string, LabDraft>>;

const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);

const isDraft = (value: unknown): value is LabDraft =>
  isRecord(value) &&
  Number.isInteger(value.rev) &&
  (value.rev as number) >= 1 &&
  Array.isArray(value.actions) &&
  Array.isArray(value.hints) &&
  value.hints.every((h) => typeof h === 'string');

/** The drafts in a kv value, dropping any malformed entry; never throws. */
export function labDraftsOf(value: unknown): LabDrafts {
  if (!isRecord(value)) return {};
  const drafts: Record<string, LabDraft> = {};
  for (const [lab, draft] of Object.entries(value)) {
    if (isDraft(draft))
      drafts[lab] = { rev: draft.rev, actions: [...draft.actions], hints: [...draft.hints] };
  }
  return drafts;
}

/** The draft of `lab`, or null when there is none or it was started on another `rev`, whose actions may not fit. */
export function labDraftOf(drafts: LabDrafts, lab: string, rev: number): LabDraft | null {
  const draft = drafts[lab];
  return draft === undefined || draft.rev !== rev ? null : draft;
}

/** The drafts with `lab` set to `draft`, or without `lab` when `draft` is null. */
export function withDraft(drafts: LabDrafts, lab: string, draft: LabDraft | null): LabDrafts {
  const { [lab]: _, ...rest } = drafts;
  return draft === null ? rest : { ...rest, [lab]: draft };
}

/** The draft with `goalId` among its opened hints, once. */
export function withHint(draft: LabDraft, goalId: string): LabDraft {
  return draft.hints.includes(goalId) ? draft : { ...draft, hints: [...draft.hints, goalId] };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the lab key map, `labHolding` and `gPending`**

Create `src/app/lab-keys.ts`:

```ts
/** The lab's keys (spec 7.4), pure so the tests run without a browser. */
import type { RunnerKeyInput } from './runner-keys.ts';

export type LabCommand =
  | { kind: 'check' }
  | { kind: 'hint' }
  | { kind: 'reset' }
  | { kind: 'place'; bucket: number }
  | { kind: 'unplace' }
  | { kind: 'move'; delta: 1 | -1 }
  | { kind: 'drop' };

export interface LabKeyState {
  engine: 'sort' | 'configure' | 'simulate';
  /** True while a Sort card is held. */
  holding: boolean;
  bucketCount: number;
  /** True when focus is on a `[data-lab-item]` of a region, where the arrow keys step through that region. */
  inItems: boolean;
  /** True when focus is on a button, link or summary that answers Enter itself, other than a card or a bucket. */
  onControl: boolean;
  /** True when a `g` is still open, so `h` and `r` belong to the `g h` and `g r` sequences. */
  gPending: boolean;
  /** True while the inline reset panel asks. */
  confirmOpen: boolean;
  /** True once the lab is complete and its debrief shows; the Runner keys take over. */
  done: boolean;
}

const MOVES: Readonly<Record<string, 1 | -1>> = { ArrowDown: 1, ArrowRight: 1, ArrowUp: -1, ArrowLeft: -1 };

/**
 * The command for one keydown on a lab, or null when the key is not the lab's.
 * `select` on the input marks a focused select or number field, where only Enter is the lab's.
 */
export function labKey(input: RunnerKeyInput, state: LabKeyState): LabCommand | null {
  if (input.meta || input.ctrl || input.alt || state.confirmOpen || state.done) return null;
  if (input.typing) return input.select === true && input.key === 'Enter' ? { kind: 'check' } : null;
  const key = input.key.length === 1 ? input.key.toLowerCase() : input.key;
  if (key === 'Enter') return state.onControl ? null : { kind: 'check' };
  if (key === 'h' || key === 'r') {
    if (state.gPending) return null;
    return key === 'h' ? { kind: 'hint' } : { kind: 'reset' };
  }
  if (state.engine !== 'sort') return null;
  if (/^[0-9]$/.test(key)) {
    const bucket = key === '0' ? 10 : Number(key);
    return state.holding && bucket <= state.bucketCount ? { kind: 'place', bucket } : null;
  }
  if (key === 'Backspace') return state.holding ? { kind: 'unplace' } : null;
  if (key === 'Escape') return state.holding ? { kind: 'drop' } : null;
  const delta = MOVES[key];
  return delta !== undefined && state.inItems ? { kind: 'move', delta } : null;
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/Lab.tsx` (the stub Task 7 replaces):

```tsx
import type { ContentIndex } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { Breadcrumbs } from '../../ui/Breadcrumbs.tsx';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { pad2, type Route } from '../router.ts';

/** A lab `#/m/NN/lab/<id>` (spec 7.2); until Task 7 fills it in, it shows the trail and the heading from the route. */
export function Lab({ idx, route }: { idx: ContentIndex; route: Extract<Route, { kind: 'lab' }> }) {
  useTitle('Lab');
  const meta = idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === route.module);
  return (
    <>
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Home', href: '#/' },
          { label: `Module ${route.module}: ${meta?.short ?? ''}`, href: `#/m/${pad2(route.module)}` },
          { label: 'Lab', href: `#/m/${pad2(route.module)}/lab/${route.id}` },
        ]}
      />
      <h1>Lab</h1>
    </>
  );
}
```

## Task 7: The lab screen with the Sort view, goals, hints, reset and the debrief

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/lab.ts`, `src/labs/sort/View.tsx`, `tests/unit/lab.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/screens/Lab.tsx` (replaces the Task 6 stub), `src/app/screens/Runner.tsx`, `src/ui/base.css`, `vite.config.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/lab.test.ts`

Design notes:
- It builds on Task 1 (`LabEngine`, `GoalResult`, `replayLab`, `allPass`, `engineOf`, `checkLab`, `EngineEntry`, `SortSpec`, `SortAction`, `SortState`), Task 4 (`ModuleChunk.labs`, `LabView`), Task 5 (`Progress.labsDone`, the `debrief` mode) and Task 6 (the route, `labHolding`, `gPending`, the drafts and the keys).
- `src/app/lab.ts`, `lab-draft.ts` and `lab-keys.ts` are imported only from `Lab.tsx` and the engine views, so they ride in the lazy `Lab` chunk with the Sort model.
- The screen waits for the module chunk and the stored log; an unknown lab id, or an engine this build does not ship, renders `NotFound`.
- The work is the actions in the lab's draft, replayed through the reducer on every change; every change is written at once with `writeKv`, so a reload resumes the board, and a draft of another `rev` is dropped.
- Goals show `Not checked` until Check; each Check runs `entry.settle` first (none for Sort, `run` for Simulate), computes `checkLab` on the settled board and shows `Met` or `Not met` with an icon and the engine's message until the next Check, and Reset returns every goal to `Not checked`.
- Hints are free: a goal's `Hint` button shows `hintLine(goal hint, engine hint for the board now)`, and the first opening of each goal adds it to `draft.hints`, which Reset keeps, so `lab.done.hints` counts distinct goals whose hint was opened; `h` opens the hint of the first goal not met.
- The first all-pass Check records `lab.done` and the debrief session start in one `recordAll`, clears the draft, announces `All N goals met.` with the XP the completion paid (read back from `xpByEvent`, as the runner does), and moves focus to the `Debrief` heading.
- The debrief shows the lab's lines and `Read in notes` links, then the exported Runner `Session` with `mode: 'debrief'` over the lab's embedded practice questions, with the bet buttons and the Runner keys; a lab reopened while its debrief session is open shows the debrief directly, and every rerun runs its debrief again.
- `Session` needed no new finish code: it already records only the submit for any mode but `check`; `EndLine` gains a `debrief` branch with `Back to Module N`, and `Runner`'s own `mode` prop excludes `debrief`, which only the lab screen starts.
- The badge line under the goals uses the lab's first completion event (`labsDone.get(id).eventId`), because `lab.done` carries no session id.
- Tap a card to pick it up (`aria-pressed`), tap a bucket to place it, tap the held card to put it down; the keys mirror every tap (`Space` picks up, `1` to `9` and `0` place, `Backspace` sends back, `Escape` puts down), and nothing needs dragging (WCAG 2.5.7).
- The tray, the buckets and the goals' Hint buttons are three regions, each one tab stop (a roving `tabIndex` kept by a `focusin` listener on the screen root), and the arrow keys move within the focused region; after a key placement focus goes to the tray's first card, or to the bucket once the tray is empty.
- Every value a key handler reads (the draft, the held card, the reset panel, done) lives in one `live` ref updated in the same call as its state, because the prototype's end-to-end run showed a fast `Space` then `Backspace` arriving before the re-render, which lost the key.
- Deviations from the brief's markup, forced by Biome's `useSemanticElements` rule: the tray is `<fieldset class="sort-tray">` with a visually hidden `<legend>Cards</legend>` instead of a `div` with `role="group"`, and the inline reset panel is `<fieldset class="lab-reset">` whose `<legend>` is `RESET_ASK` instead of `role="group"` with `aria-labelledby`; both keep the group role and name.
- The empty tray says `Every card is placed.` (`TRAY_EMPTY` in `src/app/lab.ts`, tested with the other copy).
- `base.css` gets the six anchor comments at its end and only this task's rules under `/* Labs: shared and Sort */`, with tokens only; the Check and Reset buttons use a `.lab-button` class (44 px) and the goal list a `.goal-list` class, which keeps Biome's `noDescendingSpecificity` quiet.
- Budget fix, a brief gap: with the lab screen in place the initial JS measured 50.9 KB of 50.0 KB in 7 scripts, because Rolldown splits the modules the lazy Lab and Help chunks share with the entry into separate preloaded chunks, each compressed on its own.
- `vite.config.ts` therefore groups every module the entry imports statically into one chunk (`codeSplitting.groups` with the built-in `$initial` tag, the service worker entry excluded), which brought the initial JS to 48.1 KB in 1 script on the prototype, with the `Lab` chunk at 4.9 KB gzipped and Help at 1.0 KB; the reader, practice, memory, exam and offline suites passed on it unchanged.
- The prototype also proved the screen end to end in Chromium and WebKit with a synthetic Sort lab served by `page.route`: key placement, Check, hints, Escape, Backspace, the reload that resumes the draft, the inline reset, the roving tab stops, the debrief with its `Session`, and axe with no sideways scroll; Task 9 writes that suite for real.
- Covers P4-022 (Sort view), P4-043, P4-044 (UI), P4-045 (UI), P4-055, P4-056, P4-064 (debrief), P4-107, P4-111, P4-113 (debrief), P4-114, P4-115, P4-116, P4-117, P4-118, P4-119 and P4-120 (the top bar's XP flash reads `memory.xp`, so lab XP flashes like any other).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/unit/lab.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  CREDITS_NOTE,
  ENGINE_LABEL,
  goalWord,
  hintLine,
  labCheckLine,
  labDoneLine,
  labLede,
  placedLine,
  RESET_ASK,
  returnedLine,
  TRAY_EMPTY,
} from '../../src/app/lab.ts';

describe('lab copy', () => {
  it('names the engines and says how long a lab takes', () => {
    expect(ENGINE_LABEL).toEqual({ sort: 'Sort', configure: 'Configure', simulate: 'Simulate' });
    expect(labLede('sort', 6)).toBe('Sort lab, about 6 minutes');
    expect(labLede('configure', 8)).toBe('Configure lab, about 8 minutes');
    expect(labLede('simulate', 1)).toBe('Simulate lab, about 1 minute');
  });

  it('labels prices and asks before a reset', () => {
    expect(CREDITS_NOTE).toBe('Prices are in lab credits, a made-up unit, not AWS prices.');
    expect(RESET_ASK).toBe('Reset this lab? Your work is cleared; hints you opened still count.');
    expect(TRAY_EMPTY).toBe('Every card is placed.');
  });

  it('words a goal before and after a check', () => {
    expect(goalWord(null)).toBe('Not checked');
    expect(goalWord({ goalId: 'g1', pass: true, message: '2 of 2 right' })).toBe('Met');
    expect(goalWord({ goalId: 'g1', pass: false, message: '1 of 2 right' })).toBe('Not met');
  });

  it('announces a check, with the XP only when the completion paid some', () => {
    expect(labCheckLine(1, 3, 0)).toBe('1 of 3 goals met.');
    expect(labCheckLine(0, 2, 0)).toBe('0 of 2 goals met.');
    expect(labCheckLine(3, 3, 0)).toBe('All 3 goals met.');
    expect(labCheckLine(3, 3, 40)).toBe('All 3 goals met. Plus 40 XP.');
    expect(labCheckLine(2, 2, 30)).toBe('All 2 goals met. Plus 30 XP.');
  });

  it('joins a goal hint with what the board says now', () => {
    expect(hintLine('Shared kitchens bill by the hour.', '1 card still in the tray.')).toBe(
      'Shared kitchens bill by the hour. 1 card still in the tray.',
    );
  });

  it('says when a lab was done, and what card moved where', () => {
    expect(labDoneLine('2026-10-19')).toBe('Done on Oct 19; a rerun pays no XP.');
    expect(placedLine('Rye for the market', 'Shared kitchen')).toBe(
      'Placed Rye for the market in Shared kitchen.',
    );
    expect(returnedLine('Rye for the market')).toBe('Rye for the market is back in the tray.');
  });
});
```

```ts
/** The lab screen's copy (spec 5, 7.4), pure so the tests match it word for word. */
import type { LabEngineId } from '../../schemas/ids.ts';
import type { GoalResult } from '../labs/contract.ts';
import { shortDate } from './dates.ts';

export const ENGINE_LABEL = { sort: 'Sort', configure: 'Configure', simulate: 'Simulate' } as const;

/** Shown on every lab that prices anything (spec 5): prices are never real AWS prices. */
export const CREDITS_NOTE = 'Prices are in lab credits, a made-up unit, not AWS prices.';

/** The Sort tray once every card is in a bucket. */
export const TRAY_EMPTY = 'Every card is placed.';

/** The inline reset panel's question (spec 7.5: no modal). */
export const RESET_ASK = 'Reset this lab? Your work is cleared; hints you opened still count.';

/** The line under a lab's title: `Sort lab, about 6 minutes`. */
export function labLede(engine: LabEngineId, minutes: number): string {
  return `${ENGINE_LABEL[engine]} lab, about ${minutes} minute${minutes === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
}

/** A goal's word beside its icon; colour is never the only signal (spec 7.6). */
export function goalWord(result: GoalResult | null): 'Met' | 'Not met' | 'Not checked' {
  if (result === null) return 'Not checked';
  return result.pass ? 'Met' : 'Not met';
}

/** What a Check announces: `1 of 3 goals met.`, `All 3 goals met.`, or with the XP the completion paid. */
export function labCheckLine(met: number, total: number, xp: number): string {
  if (met < total) return `${met} of ${total} goals met.`;
  return xp > 0 ? `All ${total} goals met. Plus ${xp} XP.` : `All ${total} goals met.`;
}

/** An opened hint: the goal's own hint, then what the board says about that goal now. */
export function hintLine(goalHint: string, stateHint: string): string {
  return `${goalHint} ${stateHint}`;
}

/** Under the title of a lab done before (spec 3.2: first completion only). */
export function labDoneLine(day: string): string {
  return `Done on ${shortDate(day)}; a rerun pays no XP.`;
}

/** Announced when a card lands in a bucket. */
export function placedLine(card: string, bucket: string): string {
  return `Placed ${card} in ${bucket}.`;
}

/** Announced when a card goes back to the tray. */
export function returnedLine(card: string): string {
  return `${card} is back in the tray.`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the Sort view**

Create `src/labs/sort/View.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SortAction, SortSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { TRAY_EMPTY } from '../../app/lab.ts';
import type { SortState } from './model.ts';

export interface SortViewProps {
  spec: SortSpec;
  state: SortState;
  /** The card picked up, or null. */
  held: string | null;
  onHold(card: string | null): void;
  onAction(action: SortAction): void;
}

/**
 * The Sort board (spec 5.1): tap a card to pick it up, then tap a bucket to place it; tapping the held card puts it
 * down. Nothing needs dragging (WCAG 2.5.7), and the lab screen mirrors every tap with a key.
 * The tray and the buckets are two regions, each one tab stop; the lab screen moves the roving tab stop.
 */
export function SortView({ spec, state, held, onHold, onAction }: SortViewProps) {
  const card = (id: string) => {
    const c = spec.cards.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (c === undefined) return null;
    return (
      <button
        key={c.id}
        type="button"
        class="card"
        aria-pressed={held === c.id}
        data-lab-item
        data-card={c.id}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onClick={() => onHold(held === c.id ? null : c.id)}
      >
        {c.text}
      </button>
    );
  };
  const tray = state.cards.filter((id) => state.placed[id] === undefined);
  return (
    <>
      <fieldset class="sort-tray" data-lab-region>
        <legend class="visually-hidden">Cards</legend>
        {tray.length === 0 ? <p class="muted">{TRAY_EMPTY}</p> : tray.map(card)}
      </fieldset>
      <div class="buckets" data-lab-region>
        {spec.buckets.map((b, i) => {
          const inside = state.cards.filter((id) => state.placed[id] === b.id);
          return (
            <section key={b.id} class="bucket" aria-labelledby={`bucket-${b.id}`}>
              <button
                id={`bucket-${b.id}`}
                type="button"
                class="bucket-target"
                data-lab-item
                data-bucket={b.id}
                tabIndex={-1}
                onClick={() => {
                  if (held !== null) onAction({ t: 'place', card: held, bucket: b.id });
                }}
              >
                <span class="bucket-n" aria-hidden="true">
                  {i < 9 ? i + 1 : 0}
                </span>{' '}
                {b.label} <span class="bucket-count">({inside.length})</span>
              </button>
              {b.detail !== undefined && <p class="muted bucket-detail">{b.detail}</p>}
              {inside.map(card)}
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { ContentIndex, LabView, QuestionView } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../../schemas/events.ts';
import type { SortAction, SortSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { openSession } from '../../engine/replay.ts';
import { allPass, type GoalResult, replayLab } from '../../labs/contract.ts';
import { checkLab, type EngineEntry, engineOf } from '../../labs/engines.ts';
import type { SortState } from '../../labs/sort/model.ts';
import { SortView } from '../../labs/sort/View.tsx';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../store/kv.ts';
import { ulid } from '../../store/ulid.ts';
import { Breadcrumbs } from '../../ui/Breadcrumbs.tsx';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { ensureModule, modules } from '../content.ts';
import { gPending, isControl, isTyping } from '../keyboard.ts';
import {
  CREDITS_NOTE,
  goalWord,
  hintLine,
  labCheckLine,
  labDoneLine,
  labLede,
  placedLine,
  RESET_ASK,
  returnedLine,
} from '../lab.ts';
import { type LabDraft, labDraftOf, labDraftsOf, withDraft, withHint } from '../lab-draft.ts';
import { type LabCommand, labKey } from '../lab-keys.ts';
import { memory } from '../memory.ts';
import { kvRecords, newSessionSeed, progress, recordAll, storeReady, writeKv } from '../progress.ts';
import { badgeEarnedLine, badgesIn } from '../results.ts';
import { pad2, type Route, toHash } from '../router.ts';
import { labHolding, overlay } from '../state.ts';
import { NotFound } from './NotFound.tsx';
import { Session } from './Runner.tsx';

type LabRoute = Extract<Route, { kind: 'lab' }>;

/** A lab `#/m/NN/lab/<id>` (spec 5, 7.2): the brief, the workspace, the goals checked on demand, then the debrief. */
export function Lab({ idx, route }: { idx: ContentIndex; route: LabRoute }) {
  const meta = idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === route.module);
  useEffect(() => ensureModule(route.module), [route.module]);
  const chunk = modules.value.get(route.module);
  const lab = chunk?.labs.find((l) => l.id === route.id);
  const entry = lab === undefined ? null : engineOf(lab.engine);
  useTitle(lab === undefined ? 'Lab' : `Lab: ${lab.title}`);
  if (meta === undefined) return <NotFound hash={toHash(route)} />;
  if (chunk === undefined || !storeReady.value) return <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  if (lab === undefined || entry === null) return <NotFound hash={toHash(route)} />;
  return <LabBody key={`${lab.id} ${lab.rev}`} idx={idx} short={meta.short} lab={lab} entry={entry} />;
}

interface BodyProps {
  idx: ContentIndex;
  short: string;
  lab: LabView;
  entry: EngineEntry;
}

/** The kv drafts as they are now, read without subscribing. */
const draftsNow = () => labDraftsOf(kvRecords.peek()[KV_KEYS.labDrafts]?.value);

function LabBody({ idx, short, lab, entry }: BodyProps) {
  const { engine } = entry;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<LabDraft>(
    () => labDraftOf(draftsNow(), lab.id, lab.rev) ?? { rev: lab.rev, actions: [], hints: [] },
  );
  const state = useMemo(() => replayLab(engine, lab.spec, draft.actions), [engine, lab.spec, draft.actions]);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<GoalResult[] | null>(null);
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => new Set(draft.hints));
  const [held, setHeldState] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [asking, setAskingState] = useState(false);
  const [announce, setAnnounce] = useState('');
  const [debriefSid, setDebriefSid] = useState<string | null>(
    () => openSession(progress.peek(), 'debrief', lab.id)?.sid ?? null,
  );
  // What the keys and handlers read: a key may come before the render of the key before it, so every change lands here
  // at once, and the state above only renders it.
  const live = useRef({ draft, held, asking, done: debriefSid !== null });
  const busy = useRef(false);
  const debriefTitle = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
  const keepWorking = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const resetButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const board = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const root = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const done = debriefSid !== null;

  // Escape puts a held card down before it goes up a level: keys.ts reads this signal on the very next keydown.
  const setHeld = (card: string | null): void => {
    live.current.held = card;
    setHeldState(card);
    labHolding.value = card !== null;
  };
  const setAsking = (on: boolean): void => {
    live.current.asking = on;
    setAskingState(on);
  };
  const save = (next: LabDraft | null): void => {
    void writeKv(KV_KEYS.labDrafts, withDraft(draftsNow(), lab.id, next));
  };
  const update = (next: LabDraft): void => {
    live.current.draft = next;
    setDraft(next);
    save(next);
  };
  const dispatch = (action: unknown): void => {
    const now = live.current.draft;
    update({ ...now, actions: [...now.actions, action] });
  };

  const openHint = (goalId: string): void => {
    setShown((open) => new Set([...open, goalId]));
    const next = withHint(live.current.draft, goalId);
    if (next !== live.current.draft) update(next);
  };

  const check = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (busy.current || live.current.done) return;
    const draft = live.current.draft;
    const settled = [...draft.actions, ...entry.settle];
    if (entry.settle.length > 0) update({ ...draft, actions: settled });
    const now = replayLab(engine, lab.spec, settled);
    const checked = checkLab(entry, lab.spec, now);
    setResults(checked);
    const met = checked.filter((r) => r.pass).length;
    if (!allPass(checked)) {
      setAnnounce(labCheckLine(met, checked.length, 0));
      return;
    }
    busy.current = true;
    const sid = ulid();
    const qids = lab.debrief.questions.map((q) => q.id);
    const events: NewEvent[] = [
      { t: 'lab.done', lab: lab.id, rev: lab.rev, hints: draft.hints.length },
      { t: 'session', sid, mode: 'debrief', phase: 'start', scope: lab.id, seed: newSessionSeed(), qids },
    ];
    const [labDone] = await recordAll(events);
    save(null);
    const xp = labDone === undefined ? 0 : (memory.peek()?.xpByEvent.get(labDone.id) ?? 0);
    setAnnounce(labCheckLine(met, checked.length, xp));
    setHeld(null);
    live.current.done = true;
    setDebriefSid(sid);
  };

  const reset = (): void => {
    update({ ...live.current.draft, actions: [] });
    setResults(null);
    setHeld(null);
    setAsking(false);
    requestAnimationFrame(() => resetButton.current?.focus());
  };

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      labHolding.value = false;
    },
    [],
  );
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (asking) keepWorking.current?.focus();
  }, [asking]);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (done) debriefTitle.current?.focus();
  }, [done]);
  // Each region (the tray, the buckets, the goals' hints) is one tab stop (spec 7.4): the item focused last in it, else
  // its first; the arrow keys move within it.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    for (const region of root.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-lab-region]') ?? []) {
      const items = [...region.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-lab-item]')];
      const active = items.find((item) => item.tabIndex === 0) ?? items[0];
      for (const item of items) item.tabIndex = item === active ? 0 : -1;
    }
  });
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const el = root.current;
    if (el === null) return;
    const rove = (event: FocusEvent): void => {
      const item =
        event.target instanceof HTMLElement ? event.target.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-lab-item]') : null;
      const region = item?.closest('[data-lab-region]');
      if (item === null || item === undefined || region === null || region === undefined) return;
      for (const other of region.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-lab-item]')) {
        other.tabIndex = other === item ? 0 : -1;
      }
    };
    el.addEventListener('focusin', rove);
    return () => el.removeEventListener('focusin', rove);
  }, []);

  const sort = lab.engine === 'sort' ? (lab.spec as SortSpec) : null;
  const sortState = state as SortState;

  const place = (card: string, bucket: string): void => {
    if (sort === null) return;
    dispatch({ t: 'place', card, bucket } satisfies SortAction);
    setHeld(null);
    const text = sort.cards.find((c) => c.id === card)?.text ?? '';
    const label = sort.buckets.find((b) => b.id === bucket)?.label ?? '';
    setAnnounce(placedLine(text, label));
  };
  const onSortAction = (action: SortAction): void => {
    if (action.t === 'place') place(action.card, action.bucket);
    else dispatch(action);
  };

  const run = (command: LabCommand): void => {
    switch (command.kind) {
      case 'check':
        void check();
        break;
      case 'hint': {
        const goal =
          lab.goals.find((g) => !(results?.find((r) => r.goalId === g.id)?.pass ?? false)) ?? lab.goals[0];
        if (goal === undefined) break;
        openHint(goal.id);
        const now = replayLab(engine, lab.spec, live.current.draft.actions);
        setAnnounce(hintLine(goal.hint, engine.hint(lab.spec, now, goal.id)));
        break;
      }
      case 'reset':
        setAsking(true);
        break;
      case 'place': {
        const bucket = sort?.buckets[command.bucket - 1];
        const card = live.current.held;
        if (card === null || bucket === undefined) break;
        place(card, bucket.id);
        // Sorting goes on from the tray's first card, or from the bucket once the tray is empty.
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          const next =
            board.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.sort-tray [data-lab-item]') ??
            document.getElementById(`bucket-${bucket.id}`);
          next?.focus();
        });
        break;
      }
      case 'unplace': {
        const card = live.current.held;
        if (card === null || sort === null) break;
        dispatch({ t: 'unplace', card } satisfies SortAction);
        setHeld(null);
        setAnnounce(returnedLine(sort.cards.find((c) => c.id === card)?.text ?? ''));
        requestAnimationFrame(() =>
          board.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`.sort-tray [data-card="${card}"]`)?.focus(),
        );
        break;
      }
      case 'drop':
        setHeld(null);
        break;
      case 'move': {
        const active = document.activeElement;
        const region = active instanceof HTMLElement ? active.closest('[data-lab-region]') : null;
        if (region === null) break;
        const items = [...region.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-lab-item]')];
        const at = items.indexOf(active as HTMLElement);
        items[Math.min(items.length - 1, Math.max(0, at + command.delta))]?.focus();
        break;
      }
    }
  };
  const runRef = useRef(run);
  runRef.current = run;
  const shape = { engine: lab.engine, buckets: sort?.buckets.length ?? 0 };
  const boardKeys = useRef(shape);
  boardKeys.current = shape;

  // A layout effect, so the keys work from the first render of the board.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (event.defaultPrevented || overlay.value !== null) return;
      const target = event.target;
      const element = target instanceof Element ? target : null;
      const command = labKey(
        {
          key: event.key,
          meta: event.metaKey,
          ctrl: event.ctrlKey,
          alt: event.altKey,
          shift: event.shiftKey,
          typing: isTyping(target),
          control: isControl(target),
          select:
            target instanceof HTMLSelectElement ||
            (target instanceof HTMLInputElement && target.type === 'number'),
          at: event.timeStamp,
        },
        {
          engine: boardKeys.current.engine,
          holding: live.current.held !== null,
          bucketCount: boardKeys.current.buckets,
          inItems: element?.closest('[data-lab-item]') != null,
          // A button, link or summary keeps Enter for its own action; a card, a bucket target, a select or a
          // checkbox does not, so Enter checks from them.
          onControl:
            element !== null &&
            element.closest('a, button, summary') !== null &&
            element.closest('.card, .bucket-target') === null,
          gPending: gPending(event.timeStamp),
          confirmOpen: live.current.asking,
          done: live.current.done,
        },
      );
      if (command === null) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      runRef.current(command);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, []);

  const doneBefore = progress.value.labsDone.get(lab.id);
  const shownResults =
    results ?? (done ? lab.goals.map((g) => ({ goalId: g.id, pass: true, message: '' })) : null);
  const session = debriefSid === null ? undefined : progress.value.sessions.get(debriefSid);
  const shipped = useMemo(
    () => new Map<string, QuestionView>(lab.debrief.questions.map((q) => [q.id, q])),
    [lab.debrief.questions],
  );
  const labHref = `#/m/${pad2(lab.module)}/lab/${lab.id}`;

  return (
    <div class="lab" ref={root}>
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Home', href: '#/' },
          { label: `Module ${lab.module}: ${short}`, href: `#/m/${pad2(lab.module)}` },
          { label: `Lab: ${lab.title}`, href: labHref },
        ]}
      />
      <h1>{lab.title}</h1>
      <p class="muted lede">{labLede(lab.engine, lab.minutes)}</p>
      {doneBefore !== undefined && <p class="muted lab-done-line">{labDoneLine(doneBefore.day)}</p>}
      <p class="lab-brief">{lab.brief}</p>
      {lab.credits && <p class="muted credits-note">{CREDITS_NOTE}</p>}
      {!done && (
        <section class="lab-board" aria-label="Workspace" ref={board}>
          {sort !== null && (
            <SortView spec={sort} state={sortState} held={held} onHold={setHeld} onAction={onSortAction} />
          )}
        </section>
      )}
      <section class="lab-goals" aria-labelledby="lab-goals-title">
        <h2 id="lab-goals-title">Goals</h2>
        <ul class="goal-list" data-lab-region>
          {lab.goals.map((goal) => {
            const result = shownResults?.find((r) => r.goalId === goal.id) ?? null;
            const open = shown.has(goal.id);
            return (
              <li
                key={goal.id}
                class={`goal ${result === null ? 'is-unchecked' : result.pass ? 'is-met' : 'is-open'}`}
              >
                <p class="goal-line">
                  {result !== null && <GoalIcon pass={result.pass} />}
                  <span class="goal-word">{goalWord(result)}</span> {goal.label}
                  {result !== null && result.message !== '' && (
                    <span class="muted goal-message"> {result.message}</span>
                  )}
                </p>
                {!done && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    class="link-button"
                    aria-expanded={open}
                    data-lab-item
                    tabIndex={-1}
                    onClick={() =>
                      open ? setShown(new Set([...shown].filter((g) => g !== goal.id))) : openHint(goal.id)
                    }
                  >
                    Hint
                  </button>
                )}
                {!done && open && (
                  <p class="hint">{hintLine(goal.hint, engine.hint(lab.spec, state, goal.id))}</p>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
        <LabBadgeLine idx={idx} eventId={doneBefore?.eventId ?? null} />
      </section>
      {!done && !asking && (
        <div class="lab-actions">
          <button type="button" class="button lab-button" onClick={() => void check()}>
            Check <kbd>Enter</kbd>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button button-quiet lab-button"
            ref={resetButton}
            onClick={() => setAsking(true)}
          >
            Reset <kbd>r</kbd>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {!done && asking && (
        <fieldset class="lab-reset">
          <legend>{RESET_ASK}</legend>
          <button type="button" class="button lab-button" onClick={reset}>
            Reset
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="button button-quiet lab-button"
            ref={keepWorking}
            onClick={() => {
              setAsking(false);
              requestAnimationFrame(() => resetButton.current?.focus());
            }}
          >
            Keep working
          </button>
        </fieldset>
      )}
      <p class="visually-hidden" aria-live="polite">
        {announce}
      </p>
      {done && (
        <section class="lab-debrief" aria-labelledby="debrief-title">
          <h2 id="debrief-title" tabIndex={-1} ref={debriefTitle}>
            Debrief
          </h2>
          <ul class="debrief-lines">
            {lab.debrief.lines.map((line) => (
              <li key={line}>{line}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <p class="debrief-notes">
            {lab.notes.map((note) => (
              <a key={note.route} href={note.route}>
                Read in notes: {note.title}
              </a>
            ))}
          </p>
          {session !== undefined && (
            <Session
              idx={idx}
              shipped={shipped}
              pool={[]}
              session={session}
              mode="debrief"
              module={lab.module}
              slug={null}
            />
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** A goal's icon beside its word, so colour is never the only signal (spec 7.6). */
function GoalIcon({ pass }: { pass: boolean }) {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      {pass ? (
        <path d="M3 8.5l3 3 7-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
      ) : (
        <path d="M4 4l8 8M12 4l-8 8" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
      )}
    </svg>
  );
}

/** `Badge: Hands On` under the goals when the lab's first completion earned a badge (spec 3.10). */
function LabBadgeLine({ idx, eventId }: { idx: ContentIndex; eventId: string | null }) {
  const earned = memory.value?.badges;
  if (earned === undefined || eventId === null) return null;
  const names = badgesIn(earned, new Set([eventId])).map(
    (id) => idx.badges.find((b) => b.id === id)?.name ?? id,
  );
  return names.length === 0 ? null : <p class="badge-line">{badgeEarnedLine(names)}</p>;
}
```

## Task 8: Module page labs, `coreLabs` from the index and the Home lab extra

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/app/progress.ts` (`refs.labs`), `src/app/home.ts`, `src/app/memory.ts`, `src/app/module.ts`, `src/app/screens/ModulePage.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Home.tsx` (one call site), `src/ui/base.css` (under `/* Labs: module page */`)
- Test: `tests/unit/home.test.ts`, `tests/unit/module.test.ts`

Design notes:
- `memoryContentOf` fills `coreLabs` from `idx.labs`, which lists shipped live labs only (Task 4), so Hands On counts a module once its listed core labs number at least `CORE_LABS_PER_MODULE` and all are done (Task 5), and Lab Complete waits for Phase 5 by design.
- `refs.labs` lists every lab id of the index, so a `lab.done` of a lab no longer shipped still lands in `labsDone` and counts in `unknownEvents` (Task 5).
- The lab extra comes from `weakestLab` (Task 5) over the shipped core labs, with `lessonsRead(m)` true when every lesson note of module m is in `progress.lessonsDone`; the same private `lessonsRead` helper now also feeds the boss status, so the two can never disagree.
- `extraLink` takes `{ modules, labs }` as the structural type `ExtraContent` rather than `Pick<ContentIndex, 'modules' | 'labs'>`, because its tests pass bare `{ n, short }` modules; a whole `ContentIndex` still fits, so `Home.tsx` passes `idx`.
- The lab href is written as `#/m/NN/lab/<id>`, the same string `toHash` gives the lab route (Task 6), so `home.ts` and `module.ts` need no router change.
- `labRows` takes `Pick<ContentIndex, 'labs'>`, so a test passes `{ labs }`; the engine word is the engine id with its first letter upper-cased (`Sort`, `Configure`, `Simulate`), the same words as `ENGINE_LABEL` in `src/app/lab.ts`, which stays in the lazy lab chunk.
- The Labs section sits between Practice and Boss (spec 7.3); its rows are `.rows` links with `data-nav-item`, so `j` and `k` already walk them, and `lab-rows` widens the engine column so every title starts at the same place.
- The lesson rows and the lab rows share one `CheckMark` icon component, hidden from assistive technology because the word beside it (`Read`, `Done`) carries the meaning.
- Bundle: on the prototype (HEAD plus stubs of Tasks 4 and 5), the initial JS measured 50,723 bytes gzipped before this task and 51,176 bytes after it (+453 bytes), against the 51,200-byte budget; Task 6's lazy Help sheet saved 260 bytes on the same build, so with Tasks 4 to 8 the initial JS is about 50,920 bytes, about 0.27 KB under budget.
- On the Tasks 5 to 7 prototype, whose build also ships every statically imported module in one initial chunk, plus this task, the build printed `initial JS 48.5 KB of 50.0 KB in 1 script(s)`, so the real headroom after Task 8 should sit near 1.5 KB.
- Covers P4-067 (wiring), P4-069, P4-101 (wiring), P4-104 (lab), P4-105 and P4-108.

```ts
/** What an extra's label needs from the index: module short names and lab titles. */
export interface ExtraContent {
  modules: readonly { n: number; short: string }[];
  labs: readonly { id: string; title: string }[];
}

/** The label and link of one extra: a ready boss, the next unseen mock form, or a core lab (spec 4.6 and 7.2). */
export function extraLink(extra: Extra, idx: ExtraContent): { label: string; href: string } {
  switch (extra.kind) {
    case 'boss': {
      const short = idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === extra.module)?.short ?? '';
      return { label: `Boss: ${short}`, href: `#/s/boss/m${pad2(extra.module)}` };
    }
    case 'mock':
      return { label: `Mock ${extra.form}`, href: `#/s/mock/${extra.form.toLowerCase()}` };
    case 'lab': {
      const title = idx.labs.find((l) => l.id === extra.lab)?.title ?? '';
      return { label: `Lab: ${title}`, href: `#/m/${pad2(extra.module)}/lab/${extra.lab}` };
    }
  }
}
```

The `switch` is exhaustive over `Extra`, so Task 16's incident extra fails the typecheck here until it adds its case.
If Task 16 is already on `master`, keep its line `if (extra.kind === 'incident') return { label: 'Incident', href: '#/s/incident' };` as the first statement of the new `extraLink`, before the `switch`.

```ts
/** True when every lesson of the module is marked read. */
function lessonsRead(m: { n: number; lessons: readonly { slug: string }[] }, p: Progress): boolean {
  return m.lessons.every((l) => p.lessonsDone.has(`m${pad2(m.n)}/${l.slug}`));
}
```

```ts
/** The core lab the extras line offers: the first undone one of the weakest module whose lessons are all read. */
const labExtra = computed(() => {
  const idx = index.value;
  if (idx === null) return null;
  const p = progress.value;
  return weakestLab({
    labs: idx.labs.filter((l) => l.tier === 'core'),
    done: new Set(p.labsDone.keys()),
    mastery: mastery.value ?? new Map(),
    lessonsRead: (n) => {
      const m = idx.modules.find((x) => x.n === n);
      return m !== undefined && lessonsRead(m, p);
    },
  });
});

/** The extras line's entries (spec 4.6): a ready boss, a due mock and a core lab, at most two. */
export const extraItems = computed(() => {
  const at = now.value;
  return extras({
    bosses: bosses.value,
    progress: progress.value,
    today: studyDay(at),
    now: at,
    lab: labExtra.value,
  });
});
```

```ts
/** One row of the module page's Labs section (spec 7.2): engine word (`Sort`), title, minutes and done mark. */
export interface LabRow {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  href: string;
  minutes: number;
  engine: string;
  done: boolean;
}

/** What the Labs section says when the module has no lab yet. */
export const NO_LABS = 'No labs for this module yet.';

/** The module's labs in index order (core before stretch, then id), each marked done once a `lab.done` exists. */
export function labRows(
  idx: Pick<ContentIndex, 'labs'>,
  module: number,
  done: ReadonlySet<string>,
): LabRow[] {
  return idx.labs
    .filter((lab) => lab.module === module)
    .map((lab) => ({
      id: lab.id,
      title: lab.title,
      href: `#/m/${pad2(module)}/lab/${lab.id}`,
      minutes: lab.minutes,
      engine: `${lab.engine[0]?.toUpperCase()}${lab.engine.slice(1)}`,
      done: done.has(lab.id),
    }));
}
```

```tsx
/** The check before `Read` and `Done`; the word beside it carries the meaning, so the icon is hidden. */
function CheckMark() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="14" height="14" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      <path d="M3 8.5l3 3 7-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
    </svg>
  );
}

/** The module's labs (spec 7.2), each with its engine, minutes and a Done mark once completed. */
function Labs({
  idx,
  module,
  done,
}: {
  idx: ContentIndex;
  module: number;
  done: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>;
}) {
  const rows = labRows(idx, module, new Set(done.keys()));
  return (
    <>
      <h2>Labs</h2>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p class="muted">{NO_LABS}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul class="rows lab-rows">
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <li key={row.id}>
              <a class="row" href={row.href} data-nav-item>
                <span class="row-n">{row.engine}</span>
                <span class="row-title">{row.title}</span>
                <span class="row-meta row-marks">
                  <span class="mark">{row.minutes} min</span>
                  {row.done && (
                    <span class="mark">
                      <CheckMark />
                      Done
                    </span>
                  )}
                </span>
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```
