## Task 9: End-to-end tests for a Sort lab

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/e2e/lab-helpers.ts`, `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`
- Test: `pnpm exec playwright test tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts` in both projects

Design notes:
- The tests run on a made-up bakery Sort lab, `lab-m01-bakery-sort`, served by `page.route` the way `useSyntheticExam` serves exam forms: the index gains its `LabMeta`, and the Module 1 chunk gains its `LabView`, whose debrief takes the first two live Module 1 practice questions of that real chunk inside the route, so the tests never depend on which real labs shipped.
- `useSyntheticLab` reads those two questions from the chunk response it intercepts instead of calling `chunkFor` first, so the questions and the chunk always come from the same fetch; `chunkFor` and `tests/e2e/questions.ts` do not change, and the debrief answers use the existing `shown` helper.
- The keyboard-only test pins the focus rules of the Task 7 lab screen that a keyboard user needs: the tray, the buckets and the goal list are each one roving tab stop (one `Tab` from a card reaches the first bucket target, one more the first goal's `Hint`); after a placement, focus goes back to the first card left in the tray; and when the tray empties, focus stays on the bucket that took the last card, where `Enter` still checks.
- Each keyboard placement waits for the card to land in its bucket before the next key, because the lab screen moves focus a frame after the placement and a real keyboard user is never faster than that.
- The debrief questions are the first two of the real Module 1 chunk, and one of them may be a match, so `answerDebriefByKeys` pairs a match by digit and letter the way `practice.spec.ts` does.
- `beforeEach` waits for the lab to render before it reloads, because WebKit reports a module chunk fetch that a reload aborts as a page error, which `failOnPageProblems` fails on.
- WebKit reaches buttons with `Alt+Tab` only, so the keyboard test picks the key by browser, as `exam.spec.ts` does.
- Announcements are matched as the whole text of an element that is also a polite live region (`getByText(..., { exact: true }).and(...)` with `[aria-live="polite"]`), so `1 of 2 goals met.` never matches a goal's `1 of 2 right`, and a line shown on screen but never announced fails.
- `storedDraftActions` and `labDoneCount` read IndexedDB directly, so the resume test waits for the draft write before it reloads, and the rerun proves a second `lab.done` landed while paying nothing.
- The visual screenshot test stays out, as in Phase 3; the keyboard-only test runs on this Sort lab in place of the Phase 5 Place lab spec 9 names, and Tasks 12 and 13 add a Configure and a Simulate run to this file.
- Covers P4-064 (debrief), P4-115, P4-116, P4-117, P4-120 (lab), P4-121 (lab), P4-124 and P4-126.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the lab helpers**

Create `tests/e2e/lab-helpers.ts`:

```ts
/** Helpers for the lab end-to-end tests: a made-up bakery lab served by `page.route`, and reads of the stored log. */
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import type { ContentIndex, LabMeta, LabView, ModuleChunk, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { SCHEMA_VERSION } from '../../schemas/version.ts';
import { DB_NAME, STORES } from '../../src/store/idb.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../src/store/kv.ts';

export const SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID = 'lab-m01-bakery-sort';
export const SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE = 'Sort the bakery jobs';

/**
 * Serves a made-up lab beside the real content: the index lists its `LabMeta`, and its module's chunk carries the
 * `LabView`, whose debrief takes the first two live practice questions of that real chunk.
 */
export async function useSyntheticLab(
  page: Page,
  lab: (questions: QuestionView[]) => LabView,
): Promise<void> {
  const { id, module, engine, tier, rev, title, minutes } = lab([]);
  const meta: LabMeta = { id, module, engine, tier, rev, title, minutes };
  await page.route('**/content/index.*.json', async (route) => {
    const response = await route.fetch();
    const index = (await response.json()) as ContentIndex;
    await route.fulfill({ response, json: { ...index, labs: [...index.labs, meta] } });
  });
  await page.route(`**/content/m${String(module).padStart(2, '0')}.*.json`, async (route) => {
    const response = await route.fetch();
    const chunk = (await response.json()) as ModuleChunk;
    const view = lab(chunk.questions.slice(0, 2));
    await route.fulfill({ response, json: { ...chunk, labs: [...chunk.labs, view] } });
  });
}

/** A made-up bakery Sort lab: 3 buckets, 5 cards and 2 goals, with `questions` as its debrief. */
export function syntheticSortLab(questions: QuestionView[]): LabView {
  return {
    id: SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID,
    module: 1,
    engine: 'sort',
    tier: 'core',
    rev: 1,
    title: SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE,
    minutes: 4,
    concepts: [...new Set(questions.map((q) => q.concept))],
    brief:
      'The bakery opens at six. Put each morning job where it happens, so the early shift knows where to stand.',
    spec: {
      buckets: [
        { id: 'bench', label: 'Work bench' },
        { id: 'oven', label: 'Hired oven', detail: 'Paid by the hour' },
        { id: 'shelf', label: 'Dry shelf' },
      ],
      cards: [
        {
          id: 'knead',
          text: 'Knead the dough for the morning loaves',
          bucket: 'bench',
          also: [],
          goal: 'g1',
        },
        { id: 'rolls', text: 'Bake forty rolls before opening', bucket: 'oven', also: [], goal: 'g1' },
        { id: 'flour', text: 'Keep the spare flour sacks dry', bucket: 'shelf', also: [], goal: 'g2' },
        { id: 'jars', text: 'Store the jam jars for the week', bucket: 'shelf', also: [], goal: 'g2' },
        { id: 'pies', text: 'Bake the pies ordered for noon', bucket: 'oven', also: [], goal: 'g2' },
      ],
    },
    goals: [
      {
        id: 'g1',
        label: 'The opening jobs are in place',
        hint: 'Dough is worked by hand on the bench, and only baking needs the oven you pay for by the hour.',
      },
      {
        id: 'g2',
        label: 'Storage and the noon bake are in place',
        hint: 'Anything that only waits belongs on a shelf, and anything that still has to bake goes to the oven.',
      },
    ],
    notes: questions.slice(0, 1).map((q) => ({ route: q.read.route, title: 'The lesson behind this lab' })),
    debrief: {
      lines: [
        'Each job has one place where it happens, and the hired oven costs money only while it bakes.',
        'Storage never needs the oven, so the shelf keeps the paid hours for baking.',
      ],
      questions,
    },
    credits: false,
  };
}

/** A lab as the app reads it: the index named in the page, then its module's chunk. */
export async function labFor(page: Page, id: string): Promise<LabView> {
  const lab = await page.evaluate(async (labId) => {
    const name = document.querySelector<HTMLMetaElement>('meta[name="ccp-content"]')?.content ?? '';
    const index = (await (await fetch(name)).json()) as {
      modules: { n: number; chunk: string }[];
      labs: { id: string; module: number }[];
    };
    const module = index.labs.find((l) => l.id === labId)?.module;
    const chunk = index.modules.find((m) => m.n === module)?.chunk;
    if (chunk === undefined) return null;
    return (
      ((await (await fetch(chunk)).json()) as { labs: LabView[] }).labs.find((l) => l.id === labId) ?? null
    );
  }, id);
  if (lab === null) throw new Error(`lab ${id} is not in the index`);
  return lab;
}

/** How many actions the stored draft of a lab holds, or null when it has no draft. */
export async function storedDraftActions(page: Page, id: string): Promise<number | null> {
  return page.evaluate(
    async ({ name, version, store, key, labId }) => {
      const db = await new Promise<IDBDatabase>((resolve, reject) => {
        const open = indexedDB.open(name, version);
        open.onsuccess = () => resolve(open.result);
        open.onerror = () => reject(open.error);
      });
      const record = await new Promise<{ value?: Record<string, { actions?: unknown[] }> } | undefined>(
        (resolve, reject) => {
          const get = db.transaction(store, 'readonly').objectStore(store).get(key);
          get.onsuccess = () => resolve(get.result);
          get.onerror = () => reject(get.error);
        },
      );
      db.close();
      return record?.value?.[labId]?.actions?.length ?? null;
    },
    { name: DB_NAME, version: SCHEMA_VERSION, store: STORES.kv, key: KV_KEYS.labDrafts, labId: id },
  );
}

/** How many `lab.done` events of a lab the stored log holds. */
export async function labDoneCount(page: Page, id: string): Promise<number> {
  return page.evaluate(
    async ({ name, version, store, labId }) => {
      const db = await new Promise<IDBDatabase>((resolve, reject) => {
        const open = indexedDB.open(name, version);
        open.onsuccess = () => resolve(open.result);
        open.onerror = () => reject(open.error);
      });
      const all = await new Promise<{ t?: string; lab?: string }[]>((resolve, reject) => {
        const get = db.transaction(store, 'readonly').objectStore(store).getAll();
        get.onsuccess = () => resolve(get.result);
        get.onerror = () => reject(get.error);
      });
      db.close();
      return all.filter((e) => e.t === 'lab.done' && e.lab === labId).length;
    },
    { name: DB_NAME, version: SCHEMA_VERSION, store: STORES.events, labId: id },
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the lab end-to-end tests**

Create `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, type Locator, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../src/store/kv.ts';
import { E2E_NOW } from '../fixtures/ready-module.ts';
import { HOME_TITLE } from './exam-helpers.ts';
import { expectNoAxeViolations, expectNoSidewaysScroll, failOnPageProblems, h1, seedKv } from './helpers.ts';
import {
  labDoneCount,
  labFor,
  SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID,
  SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE,
  storedDraftActions,
  syntheticSortLab,
  useSyntheticLab,
} from './lab-helpers.ts';
import { shown } from './questions.ts';

failOnPageProblems();
// UTC keeps the study day the same on every machine.
test.use({ timezoneId: 'UTC' });

const LAB = `/#/m/01/lab/${SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID}`;
const KNEAD = 'Knead the dough for the morning loaves';
const ROLLS = 'Bake forty rolls before opening';
const FLOUR = 'Keep the spare flour sacks dry';
const JARS = 'Store the jam jars for the week';
const PIES = 'Bake the pies ordered for noon';
const GOAL_1 = 'The opening jobs are in place';
const GOAL_2 = 'Storage and the noon bake are in place';
/** The synthetic lab's solution: each card with its bucket label and that bucket's digit. */
const SOLUTION: readonly (readonly [string, string, string])[] = [
  [KNEAD, 'Work bench', '1'],
  [ROLLS, 'Hired oven', '2'],
  [FLOUR, 'Dry shelf', '3'],
  [JARS, 'Dry shelf', '3'],
  [PIES, 'Hired oven', '2'],
];

const card = (page: Page, text: string): Locator => page.getByRole('button', { name: text, exact: true });
const tray = (page: Page): Locator => page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Cards' });
const bucket = (page: Page, label: string): Locator => page.locator('.bucket-target', { hasText: label });
const placedIn = (page: Page, label: string, text: string): Locator =>
  page.locator('.bucket', { has: bucket(page, label) }).getByRole('button', { name: text, exact: true });
const goal = (page: Page, label: string): Locator => page.locator('li.goal', { hasText: label });
/** An announcement of a polite live region, matched as its whole text. */
const said = (page: Page, text: string): Locator =>
  page.getByText(text, { exact: true }).and(page.locator('[aria-live="polite"]'));
const checkButton = (page: Page): Locator => page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Check/ });

/** Presses `key` until `target` has focus, the way a keyboard user tabs into a region. */
async function tabTo(page: Page, target: Locator, key: string): Promise<void> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
    if (await target.evaluate((el) => el === document.activeElement)) return;
    await page.keyboard.press(key);
  }
  throw new Error('Tab never reached the target');
}

/** Places every card of the synthetic lab by tapping the card, then its bucket. */
async function placeAllByTaps(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  for (const [text, label] of SOLUTION) {
    await card(page, text).click();
    await bucket(page, label).click();
    await expect(placedIn(page, label, text)).toBeVisible();
  }
}

/** Answers the debrief by the Runner keys: digits (and letters in a match), Enter to check, Enter for Next. */
async function answerDebriefByKeys(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const lab = await labFor(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID);
  for (let i = 0; i < lab.debrief.questions.length; i++) {
    const q = await shown(page, lab.debrief.questions);
    // The debrief runs in the Runner, so it shows the bet buttons and the Sure hint (spec 4.1).
    await expect(page.getByText('Press Sure when you are more than 71% sure.')).toBeVisible();
    await page.locator('#stem').focus();
    if (q.type === 'match') {
      for (let n = 0; n < q.pairs.length; n++) {
        await page.keyboard.press(String(n + 1));
        await page.keyboard.press('abcde'[n] ?? 'a');
      }
    } else {
      for (let n = 1; n <= (q.select ?? 1); n++) await page.keyboard.press(String(n));
    }
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.locator('.verdict')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Next/ })).toBeFocused();
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  }
}

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticSortLab);
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(E2E_NOW) });
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  await seedKv(page, { [KV_KEYS.engineVersion]: ENGINE_VERSION });
  await page.goto(LAB);
  // The lab must render before the reload, or WebKit reports the aborted chunk fetch as a page error.
  await expect(h1(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  await page.reload();
  await expect(h1(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
});

test('a Sort lab works by keyboard alone and pays 40 XP once', async ({ page, browserName }) => {
  const tab = browserName === 'webkit' ? 'Alt+Tab' : 'Tab';
  const back = browserName === 'webkit' ? 'Alt+Shift+Tab' : 'Shift+Tab';

  // Tab moves between regions (the tray, the buckets, the goal hints) and the arrow keys move within one.
  await tabTo(page, card(page, KNEAD), tab);
  await page.keyboard.press(tab);
  await expect(page.locator('.bucket-target').first()).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press(tab);
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hint' }).first()).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press(back);
  await page.keyboard.press(back);
  await expect(card(page, KNEAD)).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowDown');
  await expect(card(page, ROLLS)).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowUp');
  await expect(card(page, KNEAD)).toBeFocused();

  // Space picks a card up and Escape puts it down without leaving the lab.
  await page.keyboard.press('Space');
  await expect(card(page, KNEAD)).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await expect(card(page, KNEAD)).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  await expect(h1(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE)).toBeVisible();

  // A digit places the held card, and focus goes back to the first card left in the tray.
  await page.keyboard.press('Space');
  await page.keyboard.press('1');
  await expect(said(page, `Placed ${KNEAD} in Work bench.`)).toBeAttached();
  await expect(placedIn(page, 'Work bench', KNEAD)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(card(page, ROLLS)).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('Space');
  await page.keyboard.press('2');
  await expect(card(page, FLOUR)).toBeFocused();

  // Enter checks the board on demand: the first goal is met, the second is not.
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(said(page, '1 of 2 goals met.')).toBeAttached();
  await expect(goal(page, GOAL_1)).toContainText('Met');
  await expect(goal(page, GOAL_2)).toContainText('Not met');

  for (const [text, label, digit] of SOLUTION.slice(2)) {
    await expect(card(page, text)).toBeFocused();
    await page.keyboard.press('Space');
    await page.keyboard.press(digit);
    await expect(placedIn(page, label, text)).toBeVisible();
  }
  // With the tray empty, focus stays on the bucket that took the last card, where Enter still checks.
  await expect(bucket(page, 'Hired oven')).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  // The XP flash shows for 600 ms from the moment lab.done joins the log, so it is checked first.
  await expect(page.getByText('+40', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(said(page, 'All 2 goals met. Plus 40 XP.')).toBeAttached();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Debrief' })).toBeFocused();

  await answerDebriefByKeys(page);
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Back to Module 1' })).toBeVisible();

  await page.goto('/#/m/01');
  await expect(page.locator('.lab-rows .row', { hasText: SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE })).toContainText('Done');

  // A rerun works the same way but pays nothing.
  await page.goto(LAB);
  await expect(page.locator('.lab-done-line')).toContainText('a rerun pays no XP.');
  await tabTo(page, card(page, KNEAD), tab);
  for (const [text, label, digit] of SOLUTION) {
    await expect(card(page, text)).toBeFocused();
    await page.keyboard.press('Space');
    await page.keyboard.press(digit);
    await expect(placedIn(page, label, text)).toBeVisible();
  }
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(said(page, 'All 2 goals met.')).toBeAttached();
  await expect.poll(() => labDoneCount(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID)).toBe(2);
});

test('a lab placed by taps resumes after a reload', async ({ page }) => {
  await card(page, ROLLS).click();
  await expect(card(page, ROLLS)).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  // Tapping the held card again puts it down.
  await card(page, ROLLS).click();
  await expect(card(page, ROLLS)).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');

  await card(page, KNEAD).click();
  await bucket(page, 'Work bench').click();
  await expect(placedIn(page, 'Work bench', KNEAD)).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => storedDraftActions(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_LAB_ID)).toBe(1);

  await page.reload();
  await expect(h1(page, SYNTHETIC_SORT_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(placedIn(page, 'Work bench', KNEAD)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(tray(page).getByRole('button')).toHaveCount(4);
});

test('Reset asks inline and keeps the opened hint', async ({ page }) => {
  await card(page, KNEAD).click();
  await bucket(page, 'Work bench').click();
  await expect(placedIn(page, 'Work bench', KNEAD)).toBeVisible();

  await page.keyboard.press('h');
  await expect(page.locator('.lab-goals .hint')).toHaveCount(1);

  // Keep working closes the question and keeps the board.
  await page.keyboard.press('r');
  const panel = page.locator('.lab-reset');
  await expect(panel).toContainText('Reset this lab? Your work is cleared; hints you opened still count.');
  await expect(panel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep working' })).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(panel).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(tray(page).getByRole('button')).toHaveCount(4);

  // Reset clears the board, and the hint opened before it still costs the no-hint bonus.
  await page.keyboard.press('r');
  await panel.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(tray(page).getByRole('button')).toHaveCount(5);
  await placeAllByTaps(page);
  await checkButton(page).click();
  await expect(said(page, 'All 2 goals met. Plus 30 XP.')).toBeAttached();
});

test('the lab screen has no axe violations and no sideways scroll', async ({ page }) => {
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);

  await card(page, KNEAD).click();
  await expect(card(page, KNEAD)).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);

  await bucket(page, 'Work bench').click();
  await checkButton(page).click();
  await expect(said(page, '0 of 2 goals met.')).toBeAttached();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);

  for (const [text, label] of SOLUTION.slice(1)) {
    await card(page, text).click();
    await bucket(page, label).click();
  }
  await checkButton(page).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Debrief' })).toBeFocused();
  await expect(page.locator('section.runner')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});

test('lab cards and buckets are at least 44 px tall and stack on a phone', async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
  test.skip(
    testInfo.project.name !== 'webkit-mobile',
    'the phone layout is checked on the phone project only',
  );
  for (const target of await page.locator('.sort-tray .card, .bucket-target').all()) {
    expect((await target.boundingBox())?.height ?? 0).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44);
  }
  const boxes = await Promise.all((await page.locator('.bucket').all()).map((b) => b.boundingBox()));
  for (let i = 1; i < boxes.length; i++) {
    const [above, below] = [boxes[i - 1], boxes[i]];
    if (!above || !below) throw new Error('a bucket has no box');
    expect(below.x).toBe(above.x);
    expect(below.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(above.y + above.height);
  }
  const board = await page.locator('.lab-board').boundingBox();
  const goals = await page.locator('.lab-goals').boundingBox();
  if (!board || !goals) throw new Error('the board or the goals have no box');
  expect(goals.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(board.y + board.height);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});
```

## Task 10: The lab content fan-out (Workflow tool, controller-run), waves S and CS

**Files:**
- Create, wave S (Sort, the shippable cut), one file per lab under `content/labs/`:
  - `01-introduction-to-the-cloud/who-patches-what.yaml`, `01-introduction-to-the-cloud/name-the-cloud-benefit.yaml`, `03-exploring-compute-services/pick-the-compute.yaml`, `06-storage/block-file-or-object.yaml`
  - `07-databases/pick-the-database.yaml`, `08-ai-ml-and-data-analytics/ai-service-per-task.yaml`, `09-security/which-service-raised-it.yaml`, `10-monitoring-compliance-and-governance/which-tool.yaml`
  - `11-pricing-and-support/which-support-plan.yaml`, `12-migrating-to-the-aws-cloud/migration-strategy.yaml`, `13-well-architected-solutions/specialized-service.yaml`
- Create, wave CS (Configure and Simulate):
  - `02-compute-in-the-cloud/launch-the-order-server.yaml`, `04-going-global/choose-the-region.yaml`
  - `02-compute-in-the-cloud/morning-rush.yaml`, `03-exploring-compute-services/bursty-or-steady.yaml`, `06-storage/receipts-for-7-years.yaml`, `07-databases/cache-the-menu.yaml`, `11-pricing-and-support/the-monthly-bill.yaml`, `12-migrating-to-the-aws-cloud/online-or-offline.yaml`
- Test: `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <lab file>` per lab, `tests/content/labs-replay.test.ts` (one test per lab), and `pnpm check` through the pre-commit hook of every commit

This task is content, not code: the controller runs it with the Workflow tool, and its agents write, judge, replay and commit the lab files.
The plan does not hold the lab YAML; the worked examples the writers follow are the fixture Sort lab `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/labs/01-baking-basics/sort-the-bakes.yaml` (Task 2, `bakeryLab()`), the synthetic `syntheticSortLab` of Task 9, the Configure fixture of Task 12, the Simulate fixtures of Task 13, and the full Sort example inside `agents/lab-writer.md` (Task 3).

Design notes:
- The 19 labs and their binding id, file, engine and title live in the inner script's `ROWS`, with each lab's spec 5.2 scope; `maps/labs-content.md`, whose proposed lab ids and one M9 file name differ, served only for concepts, note lines and debrief suggestions, which the writers take from `--lab-targets`.
- The inner script takes `args = { worktree, wave, engines, targets }`, where `targets` is the whole JSON array of `pnpm --silent content:report --lab-targets`: the script builds its writer entries from `ROWS`, the wave and `engines`, so the table lives in one place and the controller passes the report as it is.
- Wave S runs 10 writers (one per module; the Module 1 writer owns both Module 1 labs); wave CS runs one writer per lab, so Module 2's Configure and Simulate labs are written apart, in two calls: `engines: ['configure']` once Task 12 is on `master`, and `engines: ['simulate']` once Task 13 is on `master` and merged into the branch.
- Each round runs the critics, then the gate, and only then the revisers, so every lab that ships was approved and replayed in the same state, and a revision is always judged by a fresh critic in the next round; the brief's Critic, Revise, Gate order would have let a round 3 revision ship unjudged.
- The gate returns `today` from `date +%F`, so the script needs no clock of its own, and the problem lines of each failing lab, which the revisers receive with the critic's issues.
- A lab ships only when approved and in `replayOk`; after three rounds, or when a round ends past `LAST_REWRITE_DAY`, a failing lab is moved under `.results/labs/held/` (git-ignored), left uncommitted and returned as `{ lab, kinds }` for Dani, and the cut rule drops that lab, not the wave.
- Moving held labs out of `content/labs/` matters because the Sync agent's `pnpm check` and every later `pnpm check` run R11 over the whole tree, and a failing lab left in place would block the wave's shipped labs.
- Sync merges `master` and runs `pnpm check` before the commits, so the labs meet the Task 4 build, the chunks and the e2e helpers before they reach `master`; a conflict or a failing check stops the wave and returns to the controller.
- The Commit agents never edit a lab: Sync already ran `pnpm check` on the same tree, so a failing hook means something changed underneath, and the wave stops for the controller rather than ship an edit no critic saw.
- Commit subjects count their labs: `Add the Sort labs for Module 1`, `Add the Sort lab for Module 3`, `Add the Configure lab for Module 2`, `Add the Simulate lab for Module 2`; bodies read `2 labs; each solution and its 4 wrong paths replay.` or `1 lab; its solution and its 2 wrong paths replay.`, with the wrong paths counted by `grep -c 'failsGoal:'`.
- Writers keep `rev: 1` through every revise round, because no lab is committed before its wave's Commit phase, so R2's `rev` rule (Task 2) first applies to the committed file.
- `HOOKS` is copied verbatim from line 29 of the Phase 3 inner script, with `WT` and `NODE` defined before it; its exam-item line stays, because lab agents must never read or quote the exam pool.
- Budget: wave S about 10 writers, 11 to 18 critics, 3 gates, about 5 revisers, 1 sync, 10 commits and 1 final, about 50 agents; wave CS about 40 over its two calls.
- Dates: wave S starts once Task 3 is on `master` and its last rewrite is Saturday, October 24; wave CS starts once Task 12 is on `master` and its last rewrite is Thursday, October 29; wave CS never waits for wave S.
- Covers P4-002 (labs replay), P4-005, P4-026, P4-028, P4-036, P4-038, P4-057 and P4-137.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run wave S with the Workflow tool**

Save this script as `$SCRATCH/p4/phase4-labs.js` (outside the repo; it is tool input, not a repo file) and run it with the Workflow tool, with `args` = `{ "worktree": "<absolute path of $SCRATCH/p4/wt-labs-sort>", "wave": "sort", "targets": <the JSON array in .results/lab-targets.json> }`:

```js
export const meta = {
  name: 'phase4-labs',
  description: 'Phase 4 labs: one lab writer per module or lab, a fresh critic per lab, an R11 replay gate, revise, a sync with master, one commit per module',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Write', detail: 'one lab writer per writer entry' },
    { title: 'Critic', detail: 'one fresh lab critic per open lab, each round' },
    { title: 'Gate', detail: 'R11 replay and the content tests, no edits' },
    { title: 'Revise', detail: 'one lab writer per entry with an open lab' },
    { title: 'Sync', detail: 'merge master, then pnpm check' },
    { title: 'Commit', detail: 'one commit per module, in module order' },
  ],
}

const WT = args.worktree
const WAVE = args.wave
const ENGINES = args.engines ?? (WAVE === 'sort' ? ['sort'] : ['configure', 'simulate'])
const TARGETS = args.targets ?? []
const NODE = 'export PATH="/Users/danielchahine/.local/share/fnm/node-versions/v24.21.0/installation/bin:$PATH";'
const HOOKS = `Start every shell command with: cd "${WT}" && ${NODE} Work only in this worktree, never in any other checkout. Stage files by path, never with git add -A or git add .; when git reports an index.lock, wait a few seconds and retry. Never use --no-verify. Never use the em dash. Never add a Co-Authored-By line. Never commit unless your task says so. Never quote an exam item's stem, options, whys, takeaway, evidence or a failure reason in your reply, a log line or a commit message: name exam items by id and count only.`
const NEVER_READ_LAB = 'Never read the course source text, ~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/, content/fingerprints/, any file under content/exam/, AWS exam questions, or .results/checks/keys.json.'
const CRITIC_ROUNDS = 3
const LAST_REWRITE_DAY = WAVE === 'sort' ? '2026-10-24' : '2026-10-29'
const BRANCH = WAVE === 'sort' ? 'content/phase-4-labs-sort' : 'content/phase-4-labs-cs'
const pad2 = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
const ENGINE_NAME = { sort: 'Sort', configure: 'Configure', simulate: 'Simulate' }

/** The 19 Phase 4 core labs (spec 5.2); id, file and title are binding, and R1 requires id = lab-mNN-<file slug>. */
const ROWS = [
  { id: 'lab-m01-who-patches-what', module: 1, file: '01-introduction-to-the-cloud/who-patches-what.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Who patches what?', scope: 'customer or AWS duties as Module 1 teaches them, such as patching the operating system against securing the physical, network and hypervisor layers' },
  { id: 'lab-m01-name-the-cloud-benefit', module: 1, file: '01-introduction-to-the-cloud/name-the-cloud-benefit.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Name the cloud benefit in each cafe story', scope: 'the six benefits Module 1 teaches' },
  { id: 'lab-m03-pick-the-compute', module: 3, file: '03-exploring-compute-services/pick-the-compute.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Pick the compute', scope: 'EC2, Lambda, ECS, EKS, Fargate, ECR, Beanstalk, Batch, Lightsail, Outposts' },
  { id: 'lab-m06-block-file-or-object', module: 6, file: '06-storage/block-file-or-object.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Block, file or object', scope: 'instance store, EBS, S3, EFS, FSx, Storage Gateway' },
  { id: 'lab-m07-pick-the-database', module: 7, file: '07-databases/pick-the-database.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Pick the database', scope: 'the services in the Module 7 lessons, leaving out Amazon Redshift, MemoryDB, Keyspaces, Timestream and QLDB' },
  { id: 'lab-m08-ai-service-per-task', module: 8, file: '08-ai-ml-and-data-analytics/ai-service-per-task.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'AI service per cafe task', scope: 'the AI services of the Module 8 lessons, leaving out Amazon Rekognition, Amazon Textract, Amazon Transcribe and Amazon Kendra' },
  { id: 'lab-m09-which-service-raised-it', module: 9, file: '09-security/which-service-raised-it.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Which service raised this finding?', scope: 'the security services of the Module 9 lessons that raise findings' },
  { id: 'lab-m10-which-tool', module: 10, file: '10-monitoring-compliance-and-governance/which-tool.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'CloudWatch, CloudTrail, Config, Trusted Advisor, Health or Artifact', scope: 'one bucket per named service' },
  { id: 'lab-m11-which-support-plan', module: 11, file: '11-pricing-and-support/which-support-plan.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Which Support plan?', scope: 'the four current plans, from the Module 11 update lines: Basic Support, AWS Business Support+, AWS Enterprise Support and AWS Unified Operations; Basic Support cards use concept aws-support-plans' },
  { id: 'lab-m12-migration-strategy', module: 12, file: '12-migrating-to-the-aws-cloud/migration-strategy.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'A migration strategy for each cafe system', scope: 'the seven strategies Module 12 teaches' },
  { id: 'lab-m13-specialized-service', module: 13, file: '13-well-architected-solutions/specialized-service.yaml', engine: 'sort', title: 'Specialized service per use case', scope: 'the specialized services of the Module 13 lessons' },
  { id: 'lab-m02-launch-the-order-server', module: 2, file: '02-compute-in-the-cloud/launch-the-order-server.yaml', engine: 'configure', title: 'Launch the order server', scope: 'instance family, purchase option, security group' },
  { id: 'lab-m04-choose-the-region', module: 4, file: '04-going-global/choose-the-region.yaml', engine: 'configure', title: 'Choose the Region', scope: 'compliance, proximity, feature availability, price' },
  { id: 'lab-m02-morning-rush', module: 2, file: '02-compute-in-the-cloud/morning-rush.yaml', engine: 'simulate', model: 'scaling', title: 'Morning rush', scope: 'Auto Scaling minimum, desired and maximum behind a load balancer over a 24 h order curve' },
  { id: 'lab-m03-bursty-or-steady', module: 3, file: '03-exploring-compute-services/bursty-or-steady.yaml', engine: 'simulate', model: 'compute-cost', title: 'Bursty or steady', scope: 'Lambda against an always-on instance, in lab credits' },
  { id: 'lab-m06-receipts-for-7-years', module: 6, file: '06-storage/receipts-for-7-years.yaml', engine: 'simulate', model: 's3-lifecycle', title: 'Receipts for 7 years', scope: 'S3 classes and lifecycle over 365 days' },
  { id: 'lab-m07-cache-the-menu', module: 7, file: '07-databases/cache-the-menu.yaml', engine: 'simulate', model: 'cache', title: 'Cache the menu', scope: 'hit rate against database load' },
  { id: 'lab-m11-the-monthly-bill', module: 11, file: '11-pricing-and-support/the-monthly-bill.yaml', engine: 'simulate', model: 'bill', title: 'The monthly bill', scope: 'pricing mix, budget thresholds, consolidated billing tiers' },
  { id: 'lab-m12-online-or-offline', module: 12, file: '12-migrating-to-the-aws-cloud/online-or-offline.yaml', engine: 'simulate', model: 'transfer', title: 'Online or offline transfer', scope: 'days = TB * 8e6 / (Mbps * 0.8) / 86400 against Snowball turnaround' },
]

if (!WT) return { stopped: 'args.worktree is missing' }
if (WAVE !== 'sort' && WAVE !== 'cs') return { stopped: `unknown wave ${WAVE}` }
if (TARGETS.length === 0) return { stopped: 'args.targets is empty: pass the JSON array of --lab-targets' }

const rows = ROWS.filter((r) => ENGINES.includes(r.engine) && (WAVE === 'sort') === (r.engine === 'sort'))
// Wave S: one writer per module (Module 1 owns two labs); wave CS: one writer per lab, so Module 2's two labs are written apart.
const groups = WAVE === 'sort' ? [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.module))].map((m) => rows.filter((r) => r.module === m)) : rows.map((r) => [r])
const entries = groups.map((labs) => ({
  module: labs[0].module,
  key: WAVE === 'sort' ? `m${pad2(labs[0].module)}` : labs[0].id,
  labs,
  target: TARGETS.find((t) => t.module === labs[0].module),
}))
const missing = entries.filter((e) => e.target === undefined).map((e) => e.module)
if (rows.length === 0) return { stopped: `no lab rows for wave ${WAVE} and engines ${ENGINES.join(', ')}` }
if (missing.length > 0) return { stopped: `--lab-targets has no entry for modules ${missing.join(', ')}` }

const LABW = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    module: { type: 'integer' },
    files: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
    labs: { type: 'integer' },
    problems: { type: 'integer' },
  },
  required: ['module', 'files', 'labs', 'problems'],
}
const LABCRIT = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    lab: { type: 'string' },
    approved: { type: 'boolean' },
    issues: {
      type: 'array',
      items: {
        type: 'object',
        properties: {
          kind: { type: 'string', enum: ['grounding', 'wrongPath', 'alternatives', 'originality', 'style', 'debrief'] },
          where: { type: 'string' },
          fix: { type: 'string' },
        },
        required: ['kind', 'where', 'fix'],
      },
    },
  },
  required: ['lab', 'approved', 'issues'],
}
const GATE = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    replayOk: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
    replayFail: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
    r11: { type: 'string' },
    today: { type: 'string' },
    lines: {
      type: 'array',
      items: {
        type: 'object',
        properties: { lab: { type: 'string' }, problems: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } },
        required: ['lab', 'problems'],
      },
    },
  },
  required: ['replayOk', 'replayFail', 'r11', 'today', 'lines'],
}
const SYNC = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { merged: { type: 'boolean' }, head: { type: 'string' }, check: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['merged', 'head', 'check'],
}
const COMMIT = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { module: { type: 'integer' }, commit: { type: 'string' }, body: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['module', 'commit', 'body'],
}
const FINAL = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { labsLine: { type: 'string' }, validate: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['labsLine', 'validate'],
}

const labLine = (r) =>
  `- ${r.id}: file content/labs/${r.file}, engine ${r.engine}${r.model ? ` (model ${r.model})` : ''}, title "${r.title}", spec 5.2 scope: ${r.scope}`
const simulateRule = (labs) =>
  labs.some((r) => r.engine === 'simulate')
    ? '\nFor a Simulate lab, copy its model, constants, parameter ids, kinds, ranges, steps, initials and option ids, thresholds, solution and wrong-path actions exactly from its row of SIM_LABS in tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts, as the Simulate labs section of agents/lab-writer.md says; write only the text (brief, goal labels and hints, mistakes, parameter labels, debrief), never change a number, and run `pnpm vitest run tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts` until it passes for your lab.'
    : ''

/** One lab writer for one entry; `revise` holds the open labs' critic issues and replay lines in revise mode. */
function writer(entry, revise, label, phaseName) {
  const mode = revise
    ? `You are in revise mode of agents/lab-writer.md. Fix only the issues below, keep each id and keep rev: 1 (no lab here has been committed yet), and touch no lab that is not listed:
${JSON.stringify(revise)}`
    : 'Write each lab below as its own new file at exactly its path.'
  return agent(`You are the lab writer for Module ${entry.module} of the CCP study site in the git worktree ${WT}.
Read agents/lab-writer.md and content/STYLE.md in full and follow the prompt exactly; read src/labs/contract.ts, schemas/lab.ts and src/labs/<engine>/model.ts for each engine below.
Your labs (id, file, engine and title are binding):
${entry.labs.map(labLine).join('\n')}
Your entry of \`pnpm --silent content:report --lab-targets\` (concepts with their home notes, tasks and live practice ids and types) is:
${JSON.stringify(entry.target)}
${mode}${simulateRule(entry.labs)}
Every lab has tier: core, rev: 1 and status: live; its text is original, set in the cafe, and rests on lines of the notes it cites; it names Support plans only by their four current names; prices are lab credits and any figure the notes do not state is called a lab value.
Pick 2 to 3 debrief questions from the live practice ids your entry lists for the lab's concepts, preferring single; you may open their practice files under content/questions/ to read their stems, and nothing else there.
Loop on \`pnpm --silent content:validate --draft content/labs/<file>\` for each of your files until its last line ends with \`0 problems\`.
Write no other file. ${NEVER_READ_LAB}
${HOOKS}
Return the module number, the files you wrote (paths from the repo root), the number of labs and the sum of the problem counts of your last --draft runs.`, { label, phase: phaseName, schema: LABW })
}

/** A fresh critic for one lab file; it never edits. */
function critic(r, round) {
  const sim = r.engine === 'simulate' ? `\nRun \`pnpm vitest run tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts\`: any failure for this lab, or any number that differs from its row of SIM_LABS there, is a wrongPath issue.` : ''
  return agent(`You are a fresh lab critic for one lab of the CCP study site in the git worktree ${WT}.
Read agents/lab-critic.md and content/STYLE.md in full and follow the prompt exactly.
Judge only content/labs/${r.file}: lab ${r.id}, engine ${r.engine}, title "${r.title}", spec 5.2 scope: ${r.scope}.${sim}
Run \`pnpm --silent content:validate --draft content/labs/${r.file}\` and read its R7 and R11 lines.
Never edit or create any file. ${NEVER_READ_LAB}
${HOOKS}
Return the lab id, approved true only when you found no issue, and every issue with its kind (grounding, wrongPath, alternatives, originality, style or debrief), where it is in the file, and the fix.`, { label: `critic:${round}:${r.id}`, phase: 'Critic', schema: LABCRIT })
}

/** The replay gate: runs the draft check per lab, the whole validator and the content tests; it never edits. */
function gate(round) {
  return agent(`${HOOKS}
Do not edit, create or commit anything.
For each of these files, run \`pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>\` and keep its output: ${rows.map((r) => `content/labs/${r.file}`).join(', ')}.
Then run \`pnpm --silent content:validate 2>&1 | tail -1\` and return that line as r11.
Then run \`pnpm vitest run tests/content 2>&1 | tail -30\`.
A lab is in replayOk when its file exists, its --draft run ends with \`0 problems\` and the test "<lab id> replays its solution and wrong paths" passed; every other lab of the list is in replayFail. Name labs by id only.
For each lab in replayFail, add to lines its id and the problem lines of its --draft run (they name lab, goal and question ids, never question text).
Return today as printed by \`date +%F\`.`, { label: `gate:${WAVE}${round}`, phase: 'Gate', schema: GATE, effort: 'low' })
}

phase('Write')
const written = await parallel(entries.map((e) => () => writer(e, null, `write:${e.key}`, 'Write')))
const writeProblems = written.filter((w) => !w || w.problems > 0).length
log(`write:${WAVE}: ${written.reduce((n, w) => n + (w?.labs ?? 0), 0)} labs from ${entries.length} writers; ${writeProblems} with problems`)

const approved = new Set()
const replayOk = new Set()
const kinds = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, ['unjudged']]))
const issues = new Map()
let lastGate = null
for (let round = 1; round <= CRITIC_ROUNDS; round++) {
  const open = rows.filter((r) => !approved.has(r.id))
  phase('Critic')
  const verdicts = await parallel(open.map((r) => () => critic(r, round)))
  open.forEach((r, i) => {
    const v = verdicts[i]
    if (v && v.approved) {
      approved.add(r.id)
      issues.delete(r.id)
    } else {
      issues.set(r.id, v ? v.issues : [{ kind: 'style', where: 'whole file', fix: 'the critic returned nothing; judge again' }])
    }
  })

  phase('Gate')
  lastGate = await gate(round)
  if (!lastGate) return { stopped: `gate ${round} returned nothing`, wave: WAVE }
  replayOk.clear()
  for (const id of lastGate.replayOk) replayOk.add(id)
  for (const r of rows) {
    const k = new Set((issues.get(r.id) ?? []).map((i) => i.kind))
    if (!replayOk.has(r.id)) k.add('replay')
    kinds.set(r.id, [...k])
  }
  const failing = rows.filter((r) => !(approved.has(r.id) && replayOk.has(r.id)))
  log(`round ${round}: ${rows.length - failing.length}/${rows.length} labs approved and replaying`)
  if (failing.length === 0) break
  if (round === CRITIC_ROUNDS) break
  if (lastGate.today > LAST_REWRITE_DAY) {
    log(`past ${LAST_REWRITE_DAY}: no more rewrites`)
    break
  }

  phase('Revise')
  // A lab that replays but lost its approval, or never had it, goes back to the critic next round.
  for (const r of failing) approved.delete(r.id)
  const revising = entries.filter((e) => e.labs.some((r) => failing.includes(r)))
  await parallel(revising.map((e) => () => {
    const list = e.labs
      .filter((r) => failing.includes(r))
      .map((r) => ({
        lab: r.id,
        file: `content/labs/${r.file}`,
        issues: issues.get(r.id) ?? [],
        replay: lastGate.lines.find((l) => l.lab === r.id)?.problems ?? [],
      }))
    return writer(e, list, `revise:${round}:${e.key}`, 'Revise')
  }))
}

const shipped = rows.filter((r) => approved.has(r.id) && replayOk.has(r.id))
const held = rows.filter((r) => !shipped.includes(r)).map((r) => ({ lab: r.id, kinds: kinds.get(r.id) ?? [] }))
if (shipped.length === 0) return { wave: WAVE, shipped: [], held, stopped: 'no lab is approved and replaying' }

phase('Sync')
const heldFiles = rows.filter((r) => !shipped.includes(r)).map((r) => `content/labs/${r.file}`)
const sync = await agent(`${HOOKS}
You bring master into the lab branch ${BRANCH} in the worktree ${WT}, so the labs meet the current build, chunks and end-to-end helpers before they reach master.
${heldFiles.length > 0 ? `First move each of these held lab files, when it exists, under .results/labs/held/ with the same relative path (mkdir -p first), so the tree holds only the shipped labs: ${heldFiles.join(', ')}.` : 'No lab is held.'}
Run \`git -C "${WT}" merge --no-edit master\` (report "up to date" when nothing is new); on a conflict, run \`git merge --abort\` and return merged false with check "conflict".
Then run \`pnpm install --frozen-lockfile | tail -3\` and \`pnpm check 2>&1 | tail -30\`.
Return merged true, head = \`git rev-parse --short HEAD\`, and check = "pass" or the names of the failing checks with the failing lab ids (never their output).`, { label: `sync:${WAVE}`, phase: 'Sync', schema: SYNC, effort: 'low' })
if (!sync || !sync.merged || sync.check !== 'pass') {
  return { wave: WAVE, stopped: `sync: ${sync ? sync.check : 'no reply'}`, shipped: shipped.map((r) => r.id), held }
}

phase('Commit')
const commits = []
for (const m of [...new Set(shipped.map((r) => r.module))].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
  const labs = shipped.filter((r) => r.module === m)
  const engines = [...new Set(labs.map((r) => ENGINE_NAME[r.engine]))]
  const subject = `Add the ${engines.join(' and ')} lab${labs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} for Module ${m}`
  const c = await agent(`${HOOKS}
Work on branch ${BRANCH}. Commit exactly these lab files of Module ${m}, staged by path: ${labs.map((r) => `content/labs/${r.file}`).join(', ')}.
Count their wrong paths with \`grep -c 'failsGoal:' <file>\` summed over the files as W.
Commit with two -m flags: the subject "${subject}" and the body "${labs.length} lab${labs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}; ${labs.length === 1 ? 'its solution and its' : 'each solution and its'} W wrong paths replay." with W filled in.
The pre-commit hook runs pnpm check, which replays every lab under R11; when it fails, edit nothing, run \`git restore --staged\` on these files, and return commit "failed" with the names of the failing checks and the failing lab ids as the body (never their output).
Return the module number, the commit hash and the body you used.`, { label: `commit:m${pad2(m)}`, phase: 'Commit', schema: COMMIT, effort: 'low' })
  commits.push(c)
  // A lab edited after its critic approved it would ship unjudged, so a failing hook stops the wave instead.
  if (!c || c.commit === 'failed') {
    return { wave: WAVE, stopped: `commit: Module ${m} ${c ? c.body : 'no reply'}`, shipped: shipped.map((r) => r.id), held, commits }
  }
}

const final = await agent(`${HOOKS}
Do not edit or commit anything.
Return as labsLine the line of \`pnpm --silent content:report\` that starts with "Labs:", and as validate the last line of \`pnpm --silent content:validate\`.`, { label: `final:${WAVE}`, phase: 'Commit', schema: FINAL, effort: 'low' })

return {
  wave: WAVE,
  engines: ENGINES,
  shipped: shipped.map((r) => r.id),
  held,
  commits,
  labsLine: final ? final.labsLine : '',
  validate: final ? final.validate : '',
}
```

What each phase does:

- `Write`: one lab writer per entry in parallel, each reading `agents/lab-writer.md` and `content/STYLE.md` in full, writing only its own files and looping on `--draft` until `0 problems`.
- `Critic`: one fresh `lab-critic` per lab that is not yet approved, judging grounding, wrong paths, alternatives, originality, style and debrief fit, and never editing.
- `Gate`: one low-effort agent runs `--draft` per lab, the whole validator and `tests/content`, and returns which labs replay, the problem lines of the rest and today's date.
- `Revise`: one writer per entry with an open lab, in revise mode, with the critic's issues and the replay lines inlined; then the next round judges again, for at most 3 rounds and never past `LAST_REWRITE_DAY`.
- `Sync`: held labs move under `.results/labs/held/`, `master` is merged in and `pnpm check` must pass.
- `Commit`: one commit per module in module order, each through the pre-commit hook, then a final agent reads the `Labs:` line and the validator's last line.

Expected return: `{ wave: 'sort', shipped: [11 lab ids], held: [], commits: [10 entries], labsLine: 'Labs: 11 live (11 core, 0 stretch); sort 11, configure 0, simulate 0', validate: 'content:validate: 13 rules, 0 findings' }`.
A non-empty `held` lists each dropped lab with the kinds of issue it still had; `labsLine` then counts only the shipped labs.
A `stopped` return names the cause (a missing argument, an empty gate, a failing sync or a failing commit hook).
When it stopped at Sync or Commit, the labs are written and judged: fix the cause in the worktree, run `pnpm check`, then commit each module's shipped files that are not yet committed by path with the subject and body the Commit phase would use, and go on with Step 4; when the fix had to change a lab file, run a fresh `lab-critic` on that file first.
Otherwise fix the cause and run the script again with the same `args`; its writers start afresh and overwrite the files at their paths.

- [ ] **Step 10: The outer stream for the controller's build script**

The controller runs Steps 1 to 9 from its own build script, the way Phase 3's build script ran its exam-pool stream; this stream uses that script's `agent`, `workflow`, `merge`, `section`, `merges`, `log`, `SCR`, `REPO` and `ENV`, and `g3`, `g12` and `g13` are its gates (made with its `gate()` helper) that resolve true when Tasks 3, 12 and 13 are on `master`:

```js
const LABS = `${SCR}/p4/phase4-labs.js`
const LABS_S_WT = `${SCR}/p4/wt-labs-sort`
const LABS_CS_WT = `${SCR}/p4/wt-labs-cs`
const LAB_SETUP = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { worktree: { type: 'string' }, head: { type: 'string' }, targetsJson: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['worktree', 'head', 'targetsJson'],
}

/** Runs the worktree and targets steps of one wave and returns the parsed `--lab-targets` array, or a reason. */
async function labSetup(wave, steps) {
  const setup = await agent(`You run ${steps} of Phase 4 Task 10 of CCP Study for wave ${wave} (read ${section(10)}). $SCRATCH is ${SCR} and $REPO is ${REPO}. ${ENV}
Return the absolute worktree path, the last line of git log --oneline -1 in it, and the full JSON array from .results/lab-targets.json as one string (it holds concept, note and practice ids and types only).`,
    { label: `labs:setup:${wave}`, phase: 'Labs', schema: LAB_SETUP })
  if (!setup) return { why: `wave ${wave} setup failed` }
  try {
    return { worktree: setup.worktree, head: setup.head, targets: JSON.parse(setup.targetsJson) }
  } catch (e) {
    return { why: `wave ${wave} targets did not parse: ${String(e).slice(0, 120)}` }
  }
}

const labsSort = (async () => {
  if (!(await g3.p)) return { ok: false, why: 'Task 3 not merged' }
  const setup = await labSetup('S', 'Steps 1 and 2')
  if (setup.why) return { ok: false, why: setup.why }
  log(`Labs S: worktree at ${setup.head}`)
  const run = await workflow({ scriptPath: LABS }, { worktree: setup.worktree, wave: 'sort', targets: setup.targets })
  if (!run || run.stopped) return { ok: false, why: run ? run.stopped : 'no return', run }
  merges.labsSort = await merge('content/phase-4-labs-sort', LABS_S_WT, 'the Phase 4 Sort labs', `Run Step 5 of ${section(10)} exactly: the merge with its message, with <n> = ${run.shipped.length} and <m> = the number of modules those labs cover, then pnpm verify.`)
  return { ok: true, run }
})()

const labsCs = (async () => {
  if (!(await g12.p)) return { ok: false, why: 'Task 12 not merged' }
  const setup = await labSetup('CS', 'Step 6')
  if (setup.why) return { ok: false, why: setup.why }
  const runs = [await workflow({ scriptPath: LABS }, { worktree: setup.worktree, wave: 'cs', engines: ['configure'], targets: setup.targets })]
  if (!runs[0] || runs[0].stopped) return { ok: false, why: runs[0] ? runs[0].stopped : 'no return', runs }
  if (!(await g13.p)) return { ok: false, why: 'Task 13 not merged', runs }
  const again = await labSetup('CS', 'the commands of Step 8 (the merge of master, the install, pnpm check and the new targets file), in the existing worktree')
  if (again.why) return { ok: false, why: again.why, runs }
  runs.push(await workflow({ scriptPath: LABS }, { worktree: again.worktree, wave: 'cs', engines: ['simulate'], targets: again.targets }))
  if (!runs[1] || runs[1].stopped) return { ok: false, why: runs[1] ? runs[1].stopped : 'no return', runs }
  const shipped = runs.flatMap((r) => (r && r.shipped) || [])
  merges.labsCs = await merge('content/phase-4-labs-cs', LABS_CS_WT, 'the Phase 4 Configure and Simulate labs', `Run Step 9 of ${section(10)} exactly: the merge with its message, with <n> = ${shipped.length} and <m> = the number of modules those labs cover, then pnpm verify.`)
  return { ok: true, runs }
})()
```

Expected: `labsSort` and `labsCs` each resolve to `{ ok: true, ... }` with their workflow returns, and the two merges land in the serialized chain in whichever order the waves finish.

## Task 11: The Sort cut gate (0.5.0)

**Files:**
- Modify: `package.json` (`version` `0.5.0`), `tests/e2e/offline.spec.ts`, `README.md`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-aws-cpe-study-site-design.md`
- Test: `pnpm exec playwright test tests/e2e/offline.spec.ts`, `pnpm verify`

Design notes:
- A builder runs this task on `master` right after the wave S merge (Task 10 Step 5), by Saturday, October 24; it needs Tasks 1 to 9 and wave S only, so the Sort cut ships as 0.5.0 even if Tasks 12 to 19 run late (spec 10.2).
- The offline test runs on the real shipped `lab-m01-who-patches-what`, or on the first live Sort lab in file order when that one was cut; it reads the lab's `solution` and card texts from its YAML with `yaml`, so it follows whatever the writers shipped, and taps each card and then its bucket target `#bucket-<id>`.
- The service worker already precaches every module chunk, and labs ride in those chunks (Task 4), so no worker change is needed for labs to work offline.
- The spec records the Sort-side deviations, one sentence each, as `NOTE: ... (Phase 4).` lines beside the text they change, the form Phase 3 used; Task 20 records the rest.
- The backup's `appVersion` follows `package.json`, so 0.5.0 reaches exported backups with no other change.
- Covers P4-002 (Sort labs replay), P4-123, P4-130 (0.5.0), P4-134 and P4-137.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the offline lab test**

In `tests/e2e/offline.spec.ts`, replace the import block with:

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { parse } from 'yaml';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { eventsOf, timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { failOnPageProblems, h1, seedEvents } from './helpers.ts';
import { labDoneCount } from './lab-helpers.ts';
import { chunkFor } from './questions.ts';
```

and append at the end of the file:

```ts
/** The parts of a shipped Sort lab file this test reads. */
interface SortLabFile {
  id: string;
  module: number;
  title: string;
  engine: string;
  status?: string;
  spec: { cards: { id: string; text: string }[] };
  solution: { t: string; card?: string; bucket?: string }[];
}

/** The shipped `lab-m01-who-patches-what`, or the first live Sort lab in file order when that one was cut. */
function shippedSortLab(): SortLabFile {
  const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../content/labs/', import.meta.url));
  const files = readdirSync(root)
    .sort()
    .flatMap((dir) =>
      readdirSync(join(root, dir))
        .filter((file) => file.endsWith('.yaml'))
        .sort()
        .map((file) => join(root, dir, file)),
    );
  const labs = files
    .map((file) => parse(readFileSync(file, 'utf8')) as SortLabFile)
    .filter((lab) => lab.engine === 'sort' && (lab.status ?? 'live') === 'live');
  const lab = labs.find((l) => l.id === 'lab-m01-who-patches-what') ?? labs[0];
  if (lab === undefined) throw new Error('no Sort lab has shipped');
  return lab;
}

test('offline, a Sort lab never opened online runs and records lab.done', async ({ page, context }) => {
  const lab = shippedSortLab();
  await installWorker(page);

  await context.setOffline(true);
  await page.goto(`/#/m/${String(lab.module).padStart(2, '0')}/lab/${lab.id}`);
  await expect(h1(page, lab.title)).toBeVisible();
  for (const action of lab.solution) {
    if (action.t !== 'place') continue;
    const text = lab.spec.cards.find((c) => c.id === action.card)?.text ?? '';
    await page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Cards' }).getByRole('button', { name: text, exact: true }).click();
    await page.locator(`#bucket-${action.bucket}`).click();
  }
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Debrief' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => labDoneCount(page, lab.id)).toBe(1);
});
```

Insert this section right before `## Course text stays out`:

```markdown
## Labs

Labs live in `content/labs/NN-module/<slug>.yaml`, one file per lab, with the id `lab-mNN-<slug>`, written by `agents/lab-writer.md` and judged by `agents/lab-critic.md`.
Each module page lists its labs in a Labs section between Practice and Boss, and the palette finds them by title.
A Sort lab is played by tapping a card, then its bucket; by keyboard, `Tab` moves between the tray, the buckets and the goal hints, the arrow keys move within one, `Space` picks a card up, `1` to `9` and `0` place it, `Backspace` sends it back and `Escape` puts it down.
Goals are checked only when you press Check or `Enter`, `h` shows a hint, and `r` asks before it resets the board.
Hints are free, but a lab finished without one pays 10 XP more; only a lab's first completion pays XP and counts for the streak, and every completion runs its 2 or 3 debrief questions.
Unfinished work stays in the browser, so a lab resumes after a reload, and labs work offline like the rest of the site.
`pnpm content:validate --draft <lab file>` checks one lab, and rule R11 replays every lab's solution and wrong paths on each commit.
```

## Task 12: The Configure engine and view

**Files:**
- Create: `src/labs/configure/model.ts`, `src/labs/configure/View.tsx`, `tests/unit/lab-configure.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts`
- Modify: `schemas/lab.ts`, `src/labs/engines.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/labs.ts`, `agents/lab-writer.md`, `agents/lab-critic.md`, `content/STYLE.md`, `src/app/screens/Lab.tsx`, `src/app/lab.ts`, `src/ui/base.css` (under `/* Labs: Configure */` only), `tests/e2e/lab-helpers.ts`, `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/lab-configure.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-schema.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r11.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab.test.ts`, `tests/property/labs.property.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`

Design notes:
- Configure is spec 5.1's stripped-down console wizard: a spec lists 1 to 8 `fields` (a `select` of 2 to 8 options, or a `toggle`) and `rules` `{ goal, field, in }`; a rule holds when the field's value is in `in`, and a goal passes when all of its rules hold.
- Goals come back in the order the rules first name them, which is the contract's "first-mention order", so R11's `goals do not match the spec` check works for Configure unchanged.
- The state keeps the spec beside the values, so `reduce` can reject an unknown field or a value of the wrong kind on its own and return the same state object, as the contract asks.
- `ENGINES.configure` settles nothing and is always complete: every field always holds a value, so a Configure wrong path is never "unfinished".
- `configureTexts` (field labels, option labels and details) joins `SPEC_TEXTS`, so R7's run check and the retired Support plan check read every Configure string; each `SPEC_TEXTS` entry parses the spec itself and returns `[]` when it does not parse, so it is safe whether or not Task 2's `labTexts` parses first.
- The view uses native controls only: a `<label>` plus `<select>` per select field with the chosen option's detail below it (tied by `aria-describedby`), and a checkbox in an `.option`-styled label per toggle; Configure adds no keys of its own, and `Enter` from a focused select or checkbox checks through Task 6's `labKey`.
- The two made-up bakery labs in `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts` (`bakeryConfigureLab` here, `bakerySimulateLab` in Task 13) are the worked examples for both engines: the unit, schema, property and R11 tests run on them, and the writer prompts point to them; the file is new because `tests/fixtures/labs.ts` belongs to Task 1.
- `checkLabel(engine)` gives the check button's words (`Check`, or `Run and check` for Simulate), and `Lab.tsx` renders the engine view through one `Board` switch, so Task 13 only adds a case.
- Covers P4-022 (Configure), P4-042, P4-049, P4-053 (Configure), P4-116, P4-121.

Create `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts` (Task 13 appends the Simulate lab):

```ts
import type { LabFile } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';

/** Made-up bakery labs for the Configure and Simulate engines; no course text. */

const DEBRIEF: LabFile['debrief'] = {
  lines: [
    'A shared kitchen is rented by the minute, so the bakery pays only while the ovens run.',
    'A bigger oven for the weekend rush costs nothing on the quiet weekdays when it is switched off.',
  ],
  questions: ['q-b4k7m2', 'q-d9p4r6'],
};

/** A complete Configure lab: pick the oven and the rental for the weekend bake. */
export function bakeryConfigureLab(over: Partial<LabFile> = {}): LabFile {
  return {
    id: 'lab-m01-pick-the-oven',
    module: 1,
    title: 'Pick the oven',
    engine: 'configure',
    tier: 'core',
    status: 'live',
    rev: 1,
    concepts: ['shared-kitchen', 'per-minute-rental'],
    notes: ['m01/what-a-bakery-cloud-is'],
    minutes: 4,
    brief:
      'The bakery rents one oven in the shared kitchen for the weekend bake. Choose the oven and how to pay for it, then lock the back door.',
    spec: {
      fields: [
        {
          kind: 'select',
          id: 'oven',
          label: 'Oven',
          options: [
            { id: 'small-oven', label: 'Small oven', detail: 'Holds 6 trays.' },
            { id: 'deck-oven', label: 'Deck oven', detail: 'Holds 20 trays.' },
            { id: 'tunnel-oven', label: 'Tunnel oven', detail: 'Holds 200 trays and needs a crew of four.' },
          ],
          initial: 'small-oven',
        },
        {
          kind: 'select',
          id: 'rental',
          label: 'Rental',
          options: [
            { id: 'per-minute', label: 'Per minute', detail: 'Billed only while the oven is on.' },
            { id: 'monthly', label: 'Monthly', detail: 'One fixed fee, on or off.' },
          ],
          initial: 'monthly',
        },
        { kind: 'toggle', id: 'door', label: 'Leave the back door open to everyone', initial: true },
      ],
      rules: [
        { goal: 'g1', field: 'oven', in: ['deck-oven'] },
        { goal: 'g2', field: 'rental', in: ['per-minute'] },
        { goal: 'g2', field: 'door', in: [false] },
      ],
    },
    goals: [
      {
        id: 'g1',
        label: 'The oven fits 20 trays',
        hint: 'The weekend bake fills 20 trays, and a crew of one runs it.',
      },
      {
        id: 'g2',
        label: 'Pay only while baking, door locked',
        hint: 'Weekdays are quiet, and only bakers should reach the oven.',
      },
    ],
    solution: [
      { t: 'set', field: 'oven', value: 'deck-oven' },
      { t: 'set', field: 'rental', value: 'per-minute' },
      { t: 'set', field: 'door', value: false },
    ],
    wrongPaths: [
      {
        actions: [
          { t: 'set', field: 'oven', value: 'tunnel-oven' },
          { t: 'set', field: 'rental', value: 'per-minute' },
          { t: 'set', field: 'door', value: false },
        ],
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        mistake: 'Picks the biggest oven to be safe',
      },
      {
        actions: [
          { t: 'set', field: 'oven', value: 'deck-oven' },
          { t: 'set', field: 'door', value: false },
        ],
        failsGoal: 'g2',
        mistake: 'Keeps the monthly fee for a weekend-only oven',
      },
    ],
    debrief: DEBRIEF,
    ...over,
  };
}
```

Create `tests/unit/lab-configure.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ConfigureSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { configureTexts } from '../../scripts/content/lib/labs.ts';
import { CONFIGURE, type ConfigureState } from '../../src/labs/configure/model.ts';
import { allPass, replayLab } from '../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import { checkLab, ENGINES } from '../../src/labs/engines.ts';
import { bakeryConfigureLab } from '../fixtures/lab-specs.ts';

const lab = bakeryConfigureLab();
const spec = ConfigureSpec.parse(lab.spec);
const run = (actions: readonly unknown[]): ConfigureState =>
  replayLab(CONFIGURE, spec, actions as Parameters<typeof CONFIGURE.reduce>[1][]);
const words = (state: ConfigureState) =>
  CONFIGURE.check(spec, state).map((r) => [r.goalId, r.pass, r.message]);

describe('the Configure checker', () => {
  it('starts from every field initial and fails every goal', () => {
    const start = CONFIGURE.init(spec);
    expect(start.values).toEqual({ oven: 'small-oven', rental: 'monthly', door: true });
    expect(words(start)).toEqual([
      ['g1', false, '0 of 1 settings right'],
      ['g2', false, '0 of 2 settings right'],
    ]);
  });

  it('passes every goal with the solution', () => {
    expect(allPass(CONFIGURE.check(spec, run(lab.solution)))).toBe(true);
    expect(words(run(lab.solution))).toEqual([
      ['g1', true, '1 of 1 settings right'],
      ['g2', true, '2 of 2 settings right'],
    ]);
  });

  it('counts a wrong option against its goal only', () => {
    const state = run([
      { t: 'set', field: 'oven', value: 'deck-oven' },
      { t: 'set', field: 'rental', value: 'per-minute' },
    ]);
    expect(words(state)).toEqual([
      ['g1', true, '1 of 1 settings right'],
      ['g2', false, '1 of 2 settings right'],
    ]);
  });

  it.each([
    ['an unknown field', { t: 'set', field: 'chimney', value: 'deck-oven' }],
    ['an option of another field', { t: 'set', field: 'oven', value: 'per-minute' }],
    ['a boolean for a select', { t: 'set', field: 'oven', value: true }],
    ['an option id for a toggle', { t: 'set', field: 'door', value: 'deck-oven' }],
    ['an unknown action', { t: 'place', card: 'oven', bucket: 'deck-oven' }],
    ['a value that is not an object', 'set'],
    ['null', null],
  ])('ignores %s and returns the same state object', (_, action) => {
    const start = CONFIGURE.init(spec);
    expect(CONFIGURE.reduce(start, action as never)).toBe(start);
  });

  it('reset restores every initial value', () => {
    expect(run([...lab.solution, { t: 'reset' }]).values).toEqual(CONFIGURE.init(spec).values);
  });

  it('names the fields whose rule fails in the hint, in rule order', () => {
    const start = CONFIGURE.init(spec);
    expect(CONFIGURE.hint(spec, start, 'g1')).toBe('Look again at Oven.');
    expect(CONFIGURE.hint(spec, start, 'g2')).toBe(
      'Look again at Rental and Leave the back door open to everyone.',
    );
    expect(CONFIGURE.hint(spec, run(lab.solution), 'g2')).toBe('Every setting of this goal is right.');
  });

  it('checks the same through the engine registry, which settles nothing and is always complete', () => {
    const entry = ENGINES.configure;
    if (entry === undefined) throw new Error('configure is not registered');
    const state = run(lab.solution);
    expect(entry.settle).toEqual([]);
    expect(entry.complete(spec, CONFIGURE.init(spec))).toBe(true);
    expect(checkLab(entry, spec, state)).toEqual(CONFIGURE.check(spec, state));
  });

  it('gives R7 every field label, option label and detail', () => {
    expect(configureTexts(lab.spec)).toEqual([
      'Oven',
      'Small oven',
      'Holds 6 trays.',
      'Deck oven',
      'Holds 20 trays.',
      'Tunnel oven',
      'Holds 200 trays and needs a crew of four.',
      'Rental',
      'Per minute',
      'Billed only while the oven is on.',
      'Monthly',
      'One fixed fee, on or off.',
      'Leave the back door open to everyone',
    ]);
    expect(configureTexts({ fields: [] })).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

Append to `tests/unit/lab-schema.test.ts` (Task 1's file), and merge `ConfigureAction` and `ConfigureSpec` into its import from `'../../schemas/lab.ts'` and add `import { bakeryConfigureLab } from '../fixtures/lab-specs.ts';`, so each name is imported once:

```ts
describe('ConfigureSpec', () => {
  const spec = ConfigureSpec.parse(bakeryConfigureLab().spec);
  const messages = (value: unknown): string[] => {
    const r = ConfigureSpec.safeParse(value);
    return r.success ? [] : r.error.issues.map((i) => i.message);
  };

  it('accepts the bakery lab', () => {
    expect(spec.fields.map((f) => f.id)).toEqual(['oven', 'rental', 'door']);
  });

  it('rejects a rule on an unknown field and a value of the wrong kind', () => {
    expect(messages({ ...spec, rules: [{ goal: 'g1', field: 'chimney', in: ['tall'] }] })).toEqual([
      "rule names unknown field 'chimney'",
    ]);
    expect(messages({ ...spec, rules: [{ goal: 'g1', field: 'door', in: ['deck-oven'] }] })).toEqual([
      "'deck-oven' is not a value of field door",
    ]);
  });

  it('rejects an initial that is not an option, and repeated field or option ids', () => {
    const [oven, rental, door] = spec.fields;
    expect(messages({ ...spec, fields: [{ ...oven, initial: 'brick-oven' }, rental, door] })).toEqual([
      "initial 'brick-oven' is not an option of oven",
    ]);
    expect(messages({ ...spec, fields: [oven, rental, door, { ...door, label: 'Door again' }] })).toEqual([
      "duplicate field id 'door'",
    ]);
  });

  it('parses set and reset actions only', () => {
    expect(ConfigureAction.safeParse({ t: 'set', field: 'door', value: false }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(ConfigureAction.safeParse({ t: 'set', field: 'door', value: 2 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(ConfigureAction.safeParse({ t: 'run' }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Append to `tests/unit/r11.test.ts` (Task 2's file), adding to its imports only the names it lacks of `LabFile` (type, `'../../schemas/lab.ts'`), `LabEntry` (type, `'../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts'`), `labProblems` (`'../../scripts/content/rules/r11-labs.ts'`) and `bakeryConfigureLab` (`'../fixtures/lab-specs.ts'`):

```ts
const labEntryOf = (lab: LabFile): LabEntry => ({
  path: `content/labs/01-baking-basics/${lab.id.slice('lab-m01-'.length)}.yaml`,
  module: 1,
  slug: lab.id.slice('lab-m01-'.length),
  lab,
});

describe('R11 on Configure labs', () => {
  it('replays the bakery Configure lab without a problem', () => {
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf(bakeryConfigureLab()))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reports a wrong path that meets the goal it should fail', () => {
    const lab = bakeryConfigureLab();
    const wrongPaths = [
      {
        ...lab.wrongPaths[0],
        actions: lab.solution,
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        mistake: 'Picks the biggest oven to be safe',
      },
    ];
    expect(
      labProblems(labEntryOf({ ...lab, wrongPaths: [...wrongPaths, ...lab.wrongPaths.slice(1)] })),
    ).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-pick-the-oven: wrongPaths[0] (Picks the biggest oven to be safe) meets g1, which it should fail',
    ]);
  });

  it('reports a set action on a field value of the wrong type', () => {
    const lab = bakeryConfigureLab({ solution: [{ t: 'set', field: 'door', value: 3 }] });
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf(lab))).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf(lab))[0]).toMatch(/^lab-m01-pick-the-oven: solution\[0\]: /);
  });
});
```

```ts
const CONFIGURE_SPEC = ConfigureSpec.parse(bakeryConfigureLab().spec);
/** Known and unknown fields with right, wrong and junk values, plus junk actions. */
const configureActionArb = fc.oneof(
  fc.record({
    t: fc.constant('set'),
    field: fc.constantFrom('oven', 'rental', 'door', 'chimney'),
    value: fc.oneof(
      fc.constantFrom('small-oven', 'deck-oven', 'tunnel-oven', 'per-minute', 'monthly', 'brick-oven'),
      fc.boolean(),
      fc.integer(),
    ),
  }),
  fc.constant({ t: 'reset' }),
  fc.anything(),
);

describe('the Configure reducer', () => {
  it('never throws, is deterministic and checks purely', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.array(configureActionArb, { maxLength: 30 }), (actions) => {
        const a = replayLab(CONFIGURE, CONFIGURE_SPEC, actions as never[]);
        const b = replayLab(CONFIGURE, CONFIGURE_SPEC, actions as never[]);
        expect(toPlain(a)).toEqual(toPlain(b));
        expect(CONFIGURE.check(CONFIGURE_SPEC, a)).toEqual(CONFIGURE.check(CONFIGURE_SPEC, b));
        for (const value of Object.values(a.values)) expect(['string', 'boolean']).toContain(typeof value);
      }),
      RUNS,
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the Configure spec and action schemas**

In `schemas/lab.ts`, insert this block right above `export const LAB_SCHEMAS` (Task 1):

```ts
const ConfigureOption = z.strictObject({
  id: Slug,
  label: z.string().min(2).max(60),
  detail: z.string().max(120).optional(),
});
const SelectField = z.strictObject({
  kind: z.literal('select'),
  id: Slug,
  label: z.string().min(2).max(40),
  options: z.array(ConfigureOption).min(2).max(8),
  initial: Slug,
});
const ToggleField = z.strictObject({
  kind: z.literal('toggle'),
  id: Slug,
  label: z.string().min(2).max(60),
  initial: z.boolean(),
});
const ConfigureRule = z.strictObject({
  goal: GoalId,
  field: Slug,
  in: z.array(z.union([Slug, z.boolean()])).min(1),
});
export const ConfigureSpec = z
  .strictObject({
    fields: z
      .array(z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [SelectField, ToggleField]))
      .min(1)
      .max(8),
    rules: z.array(ConfigureRule).min(1),
  })
  .superRefine((spec, ctx) => {
    const fields = new Map<string, (typeof spec.fields)[number]>();
    spec.fields.forEach((f, i) => {
      if (fields.has(f.id))
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['fields', i, 'id'], message: `duplicate field id '${f.id}'` });
      fields.set(f.id, f);
      if (f.kind !== 'select') return;
      const ids = f.options.map((o) => o.id);
      ids.forEach((id, j) => {
        if (ids.indexOf(id) !== j)
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: ['fields', i, 'options', j, 'id'],
            message: `duplicate option id '${id}'`,
          });
      });
      if (!ids.includes(f.initial))
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['fields', i, 'initial'],
          message: `initial '${f.initial}' is not an option of ${f.id}`,
        });
    });
    spec.rules.forEach((r, i) => {
      const f = fields.get(r.field);
      if (f === undefined) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['rules', i, 'field'],
          message: `rule names unknown field '${r.field}'`,
        });
        return;
      }
      r.in.forEach((v, j) => {
        const ok =
          f.kind === 'toggle'
            ? typeof v === 'boolean'
            : typeof v === 'string' && f.options.some((o) => o.id === v);
        if (!ok)
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: ['rules', i, 'in', j],
            message: `'${String(v)}' is not a value of field ${f.id}`,
          });
      });
    });
  });
export type ConfigureSpec = z.infer<typeof ConfigureSpec>;
export const ConfigureAction = z.discriminatedUnion('t', [
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('set'), field: Slug, value: z.union([Slug, z.boolean()]) }),
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('reset') }),
]);
export type ConfigureAction = z.infer<typeof ConfigureAction>;
```

Then add the Configure entry to `LAB_SCHEMAS`, so it reads:

```ts
export const LAB_SCHEMAS: {
  readonly [E in LabEngineId]?: { readonly spec: z.ZodType; readonly action: z.ZodType };
} = {
  sort: { spec: SortSpec, action: SortAction },
  configure: { spec: ConfigureSpec, action: ConfigureAction },
};
```
