Create `src/labs/configure/model.ts`:

```ts
import type { ConfigureAction, ConfigureSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import type { GoalResult, LabEngine } from '../contract.ts';

/** The chosen value of every field: an option id for a select, a boolean for a toggle. */
export interface ConfigureState {
  readonly spec: ConfigureSpec;
  readonly values: Readonly<Record<string, string | boolean>>;
}

type Field = ConfigureSpec['fields'][number];
type Rule = ConfigureSpec['rules'][number];

function initial(spec: ConfigureSpec): ConfigureState {
  return { spec, values: Object.fromEntries(spec.fields.map((f) => [f.id, f.initial])) };
}

/** A value fits a field when it is one of a select's option ids, or a boolean for a toggle. */
function fits(field: Field, value: unknown): value is string | boolean {
  if (field.kind === 'toggle') return typeof value === 'boolean';
  return typeof value === 'string' && field.options.some((o) => o.id === value);
}

/** Goal ids in the order the rules first name them. */
export function configureGoals(spec: ConfigureSpec): string[] {
  return [...new Set(spec.rules.map((r) => r.goal))];
}

const holds = (state: ConfigureState, rule: Rule): boolean => {
  const value = state.values[rule.field];
  return value !== undefined && rule.in.includes(value);
};

export const CONFIGURE: LabEngine<ConfigureSpec, ConfigureState, ConfigureAction> = {
  init: initial,

  reduce(state, action) {
    if (typeof action !== 'object' || action === null) return state;
    const a = action as { t?: unknown; field?: unknown; value?: unknown };
    if (a.t === 'reset') return initial(state.spec);
    if (a.t !== 'set') return state;
    const field = state.spec.fields.find((f) => f.id === a.field);
    if (field === undefined || !fits(field, a.value)) return state;
    return { spec: state.spec, values: { ...state.values, [field.id]: a.value } };
  },

  check(spec, state): GoalResult[] {
    return configureGoals(spec).map((goalId) => {
      const rules = spec.rules.filter((r) => r.goal === goalId);
      const right = rules.filter((r) => holds(state, r)).length;
      return { goalId, pass: right === rules.length, message: `${right} of ${rules.length} settings right` };
    });
  },

  hint(spec, state, goalId) {
    const wrong = spec.rules.filter((r) => r.goal === goalId && !holds(state, r)).map((r) => r.field);
    const labels = [...new Set(wrong)].map((id) => spec.fields.find((f) => f.id === id)?.label ?? id);
    return labels.length === 0
      ? 'Every setting of this goal is right.'
      : `Look again at ${labels.join(' and ')}.`;
  },
};
```

In `scripts/content/lib/labs.ts`, merge `ConfigureSpec` into the import from `'../../../schemas/lab.ts'`, add this function above `SPEC_TEXTS`, and add `configure: configureTexts,` to `SPEC_TEXTS` after its `sort` entry:

```ts
/** Field labels, option labels and option details of a Configure spec, or nothing when it does not parse. */
export function configureTexts(spec: unknown): string[] {
  const parsed = ConfigureSpec.safeParse(spec);
  if (!parsed.success) return [];
  return parsed.data.fields
    .flatMap((f) =>
      f.kind === 'toggle' ? [f.label] : [f.label, ...f.options.flatMap((o) => [o.label, o.detail ?? ''])],
    )
    .filter((t) => t !== '');
}
```

Create `src/labs/configure/View.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ConfigureAction, ConfigureSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import type { ConfigureState } from './model.ts';

/** A stripped-down console wizard: native selects and checkboxes, one decision per field (spec 5.1). */
export function ConfigureView(props: {
  spec: ConfigureSpec;
  state: ConfigureState;
  onAction(a: ConfigureAction): void;
}) {
  const { spec, state, onAction } = props;
  return (
    <fieldset class="config">
      <legend class="visually-hidden">Settings</legend>
      {spec.fields.map((field) => {
        const id = `config-${field.id}`;
        const value = state.values[field.id];
        if (field.kind === 'toggle') {
          const checked = value === true;
          return (
            <label key={field.id} class={`option config-toggle${checked ? ' chosen' : ''}`}>
              <input
                id={id}
                type="checkbox"
                checked={checked}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  onAction({ t: 'set', field: field.id, value: event.currentTarget.checked })
                }
              />
              <span class="option-text">{field.label}</span>
            </label>
          );
        }
        const detail = field.options.find((o) => o.id === value)?.detail;
        return (
          <div key={field.id} class="config-field">
            <label for={id}>{field.label}</label>
            <select
              id={id}
              value={typeof value === 'string' ? value : field.initial}
              aria-describedby={detail === undefined ? undefined : `${id}-detail`}
              onChange={(event) => onAction({ t: 'set', field: field.id, value: event.currentTarget.value })}
            >
              {field.options.map((o) => (
                <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                  {o.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            {detail !== undefined && (
              <p id={`${id}-detail`} class="muted field-detail">
                {detail}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

merging each name into an import Task 7 already has from the same file, add this component at module level:

```tsx
/** The engine view for one lab; each engine's state comes from its own reducer. */
function Board(props: {
  lab: LabView;
  state: unknown;
  held: string | null;
  onHold(card: string | null): void;
  onAction(action: unknown): void;
}) {
  const { lab, state, held, onHold, onAction } = props;
  switch (lab.engine) {
    case 'sort':
      return (
        <SortView
          spec={lab.spec as SortSpec}
          state={state as SortState}
          held={held}
          onHold={onHold}
          onAction={onAction}
        />
      );
    case 'configure':
      return (
        <ConfigureView spec={lab.spec as ConfigureSpec} state={state as ConfigureState} onAction={onAction} />
      );
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
```

```ts
export const SYNTHETIC_CONFIGURE_LAB_ID = 'lab-m01-bakery-region';

/** "Read in notes" for a synthetic lab: the section of the first debrief question. */
function notesOf(questions: QuestionView[]): LabView['notes'] {
  return [{ route: questions[0]?.read.route ?? '#/m/01', title: 'The first Module 1 lesson' }];
}

/** A made-up Configure lab with a lab-credit price index, so the screen shows the credits line. */
export function syntheticConfigureLab(questions: QuestionView[]): LabView {
  return {
    id: SYNTHETIC_CONFIGURE_LAB_ID,
    module: 1,
    engine: 'configure',
    tier: 'core',
    rev: 1,
    title: 'Choose the bakery Region',
    minutes: 4,
    concepts: ['bakery-region'],
    brief: 'The bakery moves its order service to the cloud. Customer data must stay in the home country.',
    spec: {
      fields: [
        {
          kind: 'select',
          id: 'region',
          label: 'Region',
          options: [
            {
              id: 'southvale',
              label: 'Southvale',
              detail:
                'Home country. 70 ms from the bakery. No order service yet. Price index 70 lab credits.',
            },
            {
              id: 'northport',
              label: 'Northport',
              detail:
                'Home country. 40 ms from the bakery. Runs the order service. Price index 120 lab credits.',
            },
            {
              id: 'eastmere',
              label: 'Eastmere',
              detail: 'Abroad. 15 ms from the bakery. Runs the order service. Price index 80 lab credits.',
            },
          ],
          initial: 'southvale',
        },
        { kind: 'toggle', id: 'spare', label: 'Keep a spare copy in a second zone', initial: false },
      ],
      rules: [
        { goal: 'g1', field: 'region', in: ['southvale', 'northport'] },
        { goal: 'g2', field: 'region', in: ['northport'] },
        { goal: 'g2', field: 'spare', in: [true] },
      ],
    },
    goals: [
      { id: 'g1', label: 'Customer data stays home', hint: 'Check where each Region keeps its data.' },
      { id: 'g2', label: 'Orders run, with a spare', hint: 'The order service must run there, twice over.' },
    ],
    notes: notesOf(questions),
    debrief: {
      lines: ['Residency rules come first, then the services you need.', 'Price breaks a tie last.'],
      questions: questions.slice(0, 2),
    },
    credits: true,
  };
}
```

```ts
const CONFIGURE_URL = `/#/m/01/lab/${SYNTHETIC_CONFIGURE_LAB_ID}`;

test('a Configure lab works by keyboard alone and shows the lab credits line', async ({
  page,
  browserName,
}) => {
  test.skip(
    browserName === 'webkit',
    'WebKit opens a native menu for letter keys on a select, so typing a letter is unreliable there',
  );
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticConfigureLab);
  await page.goto(CONFIGURE_URL);
  await expect(h1(page, 'Choose the bakery Region')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Prices are in lab credits, a made-up unit, not AWS prices.')).toBeVisible();
  const region = page.getByLabel('Region', { exact: true });
  await region.focus();
  await page.keyboard.press('n');
  await expect(region).toHaveValue('northport');
  await expect(
    page.getByText(
      'Home country. 40 ms from the bakery. Runs the order service. Price index 120 lab credits.',
    ),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByText('1 of 2 goals met.', { exact: true })).toBeAttached();
  await page.keyboard.press('Tab');
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Keep a spare copy in a second zone')).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('Space');
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByText('All 2 goals met. Plus 40 XP.', { exact: true })).toBeAttached();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Debrief' })).toBeFocused();
});

test('a Configure lab has no axe violations and no sideways scroll', async ({ page }) => {
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticConfigureLab);
  await page.goto(CONFIGURE_URL);
  await expect(h1(page, 'Choose the bakery Region')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Check/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('1 of 2 goals met.', { exact: true })).toBeAttached();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});

test('Configure selects and checkboxes are at least 44 px tall on a phone', async ({ page }, testInfo) => {
  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'webkit-mobile', 'the phone layout runs on webkit-mobile only');
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticConfigureLab);
  await page.goto(CONFIGURE_URL);
  await expect(h1(page, 'Choose the bakery Region')).toBeVisible();
  for (const control of [page.getByLabel('Region', { exact: true }), page.locator('label.config-toggle')]) {
    expect((await control.boundingBox())?.height ?? 0).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44);
  }
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});
```

The keyboard test runs on `chromium-desktop`, where typing a letter on a focused select picks the option that starts with it; on `webkit-mobile` the phone test checks the same controls' size and layout.

- [ ] **Step 11: Teach the writer and the critic, and add the style rules**

Append to `agents/lab-writer.md` (Task 3; its frontmatter stays `version: 1`, because no Configure lab has been written with it yet):

````markdown
## Configure labs

A Configure spec is `fields` and `rules`; `ConfigureSpec` in `schemas/lab.ts` is the shape and `bakeryConfigureLab` in `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts` is a complete example.
Each field is one decision: a `select` of 2 to 8 options with ids, labels and an optional `detail`, or a `toggle` with an `initial` boolean.
Each rule is `{ goal, field, in }`: the goal passes when every one of its rules finds the field's value in `in`.
Actions are `{ t: set, field: <field id>, value: <option id or boolean> }` and `{ t: reset }`, and every field starts at its `initial`.
Set `initial` so the start fails at least one goal, and so no goal passes by leaving a field untouched unless another goal of the lab needs that field changed.
Write `minutes` from 4 to 6.

### Launch the order server (Module 2)

Use the id `lab-m02-launch-the-order-server` in `content/labs/02-compute-in-the-cloud/launch-the-order-server.yaml`.
Declare a select `instance-family` of the five families the instance types note names, a select `purchase` of the purchase options the pricing note names, and inbound rules as toggles, such as HTTPS from anywhere, SSH from anywhere and SSH from the cafe only.
Goals: the family fits a balanced web order server; the purchase option suits a server that runs all year and must never be interrupted; the inbound rules open HTTPS to customers and SSH to the cafe only.
Wrong paths: Spot for the order server, a compute optimized family for a balanced load, and SSH open to anyone, each failing the goal it breaks.

### Choose the Region (Module 4)

Use the id `lab-m04-choose-the-region` in `content/labs/04-going-global/choose-the-region.yaml`.
Declare one select `region` of three or four fictional Regions with made-up names, never real Region codes; each option's `detail` states the data residency, the latency to the cafe's customers in ms, whether the needed service runs there, and a price index in lab credits.
Rank the four factors the Region note teaches: compliance first, then the services, then proximity, then price.
Goal g1 lists the Regions that keep the data where the law requires, g2 the Regions that also run the service, and g3 only the closest Region left after g1 and g2, so a cheaper or nearer Region never beats a compliant one.
Wrong paths: the cheapest Region abroad (fails g1) and the nearest Region without the service (fails g2).
Call every latency and price a lab value in the brief, and write prices only as lab credits.
````

Append to `agents/lab-critic.md` (Task 3; it stays `version: 1`):

````markdown
## Configure criteria

No select offers two options a learner could defend for the same goal; when one would, the rule's `in` must list both.
Each rule matches what the brief and the cited note say, and you name the note line for every rule.
Every price is in lab credits, and every figure the notes do not state is called a lab value in the brief or the option detail.
Each wrong path fails its `failsGoal` because of the setting its `mistake` names, not because a field was left at its initial value by accident.
Option labels name the choice and never the verdict; details state facts, never hints.
````

In `content/STYLE.md`, insert this H3 at the end of `## Labs` (after its `### Grounding` section, before any `## Incidents`):

````markdown
### Configure fields

Give each field one decision, labelled by the setting it changes.
Option labels name the choice and never the verdict, so no option reads "Recommended" or "Cheapest".
Option details state facts from the cited note or named lab values, never hints.
````

## Task 13: The Simulate engine, its six models and its view

**Files:**
- Create: `src/labs/simulate/models.ts`, `src/labs/simulate/model.ts`, `src/labs/simulate/View.tsx`, `tests/unit/lab-simulate.test.ts`, `tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad/r11-sim-start-passes.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r11-sim-wrong-path-no-run.yaml`
- Modify (each after Task 12): `schemas/lab.ts`, `src/labs/engines.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/labs.ts`, `agents/lab-writer.md`, `agents/lab-critic.md`, `content/STYLE.md`, `src/app/screens/Lab.tsx`, `src/app/lab.ts`, `src/ui/base.css` (under `/* Labs: Simulate */` only), `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts`, `tests/e2e/lab-helpers.ts`, `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/lab-simulate.test.ts`, `tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab-schema.test.ts`, `tests/unit/r11.test.ts`, `tests/unit/lab.test.ts`, `tests/property/labs.property.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`

Design notes:
- A Simulate lab picks one of six fixed, pure models in `src/labs/simulate/models.ts` (`scaling`, `compute-cost`, `s3-lifecycle`, `cache`, `bill`, `transfer`), supplies its constants (all lab values, every price in lab credits), declares the model's parameters as controls, and sets thresholds on the model's metrics; spec 5.1's deterministic ticks are the 24 hours of `scaling` and the 365 days of `s3-lifecycle`, and the other four are one-tick cost modelers.
- `schemas/lab.ts` validates the constants with `SIM_CONSTANTS[model]` first, and only then asks `modelParams` and `metricInfo` (imported from `models.ts`, which is pure and zod-free, so the direction is allowed) whether every parameter and threshold fits the model; the models therefore only cast their constants.
- Parameter ids use `ParamId` (`/^[a-z][a-zA-Z0-9]*(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/`) instead of `Slug`, because the fixed parameters `secondDay` and `thirdDay` are camelCase while workload and job ids such as `menu-export` are kebab-case; `SimulateAction.param` uses the same pattern.
- `ENGINES.simulate` settles with `[{ t: 'run' }]`, so the screen's `Enter`, the `Run and check` button and every R11 replay judge a run of the parameters as they stand; any accepted `set` clears the result, so no goal ever reads a stale run, and a check before any run reads `Not run yet` only when called on the bare model.
- `fmt` prints at most 2 decimals with trailing zeros trimmed and no grouping, attaches `%`, puts a space before any other unit and adds nothing for `''`; `-0` prints `0`.
- `s3-lifecycle` rounds only its final metrics, a transition to the class already in use changes nothing, leaving a class early by transition or expiry charges the rest of its minimum duration, a read on or after the expiry day finds nothing and counts as late, and an `expire` past day 364 (such as 365 or 2555) expires nothing inside the modelled year.
- The chart is an inline SVG for `scaling` (orders and capacity per hour) and `s3-lifecycle` (cost so far per 30-day month): bars in `--active` and `--muted`, the axis in `--line`, a text legend with swatches, `role="img"` with an `aria-label` that names the series, and a visually hidden table of the same numbers; no other colour, no motion.
- The six labs' numbers are fixed in the table below and in `SIM_LABS` in `tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts`, its machine copy: the test replays every row (start fails a goal, the solution meets all with the exact outcome, each wrong path fails its goal with the exact outcome), holds every shipped lab's model, constants, parameter ids, kinds, ranges, steps, initials and option ids, thresholds, solution and wrong-path actions to it (writer text such as labels is free), and asserts the orderings each lesson rests on; a lab wave CS has not written yet shows one passing `is not shipped yet` test.
- Covers P4-022 (Simulate), P4-042, P4-050, P4-051, P4-053 (Simulate), P4-116, P4-119, P4-121.

The six Simulate labs, fixed (every number is a lab value, every price in lab credits; `set` actions are written `param=value`; each start, solution and wrong path is checked after the implicit `run`; `int` parameters are `min` to `max`, step, initial):

| Lab | Constants | Parameters (initial) | Solution | Goals | Wrong paths (goal they fail, outcome) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

```ts
/** Orders per hour for the bakery's oven rush, hours 0 to 23 (lab values). */
export const OVEN_RUSH_ORDERS = [
  0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 10, 40, 90, 120, 60, 30, 20, 40, 30, 20, 10, 10, 20, 10, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
];

/** A complete Simulate lab on the `scaling` model: enough ovens for the morning, none wasted. */
export function bakerySimulateLab(over: Partial<LabFile> = {}): LabFile {
  return {
    id: 'lab-m01-oven-rush',
    module: 1,
    title: 'Oven rush',
    engine: 'simulate',
    tier: 'core',
    status: 'live',
    rev: 1,
    concepts: ['shared-kitchen', 'per-minute-rental'],
    notes: ['m01/what-a-bakery-cloud-is'],
    minutes: 6,
    brief:
      'Bread orders peak at 08:00. Set how many ovens the bakery rents each hour so no order waits, within a budget of 40 lab credits a day. Every figure here is a lab value.',
    spec: {
      model: 'scaling',
      constants: { orders: OVEN_RUSH_ORDERS, perInstance: 40, credits: 1 },
      params: [
        {
          kind: 'choice',
          id: 'mode',
          label: 'When ovens are added',
          options: [
            { id: 'dynamic', label: 'Once the orders arrive' },
            { id: 'predictive', label: 'Ahead of the forecast' },
          ],
          initial: 'dynamic',
        },
        { kind: 'int', id: 'min', label: 'Fewest ovens', min: 0, max: 4, step: 1, initial: 1 },
        { kind: 'int', id: 'desired', label: 'Ovens at opening', min: 0, max: 4, step: 1, initial: 1 },
        { kind: 'int', id: 'max', label: 'Most ovens', min: 1, max: 6, step: 1, initial: 1 },
        { kind: 'toggle', id: 'balancer', label: 'Share orders across ovens', initial: false },
      ],
      thresholds: [
        { goal: 'g1', metric: 'droppedShare', max: 1 },
        { goal: 'g2', metric: 'cost', max: 40 },
      ],
    },
    goals: [
      {
        id: 'g1',
        label: 'Almost no order waits',
        hint: 'Orders rise before 08:00, and one oven bakes 40 an hour.',
      },
      {
        id: 'g2',
        label: 'Stay within 40 lab credits a day',
        hint: 'Each oven costs 1 lab credit an hour, busy or idle.',
      },
    ],
    solution: [
      { t: 'set', param: 'mode', value: 'predictive' },
      { t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 3 },
      { t: 'set', param: 'balancer', value: true },
    ],
    wrongPaths: [
      {
        actions: [
          { t: 'set', param: 'mode', value: 'predictive' },
          { t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 3 },
        ],
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        mistake: 'Adds ovens but sends every order to the first one',
      },
      {
        actions: [
          { t: 'set', param: 'mode', value: 'predictive' },
          { t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 3 },
          { t: 'set', param: 'balancer', value: true },
          { t: 'set', param: 'min', value: 3 },
          { t: 'set', param: 'desired', value: 3 },
        ],
        failsGoal: 'g2',
        mistake: 'Keeps three ovens hot all day for a two-hour peak',
      },
    ],
    debrief: DEBRIEF,
    ...over,
  };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SIM_CONSTANTS, SimulateSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { simulateTexts } from '../../scripts/content/lib/labs.ts';
import { allPass, replayLab } from '../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import { checkLab, ENGINES } from '../../src/labs/engines.ts';
import { SIMULATE, type SimulateState } from '../../src/labs/simulate/model.ts';
import {
  type BillConstants,
  fmt,
  type LifecycleConstants,
  NEVER,
  onlineDays,
  runModel,
  type SimValues,
  tiered,
} from '../../src/labs/simulate/models.ts';
import { bakerySimulateLab, OVEN_RUSH_ORDERS } from '../fixtures/lab-specs.ts';

const lab = bakerySimulateLab();
const spec = SimulateSpec.parse(lab.spec);
const run = (actions: readonly unknown[]): SimulateState =>
  replayLab(SIMULATE, spec, actions as Parameters<typeof SIMULATE.reduce>[1][]);

/** 24 hours with a two-hour bump: 100 orders at 01:00 and 50 at 02:00. */
const BUMP = { orders: [0, 100, 50, ...Array<number>(21).fill(0)], perInstance: 50, credits: 1 };
const scaling = (values: SimValues) => runModel('scaling', BUMP, values);
const LIMITS = { min: 0, desired: 0, max: 4 };

describe('the scaling model', () => {
  it('follows the previous hour when dynamic and drops the first hour of a rise', () => {
    const r = scaling({ ...LIMITS, mode: 'dynamic', balancer: true });
    expect(r.metrics).toEqual({ dropped: 100, droppedShare: 66.7, cost: 3, fewest: 0, most: 2 });
    expect(r.series[1]?.values.slice(0, 4)).toEqual([0, 0, 100, 50]);
  });

  it('scales ahead of the forecast when predictive and drops nothing', () => {
    const r = scaling({ ...LIMITS, mode: 'predictive', balancer: true });
    expect(r.metrics).toEqual({ dropped: 0, droppedShare: 0, cost: 3, fewest: 0, most: 2 });
    expect(r.series.map((s) => s.label)).toEqual(['Orders', 'Capacity']);
    expect(r.series[1]?.values.slice(0, 4)).toEqual([0, 100, 50, 0]);
  });

  it('sends every order to one instance without a load balancer', () => {
    const r = scaling({ ...LIMITS, mode: 'predictive', balancer: false });
    expect(r.metrics).toEqual({ dropped: 50, droppedShare: 33.3, cost: 3, fewest: 0, most: 2 });
  });

  it('starts at desired, clamped between min and max', () => {
    expect(scaling({ min: 2, desired: 0, max: 3, mode: 'predictive', balancer: true }).metrics.cost).toBe(48);
    expect(scaling({ min: 1, desired: 5, max: 1, mode: 'predictive', balancer: true }).metrics.most).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('the compute-cost model', () => {
  const C = {
    workloads: [
      { id: 'bursty', label: 'Bursty job', requests: 1_000_000, ms: 100, mb: 256 },
      { id: 'steady', label: 'Steady job', requests: 100_000_000, ms: 200, mb: 1024 },
    ],
    perMillion: 0.2,
    perGbSecond: 0.00002,
    instanceMonth: 10,
  };
  it('prices requests and GB-seconds on Lambda and a flat month on an instance', () => {
    expect(runModel('compute-cost', C, { bursty: 'lambda', steady: 'lambda' }).metrics).toEqual({
      'cost.bursty': 0.7,
      'cost.steady': 420,
      total: 420.7,
    });
    expect(runModel('compute-cost', C, { bursty: 'lambda', steady: 'instance' }).metrics).toEqual({
      'cost.bursty': 0.7,
      'cost.steady': 10,
      total: 10.7,
    });
  });
});

describe('the s3-lifecycle model', () => {
  const C: LifecycleConstants = {
    gb: 30,
    classes: [
      { id: 'hot', label: 'Hot', storage: 3, retrieval: 0, minDays: 0, hours: 0, zones: 3 },
      { id: 'cold', label: 'Cold', storage: 0.3, retrieval: 1, minDays: 90, hours: 12, zones: 3 },
      { id: 'single', label: 'Single', storage: 1.5, retrieval: 0.5, minDays: 30, hours: 0, zones: 1 },
    ],
    reads: [],
    zoneLoss: 50,
  };
  const all = (id: string, expire = 0): SimValues => ({
    first: id,
    second: id,
    third: id,
    secondDay: 0,
    thirdDay: 0,
    expire,
  });

  it('charges the rest of the minimum duration when data leaves a class early', () => {
    expect(runModel('s3-lifecycle', C, all('cold', 60)).metrics).toEqual({
      cost: 27,
      lateReads: 0,
      lost: 0,
      keptDays: 60,
    });
  });

  it('charges retrieval, counts a slow read as late, and finds nothing after expiry', () => {
    const reads = [
      { day: 10, gb: 2, within: 1 },
      { day: 100, gb: 1, within: 48 },
    ];
    expect(runModel('s3-lifecycle', { ...C, reads }, all('cold', 60)).metrics).toEqual({
      cost: 29,
      lateReads: 2,
      lost: 0,
      keptDays: 60,
    });
  });

  it('loses the only copy in a one-zone class when the zone fails before expiry', () => {
    expect(runModel('s3-lifecycle', C, all('single')).metrics.lost).toBe(1);
    expect(runModel('s3-lifecycle', C, all('single', 40)).metrics.lost).toBe(0);
    expect(runModel('s3-lifecycle', C, all('hot')).metrics).toMatchObject({ lost: 0, keptDays: NEVER });
  });

  it('treats a transition to the class in use as no transition', () => {
    const values = { ...all('cold'), secondDay: 30, thirdDay: 60 };
    expect(runModel('s3-lifecycle', C, values).metrics.cost).toBe(109.5);
  });

  it('reports the cost so far at the end of each 30-day month', () => {
    const series = runModel('s3-lifecycle', C, all('hot')).series[0];
    expect(series?.label).toBe('Cost so far');
    expect(series?.values).toHaveLength(12);
    expect(series?.values[0]).toBe(90);
  });
});

describe('the cache model', () => {
  const C = {
    reads: 500,
    writes: 100,
    dbs: [{ id: 'db', label: 'Database', capacity: 200, credits: 10 }],
    caches: [
      { id: 'off', label: 'No cache', hit: 0, credits: 0 },
      { id: 'half', label: 'Half cache', hit: 0.5, credits: 5 },
    ],
    cacheMs: 2,
    dbMs: 10,
  };
  it('slows down with the square of the load once the database is over capacity', () => {
    expect(runModel('cache', C, { db: 'db', cache: 'off' }).metrics).toEqual({
      load: 600,
      spare: -400,
      latency: 90,
      cost: 10,
    });
    expect(runModel('cache', C, { db: 'db', cache: 'half' }).metrics).toEqual({
      load: 350,
      spare: -150,
      latency: 16.3,
      cost: 15,
    });
  });
});

describe('the bill model', () => {
  const C: BillConstants = {
    accounts: [
      { id: 'a', label: 'Cafe', hours: 100, s3Gb: 60, outGb: 0 },
      { id: 'b', label: 'Bakery', hours: 100, s3Gb: 60, outGb: 0 },
    ],
    hourRate: 2,
    commitRate: 1,
    s3Tiers: [
      { upTo: 100, rate: 1 },
      { upTo: null, rate: 0.5 },
    ],
    outTiers: [
      { upTo: 10, rate: 1 },
      { upTo: null, rate: 0.5 },
    ],
    budget: 500,
  };
  it('charges each band at its rate', () => {
    expect(tiered(50, C.s3Tiers)).toBe(50);
    expect(tiered(120, C.s3Tiers)).toBe(110);
    expect(tiered(0, C.outTiers)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('applies the tiers per account, or to the summed usage when consolidated', () => {
    expect(runModel('bill', C, { commit: 0, consolidated: false, alert: 100 }).metrics.bill).toBe(520);
    expect(runModel('bill', C, { commit: 0, consolidated: true, alert: 100 }).metrics.bill).toBe(510);
  });

  it('saves against no commitment and separate bills, and names the alert day', () => {
    expect(runModel('bill', C, { commit: 50, consolidated: true, alert: 50 }).metrics).toEqual({
      bill: 410,
      saved: 110,
      alertDay: 19,
    });
    expect(runModel('bill', C, { commit: 50, consolidated: true, alert: 100 }).metrics.alertDay).toBe(31);
  });
});

describe('the transfer model', () => {
  it('gives the spec 5.2 online days at 80% of the link', () => {
    expect(onlineDays(1, 100)).toBe(1.16);
    expect(onlineDays(2, 500)).toBe(0.46);
    expect(onlineDays(200, 100)).toBe(231.48);
  });

  it('ships offline jobs together in one turnaround and counts devices and late jobs', () => {
    const C = {
      jobs: [
        { id: 'photos', label: 'Photos', tb: 1, mbps: 100, deadline: 1 },
        { id: 'archive', label: 'Archive', tb: 161, mbps: 100, deadline: 20 },
      ],
      turnaround: 9,
      deviceTb: 80,
    };
    expect(runModel('transfer', C, { photos: 'online', archive: 'offline' }).metrics).toEqual({
      'days.photos': 1.16,
      'devices.photos': 1,
      'days.archive': 9,
      'devices.archive': 3,
      late: 1,
    });
  });
});

describe('fmt', () => {
  it.each([
    [2.5, '%', '2.5%'],
    [12, 'lab credits', '12 lab credits'],
    [0.46, 'days', '0.46 days'],
    [1778, 'lab credits', '1778 lab credits'],
    [3, '', '3'],
    [4.8, 'ms', '4.8 ms'],
    [1 / 3, '', '0.33'],
    [-0, '', '0'],
  ])('formats %s with unit %j as %j', (value, unit, text) => {
    expect(fmt(value, unit)).toBe(text);
  });
});

describe('the Simulate engine', () => {
  it('runs the same parameters to the same result every time', () => {
    const values = { mode: 'predictive', min: 1, desired: 1, max: 3, balancer: true };
    expect(runModel('scaling', spec.constants, values)).toEqual(runModel('scaling', spec.constants, values));
    expect(run([...lab.solution, { t: 'run' }])).toEqual(run([...lab.solution, { t: 'run' }]));
  });

  it('says Not run yet before a run and asks for one in the hint', () => {
    const start = SIMULATE.init(spec);
    expect(SIMULATE.check(spec, start)).toEqual([
      { goalId: 'g1', pass: false, message: 'Not run yet' },
      { goalId: 'g2', pass: false, message: 'Not run yet' },
    ]);
    expect(SIMULATE.hint(spec, start, 'g1')).toBe('Run the simulation first.');
  });

  it('reads the thresholds after a run, with labels, units and limits', () => {
    const state = run([...lab.solution, { t: 'run' }]);
    expect(SIMULATE.check(spec, state)).toEqual([
      { goalId: 'g1', pass: true, message: 'Dropped share: 0% (max 1%)' },
      { goalId: 'g2', pass: true, message: 'Daily cost: 29 lab credits (max 40 lab credits)' },
    ]);
    expect(SIMULATE.hint(spec, state, 'g1')).toBe('Every threshold of this goal holds.');
  });

  it('names the first failing threshold in the hint', () => {
    const state = run([{ t: 'run' }]);
    expect(SIMULATE.check(spec, state)[0]).toEqual({
      goalId: 'g1',
      pass: false,
      message: 'Dropped share: 29.4% (max 1%)',
    });
    expect(SIMULATE.hint(spec, state, 'g1')).toBe('Dropped share is 29.4%, over the 1% limit.');
    const min = SimulateSpec.parse({
      ...spec,
      thresholds: [...spec.thresholds, { goal: 'g3', metric: 'fewest', min: 2 }],
    });
    expect(SIMULATE.hint(min, SIMULATE.reduce(SIMULATE.init(min), { t: 'run' }), 'g3')).toBe(
      'Fewest instances is 1, under the 2 minimum.',
    );
  });

  it('clears the result on any accepted change, and ignores values off the range or step', () => {
    const ran = run([{ t: 'run' }]);
    expect(ran.result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(SIMULATE.reduce(ran, { t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 2 }).result).toBeNull();
    for (const action of [
      { t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 7 },
      { t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 1.5 },
      { t: 'set', param: 'mode', value: 'reactive' },
      { t: 'set', param: 'balancer', value: 'yes' },
      { t: 'set', param: 'chimney', value: 1 },
      { t: 'bake' },
      42,
    ]) {
      expect(SIMULATE.reduce(ran, action as never)).toBe(ran);
    }
    expect(SIMULATE.reduce(ran, { t: 'reset' })).toEqual(SIMULATE.init(spec));
  });

  it('runs the model before every check through the engine registry', () => {
    const entry = ENGINES.simulate;
    if (entry === undefined) throw new Error('simulate is not registered');
    expect(entry.settle).toEqual([{ t: 'run' }]);
    const results = checkLab(entry, spec, run(lab.solution));
    expect(results.map((r) => r.message)).not.toContain('Not run yet');
    expect(allPass(results)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('the Simulate spec schema', () => {
  const parse = (over: Record<string, unknown>) => SimulateSpec.safeParse({ ...spec, ...over });
  const messages = (over: Record<string, unknown>) => {
    const r = parse(over);
    return r.success ? [] : r.error.issues.map((i) => i.message);
  };
  const param = (id: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>) =>
    spec.params.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, ...patch } : p));

  it('accepts the bakery lab', () => {
    expect(parse({}).success).toBe(true);
    expect(OVEN_RUSH_ORDERS).toHaveLength(24);
  });

  it('rejects a choice option the model does not know', () => {
    const options = [
      { id: 'dynamic', label: 'Once the orders arrive' },
      { id: 'reactive', label: 'When someone notices' },
    ];
    expect(messages({ params: param('mode', { options }) })).toContain(
      'options of mode must be dynamic, predictive',
    );
  });

  it('rejects an int range outside the model bounds, an initial off its step, and a missing parameter', () => {
    expect(messages({ params: param('max', { max: 12 }) })).toContain('max must range within 1 to 10');
    expect(messages({ params: param('min', { step: 2, initial: 1 }) })).toContain(
      'initial 1 is off the range or step of min',
    );
    expect(messages({ params: spec.params.filter((p) => p.id !== 'balancer') })).toContain(
      "missing parameter 'balancer'",
    );
  });

  it('rejects a threshold on an unknown metric or with both limits', () => {
    expect(messages({ thresholds: [{ goal: 'g1', metric: 'queue', max: 1 }] })).toContain(
      "model scaling has no metric 'queue'",
    );
    expect(messages({ thresholds: [{ goal: 'g1', metric: 'cost', max: 1, min: 0 }] })).toContain(
      'a threshold has exactly one of max and min',
    );
  });

  it('rejects constants that break the model, such as tiers that do not rise', () => {
    const bill = {
      accounts: [
        { id: 'a', label: 'Cafe', hours: 1, s3Gb: 1, outGb: 1 },
        { id: 'b', label: 'Bakery', hours: 1, s3Gb: 1, outGb: 1 },
      ],
      hourRate: 1,
      commitRate: 0.7,
      s3Tiers: [
        { upTo: 500, rate: 0.5 },
        { upTo: 400, rate: 0.4 },
        { upTo: null, rate: 0.3 },
      ],
      outTiers: [{ upTo: 100, rate: 1 }],
      budget: 10,
    };
    const issues = SIM_CONSTANTS.bill
      .safeParse(bill)
      .error?.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join('.')}: ${i.message}`);
    expect(issues).toEqual([
      's3Tiers.1.upTo: upTo must rise from band to band',
      'outTiers.0.upTo: only the last band has upTo: null',
    ]);
    expect(messages({ constants: { ...BUMP, orders: [1, 2, 3] } }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

describe('the Simulate texts R7 checks', () => {
  it('lists parameter labels, option labels, units and the labels inside the constants', () => {
    expect(simulateTexts(lab.spec)).toEqual([
      'When ovens are added',
      'Once the orders arrive',
      'Ahead of the forecast',
      'Fewest ovens',
      'Ovens at opening',
      'Most ovens',
      'Share orders across ovens',
    ]);
    const cost = {
      model: 'compute-cost',
      constants: {
        workloads: [{ id: 'bread-export', label: 'Bread export', requests: 10, ms: 1, mb: 128 }],
        perMillion: 1,
        perGbSecond: 1,
        instanceMonth: 1,
      },
      params: [
        {
          kind: 'choice',
          id: 'bread-export',
          label: 'Where it runs',
          options: [
            { id: 'lambda', label: 'AWS Lambda' },
            { id: 'instance', label: 'An instance' },
          ],
          initial: 'instance',
        },
      ],
      thresholds: [{ goal: 'g1', metric: 'total', max: 5 }],
    };
    expect(simulateTexts(cost)).toEqual(['Where it runs', 'AWS Lambda', 'An instance', 'Bread export']);
    expect(simulateTexts({ model: 'scaling' })).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SimulateSpec } from '../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { loadContext } from '../../scripts/content/lib/context.ts';
import { allPass, replayLab } from '../../src/labs/contract.ts';
import { checkLab, ENGINES } from '../../src/labs/engines.ts';
import { SIMULATE } from '../../src/labs/simulate/model.ts';
import {
  type BillConstants,
  type CacheConstants,
  type ComputeCostConstants,
  type LifecycleConstants,
  runModel,
  type ScalingConstants,
  type SimModel,
  type SimValues,
  type TransferConstants,
} from '../../src/labs/simulate/models.ts';

/**
 * The six Simulate labs, fixed by Phase 4 Task 13: constants, parameters, solution, thresholds and
 * wrong paths. Writers copy them and write only the text; this test holds every shipped lab to them,
 * and its orderings hold the lessons the numbers teach.
 */

type Action = { t: 'set'; param: string; value: number | string | boolean };
const set = (param: string, value: number | string | boolean): Action => ({ t: 'set', param, value });
const choice = (id: string, options: string[], initial: string) => ({
  kind: 'choice' as const,
  id,
  label: id,
  options: options.map((o) => ({ id: o, label: o })),
  initial,
});
const int = (id: string, min: number, max: number, step: number, initial: number) => ({
  kind: 'int' as const,
  id,
  label: id,
  min,
  max,
  step,
  initial,
});
const toggle = (id: string, initial: boolean) => ({ kind: 'toggle' as const, id, label: id, initial });

interface SimLab {
  id: string;
  model: SimModel;
  constants: unknown;
  params: unknown[];
  thresholds: { goal: string; metric: string; max?: number; min?: number }[];
  solution: Action[];
  outcome: Record<string, number>;
  wrongPaths: { actions: Action[]; failsGoal: string; outcome: Record<string, number> }[];
}

const MORNING_ORDERS = [
  0, 0, 0, 0, 5, 20, 60, 180, 220, 150, 90, 80, 120, 110, 70, 60, 70, 90, 60, 40, 20, 10, 5, 0,
];
const SCALING: ScalingConstants = { orders: MORNING_ORDERS, perInstance: 50, credits: 2 };
const COMPUTE: ComputeCostConstants = {
  workloads: [
    { id: 'menu-export', label: 'Weekly menu export', requests: 30_000, ms: 800, mb: 512 },
    { id: 'order-worker', label: 'Order queue worker', requests: 20_000_000, ms: 150, mb: 1024 },
  ],
  perMillion: 1,
  perGbSecond: 0.0001,
  instanceMonth: 50,
};
const LIFECYCLE: LifecycleConstants = {
  gb: 100,
  classes: [
    { id: 'standard', label: 'S3 Standard', storage: 2, retrieval: 0, minDays: 0, hours: 0, zones: 3 },
    {
      id: 'standard-ia',
      label: 'S3 Standard-IA',
      storage: 1.2,
      retrieval: 0.1,
      minDays: 30,
      hours: 0,
      zones: 3,
    },
    {
      id: 'one-zone-ia',
      label: 'S3 One Zone-IA',
      storage: 1,
      retrieval: 0.1,
      minDays: 30,
      hours: 0,
      zones: 1,
    },
    {
      id: 'glacier-flexible',
      label: 'S3 Glacier Flexible Retrieval',
      storage: 0.4,
      retrieval: 0.3,
      minDays: 90,
      hours: 5,
      zones: 3,
    },
    {
      id: 'deep-archive',
      label: 'S3 Glacier Deep Archive',
      storage: 0.1,
      retrieval: 0.5,
      minDays: 180,
      hours: 12,
      zones: 3,
    },
  ],
  reads: [
    { day: 20, gb: 10, within: 1 },
    { day: 200, gb: 5, within: 24 },
  ],
  zoneLoss: 150,
};
const CLASS_IDS = LIFECYCLE.classes.map((c) => c.id);
const CACHE: CacheConstants = {
  reads: 900,
  writes: 50,
  dbs: [
    { id: 'small-db', label: 'Small database', capacity: 400, credits: 100 },
    { id: 'large-db', label: 'Large database', capacity: 1200, credits: 400 },
  ],
  caches: [
    { id: 'off', label: 'No cache', hit: 0, credits: 0 },
    { id: 'menu-cache', label: 'Menu cache', hit: 0.8, credits: 60 },
  ],
  cacheMs: 1,
  dbMs: 20,
};
const BILL: BillConstants = {
  accounts: [
    { id: 'cafe', label: 'Cafe account', hours: 720, s3Gb: 400, outGb: 300 },
    { id: 'bakery', label: 'Bakery account', hours: 720, s3Gb: 300, outGb: 200 },
  ],
  hourRate: 1,
  commitRate: 0.7,
  s3Tiers: [
    { upTo: 500, rate: 0.5 },
    { upTo: null, rate: 0.3 },
  ],
  outTiers: [
    { upTo: 400, rate: 1 },
    { upTo: null, rate: 0.6 },
  ],
  budget: 2000,
};
const TRANSFER: TransferConstants = {
  jobs: [
    { id: 'menu-photos', label: 'Menu photos', tb: 2, mbps: 500, deadline: 3 },
    { id: 'receipt-archive', label: 'Receipt archive', tb: 200, mbps: 100, deadline: 30 },
  ],
  turnaround: 10,
  deviceTb: 80,
};

const RUSH_FIX = [set('mode', 'predictive'), set('max', 5), set('balancer', true)];

const SIM_LABS: SimLab[] = [
  {
    id: 'lab-m02-morning-rush',
    model: 'scaling',
    constants: SCALING,
    params: [
      choice('mode', ['dynamic', 'predictive'], 'dynamic'),
      int('min', 0, 6, 1, 1),
      int('desired', 0, 6, 1, 1),

    ],
    solution: RUSH_FIX,
    outcome: { dropped: 0, cost: 90, fewest: 1 },
    wrongPaths: [
      {
        actions: [set('mode', 'predictive'), set('max', 5)],
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        outcome: { droppedShare: 48.6 },
      },
      { actions: [set('max', 5), set('balancer', true)], failsGoal: 'g1', outcome: { droppedShare: 8.9 } },
      {
        actions: [set('mode', 'predictive'), set('balancer', true)],
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        outcome: { droppedShare: 19.2 },
      },
      {
        actions: [
          set('mode', 'predictive'),
          set('balancer', true),
          set('min', 5),
          set('desired', 5),

        ],
        failsGoal: 'g2',

      { actions: [...RUSH_FIX, set('min', 0)], failsGoal: 'g3', outcome: { fewest: 0 } },
    ],

  {
    id: 'lab-m03-bursty-or-steady',
    model: 'compute-cost',
    constants: COMPUTE,
    params: [
      choice('menu-export', ['lambda', 'instance'], 'instance'),
      choice('order-worker', ['lambda', 'instance'], 'instance'),
    ],
    thresholds: [
      { goal: 'g1', metric: 'cost.menu-export', max: 10 },
      { goal: 'g2', metric: 'cost.order-worker', max: 100 },
    ],
    solution: [set('menu-export', 'lambda')],
    outcome: { 'cost.menu-export': 1.23, 'cost.order-worker': 50 },
    wrongPaths: [
      {
        actions: [set('menu-export', 'lambda'), set('order-worker', 'lambda')],
        failsGoal: 'g2',

      { actions: [set('menu-export', 'instance')], failsGoal: 'g1', outcome: { 'cost.menu-export': 50 } },
    ],

  {
    id: 'lab-m06-receipts-for-7-years',

    constants: LIFECYCLE,
    params: [
      choice('first', CLASS_IDS, 'standard'),
      choice('second', CLASS_IDS, 'standard'),
      choice('third', CLASS_IDS, 'standard'),
      int('secondDay', 0, 365, 1, 30),
      int('thirdDay', 0, 365, 1, 90),
      int('expire', 0, 3650, 1, 0),
    ],
    thresholds: [
      { goal: 'g1', metric: 'keptDays', min: 2555 },
      { goal: 'g1', metric: 'lost', max: 0 },
      { goal: 'g2', metric: 'lateReads', max: 0 },
      { goal: 'g3', metric: 'cost', max: 600 },

    solution: [set('second', 'standard-ia'), set('third', 'deep-archive')],
    outcome: { cost: 534.17, lateReads: 0, lost: 0 },

      },
      {

        failsGoal: 'g2',

      {
        actions: [set('second', 'standard-ia'), set('third', 'glacier-flexible')],

  },
  {
    id: 'lab-m07-cache-the-menu',

    ],

        failsGoal: 'g3',

    ],

  {
    id: 'lab-m11-the-monthly-bill',
    model: 'bill',
    constants: BILL,
    params: [int('commit', 0, 100, 10, 0), toggle('consolidated', false), int('alert', 50, 150, 10, 100)],
    thresholds: [
      { goal: 'g1', metric: 'bill', max: 2000 },
      { goal: 'g2', metric: 'saved', min: 500 },
      { goal: 'g3', metric: 'alertDay', max: 23 },
    ],
    solution: [set('commit', 100), set('consolidated', true), set('alert', 60)],
    outcome: { bill: 1778, saved: 512, alertDay: 21 },
    wrongPaths: [
      { actions: [set('commit', 100), set('alert', 60)], failsGoal: 'g2', outcome: { saved: 432 } },
      { actions: [set('consolidated', true), set('alert', 60)], failsGoal: 'g1', outcome: { bill: 2210 } },
      {

        outcome: { 'days.receipt-archive': 231.48 },

];

const ENTRY = ENGINES.simulate;
if (ENTRY === undefined) throw new Error('simulate is not registered');
const specOf = (lab: SimLab) =>
  SimulateSpec.parse({
    model: lab.model,
    constants: lab.constants,
    params: lab.params,
    thresholds: lab.thresholds,
  });
const runTo = (lab: SimLab, actions: readonly Action[]) => {
  const spec = specOf(lab);
  const state = SIMULATE.reduce(replayLab(SIMULATE, spec, actions), { t: 'run' });
  return { results: checkLab(ENTRY, spec, state), metrics: state.result?.metrics ?? {} };
};

/** Drops writer text (labels, units, details) so only the fixed numbers and ids compare. */
function numbersOf(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(numbersOf);
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return value;
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(value)
      .filter(([k]) => k !== 'label' && k !== 'unit' && k !== 'detail')
      .map(([k, v]) => [k, numbersOf(v)]),
  );
}

describe.each(SIM_LABS)('$id, as fixed in Task 13', (lab) => {
  it('fails a goal at the start and meets every goal with its solution, with the fixed outcome', () => {
    const spec = specOf(lab);
    expect(allPass(checkLab(ENTRY, spec, SIMULATE.init(spec)))).toBe(false);
    const { results, metrics } = runTo(lab, lab.solution);
    expect(results.filter((r) => !r.pass)).toEqual([]);
    expect(metrics).toMatchObject(lab.outcome);
  });

  it.each(lab.wrongPaths.map((w, j) => [j, w] as const))(
    'wrong path %i fails its goal for its reason',
    (_, w) => {
      const { results, metrics } = runTo(lab, w.actions);
      expect(results.find((r) => r.goalId === w.failsGoal)?.pass).toBe(false);
      expect(metrics).toMatchObject(w.outcome);
    },
  );
});

const ROOT = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
const shipped = new Map(loadContext(ROOT).labs.map((e) => [e.lab.id, e.lab] as const));

describe.each(SIM_LABS)('the shipped $id', (lab) => {
  const file = shipped.get(lab.id);
  if (file === undefined) {
    it('is not shipped yet (wave CS writes it)', () => {
      expect(shipped.has(lab.id)).toBe(false);
    });
    return;
  }
  it('copies the fixed model, constants, parameters, thresholds, solution and wrong paths', () => {
    expect(file.engine).toBe('simulate');
    expect(numbersOf(file.spec)).toEqual(
      numbersOf({
        model: lab.model,
        constants: lab.constants,
        params: lab.params,
        thresholds: lab.thresholds,
      }),
    );
    expect(file.solution).toEqual(lab.solution);
    expect(file.wrongPaths.map((w) => ({ actions: w.actions, failsGoal: w.failsGoal }))).toEqual(
      lab.wrongPaths.map((w) => ({ actions: w.actions, failsGoal: w.failsGoal })),
    );
  });
});

describe('the orderings the lessons rest on', () => {
  const rush = (values: SimValues) => runModel('scaling', SCALING, values).metrics;
  const grid = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].flatMap((min) =>
    [1, 2, 4, 5, 8].map((max) => ({ min, desired: min, max })),
  );

  it('Morning rush: without a load balancer more than 1% of orders drop, whatever the limits', () => {
    for (const limits of grid) {
      for (const mode of ['dynamic', 'predictive']) {
        expect(rush({ ...limits, mode, balancer: false }).droppedShare).toBeGreaterThan(1);
      }
    }
  });

  it('Morning rush: dynamic scaling never drops fewer orders than predictive, and drops more at the solution limits', () => {
    for (const limits of grid) {
      const dynamic = rush({ ...limits, mode: 'dynamic', balancer: true }).dropped ?? 0;
      expect(dynamic).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(
        rush({ ...limits, mode: 'predictive', balancer: true }).dropped ?? 0,
      );
    }
    const limits = { min: 1, desired: 1, max: 5, balancer: true };
    expect(rush({ ...limits, mode: 'dynamic' }).dropped).toBeGreaterThan(
      rush({ ...limits, mode: 'predictive' }).dropped ?? 0,
    );
  });

  it('Bursty or steady: Lambda is cheaper for the bursty export, the instance for the busy worker', () => {
    const cost = (v: SimValues) => runModel('compute-cost', COMPUTE, v).metrics;
    const lambda = cost({ 'menu-export': 'lambda', 'order-worker': 'lambda' });
    const instance = cost({ 'menu-export': 'instance', 'order-worker': 'instance' });
    expect(lambda['cost.menu-export']).toBeLessThan(instance['cost.menu-export'] ?? 0);
    expect(instance['cost.order-worker']).toBeLessThan(lambda['cost.order-worker'] ?? 0);
  });

  it('Receipts for 7 years: Deep Archive stores cheapest and retrieves slowest and dearest; only One Zone-IA has one zone', () => {
    const deep = LIFECYCLE.classes.find((c) => c.id === 'deep-archive');
    for (const c of LIFECYCLE.classes.filter((k) => k.id !== 'deep-archive')) {
      expect(deep?.storage).toBeLessThan(c.storage);
      expect(deep?.retrieval).toBeGreaterThan(c.retrieval);
      expect(deep?.hours).toBeGreaterThan(c.hours);
    }
    expect(LIFECYCLE.classes.filter((c) => c.zones === 1).map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['one-zone-ia']);
  });

  it('Cache the menu: the cache lowers latency on either database', () => {
    for (const db of ['small-db', 'large-db']) {
      const off = runModel('cache', CACHE, { db, cache: 'off' }).metrics.latency ?? 0;
      expect(runModel('cache', CACHE, { db, cache: 'menu-cache' }).metrics.latency).toBeLessThan(off);
    }
  });

  it('The monthly bill: consolidated is cheaper at any commitment, and more commitment costs less', () => {
    let last = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    for (let commit = 0; commit <= 100; commit += 10) {
      const together = runModel('bill', BILL, { commit, consolidated: true, alert: 100 }).metrics.bill ?? 0;
      const apart = runModel('bill', BILL, { commit, consolidated: false, alert: 100 }).metrics.bill ?? 0;
      expect(together).toBeLessThan(apart);
      expect(together).toBeLessThan(last);
      last = together;
    }
  });

  it('Online or offline transfer: the archive online takes longer than its deadline, which exceeds the turnaround', () => {
    const online = runModel('transfer', TRANSFER, { 'menu-photos': 'online', 'receipt-archive': 'online' });
    const archive = TRANSFER.jobs.find((j) => j.id === 'receipt-archive');
    expect(online.metrics['days.receipt-archive']).toBeGreaterThan(archive?.deadline ?? 0);
    expect(archive?.deadline).toBeGreaterThan(TRANSFER.turnaround);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('SimulateAction', () => {
  it('parses set, run and reset', () => {
    expect(SimulateAction.safeParse({ t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 3 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(SimulateAction.safeParse({ t: 'set', param: 'mode', value: 'predictive' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(SimulateAction.safeParse({ t: 'run' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(SimulateAction.safeParse({ t: 'run', now: true }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Append to `tests/unit/r11.test.ts`, adding `bakerySimulateLab` to its import from `'../fixtures/lab-specs.ts'`:

```ts
describe('R11 on Simulate labs', () => {
  it('replays the bakery Simulate lab without a problem', () => {
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf(bakerySimulateLab()))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('runs the model before judging the start, so a start that already passes is reported', () => {
    const lab = bakerySimulateLab();
    const spec = lab.spec as { thresholds: unknown[] };
    const easy = {
      ...spec,
      thresholds: [
        { goal: 'g1', metric: 'droppedShare', max: 50 },
        { goal: 'g2', metric: 'cost', max: 40 },
      ],
    };
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf({ ...lab, spec: easy }))).toContain(
      'lab-m01-oven-rush: the starting state already meets every goal',
    );
  });

  it('judges a wrong path without run on its outcome, never on Not run yet', () => {
    const lab = bakerySimulateLab();
    const wrongPaths = [
      {
        actions: lab.solution,
        failsGoal: 'g1',
        mistake: 'Adds ovens but sends every order to the first one',
      },
      ...lab.wrongPaths.slice(1),
    ];
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf({ ...lab, wrongPaths }))).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-oven-rush: wrongPaths[0] (Adds ovens but sends every order to the first one) meets g1, which it should fail',
    ]);
  });

  it('reports a solution that leaves a threshold unmet, with the reading', () => {
    const lab = bakerySimulateLab({ solution: [{ t: 'set', param: 'max', value: 3 }] });
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf(lab))).toEqual([
      'lab-m01-oven-rush: the solution leaves g1 unmet: Dropped share: 29.4% (max 1%)',
    ]);
  });

  it('reports a spec whose constants break the model', () => {
    const lab = bakerySimulateLab();
    const spec = { ...(lab.spec as object), constants: { orders: [1], perInstance: 40, credits: 1 } };
    expect(labProblems(labEntryOf({ ...lab, spec }))[0]).toMatch(
      /^lab-m01-oven-rush: spec: constants\.orders: /,
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
const SIMULATE_SPEC = SimulateSpec.parse(bakerySimulateLab().spec);
const simulateActionArb = fc.oneof(
  fc.record({
    t: fc.constant('set'),
    param: fc.constantFrom('mode', 'min', 'desired', 'max', 'balancer', 'oven'),
    value: fc.oneof(
      fc.constantFrom('dynamic', 'predictive', 'reactive'),
      fc.boolean(),
      fc.integer({ min: -2, max: 12 }),
      fc.double(),
    ),
  }),
  fc.constant({ t: 'run' }),
  fc.constant({ t: 'reset' }),
  fc.anything(),
);

describe('the Simulate reducer', () => {
  it('never throws, is deterministic, checks purely and keeps every value in range', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(fc.array(simulateActionArb, { maxLength: 30 }), (actions) => {
        const a = replayLab(SIMULATE, SIMULATE_SPEC, actions as never[]);
        const b = replayLab(SIMULATE, SIMULATE_SPEC, actions as never[]);
        expect(toPlain(a)).toEqual(toPlain(b));
        expect(SIMULATE.check(SIMULATE_SPEC, a)).toEqual(SIMULATE.check(SIMULATE_SPEC, b));
        const ran = SIMULATE.reduce(a, { t: 'run' });
        for (const p of SIMULATE_SPEC.params) {
          const v = ran.values[p.id];
          if (p.kind === 'int') expect(typeof v === 'number' && v >= p.min && v <= p.max).toBe(true);
        }
        for (const value of Object.values(ran.result?.metrics ?? {}))
          expect(Number.isFinite(value)).toBe(true);
      }),
      RUNS,
    );
  });
});
```
