- [ ] **Step 3: Add the six models**

Create `src/labs/simulate/models.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The six Simulate models (spec 5.1): pure, deterministic, no randomness.
 * Every money figure is in lab credits, a made-up unit; `schemas/lab.ts` validates the constants
 * with `SIM_CONSTANTS` before any of these functions sees them, so they only cast.
 */

export type SimModel = 'scaling' | 'compute-cost' | 's3-lifecycle' | 'cache' | 'bill' | 'transfer';
export type SimValue = number | string | boolean;
export type SimValues = Readonly<Record<string, SimValue>>;

export interface SimSeries {
  label: string;
  unit: string;
  values: number[];
}
export interface SimResult {
  metrics: Record<string, number>;
  series: SimSeries[];
}
export interface MetricInfo {
  label: string;
  unit: string;
}
export interface ParamShape {
  id: string;
  kind: 'int' | 'choice' | 'toggle';
  options?: string[];
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
}

export interface ScalingConstants {
  orders: number[];
  perInstance: number;
  credits: number;
}
export interface Workload {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  requests: number;
  ms: number;
  mb: number;
}
export interface ComputeCostConstants {
  workloads: Workload[];
  perMillion: number;
  perGbSecond: number;
  instanceMonth: number;
}
export interface StorageClass {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  storage: number;
  retrieval: number;
  minDays: number;
  hours: number;
  zones: 1 | 3;
}
export interface LifecycleRead {
  day: number;
  gb: number;
  within: number;
}
export interface LifecycleConstants {
  gb: number;
  classes: StorageClass[];
  reads: LifecycleRead[];
  zoneLoss: number | null;
}
export interface CacheConstants {
  reads: number;
  writes: number;
  dbs: { id: string; label: string; capacity: number; credits: number }[];
  caches: { id: string; label: string; hit: number; credits: number }[];
  cacheMs: number;
  dbMs: number;
}
export interface Tier {
  upTo: number | null;
  rate: number;
}
export interface BillAccount {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  hours: number;
  s3Gb: number;
  outGb: number;
}
export interface BillConstants {
  accounts: BillAccount[];
  hourRate: number;
  commitRate: number;
  s3Tiers: Tier[];
  outTiers: Tier[];
  budget: number;
}
export interface TransferJob {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  tb: number;
  mbps: number;
  deadline: number;
}
export interface TransferConstants {
  jobs: TransferJob[];
  turnaround: number;
  deviceTb: number;
}

const CREDITS = 'lab credits';
/** Days 0 to 364 make the modelled year of `s3-lifecycle`. */
const YEAR = 365;
/** `keptDays` when a lifecycle never expires. */
export const NEVER = 99_999;

export const round1 = (x: number): number => Math.round(x * 10) / 10;
export const round2 = (x: number): number => Math.round(x * 100) / 100;
const clamp = (x: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, x));
const sum = (xs: readonly number[]): number => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

const num = (values: SimValues, id: string): number => {
  const v = values[id];
  return typeof v === 'number' ? v : 0;
};
const str = (values: SimValues, id: string): string => {
  const v = values[id];
  return typeof v === 'string' ? v : '';
};
const bool = (values: SimValues, id: string): boolean => values[id] === true;

/** The parameters a model reads, with their kinds, allowed options and bounds. */
export function modelParams(model: SimModel, constants: unknown): ParamShape[] {
  switch (model) {
    case 'scaling':
      return [
        { id: 'mode', kind: 'choice', options: ['dynamic', 'predictive'] },
        { id: 'min', kind: 'int', min: 0, max: 10 },
        { id: 'desired', kind: 'int', min: 0, max: 10 },
        { id: 'max', kind: 'int', min: 1, max: 10 },
        { id: 'balancer', kind: 'toggle' },
      ];
    case 'compute-cost':
      return (constants as ComputeCostConstants).workloads.map((w) => ({
        id: w.id,
        kind: 'choice',
        options: ['lambda', 'instance'],
      }));
    case 's3-lifecycle': {
      const ids = (constants as LifecycleConstants).classes.map((c) => c.id);
      return [
        { id: 'first', kind: 'choice', options: ids },
        { id: 'second', kind: 'choice', options: ids },
        { id: 'third', kind: 'choice', options: ids },
        { id: 'secondDay', kind: 'int', min: 0, max: 365 },
        { id: 'thirdDay', kind: 'int', min: 0, max: 365 },
        { id: 'expire', kind: 'int', min: 0, max: 3650 },
      ];
    }
    case 'cache': {
      const c = constants as CacheConstants;
      return [
        { id: 'db', kind: 'choice', options: c.dbs.map((d) => d.id) },
        { id: 'cache', kind: 'choice', options: c.caches.map((d) => d.id) },
      ];
    }
    case 'bill':
      return [
        { id: 'commit', kind: 'int', min: 0, max: 100 },
        { id: 'consolidated', kind: 'toggle' },
        { id: 'alert', kind: 'int', min: 50, max: 150 },
      ];
    case 'transfer':
      return (constants as TransferConstants).jobs.map((j) => ({
        id: j.id,
        kind: 'choice',
        options: ['online', 'offline'],
      }));
  }
}

/** The label and unit of one metric, or null when the model has no such metric. */
export function metricInfo(model: SimModel, metric: string, constants: unknown): MetricInfo | null {
  const fixed: Record<SimModel, Record<string, MetricInfo>> = {
    scaling: {
      dropped: { label: 'Dropped orders', unit: '' },
      droppedShare: { label: 'Dropped share', unit: '%' },
      cost: { label: 'Daily cost', unit: CREDITS },
      fewest: { label: 'Fewest instances', unit: '' },
      most: { label: 'Most instances', unit: '' },
    },
    'compute-cost': { total: { label: 'Total cost', unit: CREDITS } },
    's3-lifecycle': {
      cost: { label: 'Yearly cost', unit: CREDITS },
      lateReads: { label: 'Late reads', unit: '' },
      lost: { label: 'Data lost', unit: '' },
      keptDays: { label: 'Days kept', unit: 'days' },
    },
    cache: {
      load: { label: 'Database load', unit: 'reads/s' },
      spare: { label: 'Spare capacity', unit: 'reads/s' },
      latency: { label: 'Average latency', unit: 'ms' },
      cost: { label: 'Monthly cost', unit: CREDITS },
    },
    bill: {
      bill: { label: 'Monthly bill', unit: CREDITS },
      saved: { label: 'Saved', unit: CREDITS },
      alertDay: { label: 'Alert day', unit: 'day of 30' },
    },
    transfer: { late: { label: 'Late jobs', unit: '' } },
  };
  const own = Object.hasOwn(fixed[model], metric) ? fixed[model][metric] : undefined;
  if (own !== undefined) return own;
  const dot = metric.indexOf('.');
  if (dot < 0) return null;
  const head = metric.slice(0, dot);
  const id = metric.slice(dot + 1);
  if (model === 'compute-cost' && head === 'cost') {
    const w = (constants as ComputeCostConstants).workloads.find((x) => x.id === id);
    return w === undefined ? null : { label: `Cost: ${w.label}`, unit: CREDITS };
  }
  if (model === 'transfer' && (head === 'days' || head === 'devices')) {
    const j = (constants as TransferConstants).jobs.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (j === undefined) return null;
    return head === 'days'
      ? { label: `Days: ${j.label}`, unit: 'days' }
      : { label: `Devices: ${j.label}`, unit: '' };
  }
  return null;
}

/** At most 2 decimals, trailing zeros trimmed, no grouping; `%` attaches, any other unit follows a space. */
export function fmt(value: number, unit: string): string {
  const n = String(round2(value) + 0);
  if (unit === '') return n;
  return unit === '%' ? `${n}%` : `${n} ${unit}`;
}

function scaling(c: ScalingConstants, v: SimValues): SimResult {
  const lo = num(v, 'min');
  const hi = Math.max(lo, num(v, 'max'));
  const predictive = str(v, 'mode') === 'predictive';
  const balancer = bool(v, 'balancer');
  const inst = c.orders.map((orders, t) => {
    if (t === 0) return clamp(num(v, 'desired'), lo, hi);
    const src = predictive ? orders : (c.orders[t - 1] ?? 0);
    return clamp(Math.ceil(src / c.perInstance), lo, hi);
  });
  const cap = inst.map((n) => (balancer ? n : Math.min(n, 1)) * c.perInstance);
  const dropped = sum(c.orders.map((o, t) => Math.max(0, o - (cap[t] ?? 0))));
  const total = sum(c.orders);
  return {
    metrics: {
      dropped,
      droppedShare: total === 0 ? 0 : round1((100 * dropped) / total),
      cost: round2(sum(inst) * c.credits),
      fewest: Math.min(...inst),
      most: Math.max(...inst),
    },
    series: [
      { label: 'Orders', unit: 'orders', values: [...c.orders] },
      { label: 'Capacity', unit: 'orders', values: cap },
    ],
  };
}

function computeCost(c: ComputeCostConstants, v: SimValues): SimResult {
  const metrics: Record<string, number> = {};
  let total = 0;
  for (const w of c.workloads) {
    const lambda =
      (w.requests / 1e6) * c.perMillion + w.requests * (w.ms / 1000) * (w.mb / 1024) * c.perGbSecond;
    const cost = str(v, w.id) === 'lambda' ? lambda : c.instanceMonth;
    metrics[`cost.${w.id}`] = round2(cost);
    total += cost;
  }
  metrics.total = round2(total);
  return { metrics, series: [] };
}

function lifecycle(c: LifecycleConstants, v: SimValues): SimResult {
  const byId = new Map(c.classes.map((k) => [k.id, k]));
  const expire = num(v, 'expire');
  const end = expire === 0 ? YEAR : Math.min(expire, YEAR);
  const secondDay = num(v, 'secondDay');
  const thirdDay = num(v, 'thirdDay');
  const classOn = (d: number): StorageClass | undefined =>
    byId.get(d < secondDay ? str(v, 'first') : d < thirdDay ? str(v, 'second') : str(v, 'third'));
  const perDay = (k: StorageClass): number => (c.gb * k.storage) / 30;
  let cost = 0;
  let current: StorageClass | undefined;
  let held = 0;
  const leave = (): void => {
    if (current !== undefined && held < current.minDays) cost += perDay(current) * (current.minDays - held);
  };
  const soFar: number[] = [];
  for (let d = 0; d < YEAR; d += 1) {
    if (d === end) {
      leave();
      current = undefined;
    }
    if (d < end) {
      const k = classOn(d);
      if (k !== current) {
        leave();
        current = k;
        held = 0;
      }
      if (current !== undefined) cost += perDay(current);
      held += 1;
    }
    for (const r of c.reads) {
      if (r.day !== d || d >= end || current === undefined) continue;
      cost += r.gb * current.retrieval;
    }
    if (d % 30 === 29) soFar.push(round2(cost));
  }
  let lateReads = 0;
  for (const r of c.reads) {
    const k = r.day < end ? classOn(r.day) : undefined;
    if (k === undefined || k.hours > r.within) lateReads += 1;
  }
  const lossDay = c.zoneLoss;
  const lost = lossDay !== null && lossDay < end && classOn(lossDay)?.zones === 1 ? 1 : 0;
  return {
    metrics: { cost: round2(cost), lateReads, lost, keptDays: expire === 0 ? NEVER : expire },
    series: [{ label: 'Cost so far', unit: CREDITS, values: soFar }],
  };
}

function cache(c: CacheConstants, v: SimValues): SimResult {
  const db = c.dbs.find((d) => d.id === str(v, 'db')) ?? c.dbs[0];
  const layer = c.caches.find((d) => d.id === str(v, 'cache')) ?? c.caches[0];
  const hit = layer?.hit ?? 0;
  const capacity = db?.capacity ?? 1;
  const load = Math.round(c.reads * (1 - hit) + c.writes);
  const util = load / capacity;
  return {
    metrics: {
      load,
      spare: capacity - load,
      latency: round1(hit * c.cacheMs + (1 - hit) * c.dbMs * Math.max(1, util) ** 2),
      cost: (db?.credits ?? 0) + (layer?.credits ?? 0),
    },
    series: [],
  };
}

/** Charges each band of `q` at its rate; the last band has `upTo: null`. */
export function tiered(q: number, tiers: readonly Tier[]): number {
  let total = 0;
  let floor = 0;
  for (const t of tiers) {
    const top = t.upTo ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    if (q > floor) total += (Math.min(q, top) - floor) * t.rate;
    floor = top;
  }
  return total;
}

function billOf(c: BillConstants, commit: number, consolidated: boolean): number {
  const share = commit / 100;
  const hours = sum(c.accounts.map((a) => a.hours * (share * c.commitRate + (1 - share) * c.hourRate)));
  const s3 = consolidated
    ? tiered(sum(c.accounts.map((a) => a.s3Gb)), c.s3Tiers)
    : sum(c.accounts.map((a) => tiered(a.s3Gb, c.s3Tiers)));
  const out = consolidated
    ? tiered(sum(c.accounts.map((a) => a.outGb)), c.outTiers)
    : sum(c.accounts.map((a) => tiered(a.outGb, c.outTiers)));
  return round2(hours + s3 + out);
}

function bill(c: BillConstants, v: SimValues): SimResult {
  const total = billOf(c, num(v, 'commit'), bool(v, 'consolidated'));
  const baseline = billOf(c, 0, false);
  const day =
    total <= 0 ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : Math.ceil((30 * (num(v, 'alert') / 100) * c.budget) / total);
  return {
    metrics: { bill: total, saved: round2(baseline - total), alertDay: day > 30 ? 31 : day },
    series: [],
  };
}

/** Days to send `tb` terabytes over a `mbps` link at 80% efficiency (spec 5.2). */
export function onlineDays(tb: number, mbps: number): number {
  return round2((tb * 8e6) / (mbps * 0.8) / 86_400);
}

function transfer(c: TransferConstants, v: SimValues): SimResult {
  const metrics: Record<string, number> = {};
  let late = 0;
  for (const j of c.jobs) {
    const days = str(v, j.id) === 'offline' ? c.turnaround : onlineDays(j.tb, j.mbps);
    metrics[`days.${j.id}`] = days;
    metrics[`devices.${j.id}`] = Math.ceil(j.tb / c.deviceTb);
    if (days > j.deadline) late += 1;
  }
  metrics.late = late;
  return { metrics, series: [] };
}

/** Runs one model over validated constants and the parameter values on screen. */
export function runModel(model: SimModel, constants: unknown, values: SimValues): SimResult {
  switch (model) {
    case 'scaling':
      return scaling(constants as ScalingConstants, values);
    case 'compute-cost':
      return computeCost(constants as ComputeCostConstants, values);
    case 's3-lifecycle':
      return lifecycle(constants as LifecycleConstants, values);
    case 'cache':
      return cache(constants as CacheConstants, values);
    case 'bill':
      return bill(constants as BillConstants, values);
    case 'transfer':
      return transfer(constants as TransferConstants, values);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the Simulate spec, constants and action schemas**

In `schemas/lab.ts`, add `import { metricInfo, modelParams, type SimModel } from '../src/labs/simulate/models.ts';` to the imports, insert this block right above `export const LAB_SCHEMAS` (after Task 12's Configure block):

```ts
export const SIM_MODELS = [
  'scaling',
  'compute-cost',
  's3-lifecycle',
  'cache',
  'bill',
  'transfer',
] as const satisfies readonly SimModel[];

const Label = z.string().min(2).max(40);
/** A model parameter id: `max`, `secondDay` or a workload or job id such as `menu-export`. */
export const ParamId = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[a-z][a-zA-Z0-9]*(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/, 'must look like secondDay or menu-export');
/** Ids must be unique within a list; reports every repeat at its index. */
function uniqueIds(list: readonly { id: string }[], ctx: z.RefinementCtx, at: (string | number)[]): void {
  list.forEach((x, i) => {
    if (list.findIndex((y) => y.id === x.id) !== i)
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [...at, i, 'id'], message: `duplicate id '${x.id}'` });
  });
}
const Tiers = z
  .array(z.strictObject({ upTo: z.number().positive().nullable(), rate: z.number().min(0) }))
  .min(1)
  .max(4)
  .superRefine((tiers, ctx) => {
    tiers.forEach((t, i) => {
      const last = i === tiers.length - 1;
      if (last !== (t.upTo === null))
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [i, 'upTo'], message: 'only the last band has upTo: null' });
      const prev = i > 0 ? tiers[i - 1]?.upTo : undefined;
      if (t.upTo !== null && typeof prev === 'number' && t.upTo <= prev)
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [i, 'upTo'], message: 'upTo must rise from band to band' });
    });
  });
const Named = { id: Slug, label: Label };

/** The constants each model reads; every figure is a lab value, every price is in lab credits. */
export const SIM_CONSTANTS: { readonly [M in SimModel]: z.ZodType } = {
  scaling: z.strictObject({
    orders: z.array(z.int().min(0)).length(24),
    perInstance: z.int().min(1),
    credits: z.number().positive(),
  }),
  'compute-cost': z
    .strictObject({
      workloads: z
        .array(
          z.strictObject({
            ...Named,
            requests: z.int().min(0),
            ms: z.number().positive(),
            mb: z.int().min(1),
          }),
        )
        .min(1)
        .max(3),
      perMillion: z.number().min(0),
      perGbSecond: z.number().min(0),
      instanceMonth: z.number().min(0),
    })
    .superRefine((c, ctx) => uniqueIds(c.workloads, ctx, ['workloads'])),
  's3-lifecycle': z
    .strictObject({
      gb: z.number().positive(),
      classes: z
        .array(
          z.strictObject({
            ...Named,
            storage: z.number().min(0),
            retrieval: z.number().min(0),
            minDays: z.int().min(0),
            hours: z.number().min(0),
            zones: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(3)]),
          }),
        )
        .min(2)
        .max(8),
      reads: z
        .array(
          z.strictObject({
            day: z.int().min(0).max(364),
            gb: z.number().positive(),
            within: z.number().min(0),
          }),
        )
        .max(6),
      zoneLoss: z.int().min(0).max(364).nullable(),
    })
    .superRefine((c, ctx) => uniqueIds(c.classes, ctx, ['classes'])),
  cache: z
    .strictObject({
      reads: z.int().min(0),
      writes: z.int().min(0),
      dbs: z
        .array(z.strictObject({ ...Named, capacity: z.int().min(1), credits: z.number().min(0) }))
        .min(1)
        .max(4),
      caches: z
        .array(z.strictObject({ ...Named, hit: z.number().min(0).max(0.95), credits: z.number().min(0) }))
        .min(2)
        .max(4),
      cacheMs: z.number().positive(),
      dbMs: z.number().positive(),
    })
    .superRefine((c, ctx) => {
      uniqueIds(c.dbs, ctx, ['dbs']);
      uniqueIds(c.caches, ctx, ['caches']);
      const off = c.caches.find((x) => x.id === 'off');
      if (off === undefined || off.hit !== 0 || off.credits !== 0)
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['caches'],
          message: "caches need one 'off' entry with hit 0 and credits 0",
        });
    }),
  bill: z
    .strictObject({
      accounts: z
        .array(
          z.strictObject({
            ...Named,
            hours: z.number().min(0),
            s3Gb: z.number().min(0),
            outGb: z.number().min(0),
          }),
        )
        .min(2)
        .max(3),
      hourRate: z.number().positive(),
      commitRate: z.number().positive(),
      s3Tiers: Tiers,
      outTiers: Tiers,
      budget: z.number().positive(),
    })
    .superRefine((c, ctx) => uniqueIds(c.accounts, ctx, ['accounts'])),
  transfer: z
    .strictObject({
      jobs: z
        .array(
          z.strictObject({
            ...Named,
            tb: z.number().positive(),
            mbps: z.number().positive(),
            deadline: z.number().positive(),
          }),
        )
        .min(1)
        .max(3),
      turnaround: z.number().positive(),
      deviceTb: z.number().positive(),
    })
    .superRefine((c, ctx) => uniqueIds(c.jobs, ctx, ['jobs'])),
};

const IntParam = z.strictObject({
  kind: z.literal('int'),
  id: ParamId,
  label: Label,
  min: z.int(),
  max: z.int(),
  step: z.int().min(1),
  initial: z.int(),
  unit: z.string().max(20).optional(),
});
const ChoiceParam = z.strictObject({
  kind: z.literal('choice'),
  id: ParamId,
  label: z.string().min(2).max(60),
  options: z
    .array(z.strictObject({ id: Slug, label: Label }))
    .min(2)
    .max(8),
  initial: Slug,
});
const ToggleParam = z.strictObject({
  kind: z.literal('toggle'),
  id: ParamId,
  label: z.string().min(2).max(60),
  initial: z.boolean(),
});
const SimThreshold = z.strictObject({
  goal: GoalId,
  metric: z.string().min(1),
  max: z.number().optional(),
  min: z.number().optional(),
});

export const SimulateSpec = z
  .strictObject({
    model: z.enum(SIM_MODELS),
    constants: z.unknown(),
    params: z
      .array(z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [IntParam, ChoiceParam, ToggleParam]))
      .min(1)
      .max(12),
    thresholds: z.array(SimThreshold).min(1),
  })
  .superRefine((spec, ctx) => {
    const constants = SIM_CONSTANTS[spec.model].safeParse(spec.constants);
    if (!constants.success) {
      for (const issue of constants.error.issues)
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['constants', ...issue.path.map(String)],
          message: issue.message,
        });
      return;
    }
    const shapes = modelParams(spec.model, constants.data);
    const declared = new Set(spec.params.map((p) => p.id));
    for (const s of shapes)
      if (!declared.has(s.id))
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['params'], message: `missing parameter '${s.id}'` });
    spec.params.forEach((p, i) => {
      const at = ['params', i];
      if (spec.params.findIndex((q) => q.id === p.id) !== i)
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [...at, 'id'], message: `duplicate parameter '${p.id}'` });
      const shape = shapes.find((s) => s.id === p.id);
      if (shape === undefined) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: [...at, 'id'],
          message: `model ${spec.model} has no parameter '${p.id}'`,
        });
        return;
      }
      if (shape.kind !== p.kind) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: [...at, 'kind'],
          message: `parameter ${p.id} must be ${shape.kind}`,
        });
        return;
      }
      if (p.kind === 'choice') {
        const ids = p.options.map((o) => o.id);
        const allowed = shape.options ?? [];
        if (ids.length !== allowed.length || !allowed.every((id) => ids.includes(id)))
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: [...at, 'options'],
            message: `options of ${p.id} must be ${allowed.join(', ')}`,
          });
        if (!ids.includes(p.initial))
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: [...at, 'initial'],
            message: `initial '${p.initial}' is not an option of ${p.id}`,
          });
      }
      if (p.kind === 'int') {
        const lo = shape.min ?? 0;
        const hi = shape.max ?? 0;
        if (p.min < lo || p.max > hi || p.min > p.max)
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: [...at, 'min'],
            message: `${p.id} must range within ${lo} to ${hi}`,
          });
        if (p.initial < p.min || p.initial > p.max || (p.initial - p.min) % p.step !== 0)
          ctx.addIssue({
            code: 'custom',
            path: [...at, 'initial'],
            message: `initial ${p.initial} is off the range or step of ${p.id}`,
          });
      }
    });
    spec.thresholds.forEach((t, i) => {
      if ((t.max === undefined) === (t.min === undefined))
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['thresholds', i],
          message: 'a threshold has exactly one of max and min',
        });
      if (metricInfo(spec.model, t.metric, constants.data) === null)
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['thresholds', i, 'metric'],
          message: `model ${spec.model} has no metric '${t.metric}'`,
        });
    });
  });
export type SimulateSpec = z.infer<typeof SimulateSpec>;
export const SimulateAction = z.discriminatedUnion('t', [
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('set'), param: ParamId, value: z.union([z.number(), Slug, z.boolean()]) }),
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('run') }),
  z.strictObject({ t: z.literal('reset') }),
]);
export type SimulateAction = z.infer<typeof SimulateAction>;
```

and add `simulate: { spec: SimulateSpec, action: SimulateAction },` to `LAB_SCHEMAS` after its `configure` entry.

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the Simulate model and register it**

Create `src/labs/simulate/model.ts`:

```ts
import type { SimulateAction, SimulateSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import type { GoalResult, LabEngine } from '../contract.ts';
import { fmt, metricInfo, runModel, type SimResult, type SimValue } from './models.ts';

/** The parameter values on screen and the result of the last run, cleared by any change. */
export interface SimulateState {
  readonly spec: SimulateSpec;
  readonly values: Readonly<Record<string, SimValue>>;
  readonly result: SimResult | null;
}

type Param = SimulateSpec['params'][number];
type Threshold = SimulateSpec['thresholds'][number];

function initial(spec: SimulateSpec): SimulateState {
  return { spec, values: Object.fromEntries(spec.params.map((p) => [p.id, p.initial])), result: null };
}

/** An int within range and on its step, an option id, or a boolean, by the parameter's kind. */
function fits(p: Param, value: unknown): value is SimValue {
  if (p.kind === 'toggle') return typeof value === 'boolean';
  if (p.kind === 'choice') return typeof value === 'string' && p.options.some((o) => o.id === value);
  return (
    typeof value === 'number' &&
    Number.isInteger(value) &&
    value >= p.min &&
    value <= p.max &&
    (value - p.min) % p.step === 0
  );
}

/** Goal ids in the order the thresholds first name them. */
export function simulateGoals(spec: SimulateSpec): string[] {
  return [...new Set(spec.thresholds.map((t) => t.goal))];
}

interface Reading {
  label: string;
  unit: string;
  value: number;
  holds: boolean;
}

function read(spec: SimulateSpec, result: SimResult, t: Threshold): Reading {
  const info = metricInfo(spec.model, t.metric, spec.constants) ?? { label: t.metric, unit: '' };
  const value = result.metrics[t.metric] ?? Number.NaN;
  const holds = t.max !== undefined ? value <= t.max : t.min !== undefined && value >= t.min;
  return { ...info, value, holds };
}

function limitText(t: Threshold, unit: string): string {
  return t.max !== undefined ? `max ${fmt(t.max, unit)}` : `min ${fmt(t.min ?? 0, unit)}`;
}

export const SIMULATE: LabEngine<SimulateSpec, SimulateState, SimulateAction> = {
  init: initial,

  reduce(state, action) {
    if (typeof action !== 'object' || action === null) return state;
    const a = action as { t?: unknown; param?: unknown; value?: unknown };
    if (a.t === 'reset') return initial(state.spec);
    if (a.t === 'run') {
      return { ...state, result: runModel(state.spec.model, state.spec.constants, state.values) };
    }
    if (a.t !== 'set') return state;
    const p = state.spec.params.find((x) => x.id === a.param);
    if (p === undefined || !fits(p, a.value)) return state;
    return { spec: state.spec, values: { ...state.values, [p.id]: a.value }, result: null };
  },

  check(spec, state): GoalResult[] {
    return simulateGoals(spec).map((goalId) => {
      const result = state.result;
      if (result === null) return { goalId, pass: false, message: 'Not run yet' };
      const mine = spec.thresholds.filter((t) => t.goal === goalId);
      const readings = mine.map((t) => ({ t, r: read(spec, result, t) }));
      return {
        goalId,
        pass: readings.every(({ r }) => r.holds),
        message: readings
          .map(({ t, r }) => `${r.label}: ${fmt(r.value, r.unit)} (${limitText(t, r.unit)})`)
          .join('; '),
      };
    });
  },

  hint(spec, state, goalId) {
    const result = state.result;
    if (result === null) return 'Run the simulation first.';
    for (const t of spec.thresholds.filter((x) => x.goal === goalId)) {
      const r = read(spec, result, t);
      if (r.holds) continue;
      return t.max !== undefined
        ? `${r.label} is ${fmt(r.value, r.unit)}, over the ${fmt(t.max, r.unit)} limit.`
        : `${r.label} is ${fmt(r.value, r.unit)}, under the ${fmt(t.min ?? 0, r.unit)} minimum.`;
    }
    return 'Every threshold of this goal holds.';
  },
};
```

In `src/labs/engines.ts`, add `import { SIMULATE } from './simulate/model.ts';` and this entry after `configure` in `ENGINES`:

```ts

```

- [ ] **Step 6: Give R7 the Simulate texts**

In `scripts/content/lib/labs.ts`, merge `SimulateSpec` into the import from `'../../../schemas/lab.ts'`, add this function after `configureTexts`, and add `simulate: simulateTexts,` to `SPEC_TEXTS` after its `configure` entry:

```ts
/** Parameter, option and unit labels of a Simulate spec, and the labels inside its constants. */
export function simulateTexts(spec: unknown): string[] {
  const parsed = SimulateSpec.safeParse(spec);
  if (!parsed.success) return [];
  const { params, constants } = parsed.data;
  const named = Object.values(constants as Record<string, unknown>)
    .filter(Array.isArray)
    .flatMap((list: unknown[]) =>
      list.flatMap((x) =>
        typeof x === 'object' && x !== null && 'label' in x && typeof x.label === 'string' ? [x.label] : [],
      ),
    );
  return [
    ...params.flatMap((p) => [
      p.label,
      ...(p.kind === 'choice' ? p.options.map((o) => o.label) : []),
      ...(p.kind === 'int' && p.unit !== undefined ? [p.unit] : []),
    ]),
    ...named,
  ];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the Simulate copy, the view and its styles**

Append to `src/app/lab.ts`:

```ts
export const SIM_IDLE = 'Not run yet. Press Run and check to run the simulation with these parameters.';
export const SIM_RESULTS = 'Results of the last run';
/** The accessible name of a Simulate chart, naming its series and its points. */
export function simChartLabel(model: 'scaling' | 's3-lifecycle', series: readonly string[]): string {
  return model === 'scaling'
    ? `${series.join(' and ')} for each hour from 0:00 to 23:00`
    : `${series.join(' and ')} at the end of each 30-day month`;
}
```

Create `src/labs/simulate/View.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SimulateAction, SimulateSpec } from '../../../schemas/lab.ts';
import { SIM_IDLE, SIM_RESULTS, simChartLabel } from '../../app/lab.ts';
import type { SimulateState } from './model.ts';
import { fmt, metricInfo, type SimSeries } from './models.ts';

const W = 480;
const H = 160;
const FILLS = ['var(--active)', 'var(--muted)'];

/** Bars for one or two series on a shared scale, with a visually hidden table of the same numbers. */
function SimChart(props: { label: string; xs: string[]; series: SimSeries[] }) {
  const { label, xs, series } = props;
  const top = Math.max(1, ...series.flatMap((s) => s.values));
  const slot = W / Math.max(1, xs.length);
  const bar = (slot * 0.8) / Math.max(1, series.length);
  return (
    <figure class="sim-chart">
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H + 1}`} role="img" aria-label={label} focusable="false">
        {series.map((s, k) =>
          s.values.map((v, i) => {
            const h = (Math.max(0, v) / top) * H;
            return (
              <rect
                key={`${s.label}-${xs[i] ?? i}`}
                x={i * slot + slot * 0.1 + k * bar}
                y={H - h}
                width={bar}
                height={h}
                fill={FILLS[k] ?? 'var(--muted)'}
              />
            );
          }),
        )}
        <line x1="0" y1={H + 0.5} x2={W} y2={H + 0.5} stroke="var(--line)" />
      </svg>
      <figcaption class="muted sim-legend">
        {series.map((s, k) => (
          <span key={s.label} class="sim-key">
            <span class={`sim-swatch sim-swatch-${k + 1}`} aria-hidden="true" />
            {s.label}
          </span>
        ))}
      </figcaption>
      <table class="visually-hidden">
        <caption>{label}</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col">Point</th>
            {series.map((s) => (
              <th key={s.label} scope="col">
                {s.label}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {xs.map((x, i) => (
            <tr key={x}>
              <th scope="row">{x}</th>
              {series.map((s) => (
                <td key={s.label}>{fmt(s.values[i] ?? 0, s.unit)}</td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </figure>
  );
}

/** Parameters, then the metrics of the last run and, for two models, its chart (spec 5.1). */
export function SimulateView(props: {
  spec: SimulateSpec;
  state: SimulateState;
  onAction(a: SimulateAction): void;
}) {
  const { spec, state, onAction } = props;
  const result = state.result;
  const xs =
    spec.model === 'scaling'
      ? (result?.series[0]?.values ?? []).map((_, i) => `${i}:00`)
      : (result?.series[0]?.values ?? []).map((_, i) => `Day ${30 * i + 29}`);
  return (
    <div class="sim">
      <fieldset class="sim-params">
        <legend class="visually-hidden">Parameters</legend>
        {spec.params.map((p) => {
          const id = `sim-${p.id}`;
          const value = state.values[p.id];
          if (p.kind === 'toggle') {
            const checked = value === true;
            return (
              <label key={p.id} class={`option sim-toggle${checked ? ' chosen' : ''}`}>
                <input
                  id={id}
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={checked}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    onAction({ t: 'set', param: p.id, value: event.currentTarget.checked })
                  }
                />
                <span class="option-text">{p.label}</span>
              </label>
            );
          }
          if (p.kind === 'choice') {
            return (
              <div key={p.id} class="sim-field">
                <label for={id}>{p.label}</label>
                <select
                  id={id}
                  value={typeof value === 'string' ? value : p.initial}
                  onChange={(event) => onAction({ t: 'set', param: p.id, value: event.currentTarget.value })}
                >
                  {p.options.map((o) => (
                    <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                      {o.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            );
          }
          const shown = typeof value === 'number' ? value : p.initial;
          return (
            <div key={p.id} class="sim-field">
              <label for={id}>
                {p.label}
                {p.unit !== undefined && <span class="muted"> ({p.unit})</span>}
              </label>
              <input
                id={id}
                type="number"
                inputMode="numeric"
                min={p.min}
                max={p.max}
                step={p.step}
                value={shown}
                onInput={(event) => {
                  const n = event.currentTarget.valueAsNumber;
                  if (Number.isFinite(n)) onAction({ t: 'set', param: p.id, value: n });
                }}
                onBlur={(event) => {
                  event.currentTarget.value = String(shown);
                }}
              />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </fieldset>
      {result === null ? (
        <p class="muted sim-idle">{SIM_IDLE}</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <table class="sim-metrics">
            <caption>{SIM_RESULTS}</caption>
            <tbody>
              {Object.entries(result.metrics).map(([metric, value]) => {
                const info = metricInfo(spec.model, metric, spec.constants) ?? { label: metric, unit: '' };
                return (
                  <tr key={metric}>
                    <th scope="row">{info.label}</th>
                    <td>{fmt(value, info.unit)}</td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          {(spec.model === 'scaling' || spec.model === 's3-lifecycle') && result.series.length > 0 && (
            <SimChart
              label={simChartLabel(
                spec.model,
                result.series.map((s) => s.label),
              )}
              xs={xs}
              series={result.series}
            />
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

In `src/ui/base.css`, under `/* Labs: Simulate */` and nowhere else, add:

```css
.sim {
  display: grid;
  gap: 16px;
}

.sim-params {
  display: grid;
  gap: 12px;
  min-width: 0;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
}

@media (min-width: 640px) {
  .sim-params {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

.sim-field label {
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 4px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sim-field select,
.sim-field input {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 44px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  font: inherit;
  color: var(--text);
  background: var(--pane);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
}

.sim-field input {
  max-width: 10rem;
}

.sim-field select:hover,
.sim-field input:hover {
  border-color: var(--active);
}

.sim-idle {
  margin: 0;
}

.sim-metrics {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
}

.sim-metrics caption {
  margin-bottom: 4px;
  font-weight: 600;
  text-align: left;
}

.sim-metrics th,
.sim-metrics td {
  padding: 6px 0;
  text-align: left;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}

.sim-metrics td {

}

.sim-swatch-1 {

}

.sim-swatch-2 {
```

```ts
export const SYNTHETIC_SIMULATE_LAB_ID = 'lab-m01-bakery-rush';

/** The bakery Oven rush lab of `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts` as the build ships it. */
export function syntheticSimulateLab(questions: QuestionView[]): LabView {
  const lab = bakerySimulateLab();
  return {
    id: SYNTHETIC_SIMULATE_LAB_ID,
    module: 1,
    engine: 'simulate',
    tier: 'core',
    rev: 1,
    title: 'Bakery morning rush',
    minutes: lab.minutes,
    concepts: lab.concepts,
    brief: lab.brief,
    spec: lab.spec,
    goals: lab.goals,
    notes: notesOf(questions),
    debrief: { lines: lab.debrief.lines, questions: questions.slice(0, 2) },
    credits: true,
  };
}
```

In `tests/e2e/labs.spec.ts`, add `SYNTHETIC_SIMULATE_LAB_ID` and `syntheticSimulateLab` to the import from `'./lab-helpers.ts'` and append:

```ts
const SIMULATE_URL = `/#/m/01/lab/${SYNTHETIC_SIMULATE_LAB_ID}`;
const SIM_IDLE_LINE = 'Not run yet. Press Run and check to run the simulation with these parameters.';

test('a Simulate lab works by keyboard alone and runs before it checks', async ({ page, browserName }) => {
  test.skip(
    browserName === 'webkit',
    'WebKit opens a native menu for letter keys on a select, so typing a letter is unreliable there',
  );
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticSimulateLab);
  await page.goto(SIMULATE_URL);
  await expect(h1(page, 'Bakery morning rush')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Prices are in lab credits, a made-up unit, not AWS prices.')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(SIM_IDLE_LINE)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Run and check/ })).toBeVisible();
  const mode = page.getByLabel('When ovens are added');
  await mode.focus();
  await page.keyboard.press('a');
  await expect(mode).toHaveValue('predictive');
  await page.keyboard.press('Tab');
  await page.keyboard.press('Tab');
  await page.keyboard.press('Tab');
  const most = page.getByLabel('Most ovens', { exact: true });
  await expect(most).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowUp');
  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowUp');
  await expect(most).toHaveValue('3');
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByText('1 of 2 goals met.', { exact: true })).toBeAttached();
  const results = page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Results of the last run' });
  await expect(results.getByRole('row', { name: 'Daily cost 29 lab credits' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(results.getByRole('row', { name: 'Dropped share 29.4%' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('img', { name: 'Orders and Capacity for each hour from 0:00 to 23:00' }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press('Tab');
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Share orders across ovens')).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('Space');
  await expect(page.getByText(SIM_IDLE_LINE)).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByText('All 2 goals met. Plus 40 XP.', { exact: true })).toBeAttached();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Debrief' })).toBeFocused();
});

test('a Simulate lab has no axe violations and no sideways scroll, before and after a run', async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticSimulateLab);
  await page.goto(SIMULATE_URL);
  await expect(h1(page, 'Bakery morning rush')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Run and check/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Results of the last run' })).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});

test('Simulate inputs, selects and checkboxes are at least 44 px tall on a phone', async ({
  page,
}, testInfo) => {
  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'webkit-mobile', 'the phone layout runs on webkit-mobile only');
  await useSyntheticLab(page, syntheticSimulateLab);
  await page.goto(SIMULATE_URL);
  await expect(h1(page, 'Bakery morning rush')).toBeVisible();
  const controls = [
    page.getByLabel('When ovens are added'),
    page.getByLabel('Fewest ovens', { exact: true }),
    page.getByLabel('Ovens at opening', { exact: true }),
    page.getByLabel('Most ovens', { exact: true }),
    page.locator('label.sim-toggle'),
  ];
  for (const control of controls) {
    expect((await control.boundingBox())?.height ?? 0).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44);
  }
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Run and check/ }).click();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole('img', { name: 'Orders and Capacity for each hour from 0:00 to 23:00' }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Teach the writer and the critic, and add the style rules**

Append to `agents/lab-writer.md` (it stays `version: 1`, because no Simulate lab has been written with it yet):

````markdown
## Simulate labs

A Simulate spec is `model`, `constants`, `params` and `thresholds`; `SimulateSpec` and `SIM_CONSTANTS` in `schemas/lab.ts` are the shapes, `modelParams` and `metricInfo` in `src/labs/simulate/models.ts` list each model's parameters and metrics, and `bakerySimulateLab` in `tests/fixtures/lab-specs.ts` is a complete example.
Actions are `{ t: set, param: <id>, value: <number, option id or boolean> }`, `{ t: run }` and `{ t: reset }`; the screen and R11 run the model before every check, so a solution or wrong path never needs a `run` action.
Your lab is one row of `SIM_LABS` in `tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts`: copy its model, constants, parameter ids, kinds, ranges, steps, initials and option ids, thresholds, solution and wrong-path actions exactly, and write only the text: the title, the brief, goal labels and hints, parameter and option labels, units, constant labels, wrong-path mistakes and the debrief.
`pnpm vitest run tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts` fails when any number differs from the table; never retune a number to make a check pass.
Name every constant a lab value in the brief, write every price as lab credits, and write `minutes` from 6 to 10.
Each threshold fails each wrong path for the reason its `mistake` names: `min 0` leaves no instance ready overnight, a one-zone class for the only copy loses data, Lambda for the steady high-volume worker costs more, and so on; say that reason in the goal's hint without naming the setting.

### Model notes for the brief and the debrief

- `scaling` (Morning rush): `dynamic` follows the previous hour, `predictive` scales ahead of the forecast, and without a load balancer one instance takes every order.
- `compute-cost` (Bursty or steady): Lambda charges per request and per GB-second while the code runs, and the instance costs the same flat month whether busy or idle.
- `s3-lifecycle` (Receipts for 7 years): each class has a storage price, a retrieval price, a minimum duration charged when data leaves early, a retrieval time and its zone count; a read slower than its deadline is late, and a zone loss on day 150 destroys data held in a one-zone class.
- `cache` (Cache the menu): the cache serves its hit share of reads, and the database slows down once its load passes its capacity.
- `bill` (The monthly bill): consolidated billing sums usage before the tiers apply, a commitment lowers the hourly rate, inbound transfer is free, and the alert day is when spending crosses the alert share of the budget.
- `transfer` (Online or offline transfer): online days use 80% of the link, offline jobs ship together in one lab turnaround, and the device count is the data divided by the lab device capacity, rounded up; neither Snowball figure is in the notes, so both are lab values.
````

````markdown
## Simulate criteria

Run `pnpm vitest run tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts` and report any failure for this lab as a `wrongPath` issue: the numbers must match `SIM_LABS` exactly.
Each threshold fails each wrong path for the mistake that path names, not by accident; a wrong path that also fails other goals is fine when its own goal fails for its own reason.
Every constant is called a lab value, and every price is in lab credits.
The debrief explains the ordering the lesson rests on; for Bursty or steady, the instance wins for the order worker because it is never idle, and Lambda wins for the weekly export because it is idle almost all month.
Parameter labels name the lever, units are always shown, and no label hints at the right value.
````

````markdown
Name every constant a lab value in the brief, and every price in lab credits.
Thresholds read as the cafe's needs, such as a daily budget or a deadline, never as the answer.
````

## Task 14: Incident schema, loading, rules, checks and the build

**Files:**
- Create: `schemas/incident.ts`, `tests/unit/incident-schema.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/content-root/content/incidents/inc-001.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r04-incident-missing-why.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r05-incident-evidence.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r06-incident-step-concept.yaml`, `tests/content/bad/r07-incident-run.yaml`
- Modify: `scripts/content/lib/context.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/qid.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/question-files.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/checks.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/coverage.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/report.ts`, `scripts/content/lib/draft.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r02-ids.ts`, `scripts/content/rules/r06-references.ts`, `scripts/content/checks.ts`, `scripts/content/report.ts`, `scripts/content/validate.ts`, `scripts/content/build.ts`, `schemas/chunks.ts`, `tests/fixtures/index.ts`, `content/STYLE.md`, `agents/question-writer.md`
- Test: `tests/unit/incident-schema.test.ts`, `context.test.ts`, `r02.test.ts`, `r06.test.ts`, `r09.test.ts`, `r10.test.ts`, `qid.test.ts`, `checks.test.ts`, `draft.test.ts`, `coverage.test.ts`, `build.test.ts`, `agents.test.ts`, `tests/content/bad.test.ts`

Design notes:
- An incident step is a `QuestionEntry` like any other, with a new field `incident: string | null` (the incident id, null for lesson and exam-pool files), so R3, R4, R5, R7, R9, the style checks, `content:checks` and `content:stamp` cover steps with no change of their own.
- Every count keeps to `incident === null`: `conceptCounts` and `coverage` in `coverage.ts` (which feed R10, `--coverage`, `--homed` and `--targets`), the practice and style lines of `report(ctx)`, `homed`, `targets` and the build's module questions.
- R10 and `style.ts` need no edit: R10 counts through `practiceCounts`, which `coverage.ts` filters, and `coverageProblems` counts only entries whose `path` is the lesson file, which a step never is.
- `--needs-dani` keeps steps on purpose, because a step that fails `NEEDS_DANI_ATTEMPTS` (3) times goes to Dani like a practice item and is never retired.
- R6 skips its "practice file is the note's file" check for steps and adds the incident checks: each incident concept is registered and tested, and each step's concept (alias resolved) is one of the incident's concepts; a retired incident is skipped.
- `runRules` sorts findings by file and then line, so in R6's exact lists the incident file's findings (the incident-level one, then its steps by line) come before Task 2's lab findings and the question-file findings.
- A lab debrief never names an incident step: Task 2's `labTargets` and R6 lab check and Task 4's build (through the filtered `live` list) keep to `incident === null`, so a step id reads `<lab>: debrief question <qid> is not a live practice question`.
- R2 claims incident ids, and claims a step's qid as `step of <inc>`, so a step that repeats a question id reads `id 'q-...' (question) is already used by step of inc-001 in ...`.
- R2 keeps a shipped incident from vanishing: an incident whose every step was `live` or `retired` in HEAD must still have its id in the tree, and leaves only with `status: retired`; an incident that still had a draft step may be deleted.
- `incidentShips(entry)` in `context.ts` is the one rule the build and the report share: `status` is `live` and every step is `live`.
- The fixture incident `inc-001` sits on the two fixture concepts with two live, stamped `single` steps, so the baseline runs R4, R5, R7 and R9 over real steps, and the four bad fixtures prove each of those rules reaches a step.
- The R7 fixture copies a course knowledge-check stem into a step, because R7's stem check runs against the course item shingles (`tests/fixtures/source/module-01.md`); the brief's "copies a run of its note" is resolved to that, the run R7 actually checks.
- The bad fixtures are written by shell commands from the passing fixture, so each differs from it by exactly one line.
- Every diff below is against the tree after Tasks 2, 3 and 4, so its context lines already hold their lab code, the second live fixture question `q-d9p4r6` and the fixture lab.
- Covers P4-006, P4-007, P4-009, P4-010, P4-011, P4-013, P4-016, P4-017, P4-019 (incidents), P4-021, P4-024 (schema), P4-025 (incidents), P4-027 (incidents), P4-030, P4-033, P4-034, P4-035 (incidents), P4-100 and P4-135.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the schema test and the fixture incident**

Create `tests/unit/incident-schema.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { IncidentFile, IncidentId } from '../../schemas/incident.ts';

/** A made-up bakery incident with two placeholder steps; the loader, not this schema, parses each step. */
const incident = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: 'inc-005',
  title: 'The proofing cabinets ran all night',
  modules: [3, 2],
  concepts: ['proof-box'],
  brief: 'The morning bill shows proofing cabinets that ran all night with no dough inside them.',
  steps: [{ id: 'q-a1b2c3' }, { id: 'q-d4e5f6' }],
  ...over,
});

const messages = (data: unknown): string[] => {
  const parsed = IncidentFile.safeParse(data);
  return parsed.success ? [] : parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${i.path.join('.')}: ${i.message}`);
};

describe('IncidentFile (spec 2.6, 4.5)', () => {
  it('accepts an incident and defaults its status to live', () => {
    const parsed = IncidentFile.parse(incident());
    expect(parsed.status).toBe('live');
    expect(IncidentFile.parse(incident({ status: 'retired' })).status).toBe('retired');
  });

  it('takes ids like inc-007 only', () => {
    expect(IncidentId.safeParse('inc-007').success).toBe(true);
    expect(IncidentId.safeParse('inc-7').success).toBe(false);
    expect(messages(incident({ id: 'incident-7' }))).toEqual(['id: must look like inc-007']);
  });

  it('needs 2 to 4 steps', () => {
    expect(messages(incident({ steps: [{ id: 'q-a1b2c3' }] }))).toEqual([
      'steps: Too small: expected array to have >=2 items',
    ]);
    expect(messages(incident({ steps: Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => ({})) }))).toEqual([
      'steps: Too big: expected array to have <=4 items',
    ]);
  });

  it('rejects a later module that is not lower than the home module, and a repeated module', () => {
    expect(messages(incident({ modules: [3, 4] }))).toEqual([
      'modules.1: module 4 must be lower than the home module 3',
    ]);
    expect(messages(incident({ modules: [3, 3] }))).toEqual([
      'modules: modules must be distinct',
      'modules.1: module 3 must be lower than the home module 3',
    ]);
  });

  it('rejects unknown keys', () => {
    expect(messages(incident({ hint: 'look at the bill' }))).toEqual([': Unrecognized key: "hint"']);
  });
});
```

```yaml
        correct: true
```

```diff
 import { stringify } from 'yaml';
@@ -321,3 +321,57 @@ describe('R2 labs (spec 5.1)', () => {
     ).toEqual([]);
   });
 });
+
+describe('R2 incidents (spec 4.5)', () => {
+  const INCIDENT = 'content/incidents/inc-001.yaml';
+  const DRAFT_STEP = (text: string): string => text.replace('    status: live\n', '    status: draft\n');
+
+  /** R2 findings with the fixture incident in HEAD as `headEdit` makes it, after `edit` changes the working tree. */
+  function withIncidentHead(headEdit: (text: string) => string, edit: (root: string) => void): string[] {
+    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
+    const headText = headEdit(readFileSync(join(root, INCIDENT), 'utf8'));
+    edit(root);
+    const ctx = loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY });
+    const withHeadFiles: Context = {
+      ...ctx,
+      headPaths: (prefix) => (INCIDENT.startsWith(prefix) ? [INCIDENT] : []),
+      head: (path) => (path === INCIDENT ? headText : null),
+    };
+    return runRules(withHeadFiles, ['R2']).findings.map((f) => `${f.file} ${f.message}`);
+  }
+  const remove = (root: string): void => rmSync(join(root, INCIDENT));
+
+  it('reports a step id that repeats a question id, and an incident id used twice', () => {
+    const root = makeFixtureRoot();
+    replaceIn(root, INCIDENT, '  - id: q-d2f6h8\n', '  - id: q-b4k7m2\n');
+    copyFileSync(join(root, INCIDENT), join(root, 'content/incidents/inc-002.yaml'));
+    const findings = runRules(loadContext(root, { today: FIXTURE_TODAY }), ['R2']).findings.map(
+      (f) => f.message,
+    );
+    expect(findings).toContain(
+      "id 'q-b4k7m2' (question) is already used by step of inc-001 in content/incidents/inc-001.yaml",
+    );
+    expect(findings).toContain(
+      "id 'inc-001' (incident) is already used by incident in content/incidents/inc-001.yaml",
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('lets an incident whose HEAD version still had a draft step be deleted', () => {
+    expect(withIncidentHead(DRAFT_STEP, remove)).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('reports a shipped incident of HEAD that is gone', () => {
+    expect(withIncidentHead((text) => text, remove)).toEqual([
+      `${INCIDENT} incident inc-001 was in HEAD and is gone; retire it with status: retired instead`,
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('accepts a shipped incident retired in place', () => {
+    expect(
+      withIncidentHead(
+        (text) => text,
+        (root) => replaceIn(root, INCIDENT, 'modules: [1]', 'status: retired\nmodules: [1]'),
+      ),
+    ).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the tests and see them fail**

```bash
pnpm vitest run tests/unit/incident-schema.test.ts tests/unit/context.test.ts tests/unit/r02.test.ts tests/unit/r06.test.ts tests/unit/r09.test.ts tests/unit/qid.test.ts tests/unit/checks.test.ts tests/unit/draft.test.ts tests/unit/coverage.test.ts tests/unit/build.test.ts tests/unit/agents.test.ts tests/content 2>&1 | grep -E '^ FAIL |Error' | head -50
pnpm typecheck 2>&1 | grep 'error TS' | grep -E 'r10|incident-schema|coverage' | head
```

Expected: FAIL; `incident-schema.test.ts` cannot find `../../schemas/incident.ts`; `coverage.test.ts` reads `INCIDENT_MODULES` as `undefined`; the new loader, R2, R6, R9, qid, checks, draft and build tests and the pinned lists that now hold the fixture's steps fail, because nothing loads `content/incidents/` yet; `agents.test.ts` still finds version 2; and `tests/content/bad.test.ts` fails the four `r0[4-7]-incident-*` fixtures with `expected 0 to be greater than 0`.
The prototype counted 37 failed tests in 12 files.
`tsc` rejects `incident` in the `QuestionEntry` literals of `r10.test.ts` (TS2353), cannot find `schemas/incident.ts` (TS2307) and finds no `INCIDENT_MODULES` or `incidentTargets` export (TS2305); `r10.test.ts` itself passes under Vitest, which strips types.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the incident schema and load incident files**

Create `schemas/incident.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { ConceptId, ModuleNumber } from './common.ts';
import { INCIDENT_ID } from './ids.ts';

/** An incident id: `inc-` and three digits; Module m owns `inc-<4(m-2)+1>` to `inc-<4(m-1)>` (spec 4.5). */
export const IncidentId = z.string().regex(INCIDENT_ID, 'must look like inc-007');

/**
 * One daily incident (spec 2.6, 4.5): a short cafe story told over 2 to 4 steps. `modules[0]` is its home module,
 * whose chunk ships it; any later entry is an earlier module that must also be read. The steps stay `unknown` here
 * because the loader parses each one with the question schema, so a bad step reports its own path and line.
 */
export const IncidentFile = z
  .strictObject({
    id: IncidentId,
    title: z.string().min(8).max(60),
    status: z.enum(['live', 'retired']).default('live'),
    modules: z.array(ModuleNumber).min(1).max(3),
    concepts: z.array(ConceptId).min(1).max(4),
    brief: z.string().min(40).max(400),
    steps: z.array(z.unknown()).min(2).max(4),
  })
  .superRefine((file, ctx) => {
    const [home, ...earlier] = file.modules;
    if (new Set(file.modules).size !== file.modules.length) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['modules'], message: 'modules must be distinct' });
    }
    for (const [i, m] of earlier.entries()) {
      if (home !== undefined && m >= home) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['modules', i + 1],
          message: `module ${m} must be lower than the home module ${home}`,
        });
      }
    }
  });
export type IncidentFile = z.infer<typeof IncidentFile>;
```

```diff
   return JSON.stringify(rest);

+/**
+ * The id of an incident file text and whether it shipped (it has steps and every one is live or retired), tolerating a
+ * file that fails the schema; null when it does not parse or names no id.
+ */
+function incidentIn(yamlText: string | null): { id: string; shipped: boolean } | null {
+  if (yamlText === null) return null;
+  let data: unknown;
+  try {
+    data = parseYaml(yamlText);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+  if (typeof data !== 'object' || data === null) return null;
+  const { id, steps } = data as { id?: unknown; steps?: unknown };
+  if (typeof id !== 'string') return null;
+  const statuses = Array.isArray(steps)
+    ? steps.map((s: unknown) =>
+        typeof s === 'object' && s !== null ? (s as { status?: unknown }).status : null,
+      )
+    : [];
+  const shipped = statuses.length > 0 && statuses.every((s) => s === 'live' || s === 'retired');
+  return { id, shipped };
+}
+
 /** The forms of a forms.yaml text that pass the schema; [] when it is missing or does not parse. */
 function formsIn(yamlText: string | null): Forms['forms'] {
   if (yamlText === null) return [];

- * version; a lab in HEAD stays, and any edit to it raises its `rev`; a form frozen in HEAD keeps its qids, their
- * order and `frozen: true` (spec 4.3, 5.1).

+ * changes takes a key-fix version; a lab in HEAD stays, and any edit to it raises its `rev`; an incident that shipped
+ * in HEAD stays; a form frozen in HEAD keeps its qids, their order and `frozen: true` (spec 4.3, 4.5, 5.1).
```

```diff
+    (e) => e.incident === null && e.q.pool === 'practice' && e.q.status === 'live',

   const out: LabTarget[] = [];
   for (const readme of ctx.notes.filter((n) => n.kind === 'module')) {
     const m = readme.module;
@@ -201,8 +219,8 @@ export function targets(ctx: Context): Target[] {
     const need = homed(ctx, note.id).concepts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.need, 0);
     if (need === 0) continue;
     const file =
-      ctx.questions.find((e) => e.q.pool === 'practice' && e.noteId === note.id)?.path ??
-      questionFileOf(note.path);
+      ctx.questions.find((e) => e.incident === null && e.q.pool === 'practice' && e.noteId === note.id)
+        ?.path ?? questionFileOf(note.path);
     out.push({ note: note.id, file, need });
   }
   return out;
@@ -287,3 +305,35 @@ export function examTargets(ctx: Context): ExamTarget[] {
     return { module, file, cells, multi, concepts };
   });
 }
+
+/** One module's entry of `--incident-targets`: the incident ids it owns, the earlier modules and its tested concepts. */
+export interface IncidentTarget {
+  module: number;
+  ids: string[];
+  earlier: number[];
+  concepts: { id: string; name: string; note: string; tasks: string[] }[];
+}
+
+/** The first and last module with incidents, and how many incident ids each module owns (spec 4.5). */
+export const INCIDENT_MODULES = { first: 2, last: 13, each: 4 } as const;
+
+/**
+ * `--incident-targets`: Modules 2 to 13, each owning four pre-assigned ids (Module m owns `inc-<4(m-2)+1>` to
+ * `inc-<4(m-1)>`), the modules before it, and the tested concepts homed in it, so twelve writers never share a file.
+ */
+export function incidentTargets(ctx: Context): IncidentTarget[] {
+  const { first, last, each } = INCIDENT_MODULES;
+  const out: IncidentTarget[] = [];
+  for (let module = first; module <= last; module++) {
+    const ids = Array.from(
+      { length: each },
+      (_, i) => `inc-${String(each * (module - first) + i + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`,
+    );
+    const earlier = Array.from({ length: module - 1 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
+    const concepts = ctx.concepts
+      .filter((c) => c.tested !== false && Number(c.home.slice(1, 3)) === module)
+      .map((c) => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name, note: c.home, tasks: [...c.tasks] }));
+    out.push({ module, ids, earlier, concepts });
+  }
+  return out;
+}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the incident style rules and the question writer's Incident mode**

Append to the end of `content/STYLE.md`, after the `## Labs` section (Task 3, with any H3s Tasks 12 and 13 have added inside it), leaving one blank line before the new heading:

```markdown
## Incidents

A daily incident is a short cafe story told over 2 to 4 steps in `content/incidents/inc-NNN.yaml` (spec 4.5).
The question rules above `## Labs` apply to each step, which is a full practice question with its own whys, evidence and takeaway.

- Tell one story: the brief sets the scene, and each step moves it on, from noticing the problem to fixing it.
- Make each step stand on its own, so a reader who missed the step before can still answer it.
- Never let the brief or an earlier step give away a later step's answer.
- Keep the brief to plain text, 40 to 400 characters, with no Markdown and no answer in it.
- Use `single` or `multi` steps only, since a `match` step breaks the story into a table.
- Draw on earlier modules only when the story needs them, and list each one in `modules` after the home module.

- Good: `The bill for the loyalty app doubled overnight, and the manager wants to know why before the morning rush.`
- Bad: `The cafe should have used Auto Scaling, so the bill doubled.` gives the answer in the brief.
```

In `agents/question-writer.md`:

```diff
--- a/agents/question-writer.md
+++ b/agents/question-writer.md
@@ -1,6 +1,6 @@
 ---
 name: question-writer
-version: 2
+version: 3
 ---
 
 # Question writer
@@ -99,6 +99,31 @@ When your task says to revise, read each draft's `review.lastFailure`, which nam
 - Revise only drafts whose `review.attempts` is below 3, and never touch a `live` or `retired` question.
 - Loop on `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>` until it ends with `0 problems` again.
 
+## Incident mode
+
+When your task says Incident mode, you write the daily incidents of one module instead of a lesson's questions (spec 4.5).
+An incident is a short cafe story told over 2 to 4 steps, and each step is a full practice question in the format above.
+
+Inputs:
+
+- One entry of `pnpm --silent content:report --incident-targets`, inlined in your task: `module`, the four pre-assigned `ids`, the `earlier` modules and the module's tested `concepts` with their `note` and `tasks`.
+- The notes of your module under `resources/`, and the notes of any earlier module you bring into an incident.
+- `content/STYLE.md`, whose `## Incidents` section adds the incident rules, and `content/concepts.yaml`, read only.
+- The live practice stems of your module under `content/questions/`, read only, so no step repeats one.
+
+What to write:
+
+- Write one file per id, `content/incidents/<id>.yaml`, and never any other file.
+- Each file holds `id`, `title` (8 to 60 characters), `modules`, `concepts` (1 to 4), `brief` (40 to 400 characters, plain text) and `steps` (2 to 4).
+- `modules[0]` is your module; add at most two entries from `earlier`, and only for a module whose concept a step uses.
+- Every step's `concept` is one of the incident's `concepts`, and its `note` is the concept's home note.
+- Steps are `single` or `multi` only, with `status: draft`, `version: 1`, `pool: practice` and `history: []`, and no `verified` or `review` block.
+- Take each step id from `pnpm --silent content:new-question N`, where N is the step's module.
+- Leave out `status` at the top of the file, so the incident is live once every step is.
+
+Checking: run `pnpm --silent content:validate --draft content/incidents/<id>.yaml` for each file until its last line ends with `0 problems`, then stop.
+Revising: follow "Revising after the checks" above for each step, and never delete an incident whose steps were all live once; set `status: retired` only when your task says so.
+
 ## Rules
 
 - Write only your one target file, and never edit a note, `content/concepts.yaml`, another question file or anything under `.results/`.
```

## Task 15: The incident content fan-out (Workflow tool, controller-run)

**Files:**
- Create: up to 48 incident files, `content/incidents/inc-001.yaml` to `content/incidents/inc-048.yaml`; Module m (2 to 13) owns `inc-<4(m-2)+1>` to `inc-<4(m-1)>`, zero-padded, so Module 2 owns `inc-001` to `inc-004` and Module 13 owns `inc-045` to `inc-048`
- Test: `pnpm --silent content:report` (the `Incidents:` line), `pnpm --silent content:report --needs-dani`, and `pnpm check` through the pre-commit hook of every commit

This task is content, not code: the controller runs it with the Workflow tool once Task 14 is on `master`, and its agents write, check, stamp and commit the incidents.
Incident steps are practice items that Dani sees, so the exam firewall here is that no agent reads `content/exam/`; agent returns, `log` lines and commit bodies still hold counts and ids only.
Writers may read the live practice stems of the modules they use under `content/questions/`, so no step repeats one; checkers never open `content/questions/` or `content/incidents/`, and judge only their batches.
An incident still short after the drain (a step that needs Dani, or a module whose writer could not fill four good stories) ships later or is cut; there is no regenerate phase.
Budget: about 144 steps, about 58 check batches in the first round and about 60 agents over the whole run (fan-out map 3.8).
Covers P4-024 (content), P4-026, P4-029, P4-030 and P4-031.

- [ ] **Step 1: Make the incident worktree**

```bash
git worktree add -b content/phase-4-incidents "$SCRATCH/p4/wt-incidents" master
cd "$SCRATCH/p4/wt-incidents"
export PATH="/Users/danielchahine/.local/share/fnm/node-versions/v24.21.0/installation/bin:$PATH"
node --version
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
mkdir -p .results && cp -R "$REPO/.results/checks" .results/
git log --oneline -1
```

Expected: `v24.21.0`, then a `master` commit at or after Task 14's `Load, check and ship the daily incidents`.
Copying `.results/checks` carries the practice pass history, so the merge of `passes.json` in Step 5 loses nothing.
Every agent below works only in this worktree, and every later command of this task runs in it.
