
Expected: `12 48 true`: Modules 2 to 13, 48 pre-assigned ids, and tested concepts homed in every module.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the workflow**

Run this script with the Workflow tool; it is tool input, not a repo file.
Its `args` are `{ "worktree": "<absolute path of $SCRATCH/p4/wt-incidents>", "targets": <the JSON array in .results/incident-targets.json> }`.
It runs these phases:
- `Write`: 12 incident writers in parallel, each `agents/question-writer.md` in Incident mode with its own `--incident-targets` entry, writing only its four `content/incidents/inc-NNN.yaml` files and looping on `--draft` until `0 problems`.
- `Prepare`, `Check`, `Stamp`, `Revise`: up to 3 rounds (seeds 401, 402 and 403) of `content:checks prepare --pool incident`, checker agents grouped by kind at up to 8 batches each, `grade` and `content:stamp`, then one writer per incident file to revise its failed draft steps; no revise starts after Wednesday, October 28.
- `Drain`: one last round (seed 411) that checks, stamps and revises the open drafts and writes nothing new.
- `Commit`: one commit per module in module order, `Add the daily incidents for Module N` with the body `<s> of 4 incidents ship; <l> live steps, <d> drafts.`, each staging that module's incident files by path through the pre-commit hook; a last agent reports the counts.

```js
export const meta = {
  name: 'phase4-incidents',
  description: 'Phase 4 daily incidents: one incident writer per module, check rounds with --pool incident, drain, one commit per module',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Write', detail: 'one incident writer per module, Modules 2 to 13' },
    { title: 'Prepare' },
    { title: 'Check', detail: 'checkers grouped by kind, up to 8 batches each' },
    { title: 'Stamp' },
    { title: 'Revise', detail: 'one incident writer per incident file' },
    { title: 'Drain', detail: 'one last round that writes nothing new' },
    { title: 'Commit', detail: 'one commit per module, in module order' },
  ],
}

const WT = args.worktree
const TARGETS = args.targets ?? []
const NODE = 'export PATH="/Users/danielchahine/.local/share/fnm/node-versions/v24.21.0/installation/bin:$PATH";'
const LAST_REWRITE_DAY = '2026-10-28'
const ROUND_SEEDS = [401, 402, 403]
const DRAIN_SEED = 411
const INCIDENT_MODULES = '02,03,04,05,06,07,08,09,10,11,12,13'
const pad2 = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
const HOOKS = `Start every shell command with: cd "${WT}" && ${NODE} Work only in this worktree, never in any other checkout. Stage files by path, never with git add -A or git add .; when git reports an index.lock, wait a few seconds and retry. Never use --no-verify. Never use the em dash. Never add a Co-Authored-By line. Never commit unless your task says so. Never quote an exam item's stem, options, whys, takeaway, evidence or a failure reason in your reply, a log line or a commit message: name exam items by id and count only.`
const NEVER_READ = 'Never read the course source text, ~/Library/Application Support/ccp-study/, content/fingerprints/, AWS exam questions, anything under content/exam/, or .results/checks/keys.json.'
const COUNT_STATUS = "grep -hE '^ +status: (live|draft|retired)$' content/incidents/*.yaml | sort | uniq -c"

if (!WT) return { stopped: 'args.worktree is missing' }
if (TARGETS.length !== 12) return { stopped: 'args.targets must hold the 12 module entries of --incident-targets' }

const INCW = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    module: { type: 'integer' },
    files: { type: 'integer' },
    steps: { type: 'integer' },
    problems: { type: 'integer' },
  },
  required: ['module', 'files', 'steps', 'problems'],
}
const PREP = { type: 'object', properties: { batches: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['batches'] }
const STAMP = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    today: { type: 'string' },
    live: { type: 'integer' },
    draft: { type: 'integer' },
    retired: { type: 'integer' },
    revise: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
    summary: { type: 'string' },
  },
  required: ['today', 'live', 'draft', 'retired', 'revise', 'summary'],
}
const COMMIT = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { module: { type: 'integer' }, commit: { type: 'string' }, body: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['module', 'commit', 'body'],
}
const FINAL = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { counts: { type: 'string' }, incidents: { type: 'string' }, needsDani: { type: 'integer' }, validate: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['counts', 'incidents', 'needsDani', 'validate'],
}

/** One incident writer for one module entry of --incident-targets: writes its four incident files and loops on --draft. */
function writer(entry) {
  const files = entry.ids.map((id) => `content/incidents/${id}.yaml`)
  return agent(`You are the incident writer for Module ${entry.module} of the CCP study site in the git worktree ${WT}.
Read agents/question-writer.md and content/STYLE.md in full, then follow the prompt's "Incident mode" section exactly.
Your entry of \`pnpm --silent content:report --incident-targets\` is:
${JSON.stringify(entry)}
Write exactly these four files and no other: ${files.join(', ')}.
modules[0] of each is ${entry.module}; any other entry comes from earlier (${entry.earlier.join(', ')}), only when a step uses a concept homed there.
Every step is a draft practice item (status: draft, version: 1, pool: practice, history: []) with an id from \`pnpm --silent content:new-question ${entry.module}\`, or the earlier module's number for a step homed there.
You may read the live practice stems under content/questions/ of the modules you use, so no step repeats one; never edit them.
Loop on \`pnpm --silent content:validate --draft <file>\` for each of your files until its last line ends with \`0 problems\`. ${NEVER_READ}
${HOOKS}
Return the module, the number of files you wrote, the number of steps over them, and the summed problem count of your last --draft runs.`, { label: `write:m${pad2(entry.module)}`, phase: 'Write', schema: INCW })
}

/** One check round over every incident step: prepare, grouped checkers, grade and stamp. */
async function checkRound(seed, tag) {
  phase('Prepare')
  const prep = await agent(`${HOOKS}
Run exactly: pnpm --silent content:checks prepare --pool incident --modules ${INCIDENT_MODULES} --seed ${seed}
Return the batch paths it printed (the lines ending in .json), relative to the worktree root. Do not edit any file. If it fails, return an empty list.`, { label: `prepare:${tag}`, phase: 'Prepare', schema: PREP, effort: 'low' })
  const batches = prep?.batches ?? []
  log(`${tag}: ${batches.length} batches`)
  if (batches.length === 0) return null
  const kinds = {}
  for (const b of batches) {
    const k = b.split('/').pop().replace(/\.json$/, '').replace(/-\d+$/, '')
    ;(kinds[k] ??= []).push(b)
  }
  const groups = []
  for (const [kind, bs] of Object.entries(kinds)) for (let i = 0; i < bs.length; i += 8) groups.push({ kind, bs: bs.slice(i, i + 8) })
  log(`${tag}: ${groups.length} checker agents`)
  phase('Check')
  await parallel(groups.map((g, gi) => () => {
    const file = g.kind.startsWith('critic') ? 'agents/critic.md' : `agents/${g.kind}.md`
    return agent(`You are a fresh checker for the CCP study site in the git worktree ${WT}.
Read ${file} first and follow it exactly; your kind is ${g.kind}${g.kind.startsWith('critic') ? ' (critic-cue-NN is the cue round, critic-NN the full round)' : ''}. A cue answer carries basis (tell, knowledge or guess) and, unless the basis is guess, a one-line reason.
Your batches, each judged on its own as the prompt says:
${g.bs.join('\n')}
For each batch <name>.json write .results/checks/answers/<name>.json and loop on \`pnpm --silent content:checks lint .results/checks/answers/<name>.json\` until it prints OK.
Read only what the prompt file allows. Never open .results/checks/keys.json, content/exam/, content/questions/, content/incidents/, the notes or the course. Edit only your answer files.
${HOOKS}
Return one line per batch: OK and the answer count, or the last lint error.`, { label: `check:${tag}:${g.kind}#${gi}`, phase: 'Check' })
  }))
  phase('Stamp')
  const st = await agent(`${HOOKS}
Run \`date +%F\` and return its output as today.
Run \`pnpm --silent content:checks grade\`, then \`pnpm --silent content:stamp\`. Do not edit files and do not commit.
Then count the incident steps by status with \`${COUNT_STATUS}\`.
Return live, draft and retired from those counts, revise = the paths of the stamp's \`revise <path>\` lines that start with content/incidents/, and summary = the grade summary line and the stamp summary line, joined by "; ".`, { label: `stamp:${tag}`, phase: 'Stamp', schema: STAMP, effort: 'low' })
  log(`${tag}: ${st?.live} live, ${st?.draft} draft, ${st?.retired} retired steps, ${st?.revise?.length ?? 0} files to revise`)
  return st
}

/** Revises the failed drafts of each listed incident file, one writer per file. */
function revise(files, tag) {
  return parallel(files.map((f) => () => agent(`You are the incident writer for the CCP study site in the git worktree ${WT}.
Read agents/question-writer.md and content/STYLE.md in full, then revise the failed steps in \`${f}\` as "Revising after the checks" and "Incident mode" say.
Each failed step's review.lastFailure names the failed check and its reason; revise only draft steps whose review.attempts is below 3, keep every id, concept and task, and never touch a live step or a draft step with 3 attempts.
Fix the real cause: a solve-closed failure needs a change to the stem, the option texts or the key; a cue failure with basis tell needs the tell removed; with basis knowledge the distractors become true statements about AWS that do not answer the stem; a critic failure needs what it names fixed.
Keep the story whole: when a fix changes what a step asks, check that the brief and the other steps still lead into it, and give away no later step's answer.
Loop on \`pnpm --silent content:validate --draft ${f}\` until it ends with \`0 problems\`. Edit only this file. ${NEVER_READ}
${HOOKS}
Return at most 3 lines: the number of steps you revised and the problem count of your last --draft run.`, { label: `revise:${tag}:${f.split('/').pop()}`, phase: 'Revise' })))
}

/** Rounds with the given seeds, revising after each round, but never starting a revise after LAST_REWRITE_DAY. */
async function rounds(seeds, tag) {
  const out = []
  for (const [i, seed] of seeds.entries()) {
    const st = await checkRound(seed, `${tag}${i + 1}`)
    if (!st) break
    out.push({ tag: `${tag}${i + 1}`, seed, live: st.live, draft: st.draft, retired: st.retired, summary: st.summary })
    if (st.revise.length === 0) break
    if (st.today > LAST_REWRITE_DAY) {
      log(`${tag}${i + 1}: today is ${st.today}, after ${LAST_REWRITE_DAY}; no rewrite starts`)
      break
    }
    phase('Revise')
    await revise(st.revise, `${tag}${i + 1}`)
  }
  return out
}

phase('Write')
const written = await parallel(TARGETS.map((e) => () => writer(e)))
const failed = written.filter((w) => !w || w.problems > 0).length
log(`write: ${written.filter(Boolean).reduce((n, w) => n + w.steps, 0)} steps in ${written.filter(Boolean).reduce((n, w) => n + w.files, 0)} files; ${failed} modules with problems`)

const done = await rounds(ROUND_SEEDS, 'r')

phase('Drain')
const drain = await checkRound(DRAIN_SEED, 'drain')
if (drain) {
  done.push({ tag: 'drain', seed: DRAIN_SEED, live: drain.live, draft: drain.draft, retired: drain.retired, summary: drain.summary })
  if (drain.revise.length > 0 && drain.today <= LAST_REWRITE_DAY) await revise(drain.revise, 'drain')
}

phase('Commit')
const commits = []
for (const e of TARGETS) {
  const m = e.module
  const files = e.ids.map((id) => `content/incidents/${id}.yaml`)
  const c = await agent(`${HOOKS}
Work on branch content/phase-4-incidents. Your module's files are ${files.join(' ')}; skip any that does not exist.
When \`git status --short ${files.join(' ')}\` prints nothing, make no commit and return commit "none".
Run \`pnpm --silent content:validate\`; if it reports an R2 duplicate id in a draft step of one of your files, give that step a fresh id from \`pnpm --silent content:new-question ${m}\` and run it again.
For each of your files, count its live and draft steps with \`grep -cE '^    status: live$' <file>\` and \`grep -cE '^    status: draft$' <file>\`; a file ships when it has no draft or retired step (\`grep -cE '^    status: (draft|retired)$' <file>\` prints 0) and no top-level \`status: retired\` line.
Stage your existing files by path (\`git add <file>\` for each), then commit with two -m flags: the subject "Add the daily incidents for Module ${m}" and the body "<s> of 4 incidents ship; <l> live steps, <d> drafts." with s the shipping files, l the live steps and d the draft steps over your files.
The pre-commit hook runs pnpm check; when it fails, fix the cause in your files only, as the question writer's Incident mode allows, and commit again.
Return the module number, the commit hash (or "none") and the body you used.`, { label: `commit:m${pad2(m)}`, phase: 'Commit', schema: COMMIT, effort: 'low' })
  commits.push(c)
}

const final = await agent(`${HOOKS}
Do not edit or commit anything.
Run \`${COUNT_STATUS}\` and return its counts on one line as counts.
Run \`pnpm --silent content:report\` and return its line that starts with \`Incidents:\` as incidents.
Run \`pnpm --silent content:report --needs-dani\` and return the number of its lines that name an incident step id (a qid found in content/incidents/), or 0 when it prints none, as needsDani.
Run \`pnpm --silent content:validate\` and return its last line as validate.`, { label: 'final', phase: 'Commit', schema: FINAL, effort: 'low' })

return { rounds: done, commits, counts: final?.counts, incidents: final?.incidents, needsDani: final?.needsDani, validate: final?.validate }
```

Expected return: `rounds` with one entry per round (`r1` to `r3` and `drain`), each with live, draft and retired step counts; 12 commits (or `none` for a module whose writer wrote nothing); `incidents` reading `Incidents: <s> shipped of <n>; steps <l> live, <d> draft` with `s` close to 48; `needsDani` counting the steps left for Dani; and `validate` reading `content:validate: 13 rules, 0 findings`.

```bash
git log --oneline master..HEAD
git status --short
pnpm --silent content:validate
pnpm --silent content:report | grep '^Incidents:'
pnpm --silent content:report --needs-dani
```

For a module whose commit agent stopped, commit by hand: count with `grep -cE '^    status: (live|draft)$' <file>`, stage that module's incident files by path, and commit with the subject and body of Step 3.

- [ ] **Step 5: Merge the incident branch on `master`**

```bash

node -e "const fs = require('node:fs'); const read = (p) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8')); const main = '.results/checks/passes.json'; fs.writeFileSync(main, JSON.stringify({ ...read(main), ...read(process.argv[1]) }, null, 2) + '\n')" "$SCRATCH/p4/wt-incidents/.results/checks/passes.json"

```

Here `<s>` is the shipped count of the `Incidents:` line in Step 4.
The `node -e` line merges the worktree's passes into the main `passes.json` instead of overwriting it, so a pass recorded on `master` after Step 1 is kept.
Expected: the merge passes the pre-commit hook; `content:validate: 13 rules, 0 findings`; the same `Incidents:` line as Step 4.
The worktree stays until Task 20, which removes it with the other content worktrees.

## Task 16: Incidents in the engine: the pick, the XP and the extra

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/incident.ts`, `tests/unit/incident.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/engine/replay.ts`, `src/engine/memory.ts`, `src/engine/next.ts`, `src/app/memory.ts` (one call), `src/app/home.ts` (one line), `tests/fixtures/memory.ts`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts`, `tests/property/arbitraries.ts`
- Regenerate: `tests/fixtures/backups/v1.snapshot.json`, `v1-exam.snapshot.json`, `v1-memory.snapshot.json`, `v1-labs.snapshot.json`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/hands-on-week.snapshot.json`
- Test: `tests/unit/incident.test.ts`, `memory.test.ts`, `next.test.ts`, `replay.test.ts`, `streak.test.ts`, `journeys.test.ts`, `backups.test.ts`, `tests/property`

Design notes:
- `derive` gains two content-free maps: `incidentsOffered` (study day to the first incident offered that day) and `incidentsDone` (incident to the day of its first `incident.done`); both check ids against `ContentRefs.incidents`, which Task 5 added, and an unknown id is kept, applied and counted in `unknownEvents`.
- `ENGINE_VERSION` stays 5; its doc line gains `, pays incident XP`.
- `pickIncident` in `src/engine/incident.ts` is the whole pick of spec 4.5: nothing before every Module 2 lesson is read, never a solved incident, only incidents whose every required module is read, the largest sum of `100 - mastery`, ties sorted by id and broken by `fnv1a(day) % ties.length`; a missed day changes nothing, so there is never a backlog.
- Only the lazy `src/app/incident-offer.ts` (Task 17) imports `src/engine/incident.ts`; Home and the extras line read the recorded offer through `incidentToday` in `src/engine/next.ts`, which is already in the initial bundle.
- `deriveMemory` pays `incident.done` once per study day, 15 XP when `firstTry === true` and 8 otherwise, and every `incident.done` sets `completion`, so the day qualifies; `incident.offered` pays and qualifies nothing; step answers already count in mode `incident` (Phase 2's `COUNTING_MODES`).
- `extras` gains `incident: string | null` and pushes it first; Task 8's exhaustive `extraLink` switch needs the new kind handled, so this task adds a first line to `extraLink` that Task 17 replaces with the titled label; the app passes `incident: null` until Task 17 reads the offer.
- `Journey.incident(at, id, steps, firstTry)` writes the offer, the incident session (the last step wrong and not Sure unless `firstTry`) and the `incident.done`; `handsOnWeek()` gains one incident day.
- Every diff below is against the tree after Task 5 (and Task 8 where it already landed); apply each hunk by its `+` and `-` lines where a context line differs.
- Covers P4-059, P4-061, P4-063, P4-065, P4-066, P4-074, P4-075, P4-077, P4-078, P4-090, P4-092, P4-093, P4-094, P4-095, P4-096, P4-099, P4-101, P4-102, P4-129 and P4-131.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```diff
 export function labDoneOf(lab: string, hints = 0, rev = 1): NewEvent {
   return { t: 'lab.done', lab, rev, hints };
 }
+
+/** Today's incident offer, as Home records it on its first render of a study day. */
+export function incidentOfferedOf(incident: string): NewEvent {
+  return { t: 'incident.offered', incident };
+}
+
+/** A solved incident; `firstTry` is true when every step was answered right. */
+export function incidentDoneOf(incident: string, firstTry?: boolean): NewEvent {
+  return { t: 'incident.done', incident, ...(firstTry === undefined ? {} : { firstTry }) };
+}
```

Create `tests/unit/incident.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { INCIDENT_UNLOCK_MODULE, type IncidentRef, pickIncident } from '../../src/engine/incident.ts';
import { fnv1a } from '../../src/engine/rng.ts';

/** Made-up incidents: two on Module 2, one on Modules 3 and 2, one on Module 4. */
const INCIDENTS: readonly IncidentRef[] = [
  { id: 'inc-001', modules: [2] },
  { id: 'inc-002', modules: [2] },
  { id: 'inc-005', modules: [3, 2] },
  { id: 'inc-009', modules: [4] },
];

const readUpTo =
  (last: number) =>
  (module: number): boolean =>
    module <= last;

const pick = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof pickIncident>[0]> = {}) =>
  pickIncident({
    incidents: INCIDENTS,
    solved: new Set(),
    lessonsRead: readUpTo(4),
    mastery: new Map([
      [2, 90],
      [3, 90],
      [4, 90],
    ]),
    day: '2026-10-20',
    ...over,
  });

/** Two days whose `fnv1a` parities differ, so a two-way tie picks a different incident on each. */
const DAY1 = '2026-10-20';
const DAY2 = '2026-10-21';

describe('pickIncident (spec 4.5)', () => {
  it('unlocks with Module 2', () => {
    expect(INCIDENT_UNLOCK_MODULE).toBe(2);
    expect(pick({ lessonsRead: readUpTo(1) })).toBeNull();
    expect(pick({ lessonsRead: (m) => m === 4 })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('skips an incident whose required modules are not all read', () => {
    const picked = pick({ lessonsRead: readUpTo(2), mastery: new Map([[2, 0]]) });
    expect(['inc-001', 'inc-002']).toContain(picked);
  });

  it('picks the incident over the weakest modules, summing 100 minus mastery over its modules', () => {
    expect(pick()).toBe('inc-005');
    expect(
      pick({
        mastery: new Map([
          [2, 90],
          [3, 90],
          [4, 0],
        ]),
      }),
    ).toBe('inc-009');
    expect(pick({ mastery: new Map() })).toBe('inc-005');
  });

  it('breaks a tie with fnv1a of the day, so the pick holds all day and changes with the day', () => {
    expect(fnv1a(DAY1) % 2).not.toBe(fnv1a(DAY2) % 2);
    const tied = { incidents: INCIDENTS.slice(0, 2), mastery: new Map([[2, 50]]) };
    const first = pick({ ...tied, day: DAY1 });
    expect(pick({ ...tied, day: DAY1 })).toBe(first);
    expect(first).toBe(['inc-001', 'inc-002'][fnv1a(DAY1) % 2]);
    expect(pick({ ...tied, day: DAY2 })).toBe(['inc-001', 'inc-002'][fnv1a(DAY2) % 2]);
    expect(pick({ ...tied, day: DAY2 })).not.toBe(first);
  });

  it('sorts tied ids before the tie-break, whatever the input order', () => {
    const forward = pick({ incidents: INCIDENTS.slice(0, 2), day: DAY1 });
    const backward = pick({ incidents: [...INCIDENTS.slice(0, 2)].reverse(), day: DAY1 });
    expect(backward).toBe(forward);
  });

  it('never returns a solved incident, and returns null once every eligible one is solved', () => {
    expect(pick({ solved: new Set(['inc-005']) })).not.toBe('inc-005');
    expect(pick({ solved: new Set(INCIDENTS.map((i) => i.id)) })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('leaves no backlog: a missed day changes nothing, so the next day offers one incident', () => {
    const tied = { incidents: INCIDENTS.slice(0, 2), mastery: new Map([[2, 50]]) };
    expect(typeof pick({ ...tied, day: DAY2 })).toBe('string');
    expect(pick({ ...tied, day: '2026-10-25' })).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the pick**

Create `src/engine/incident.ts`:

```ts
import { fnv1a } from './rng.ts';

/**
 * The daily incident pick (spec 4.5). Only the lazy `src/app/incident-offer.ts` imports this file, so it stays out
 * of the initial bundle; Home and the extras line read the recorded offer through `incidentToday` instead.
 */

/** Nothing is offered until every lesson of this module is read (spec 4.1). */
export const INCIDENT_UNLOCK_MODULE = 2;

/** A shipped incident: its id and its required modules, the first of which is its home module. */
export interface IncidentRef {
  id: string;
  modules: readonly number[];
}

/**
 * Today's incident: among the unsolved incidents whose required modules are all read, the one with the largest
 * sum of `100 - mastery` over its modules; ties sort by id and `fnv1a(day)` picks one, so the pick holds all day.
 * A day without a pick leaves no backlog, because nothing here counts missed days.
 */
export function pickIncident(args: {
  incidents: readonly IncidentRef[];
  solved: ReadonlySet<string>;
  lessonsRead: (module: number) => boolean;
  mastery: ReadonlyMap<number, number>;
  day: string;
}): string | null {
  if (!args.lessonsRead(INCIDENT_UNLOCK_MODULE)) return null;
  let best = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
  let ties: string[] = [];
  for (const incident of args.incidents) {
    if (args.solved.has(incident.id)) continue;
    if (!incident.modules.every((m) => args.lessonsRead(m))) continue;
    const score = incident.modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + 100 - (args.mastery.get(m) ?? 0), 0);
    if (score > best) {
      best = score;
      ties = [incident.id];
    } else if (score === best) {
      ties.push(incident.id);
    }
  }
  if (ties.length === 0) return null;
  ties.sort();
  return ties[fnv1a(args.day) % ties.length] ?? null;
}
```

## Task 17: The incident on Home and in the runner

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/incident.ts`, `src/app/incident-offer.ts`, `src/app/screens/Incident.tsx`, `tests/unit/incident-app.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/incident.spec.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/router.ts`, `src/app/nav.ts`, `src/app/progress.ts`, `src/app/memory.ts`, `src/app/home.ts`, `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Runner.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Home.tsx`, `src/ui/base.css` (under `/* Incident */`)
- Test: `tests/unit/incident-app.test.ts`, `router.test.ts`, `nav.test.ts`, `home.test.ts`, `runner.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/incident.spec.ts`

Design notes:
- The route is `{ kind: 'incident' }` at `#/s/incident`, and Escape goes Home; nothing under it parses, so `#/s/incident/inc-001` is not found.
- `src/app/incident.ts` holds the copy, `firstTryOf` and `modulesRead`; `home.ts` imports `incidentLabel` from it, so it is in the initial bundle and stays small.
- `src/app/incident-offer.ts` is loaded only through `import()`, from Home's effect and from the incident screen; it records `incident.offered` when the store is ready, today has no offer and `pickIncident` returns an id, and a module-level promise keeps two calls from offering twice.
- `incidentNow` in `src/app/memory.ts` is today's offer and its done state from `incidentToday`; `extraItems` passes the offer to `extras` while it is unsolved, and the incident screen reads the same signal, so neither imports the engine's day or pick code.
- The incident screen is its own lazy `Incident.tsx`: it loads the chunk of the incident's home module, resumes the open incident session or records a start with `mode: 'incident'`, `scope` = the incident id and `qids` = its steps in file order, and renders the exported Runner `Session` under `<h1>{title}</h1>` and the brief.
- The Runner's `Session` ends an incident with one `recordAll` of the submit and `{ t: 'incident.done', incident, firstTry }`, so a reload never keeps one without the other; its end line shows `incidentPaidLine(xp)` with the XP that event earned, the badge line and `Back to Home`.
- A solve keeps its end line on screen until Dani leaves; a later visit the same day shows `Solved. The next incident comes tomorrow.`.
- The incident is not resumable from Next (Phase 2's `RESUMABLE` leaves `incident` out), and the Runner's routed `Props` exclude `incident` as they exclude `debrief`.
- The end-to-end test serves two made-up incidents of equal score on Module 2 through `page.route`, built from Module 2's own live single practice items, and strips every other incident from the index and the chunks, so shipped incidents never compete; it clears its routes after each test, so a chunk request in flight at the end never fails it.
- The run test checks the bet hint `Press Sure when you are more than 71% sure.` on each step and bets Sure once with its button and once with `Shift+Enter`.
- The test clicks Next because, in the prototype on chromium, `Enter` for Next on the last step after a `Shift+Enter` bet never reached the end line (4 of 4 runs; WebKit passed).
- That may be a Runner key bug, so the builder reproduces it in `tests/e2e/practice.spec.ts` on the last step of a check run; if it reproduces there, it is a Phase 2 bug that gets its own `tests/regressions/` test and fix before this task commits, and the incident test then presses `Enter` for Next.
- Bundle: with Task 7's `vite.config.ts` grouping (every module the entry imports statically in one initial chunk) the prototype's initial JS went from 48.5 KB to 49.0 KB in 1 script with this task, so it adds about 0.5 KB; without that grouping the first lazy screen that imports the Runner's `Session` splits `progress`, `memory` and two small shared chunks out of the entry and the same tree measures 51.3 KB in 6 scripts, so this task must land after Task 7; the report states the measured size and headroom, and stops rather than raising the budget if the line passes 50.0 KB.
- Every diff below is against the tree after Tasks 7, 8, 14 and 16; apply each hunk by its `+` and `-` lines where a context line differs.
- Covers P4-064, P4-090, P4-091, P4-095, P4-097, P4-098, P4-104, P4-113, P4-118, P4-121, P4-125 and P4-126.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing unit tests**

Create `tests/unit/incident-app.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  firstTryOf,
  INCIDENT_LOCKED,
  INCIDENT_NONE,
  INCIDENT_SOLVED,
  incidentLabel,
  incidentPaidLine,
  modulesRead,
} from '../../src/app/incident.ts';
import type { AnswerRecord, SessionState } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { fnv1a } from '../../src/engine/rng.ts';

/** The days `tests/e2e/incident.spec.ts` runs on, which must pick different incidents of a two-way tie. */
const DAY1 = '2026-10-20';
const DAY2 = '2026-10-21';

const answer = (qid: string, correct: boolean): AnswerRecord => ({
  id: `01JA0000000000000000000${qid.slice(-3).toUpperCase()}`,
  qid,
  qv: 1,
  sid: '01JA00000000000000000000S1',
  mode: 'incident',
  at: '2026-10-20T10:00:00.000Z',
  day: DAY1,
  correct,
  sure: true,
  picked: ['a'],
  concept: 'proof-box',
  relearn: false,
  flagged: false,
  changes: [],
});

const session = (answers: readonly AnswerRecord[], qids = ['q-aaa', 'q-bbb']): SessionState => ({
  sid: '01JA00000000000000000000S1',
  mode: 'incident',
  scope: 'inc-001',
  seed: 1,
  qids,
  answers: new Map(answers.map((a) => [a.qid, a])),
  startedAt: '2026-10-20T09:59:00.000Z',
  submittedAt: null,
  abandoned: false,
  form: null,
  deadline: null,
  submitId: null,
});

describe('incident copy (spec 4.5, 7.2)', () => {
  it('words every line exactly', () => {
    expect(INCIDENT_LOCKED).toBe('Starts after Module 2.');
    expect(INCIDENT_NONE).toBe('No incident today.');
    expect(INCIDENT_SOLVED).toBe('Solved. The next incident comes tomorrow.');
    expect(incidentLabel('The cold ovens')).toBe('Incident "The cold ovens"');
    expect(incidentPaidLine(15)).toBe('Incident solved. Plus 15 XP.');
    expect(incidentPaidLine(8)).toBe('Incident solved. Plus 8 XP.');
    expect(incidentPaidLine(0)).toBe('Incident solved.');
  });
});

describe('firstTryOf', () => {
  it('is true only when every step has a correct answer', () => {
    expect(firstTryOf(session([answer('q-aaa', true), answer('q-bbb', true)]))).toBe(true);
    expect(firstTryOf(session([answer('q-aaa', true), answer('q-bbb', false)]))).toBe(false);
    expect(firstTryOf(session([answer('q-aaa', true)]))).toBe(false);
    expect(firstTryOf(session([], []))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('modulesRead', () => {
  it('needs every lesson of the module in lessonsDone, and is false for an unknown module', () => {
    const modules = [{ n: 2, lessons: [{ slug: 'a' }, { slug: 'b' }] }];
    expect(modulesRead(modules, new Map([['m02/a', 't']]))(2)).toBe(false);
    expect(
      modulesRead(
        modules,
        new Map([
          ['m02/a', 't'],
          ['m02/b', 't'],
        ]),
      )(2),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(modulesRead(modules, new Map())(9)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('the end-to-end days', () => {
  it('give different fnv1a parities, so a two-way tie changes between them', () => {
    expect(fnv1a(DAY1) % 2).not.toBe(fnv1a(DAY2) % 2);
  });
});
```

Create `src/app/incident.ts`:

```ts
/** The daily incident's copy and pure helpers (spec 4.5); small, because `home.ts` puts it in the initial bundle. */
import type { SessionState } from '../engine/replay.ts';
import { pad2 } from './router.ts';

export const INCIDENT_LOCKED = 'Starts after Module 2.';
export const INCIDENT_NONE = 'No incident today.';
export const INCIDENT_SOLVED = 'Solved. The next incident comes tomorrow.';

/** The extras line label of today's incident. */
export function incidentLabel(title: string): string {
  return `Incident "${title}"`;
}

/** The end line of a solved incident: the XP its `incident.done` paid, which is 0 for a second solve that day. */
export function incidentPaidLine(xp: number): string {
  return xp > 0 ? `Incident solved. Plus ${xp} XP.` : 'Incident solved.';
}

/** Right on the first try: every step answered, and every first answer correct (spec 3.2). */
export function firstTryOf(session: SessionState): boolean {
  return session.qids.length > 0 && session.qids.every((qid) => session.answers.get(qid)?.correct === true);
}

/** Whether every lesson of a module is read, by the lesson notes in `lessonsDone`. */
export function modulesRead(
  modules: readonly { n: number; lessons: readonly { slug: string }[] }[],
  lessonsDone: ReadonlyMap<string, string>,
): (module: number) => boolean {
  return (module) => {
    const m = modules.find((x) => x.n === module);
    return m?.lessons.every((l) => lessonsDone.has(`m${pad2(m.n)}/${l.slug}`)) === true;
  };
}
```

Create `src/app/incident-offer.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Records today's incident offer (spec 4.5): on the first Home render of a study day with no offer yet, the pick of
 * `src/engine/incident.ts` becomes an `incident.offered` event, so it holds all day. Loaded only through `import()`.
 */
import { studyDay } from '../engine/day.ts';
import { pickIncident } from '../engine/incident.ts';
import { index } from './content.ts';
import { modulesRead } from './incident.ts';
import { mastery } from './memory.ts';
import { progress, record, storeReady } from './progress.ts';

let inFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;

/** Offers today's incident once; a call while one is being recorded waits for it instead of offering twice. */
export function offerIncident(at: Date): Promise<void> {
  if (inFlight === null) inFlight = offer(at).finally(() => (inFlight = null));
  return inFlight;
}

async function offer(at: Date): Promise<void> {
  const idx = index.peek();
  const byModule = mastery.peek();
  if (!storeReady.peek() || idx === null || byModule === null) return;
  const today = studyDay(at);
  const p = progress.peek();
  if (p.incidentsOffered.has(today)) return;
  const incident = pickIncident({
    incidents: idx.incidents,
    solved: new Set(p.incidentsDone.keys()),
    lessonsRead: modulesRead(idx.modules, p.lessonsDone),
    mastery: byModule,
    day: today,
  });
  if (incident !== null) await record({ t: 'incident.offered', incident }, at);
}
```

```ts
/** Today's offered incident and whether it is solved, or null before Home records the day's offer (spec 4.5). */
export const incidentNow = computed(() => incidentToday(progress.value, studyDay(now.value)));

/** The extras line's entries (spec 4.6): today's unsolved incident, a ready boss, a due mock and a lab, at most two. */
export const extraItems = computed(() => {
  const at = now.value;
  const offered = incidentNow.value;
  return extras({
    bosses: bosses.value,
    progress: progress.value,
    today: studyDay(at),
    now: at,
    lab: labExtra.value,
    incident: offered === null || offered.done ? null : offered.incident,
  });
});
```

In `src/app/home.ts`, import `incidentLabel` from `./incident.ts`, add `incidents` to Task 8's `ExtraContent`, and replace Task 16's first line of `extraLink` with the titled label:

```ts
/** What an extra's label needs from the index: module short names, lab titles and incident titles. */
export interface ExtraContent {
  modules: readonly { n: number; short: string }[];
  labs: readonly { id: string; title: string }[];
  incidents: readonly { id: string; title: string }[];
}

/** The label and link of one extra: today's incident, a ready boss, the next unseen mock form, or a core lab. */
export function extraLink(extra: Extra, idx: ExtraContent): { label: string; href: string } {
  if (extra.kind === 'incident') {
    const title = idx.incidents.find((i) => i.id === extra.incident)?.title ?? '';
    return { label: incidentLabel(title), href: toHash({ kind: 'incident' }) };
  }
  switch (extra.kind) {
    case 'boss': {
      const short = idx.modules.find((m) => m.n === extra.module)?.short ?? '';
      return { label: `Boss: ${short}`, href: `#/s/boss/m${pad2(extra.module)}` };
    }
    case 'mock':
      return { label: `Mock ${extra.form}`, href: `#/s/mock/${extra.form.toLowerCase()}` };
    case 'lab': {
      const title = idx.labs.find((l) => l.id === extra.lab)?.title ?? '';
      return { label: `Lab: ${title}`, href: `#/m/${pad2(extra.module)}/lab/${extra.lab}` };
    }
  }
}
```

Create `src/app/screens/Incident.tsx`:

```tsx
/** The daily incident (spec 4.1, 4.5): today's offered incident, run step by step in the Runner's Session. */
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { ContentIndex, IncidentView, QuestionView } from '../../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { INCIDENT_UNLOCK_MODULE } from '../../engine/incident.ts';
import { openSession } from '../../engine/replay.ts';
import { ulid } from '../../store/ulid.ts';
import { Breadcrumbs } from '../../ui/Breadcrumbs.tsx';
import { useTitle } from '../../ui/useTitle.ts';
import { now } from '../clock.ts';
import { ensureModule, modules } from '../content.ts';
import { INCIDENT_LOCKED, INCIDENT_NONE, INCIDENT_SOLVED, modulesRead } from '../incident.ts';
import { offerIncident } from '../incident-offer.ts';
import { incidentNow } from '../memory.ts';
import { newSessionSeed, progress, record, storeReady } from '../progress.ts';
import { Session } from './Runner.tsx';

const CRUMBS = [
  { label: 'Home', href: '#/' },
  { label: 'Incident', href: '#/s/incident' },
];

/** Resumes the open session of this incident, or records the start of a new one with its steps in file order. */
function startOrResume(incident: IncidentView): string {
  const open = openSession(progress.peek(), 'incident', incident.id);
  if (open !== null) return open.sid;
  const sid = ulid();
  void record({
    t: 'session',
    sid,
    mode: 'incident',
    phase: 'start',
    scope: incident.id,
    seed: newSessionSeed(),
    qids: incident.steps.map((s) => s.id),
  });
  return sid;
}

export function Incident({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  const ready = storeReady.value;
  const p = progress.value;
  const today = ready ? incidentNow.value : null;
  const meta = today === null ? undefined : idx.incidents.find((i) => i.id === today.incident);
  const home = meta?.modules[0];
  useTitle(meta?.title ?? 'Incident');
  // A direct visit on a new day offers today's incident as Home would.
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: Biome takes a signal's value for a constant import; it changes, so it is a dependency
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) void offerIncident(now.value);
  }, [ready]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (home !== undefined) ensureModule(home);
  }, [home]);

  const [sid, setSid] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const view =
    home === undefined ? undefined : modules.value.get(home)?.incidents.find((i) => i.id === meta?.id);
  // A solve keeps its end line on screen until Dani leaves; a later visit shows the solved line instead.
  const startable = today !== null && !today.done && sid === null ? view : undefined;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (startable !== undefined) setSid(startOrResume(startable));
  }, [startable]);

  const session = sid === null ? undefined : p.sessions.get(sid);
  if (meta !== undefined && view !== undefined && session !== undefined) {
    const shipped = new Map<string, QuestionView>(view.steps.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
    return (
      <>
        <Breadcrumbs items={CRUMBS} />
        <h1>{meta.title}</h1>
        <p class="lede incident-brief">{view.brief}</p>
        <Session
          idx={idx}
          shipped={shipped}
          pool={[]}
          session={session}
          mode="incident"
          module={null}
          slug={null}
        />
      </>
    );
  }
  const line = !ready
    ? null
    : !modulesRead(idx.modules, p.lessonsDone)(INCIDENT_UNLOCK_MODULE)
      ? INCIDENT_LOCKED
      : today === null
        ? INCIDENT_NONE
        : today.done
          ? INCIDENT_SOLVED
          : null;
  return (
    <>
      <Breadcrumbs items={CRUMBS} />
      <h1>Incident</h1>
      <p class={line === null ? 'muted' : 'incident-empty'}>{line ?? 'Loading'}</p>
    </>
  );
}
```

```diff
       return <NotFound hash={route.hash} />;
   }
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the end-to-end test**

Create `tests/e2e/incident.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { ContentIndex, IncidentView, ModuleChunk, QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import { SURE_HINT } from '../../src/engine/bets.ts';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { fnv1a } from '../../src/engine/rng.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../src/store/kv.ts';
import { timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { HOME_TITLE } from './exam-helpers.ts';
import {
  expectNoAxeViolations,
  expectNoSidewaysScroll,
  failOnPageProblems,
  h1,
  seedEvents,
  seedKv,
} from './helpers.ts';
import { chunkFor, lessonsOf, pickRight, shown } from './questions.ts';

/** Two study days whose `fnv1a` parities differ (tests/unit/incident-app.test.ts pins it), at 10:00 UTC. */
const DAY1 = '2026-10-20';
const DAY2 = '2026-10-21';
const at = (day: string, hour = 10): Date => new Date(`${day}T${String(hour).padStart(2, '0')}:00:00.000Z`);

/** Two made-up incidents of equal score on Module 2, so the day alone decides between them. */
const SYNTHETIC = [
  { id: 'inc-901', title: 'The ovens went cold', modules: [2] },
  { id: 'inc-902', title: 'The till slowed down', modules: [2] },
] as const;
const expectedOn = (day: string) => SYNTHETIC[fnv1a(day) % 2] ?? SYNTHETIC[0];

failOnPageProblems();
test.use({ timezoneId: 'UTC' });

/**
 * Serves the two synthetic incidents in place of any shipped one: the index lists exactly them, every module chunk
 * loses its incidents, and Module 2's chunk carries the two, each with two of its live single practice items.
 */
async function useSyntheticIncidents(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.route('**/content/index.*.json', async (route) => {
    const response = await route.fetch();
    const index = (await response.json()) as ContentIndex;
    await route.fulfill({ response, json: { ...index, incidents: SYNTHETIC } });
  });
  await page.route('**/content/m*.json', async (route) => {
    const response = await route.fetch();
    const chunk = (await response.json()) as ModuleChunk;
    const singles = chunk.questions.filter((q) => q.type === 'single');
    const incidents: IncidentView[] =
      chunk.module === 2
        ? SYNTHETIC.map((s, i) => ({
            ...s,
            modules: [...s.modules],
            brief: `A made-up incident ${i + 1} for the end-to-end test, told over two steps at the cafe.`,
            steps: singles.slice(2 * i, 2 * i + 2),
          }))
        : [];
    await route.fulfill({ response, json: { ...chunk, incidents } });
  });
}

/** Seeds every Module 2 lesson as read the day before `DAY1`, with the engine version stamped. */
async function readModule2(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  const lessons = await lessonsOf(page, 2);
  await seedKv(page, { [KV_KEYS.engineVersion]: ENGINE_VERSION });
  await seedEvents(
    page,
    timelineOf(
      lessons.map((slug, i) => [
        at('2026-10-19', 9 + i).toISOString(),
        { t: 'lesson.done', note: `m02/${slug}` },
      ]),
    ),
  );
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
}

/** Loads Home, which records the day's offer, then moves to the incident screen in the page; returns its heading. */
async function incidentTitle(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    location.hash = '#/s/incident';
  });
  const heading = page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1 });
  await expect(heading).not.toHaveText(HOME_TITLE);
  await expect(heading).not.toHaveText('Incident');
  return (await heading.textContent()) ?? '';
}

test.describe('the daily incident (spec 4.5)', () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    await page.clock.install({ time: at(DAY1) });
    await useSyntheticIncidents(page);
  });

  // A chunk request still in flight when a test ends or the page reloads must not fail the test.
  test.afterEach(async ({ page }) => {
    await page.unrouteAll({ behavior: 'ignoreErrors' });
  });

  test('the incident offered on Home stays the same all day and changes the next day', async ({ page }) => {
    await readModule2(page);
    expect(await incidentTitle(page)).toBe(expectedOn(DAY1).title);
    await page.clock.setSystemTime(at(DAY1, 22));
    expect(await incidentTitle(page)).toBe(expectedOn(DAY1).title);
    await page.clock.setSystemTime(at(DAY2));
    expect(await incidentTitle(page)).toBe(expectedOn(DAY2).title);
    expect(expectedOn(DAY2).title).not.toBe(expectedOn(DAY1).title);
  });

  test('an incident runs step by step with the bet buttons and keys and pays 15 XP when every step is right', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await readModule2(page);
    await incidentTitle(page);
    await expect(page.locator('.incident-brief')).toContainText('A made-up incident');
    const steps: QuestionView[] = (await chunkFor(page, 2)).filter((q) => q.type === 'single').slice(0, 4);
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
      const q = await shown(page, steps);
      await expect(page.locator('.runner-position')).toHaveText(`${i + 1} of 2`);
      await expect(page.locator('.submit-hint')).toHaveText(SURE_HINT);
      await pickRight(page, q);
      // The first step bets Sure with its button, the second with the Runner's Shift+Enter key.
      if (i === 0) await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Sure/ }).click();
      else await page.keyboard.press('Shift+Enter');
      await expect(page.locator('.verdict')).toHaveText(/Correct/);
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Next/ }).click();
    }
    await expect(page.locator('.incident-paid')).toHaveText('Incident solved. Plus 15 XP.');
    await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Back to Home', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page.locator('.incident-empty')).toHaveText('Solved. The next incident comes tomorrow.');
  });

  test('the incident screen has no axe violations and no sideways scroll', async ({ page }) => {
    await readModule2(page);
    await incidentTitle(page);
    await expect(page.locator('section.runner')).toBeVisible();
    await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
    await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
  });

  test('before Module 2 is read the incident says Starts after Module 2.', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/#/s/incident');
    await expect(h1(page, 'Incident')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('.incident-empty')).toHaveText('Starts after Module 2.');
  });
});
```

## Task 18: Lightning in the engine: XP, `review.now` and the selector

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/lightning.ts`, `tests/unit/lightning.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/engine/memory.ts`, `src/engine/replay.ts` (the `ENGINE_VERSION` doc comment only; the number stays 5), `tests/fixtures/memory.ts`, `tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts`, `tests/property/arbitraries.ts`, `tests/property/grind.property.test.ts`, `tests/unit/anti-grind.test.ts`, `tests/unit/memory.test.ts`, `tests/unit/queue.test.ts`, `tests/unit/leakage.test.ts`
- Regenerate: `tests/fixtures/journeys/hands-on-week.jsonl` and `hands-on-week.snapshot.json`
- Test: `tests/unit/lightning.test.ts`, `anti-grind.test.ts`, `memory.test.ts`, `queue.test.ts`, `leakage.test.ts`, `journeys.test.ts`, `tests/property`, `tests/regressions`

Design notes:
- `src/engine/lightning.ts` is pure and small, and only the lazy lightning screen and the lazy settle import it, so it stays out of the initial bundle; `memory.ts` pays lightning XP and applies `review.now` itself, because the replay runs at start.
- The paid round of a study day is the first `session` start of mode `lightning` whose `day` is that day; it is keyed by its `sid`, so a round started at 03:59 keeps paying after 04:00 on its start day's cap, and the next day still gets its own paid round.
- In the paid round only the first answer to each question counts: right, at or before the session's `deadline`, and while the round has paid under `XP.lightningDailyCap` (10), it earns `XP.lightningPerCorrect` (1); a second round the same day pays 0.
- A lightning answer only joins `answered`: no FSRS step, no `lastAnswerDay`, no `countedAnswer`, no `completion`, no calibration window and no misconception mark, so a lightning-only day never qualifies (spec 3.5 and 3.11).
- `Step` becomes the union `AnswerStep | NowStep`: the three answer kinds keep `e`, `start`, `stale` and `made`, while `{ kind: 'now'; tick; e: ReviewNowEvent; before }` carries no session start and no `made` (the brief's `stale` member is dropped, since nothing reads it on a `'now'` step).
- `runStep` and `rebase` branch on `kind` before they read an answer's fields; a `'now'` step sets `card.due` to the earlier of its due and `dayStartAt(e.day, e.tz)`, with no review and no rating, and `rebase` runs it again in its place, so an exam date set later keeps the concept due.
- A `review.now` on an unknown concept, an untested one, one never studied, or one already answered that study day is ignored; the last case is a fix found while building this task: a concept whose one update of the day is spent could never leave the queue that day, so a review would loop on it.
- `selectLightning` keeps `type === 'single'` items with no `form` field whose concept is listed, shuffles the concepts with `mulberry32(seed)`, picks one variant per concept with `pickVariant` from the same stream, and stops at `size` (30).
- `learnPool` drops the `form` field of the taken-form items it lets in, so the round must take `practicePoolOf` (the practice pool) and never `learnPool` or `learnPoolNow`; the leakage test feeds the selector exam items of every form, taken or not, with their form field.
- `missedConcepts` lists the concepts of wrong first answers once each, in answer order, as written in the answer (the replay resolves aliases).
- Prototype measure: on HEAD 56f2c1a this worktree's build printed `initial JS 49.4 KB of 50.0 KB` (not the 48.7 KB the header assumes), and this task's `memory.ts` code adds about 0.3 KB.
- Covers P4-035 (lightning), P4-060, P4-066 (lightning), P4-073, P4-075, P4-077, P4-078, P4-080, P4-081, P4-082, P4-083, P4-085, P4-086, P4-087, P4-128 and P4-136.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the fixture helpers**

In `tests/fixtures/memory.ts`, insert these two functions right before the doc comment that starts `/** A boss or mock start`:

```ts
/** A lightning round start (spec 4.1): scope `lightning`, its questions and its deadline. */
export function lightningStartOf(sid: string, qids: readonly string[], deadline: string): NewEvent {
  return {
    t: 'session',
    sid,
    mode: 'lightning',
    phase: 'start',
    seed: 7,
    scope: 'lightning',
    qids: [...qids],
    deadline,
  };
}

/** The `review.now` a lightning miss writes (spec 3.6). */
export function reviewNowOf(concept: string): NewEvent {
  return { t: 'review.now', concept };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing engine tests**

Create `tests/unit/lightning.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import {
  LIGHTNING_SCOPE,
  LIGHTNING_SECONDS,
  LIGHTNING_SIZE,
  LIGHTNING_STABILITY_DAYS,
  LIGHTNING_UNLOCK,
  lightningDeadline,
  lightningOpen,
  missedConcepts,
  selectLightning,
  settledConcepts,
} from '../../src/engine/lightning.ts';
import { type ConceptMemory, deriveMemory, type MemoryState } from '../../src/engine/memory.ts';
import { derive, type SessionState } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import type { ExamPoolQuestion, PoolQuestion } from '../../src/engine/select.ts';
import { review } from '../../src/engine/srs.ts';
import { SID, SID2, timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, at, contentOf, lightningStartOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';

/** Made-up bakery concepts: 25 in 3 modules. */
const IDS = Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => `bake-${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`);
const CONTENT = contentOf(IDS.map((id, i) => ({ id, module: (i % 3) + 1 })));

/** Memory where each listed concept has a card of the given stability, and the rest have none. */
function memoryWith(stability: Readonly<Record<string, number>>): MemoryState {
  const base = review(null, 'good', new Date(at('2026-10-01')));
  const concepts = new Map<string, ConceptMemory>(
    Object.entries(stability).map(([id, s]) => [
      id,
      {
        card: { ...base, stability: s },
        enteredOn: '2026-10-01',
        lastAnswerDay: '2026-10-01',
        fsrsDay: '2026-10-01',
        fsrsRating: 'good',
        extraAgainDay: null,
        successDays: ['2026-10-01'],
        misconception: null,
      },
    ]),
  );
  return { ...deriveMemory([], CONTENT), concepts };
}

const settled = (n: number): Record<string, number> =>
  Object.fromEntries(IDS.slice(0, n).map((id) => [id, LIGHTNING_STABILITY_DAYS]));

/** Two single variants and one multi per concept, plus one single exam item of Form A on each concept. */
const POOL: PoolQuestion[] = IDS.flatMap((concept, i) => [
  { id: `q-${concept}-1`, concept, note: 'm01/a', type: 'single' as const, module: (i % 3) + 1 },
  { id: `q-${concept}-2`, concept, note: 'm01/a', type: 'single' as const, module: (i % 3) + 1 },
  { id: `q-${concept}-3`, concept, note: 'm01/a', type: 'multi' as const, module: (i % 3) + 1 },
]);
const EXAM: ExamPoolQuestion[] = IDS.map((concept, i) => ({
  id: `xa-${concept}`,
  concept,
  note: 'm01/a',
  type: 'single',
  module: (i % 3) + 1,
  form: 'A',
}));

type Log = readonly (readonly [string, NewEvent])[];
const lightning = (
  concept: string,
  correct: boolean,
  when: string,
  sid = SID,
): readonly [string, NewEvent] => [
  when,
  answerOf({ concept, qid: `q-${concept}-1`, correct, sure: false, mode: 'lightning', sid }),
];

describe('lightning constants (spec 4.1)', () => {
  it('runs 90 seconds, opens at 20 settled concepts of stability 3 days or more, draws at most 30', () => {
    expect([LIGHTNING_SECONDS, LIGHTNING_UNLOCK, LIGHTNING_STABILITY_DAYS, LIGHTNING_SIZE]).toEqual([
      90, 20, 3, 30,
    ]);
    expect(LIGHTNING_SCOPE).toBe('lightning');
  });
});

describe('settledConcepts and lightningOpen', () => {
  it('lists the concepts whose card has a stability of at least 3 days, sorted', () => {
    const memory = memoryWith({ 'bake-03': 3, 'bake-01': 12.5, 'bake-02': 2.99 });
    expect(settledConcepts(memory)).toEqual(['bake-01', 'bake-03']);
  });

  it('opens at 20 settled concepts and not at 19', () => {
    expect(lightningOpen(memoryWith(settled(19)))).toBe(false);
    expect(lightningOpen(memoryWith(settled(20)))).toBe(true);
    expect(lightningOpen(deriveMemory([], CONTENT))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('lightningDeadline', () => {
  it('is 90 seconds after the start', () => {
    expect(lightningDeadline(new Date('2026-10-12T09:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-10-12T09:01:30.000Z');
  });
});

describe('selectLightning', () => {
  const progress = derive([], null);

  it('draws one single practice item per listed concept and nothing else', () => {
    const concepts = IDS.slice(0, 12);
    const qids = selectLightning({ pool: POOL, concepts, progress, seed: 4 });
    expect(qids).toHaveLength(12);
    const picked = qids.map((id) => POOL.find((q) => q.id === id));
    expect(picked.every((q) => q?.type === 'single')).toBe(true);
    expect(new Set(picked.map((q) => q?.concept))).toEqual(new Set(concepts));
  });

  it('orders by the seed: the same seed gives the same round, another seed another order', () => {
    const args = { pool: POOL, concepts: IDS, progress };
    expect(selectLightning({ ...args, seed: 9 })).toEqual(selectLightning({ ...args, seed: 9 }));
    expect(selectLightning({ ...args, seed: 9 })).not.toEqual(selectLightning({ ...args, seed: 10 }));
  });

  it('stops at the size, 30 by default', () => {
    const many = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => `c-${i}`);
    const pool = many.map((concept) => ({
      id: `q-${concept}`,
      concept,
      note: 'm01/a',
      type: 'single' as const,
      module: 1,
    }));
    expect(selectLightning({ pool, concepts: many, progress, seed: 1 })).toHaveLength(LIGHTNING_SIZE);
    expect(selectLightning({ pool, concepts: many, progress, seed: 1, size: 5 })).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  it('prefers the variant never answered', () => {
    const answered = derive(timelineOf([lightning('bake-01', true, at('2026-10-10'))]), null);
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 10; seed++) {
      expect(selectLightning({ pool: POOL, concepts: ['bake-01'], progress: answered, seed })).toEqual([
        'q-bake-01-2',
      ]);
    }
  });

  it('never returns an item with a form, even a single one on a listed concept', () => {
    for (let seed = 0; seed < 25; seed++) {
      const qids = selectLightning({ pool: [...EXAM, ...POOL], concepts: IDS, progress, seed });
      expect(qids.filter((id) => id.startsWith('xa-'))).toEqual([]);
    }
    expect(selectLightning({ pool: EXAM, concepts: IDS, progress, seed: 3 })).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('missedConcepts', () => {
  it('lists the concepts of wrong answers once each, in answer order', () => {
    const log: Log = [
      [at('2026-10-12', 9), lightningStartOf(SID, ['q-bake-02-1', 'q-bake-01-1'], at('2026-10-12', 9, 2))],
      lightning('bake-02', false, '2026-10-12T09:00:10.000Z'),
      lightning('bake-03', true, '2026-10-12T09:00:20.000Z'),
      lightning('bake-01', false, '2026-10-12T09:00:30.000Z'),
      [
        '2026-10-12T09:00:40.000Z',
        answerOf({ concept: 'bake-02', qid: 'q-bake-02-2', correct: false, sure: false, mode: 'lightning' }),
      ],
    ];
    const session = derive(timelineOf(log), null).sessions.get(SID) as SessionState;
    expect(missedConcepts(session)).toEqual(['bake-02', 'bake-01']);
  });
});

describe('lightning XP (spec 3.2)', () => {
  it('pays nothing for an answer after the deadline', () => {
    const log: Log = [
      [
        at('2026-10-12', 9),
        lightningStartOf(SID, ['q-bake-01-1', 'q-bake-02-1'], '2026-10-12T09:01:30.000Z'),
      ],
      lightning('bake-01', true, '2026-10-12T09:01:29.000Z'),
      lightning('bake-02', true, '2026-10-12T09:01:31.000Z'),
    ];
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), CONTENT).xp).toBe(1);
  });

  it('pays only the first answer to each question of the round', () => {
    const log: Log = [
      [at('2026-10-12', 9), lightningStartOf(SID, ['q-bake-01-1'], '2026-10-12T09:01:30.000Z')],
      lightning('bake-01', false, '2026-10-12T09:00:10.000Z'),
      lightning('bake-01', true, '2026-10-12T09:00:20.000Z'),
    ];
    expect(deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), CONTENT).xp).toBe(0);
  });

  it('pays a round started before 04:00 on its start day, and the next day still has its own paid round', () => {
    const log: Log = [
      [
        '2026-10-13T03:59:00.000Z',
        lightningStartOf(SID, ['q-bake-01-1', 'q-bake-02-1'], '2026-10-13T04:00:30.000Z'),
      ],
      lightning('bake-01', true, '2026-10-13T03:59:30.000Z'),
      lightning('bake-02', true, '2026-10-13T04:00:10.000Z'),
      [at('2026-10-13', 9), lightningStartOf(SID2, ['q-bake-03-1'], at('2026-10-13', 9, 2))],
      lightning('bake-03', true, at('2026-10-13', 9, 1), SID2),
    ];
    const memory = deriveMemory(timelineOf(log), CONTENT);
    expect(memory.xp).toBe(3);
    expect(memory.xpByDay.get('2026-10-12')).toBe(1);
    expect(memory.xpByDay.get('2026-10-13')).toBe(2);
  });
});
```

```ts
  it('Lightning spam: the first round of a day pays at most 10, later rounds 0, no card moves, no streak', () => {
    const studied: Log = [
      [at('2026-10-10'), right('oven', 'q-oven-1')],
      [at('2026-10-10', 10, 1), right('proofing', 'q-proofing-1')],
    ];
    const names = ['oven', 'proofing', 'crust', 'flour', 'yeast', 'salt', 'water'];
    /** A round of 15 right answers on 2026-10-11 at `hour`, three seconds apart, inside its 90 seconds. */
    const round = (sid: string, hour: number): Log => {
      const qids = Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => `ql-${i}`);
      const start = Date.parse(at('2026-10-11', hour));
      return [
        [at('2026-10-11', hour), lightningStartOf(sid, qids, new Date(start + 90_000).toISOString())],
        ...qids.map(
          (qid, i) =>
            [
              new Date(start + (i + 1) * 3_000).toISOString(),
              answerOf({
                concept: names[i % names.length] ?? 'oven',
                qid,
                correct: true,
                sure: false,
                mode: 'lightning',
                sid,
              }),
            ] as const,
        ),
      ];
    };
    const base = memoryOf(studied);
    const first = memoryOf([...studied, ...round(SID2, 10)]);
    const both = memoryOf([...studied, ...round(SID2, 10), ...round(SID3, 11)]);
    expect(first.xp - base.xp).toBe(10);
    expect(both.xp - base.xp).toBe(10);
    for (const concept of names)
      expect(both.concepts.get(concept)?.card).toEqual(base.concepts.get(concept)?.card);
    expect(both.qualifyingDays.has('2026-10-11')).toBe(false);
  });
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the selector**

Create `src/engine/lightning.ts`:

```ts
import type { MemoryState } from './memory.ts';
import type { Progress, SessionState } from './replay.ts';
import { mulberry32, shuffle } from './rng.ts';
import { type PoolQuestion, pickVariant } from './select.ts';

/**
 * The lightning round (spec 4.1): single practice items for settled concepts, 90 seconds, a summary at the end.
 * Its answers never touch FSRS (spec 3.6); a miss writes `review.now`, which `deriveMemory` turns into a due card,
 * and only the first round of a study day pays, 1 XP per correct answer up to 10 (spec 3.2 and 3.11).
 */
export const LIGHTNING_SECONDS = 90;
/** Settled concepts needed before the round opens (spec 7.2 empty state `Opens after 20 concepts settle.`). */
export const LIGHTNING_UNLOCK = 20;
/** A concept is settled once its card's FSRS stability reaches this many days. */
export const LIGHTNING_STABILITY_DAYS = 3;
/** The most items one round draws: more than anyone answers in 90 seconds. */
export const LIGHTNING_SIZE = 30;
/** The scope every lightning session start carries, so `openSession` finds the open round. */
export const LIGHTNING_SCOPE = 'lightning';

/** Concepts whose card has a stability of at least `LIGHTNING_STABILITY_DAYS`, sorted by id. */
export function settledConcepts(memory: MemoryState): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const [id, cm] of memory.concepts) {
    if (cm.card !== null && cm.card.stability >= LIGHTNING_STABILITY_DAYS) out.push(id);
  }
  return out.sort();
}

/** Whether the round is open: at least `LIGHTNING_UNLOCK` settled concepts. */
export function lightningOpen(memory: MemoryState): boolean {
  return settledConcepts(memory).length >= LIGHTNING_UNLOCK;
}

/** The deadline of a round started at `start`, as the session start event stores it. */
export function lightningDeadline(start: Date): string {
  return new Date(start.getTime() + LIGHTNING_SECONDS * 1000).toISOString();
}

/**
 * The items of a round: one `single` item per concept in `concepts`, the concepts in a seeded order, each variant
 * picked never answered first, then answered longest ago; at most `size`.
 * Only items without a `form` field qualify, so no exam item, taken or not, ever reaches a round.
 */
export function selectLightning(args: {
  pool: readonly PoolQuestion[];
  concepts: readonly string[];
  progress: Progress;
  seed: number;
  size?: number;
}): string[] {
  const { pool, progress, seed } = args;
  const size = args.size ?? LIGHTNING_SIZE;
  const wanted = new Set(args.concepts);
  const byConcept = new Map<string, PoolQuestion[]>();
  for (const q of pool) {
    if (q.type !== 'single' || 'form' in q || !wanted.has(q.concept)) continue;
    const list = byConcept.get(q.concept);
    if (list === undefined) byConcept.set(q.concept, [q]);
    else list.push(q);
  }
  const random = mulberry32(seed);
  const order = shuffle([...byConcept.keys()].sort(), random);
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const concept of order) {
    if (out.length >= size) break;
    out.push(pickVariant(byConcept.get(concept) ?? [], progress, random).id);
  }
  return out;
}

/** The concepts of a round's wrong answers, each once, in answer order. */
export function missedConcepts(session: SessionState): string[] {
  const answers = [...session.answers.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const a of answers) if (!a.correct && !out.includes(a.concept)) out.push(a.concept);
  return out;
}
```

```ts
 * with no review and no rating, and stays on the track so a new exam date runs it again in its place.
```

```ts
/** The card made due at the start of the study day of `e`, unless it already falls due earlier. */
function dueToday(s: CardState, e: ReviewNowEvent): CardState {
  if (s.card === null) return s;
  const due = Math.min(Date.parse(s.card.due), dayStartAt(e.day, e.tz).getTime());
  if (due === Date.parse(s.card.due)) return s;
  return { ...s, card: { ...s.card, due: new Date(due).toISOString() } };
}
```

```ts
/** The session id of the lightning rounds on day `n`, so a day's starts and answers share it. */
const roundSid = (n: number): string => `01JD${String(n).padStart(22, '0')}`;

/**
 * Lightning events (spec 4.1): a round start with its 90 second deadline, an answer in that day's round, or the
 * `review.now` of a miss; a day may start several rounds.
 */
const lightningItemArb = (days: { min: number; max: number }): fc.Arbitrary<Item> =>
  fc
    .record({
      day: fc.integer(days),
      minute: fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 1_400 }),
      kind: fc.constantFrom('start', 'answer', 'answer', 'answer', 'answer', 'review.now'),
      concept: fc.constantFrom(...CONCEPTS),
      variant: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 20 }),
      correct: fc.boolean(),
    })
    .map((s): Item => {
      const sid = roundSid(s.day);
      if (s.kind === 'start') {
        return [
          timeOf(s.day, s.minute),
          {
            t: 'session',
            sid,
            mode: 'lightning',
            phase: 'start',
            seed: 7,
            scope: 'lightning',
            qids: [],
            deadline: timeOf(s.day, s.minute + 1.5),
          },
        ];
      }
      if (s.kind === 'review.now') return [timeOf(s.day, s.minute), { t: 'review.now', concept: s.concept }];
      return [
        timeOf(s.day, s.minute),
        answerOf({
          concept: s.concept,
          qid: `ql-${s.concept}-${s.variant}`,
          correct: s.correct,
          sure: false,
          mode: 'lightning',
          sid,
        }),
      ];
    });
```

- [ ] **Step 8: A lightning day in the hands-on week**

In `tests/fixtures/journeys/journeys.ts`, add this method to the `Journey` class right before `  events(): StudyEvent[] {`:

```ts
  /**
   * A lightning round at `at` (spec 4.1), as the screen writes it: the start with its 90 second deadline, one answer
   * per `[concept, correct]` five seconds apart on the concept's variant a, then at the end the submit and one
   * `review.now` per missed concept, a millisecond apart.
   */
  lightning(at: string, answers: readonly (readonly [string, boolean])[]): this {
    this.sessions += 1;
    const sid = `01JB${String(this.sessions).padStart(22, '0')}`;
    const start = Date.parse(at);
    const iso = (ms: number): string => new Date(ms).toISOString();
    this.items.push([
      iso(start),
      {
        t: 'session',
        sid,
        mode: 'lightning',
        phase: 'start',
        seed: this.sessions,
        scope: 'lightning',
        qids: answers.map(([concept]) => variantId(concept, 'a')),
        deadline: iso(start + 90_000),
      },
    ]);
    answers.forEach(([concept, correct], i) => {
      this.items.push([
        iso(start + (i + 1) * 5_000),
        {
          t: 'answer',
          sid,
          mode: 'lightning',
          qid: variantId(concept, 'a'),
          qv: 1,
          concept,
          picked: [correct ? 'a' : 'b'],
          correct,
          sure: false,
          ms: 5_000,
        },
      ]);
    });
    const end = start + (answers.length + 1) * 5_000;
    this.items.push([
      iso(end),
      { t: 'session', sid, mode: 'lightning', phase: 'submit', seed: this.sessions },
    ]);
    const missed = [...new Set(answers.filter(([, correct]) => !correct).map(([concept]) => concept))];
    missed.forEach((concept, i) => {
      this.items.push([iso(end + i + 1), { t: 'review.now', concept }]);
    });
    return this;
  }
```

## Task 19: The lightning screen

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/lightning.ts`, `src/app/lightning-settle.ts`, `src/app/screens/Lightning.tsx`, `tests/unit/lightning-app.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/lightning.spec.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/router.ts`, `src/app/nav.ts`, `src/app/screens/Screen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/Home.tsx`, `src/app/home.ts` (`overdueLightning`), `src/ui/base.css` (under `/* Lightning */`)
- Test: `tests/unit/lightning-app.test.ts`, `tests/unit/router.test.ts`, `tests/unit/nav.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/lightning.spec.ts`

Design notes:
- The round is its own lazy screen at `#/s/lightning` (`{ kind: 'lightning' }`, Escape goes Home), keyed by the route so `Another round` starts afresh; `#/s/lightning/m03` stays not-found.
- Home links it with `Lightning round` under `Mock exams` (spec 4.6 keeps it out of Next and the extras line), and `src/app/memory.ts` needs no change: the screen reads `memory` and computes the settled concepts itself, so `src/engine/lightning.ts` never reaches the initial bundle.
- Before Start shows, the screen loads, with `loadModules` from `exam-settle.ts`, the chunk of every module that homes a settled concept, so `practicePoolOf` sees the whole pool on a cold start; for a round opened before this visit it loads every module, since its items may sit in any of them.
- Start records the session start (`mode: 'lightning'`, `scope: LIGHTNING_SCOPE`, `seed`, `qids` from `selectLightning` over `practicePoolOf`, never `learnPoolNow`, and `deadline: lightningDeadline(at)`) at the real time `new Date()`, not the minute clock.
- Each item is a `section.runner.lightning-item` with `data-qid`, so the e2e helpers `shown()` and `pickWrong()` find it; digits pick, `Enter` (or the `Answer` button) records the answer with `sure: false`, and the next item shows at once with no verdict.
- The round ends at its deadline or when its items run out; the first records the shared `settleLightning`, the second `roundEndEvents(session)`, each one `recordAll` of the submit plus one `review.now` per missed concept.
- The summary's end line is `endSummaryLine` with `total` set to the number of answers, so a round reads `7/9`, not `7/30` over items never shown; the miss line counts only the misses now due, because a concept already answered today keeps its card (Task 18).
- The summary lists every answer with the runner's verdict icon and word, the stem, and the tags `Your answer, correct`, or `Your answer: <text>` and `Correct answer: <text>`.
- A round left open past its deadline is settled when the store is ready: Home's effect (a signals `effect`, the pattern `useExamTicker` uses) finds it with `overdueLightning` from `home.ts` and only then loads `lightning-settle.ts` through `import()`; the screen settles it on arrival through the same function and then shows its summary.
- Prototype measure: this task adds about 0.1 KB to the initial bundle (the route, the Home link and effect, and `overdueLightning`); the lazy `Lightning` chunk is 2.7 KB and `lightning-settle` 1.1 KB gzipped.
- Covers P4-064 (lightning has no bet), P4-080, P4-081, P4-084, P4-085, P4-088, P4-089, P4-117, P4-121, P4-125 and P4-126.

Create `tests/e2e/lightning.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import type { QuestionView } from '../../schemas/chunks.ts';
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from '../../src/engine/replay.ts';
import { KV_KEYS } from '../../src/store/kv.ts';
import { timelineOf } from '../fixtures/events.ts';
import { answerOf, startOf, submitOf } from '../fixtures/memory.ts';
import { HOME_TITLE } from './exam-helpers.ts';
import {
  expectNoAxeViolations,
  expectNoSidewaysScroll,
  failOnPageProblems,
  h1,
  seedEvents,
  seedKv,
} from './helpers.ts';
import { chunkFor, pickWrong, shown } from './questions.ts';

/** Every lightning test runs at 09:00 UTC on Tuesday, October 20, 2026. */
const NOW = '2026-10-20T09:00:00.000Z';
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
/** The days before `NOW` of the two right Sure answers that settle a concept: stability about 14 days, due a week on. */
const SETTLE_DAYS = [10, 7] as const;

failOnPageProblems();
// UTC keeps the study day and the 04:00 boundary the same on every machine.
test.use({ timezoneId: 'UTC' });

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date(NOW) });
});

/** The top bar's review button, which shows only while something is due. */
const dueButton = (page: Page) => page.getByRole('button', { name: /^\d+ due: / });

/**
 * Seeds a log in which 24 concepts of Modules 1 to 3 are settled (stability of at least 3 days) and none is due
 * before next week, then returns the live questions of those modules.
 */
async function seedSettled(page: Page): Promise<QuestionView[]> {
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  const questions = (await Promise.all([1, 2, 3].map((m) => chunkFor(page, m)))).flat();
  const first = new Map<string, QuestionView>();
  for (const q of questions) if (q.type === 'single' && !first.has(q.concept)) first.set(q.concept, q);
  const picks = [...first.values()].slice(0, 24);
  const iso = (ms: number): string => new Date(ms).toISOString();
  const items: (readonly [string, NewEvent])[] = [];
  for (const [i, days] of SETTLE_DAYS.entries()) {
    const sid = `01JA00000000000000000000L${i + 1}`;
    let t = Date.parse(NOW) - days * DAY_MS;
    items.push([
      iso(t),
      startOf(
        sid,
        'quiz',
        picks.map((q) => q.id),
        'm01',
      ),
    ]);
    for (const q of picks) {
      t += 1000;
      items.push([
        iso(t),
        answerOf({
          concept: q.concept,
          qid: q.id,
          qv: q.version,
          correct: true,
          sure: true,
          mode: 'quiz',
          sid,
        }),
      ]);
    }
    items.push([iso(t + 1000), submitOf(sid, 'quiz')]);
  }
  await seedKv(page, { [KV_KEYS.engineVersion]: ENGINE_VERSION });
  await seedEvents(page, timelineOf(items));
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  return questions;
}

/** Starts a round from the lightning screen and answers its first item wrong; returns that item. */
async function startAndMissOne(page: Page, questions: readonly QuestionView[]): Promise<QuestionView> {
  await page.goto('/#/s/lightning');
  await expect(h1(page, 'Lightning round')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the round', exact: true }).click();
  const q = await shown(page, questions);
  await expect(page.locator('#stem')).toBeFocused();
  await pickWrong(page, q);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Answer/ }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('.runner-position')).toHaveText('1 answered');
  await expect(page.locator('section.runner')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-qid', q.id);
  return q;
}

test('a lightning round runs 90 seconds, shows a summary and sends a miss to review', async ({ page }) => {
  const questions = await seedSettled(page);
  await expect(dueButton(page)).toHaveCount(0);
  await startAndMissOne(page, questions);
  await expect(page.getByRole('timer')).toHaveText(/^1:[0-2]\d$/);
  await page.clock.fastForward('01:30');
  await expect(page.locator('.end-line')).toHaveText('0/1');
  await expect(page.getByText("1 concept goes back into today's review.", { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  const summary = page.locator('.lightning-summary li');
  await expect(summary).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(summary.locator('.verdict')).toHaveText('Incorrect');
  await expect(summary.locator('.tag.ok')).toContainText('Correct answer: ');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Back to Home', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(dueButton(page)).toHaveAccessibleName('1 due: 1 concept, start a review');
});

test('a round left mid-way is settled on the next app start after its deadline', async ({ page }) => {
  const questions = await seedSettled(page);
  await startAndMissOne(page, questions);
  await page.goto('about:blank');
  await page.clock.fastForward('02:00');
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(h1(page, HOME_TITLE)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(dueButton(page)).toHaveAccessibleName('1 due: 1 concept, start a review');
  await page.goto('/#/s/lightning');
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the round', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});

test('a second round the same day says it pays no XP', async ({ page }) => {
  const questions = await seedSettled(page);
  await page.goto('/#/s/lightning');
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the round', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Only the first round each day pays XP.')).toHaveCount(0);
  await startAndMissOne(page, questions);
  await page.clock.fastForward('01:30');
  await expect(page.locator('.end-line')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Another round', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the round', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Only the first round each day pays XP.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});

test('the lightning screen has no axe violations and no sideways scroll', async ({ page }) => {
  const questions = await seedSettled(page);
  await page.goto('/#/s/lightning');
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the round', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
  await startAndMissOne(page, questions);
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
  await page.clock.fastForward('01:30');
  await expect(page.locator('.lightning-summary')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoAxeViolations(page);
  await expectNoSidewaysScroll(page);
});

test('before 20 concepts settle the round says when it opens', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/#/s/lightning');
  await expect(h1(page, 'Lightning round')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Opens after 20 concepts settle.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start the round' })).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Lightning round', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(h1(page, 'Lightning round')).toBeVisible();
});
```
