- [ ] **Step 4: The copy, the end events and the overdue rounds**

Create `src/app/lightning.ts`:

```ts
/** The lightning round's copy and pure helpers (spec 4.1 and 7.2), so the tests run without a browser. */
import type { NewEvent } from '../../schemas/events.ts';
import { studyDay } from '../engine/day.ts';
import { missedConcepts } from '../engine/lightning.ts';
import type { Progress, SessionState } from '../engine/replay.ts';

export const LIGHTNING_LOCKED = 'Opens after 20 concepts settle.';
export const LIGHTNING_LEDE = "Answer as many as you can in 90 seconds; misses come back in today's review.";
export const LIGHTNING_UNPAID = 'Only the first round each day pays XP.';
export const LIGHTNING_START = 'Start the round';
export const LIGHTNING_LOAD_ERROR = 'This round could not load. Check your connection and try again.';

/** The time left as `m:ss`, rounded up to the second and never below `0:00`. */
export function clockText(ms: number): string {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms / 1000));
  return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:${String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/** The line under the summary: how many missed concepts go back into today's review, or null for none. */
export function missLine(n: number): string | null {
  if (n === 0) return null;
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'concept goes' : 'concepts go'} back into today's review.`;
}

/** Whether a lightning round has already started on study day `today`, so a new round pays nothing (spec 3.2). */
export function paidToday(progress: Progress, today: string): boolean {
  for (const s of progress.sessions.values()) {
    if (s.mode === 'lightning' && studyDay(new Date(s.startedAt)) === today) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** The events that close a round (spec 4.1 and 3.6): its submit, then one `review.now` per missed concept. */
export function roundEndEvents(session: SessionState): NewEvent[] {
  return [
    { t: 'session', sid: session.sid, mode: 'lightning', phase: 'submit', seed: session.seed },
    ...missedConcepts(session).map((concept): NewEvent => ({ t: 'review.now', concept })),
  ];
}
```

In `src/app/home.ts`, add `import type { Progress, SessionState } from '../engine/replay.ts';` right after the `../engine/queue.ts` import (merge it into an existing import of that module if an earlier task added one), and append at the end of the file:

```ts
/** The lightning rounds still open after their deadline at `at` (spec 4.1), which the app settles when it starts. */
export function overdueLightning(progress: Progress, at: Date): SessionState[] {
  const out: SessionState[] = [];
  for (const s of progress.sessions.values()) {
    if (s.mode !== 'lightning' || s.submittedAt !== null || s.abandoned || s.deadline === null) continue;
    if (Date.parse(s.deadline) <= at.getTime()) out.push(s);
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The settle**

Create `src/app/lightning-settle.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Settling lightning rounds left open past their deadline (spec 4.1): a tab closed mid-round still closes its
 * session and sends its misses back into today's review. Home loads it through import() when it finds such a round,
 * and the lightning screen calls it on arrival, so it stays out of the initial bundle (spec 2.10).
 */
import { overdueLightning } from './home.ts';
import { roundEndEvents } from './lightning.ts';
import { progress, recordAll, storeReady } from './progress.ts';

let inFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;

/** Records, in one write, the submit and the `review.now` events of every lightning round past its deadline at `at`. */
export function settleLightning(at: Date): Promise<void> {
  inFlight ??= (async () => {
    if (!storeReady.peek()) return;
    const events = overdueLightning(progress.peek(), at).flatMap(roundEndEvents);
    if (events.length > 0) await recordAll(events);
  })().finally(() => {
    inFlight = null;
  });
  return inFlight;
}
```

```tsx
  LIGHTNING_START,
  LIGHTNING_UNPAID,
  missLine,
  paidToday,
  roundEndEvents,
} from '../lightning.ts';
import { settleLightning } from '../lightning-settle.ts';
import { memory, memoryContent } from '../memory.ts';
import { newSessionSeed, progress, record, recordAll, storeReady } from '../progress.ts';
import { displayOrder, endSummaryLine, isCorrect } from '../runner.ts';
import { runnerKey } from '../runner-keys.ts';
import { overlay } from '../state.ts';

const CRUMBS = [
  { label: 'Home', href: '#/' },
  { label: 'Lightning round', href: '#/s/lightning' },
];

/** The memory key of a concept id, as the pool and the cards use it. */
function memoryConcept(concept: string): string {
  const content = memoryContent.peek();
  return (content === null ? null : resolveConcept(content, concept)) ?? concept;
}

/**
 * The modules whose chunks a visit needs: every module for a round opened before this visit, whose items may sit in
 * any of them, else the modules that home a settled concept, so the pool is whole on a cold start.
 */
function modulesFor(idx: ContentIndex, memory: MemoryState, resumed: boolean): number[] {
  if (resumed) return idx.modules.map((m) => m.n);
  const content = memoryContent.peek();
  const homes = new Set<number>();
  for (const id of settledConcepts(memory)) {
    const module = content?.concepts.get(id)?.module;
    if (module !== undefined) homes.add(module);
  }
  return [...homes].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

/** The lightning round `#/s/lightning` (spec 4.1): the start screen, a 90 second round, then its summary. */
export function Lightning({ idx }: { idx: ContentIndex }) {
  useTitle('Lightning round');
  const mem = memory.value;
  const [sid, setSid] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [chunks, setChunks] = useState<readonly ModuleChunk[] | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const asked = useRef(false);

  useEffect(
    () =>
      effect(() => {
        const m = memory.value;
        if (!storeReady.value || m === null || asked.current) return;
        asked.current = true;
        const open = openSession(progress.peek(), 'lightning', LIGHTNING_SCOPE);
        if (open !== null) setSid(open.sid);
        void loadModules(modulesFor(idx, m, open !== null)).then(setChunks);
      }),
    [idx],
  );

  const shipped = useMemo(() => {
    const out = new Map<string, QuestionView>();
    for (const c of chunks ?? []) for (const q of c.questions) out.set(q.id, q);
    return out;
  }, [chunks]);

  const session = sid === null ? null : (progress.value.sessions.get(sid) ?? null);
  let body = <p class="muted">Loading</p>;
  if (chunks === null) body = <p role="alert">{LIGHTNING_LOAD_ERROR}</p>;
  else if (chunks !== undefined && mem !== null) {
    if (session === null) body = <Start memory={mem} chunks={chunks} onStart={setSid} />;
    else if (session.submittedAt === null && !session.abandoned)
      body = <Round session={session} shipped={shipped} />;
    else body = <Summary session={session} memory={mem} shipped={shipped} />;
  }
  return (
    <>
      <Breadcrumbs items={CRUMBS} />
      <h1>Lightning round</h1>
      {body}
    </>
  );
}

function Start({
  memory: mem,
  chunks,
  onStart,
}: {
  memory: MemoryState;
  chunks: readonly ModuleChunk[];
  onStart: (sid: string) => void;
}) {
  if (!lightningOpen(mem)) return <p class="lede">{LIGHTNING_LOCKED}</p>;
  const start = (): void => {
    const seed = newSessionSeed();
    const qids = selectLightning({
      pool: practicePoolOf(chunks, memoryConcept),
      concepts: settledConcepts(mem),
      progress: progress.peek(),
      seed,
    });
    if (qids.length === 0) return;
    const at = new Date();
    const sid = ulid();
    const deadline = lightningDeadline(at);
    void record(
      { t: 'session', sid, mode: 'lightning', phase: 'start', scope: LIGHTNING_SCOPE, seed, qids, deadline },
      at,
    );
    onStart(sid);
  };
  return (
    <>
      <p class="lede">{LIGHTNING_LEDE}</p>
      {paidToday(progress.value, studyDay(now.value)) && (
        <p class="muted lightning-unpaid">{LIGHTNING_UNPAID}</p>
      )}
      <p>
        <button type="button" class="button" onClick={start}>
          {LIGHTNING_START}
        </button>
      </p>
    </>
  );
}

/** The round: one item at a time, no feedback, until the deadline or the last item; then its end is recorded. */
function Round({ session, shipped }: { session: SessionState; shipped: ReadonlyMap<string, QuestionView> }) {
  const deadline = Date.parse(session.deadline ?? session.startedAt);
  const [t, setT] = useState(() => Date.now());
  const ended = useRef(false);
  const next = session.qids.find((qid) => shipped.has(qid) && !session.answers.has(qid)) ?? null;
  const over = t >= deadline || next === null;

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setT(Date.now()), 250);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!over || ended.current) return;
    ended.current = true;
    // Past the deadline the shared settle closes every overdue round once; a round out of items closes itself.
    if (Date.now() >= deadline) void settleLightning(new Date());
    else void recordAll(roundEndEvents(session));
  }, [over, deadline, session]);

  const q = next === null ? undefined : shipped.get(next);
  if (over || q === undefined) return <p class="muted">Loading</p>;

  const answer = (picked: string[], ms: number): void => {
    if (Date.now() >= deadline) {
      setT(Date.now());
      return;
    }
    void record({
      t: 'answer',
      sid: session.sid,
      mode: 'lightning',
      qid: q.id,
      qv: q.version,
      concept: q.concept,
      picked,
      correct: isCorrect(q, picked),
      sure: false,
      ms,
    });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div class="lightning-bar">
        <p class="muted runner-position">{session.answers.size} answered</p>
        <span class="lightning-clock" role="timer" aria-live="off">
          {clockText(deadline - t)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <Item key={q.id} q={q} seed={session.seed} onAnswer={answer} />
    </>
  );
}

/** One item: digits pick, `Enter` answers with Not sure, and the next item follows at once (no bet, no feedback). */
function Item({
  q,
  seed,
  onAnswer,
}: {
  q: QuestionView;
  seed: number;
  onAnswer: (picked: string[], ms: number) => void;
}) {
  const order = useMemo(() => displayOrder(q, seed), [q, seed]);
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const stem = useRef<HTMLParagraphElement>(null);
  const shownAt = useRef(Date.now());
  const submit = (): void => {
    if (choice !== null) onAnswer([choice], Date.now() - shownAt.current);
  };
  const latest = useRef({ order, submit });
  latest.current = { order, submit };

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    // Each item starts with focus on its stem (spec 7.5).
    stem.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }, []);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (event.defaultPrevented || overlay.value !== null) return;
      const target = event.target;
      const native =
        event.key === 'Enter' && target instanceof Element && target.closest('a, button') !== null;
      if (native) return;
      const command = runnerKey(
        {
          key: event.key,
          meta: event.metaKey,
          ctrl: event.ctrlKey,
          alt: event.altKey,
          shift: event.shiftKey,
          typing: isTyping(target),
          control: isControl(target),
          at: event.timeStamp,
        },
        {
          type: 'single',
          answered: false,
          optionCount: latest.current.order.length,
          leftCount: 0,
          reportOpen: false,
        },
      );
      if (command?.kind === 'choose') {
        event.preventDefault();
        setChoice(latest.current.order[command.n - 1] ?? null);
      } else if (command?.kind === 'check') {
        event.preventDefault();
        latest.current.submit();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, []);

  return (
    <section class="runner lightning-item" data-qid={q.id} aria-labelledby="stem">
      <p id="stem" class="stem" tabIndex={-1} ref={stem}>
        {q.stem}
      </p>
      <fieldset class="options" aria-labelledby="stem">
        {order.map((id, i) => {
          const option = q.options.find((o) => o.id === id);
          if (option === undefined) return null;
          return (
            <label key={id} class={`option${choice === id ? ' chosen' : ''}`}>
              <input
                type="radio"
                name={`q-${q.id}`}
                value={id}
                checked={choice === id}
                onChange={() => setChoice(id)}
              />
              <span class="option-n" aria-hidden="true">
                {i + 1}
              </span>
              <span class="option-text">{option.text}</span>
            </label>
          );
        })}
      </fieldset>
      <div class="submit-bar">
        <button type="button" class="button" disabled={choice === null} onClick={submit}>
          Answer <kbd>Enter</kbd>
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/** The end of a round (spec 4.1): the end line, how many misses went back into review, and every answer. */
function Summary({
  session,
  memory: mem,
  shipped,
}: {
  session: SessionState;
  memory: MemoryState;
  shipped: ReadonlyMap<string, QuestionView>;
}) {
  const content = memoryContent.value;
  if (content === null) return null;
  const at = now.value;
  const tz = tzOffset(at);
  const answers = [...session.answers.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
  const summary = sessionSummary(session, mem, content, at, tz);
  // A miss on a concept already answered today stays as it is (its one update of the day is spent), so count only
  // the misses that are due now.
  const back = missedConcepts(session).filter((c) => {
    const card = mem.concepts.get(memoryConcept(c))?.card ?? null;
    return card !== null && isDue(card, at, tz);
  });
  const line = missLine(back.length);
  return (
    <>
      <p class="end-line" aria-live="polite">
        {endSummaryLine({ ...summary, total: answers.length })}
      </p>
      {line !== null && <p class="muted lightning-misses">{line}</p>}
      <ol class="lightning-summary">
        {answers.map((a) => (
          <SummaryItem key={a.qid} answer={a} q={shipped.get(a.qid)} />
        ))}
      </ol>
      <ul class="end-links">
        <li>
          <a href="#/">Back to Home</a>
        </li>
        <li>
          <a href="#/s/lightning">Another round</a>
        </li>
      </ul>
    </>
  );
}

function SummaryItem({ answer, q }: { answer: AnswerRecord; q: QuestionView | undefined }) {
  if (q === undefined) return null;
  const mine = q.options.find((o) => (answer.picked as string[]).includes(o.id))?.text ?? '';
  const right = q.options.find((o) => o.correct)?.text ?? '';
  return (
    <li class={`review-item ${answer.correct ? 'is-correct' : 'is-wrong'}`} data-qid={q.id}>
      <p class={`verdict ${answer.correct ? 'ok' : 'bad'}`}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
          {answer.correct ? (
            <path d="M3 8.5l3 3 7-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
          ) : (
            <path d="M4 4l8 8M12 4l-8 8" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" />
          )}
        </svg>
        {answer.correct ? 'Correct' : 'Incorrect'}
      </p>
      <p class="review-line">{q.stem}</p>
      <p class="tags">
        {answer.correct ? (
          <span class="tag ok">Your answer, correct</span>
        ) : (
          <>
            <span class="tag bad">Your answer: {mine}</span>
            <span class="tag ok">Correct answer: {right}</span>
          </>
        )}
      </p>
    </li>
  );
}
```

```tsx
/**
 * Settles a lightning round left open past its deadline (spec 4.1) once the store is ready, so a tab closed
 * mid-round still closes its session and queues its misses; the settle code loads only when one is found.
 */
function useLightningSettle(): void {
  useEffect(
    () =>
      effect(() => {
        if (!storeReady.value) return;
        const at = now.value;
        if (overdueLightning(progress.value, at).length === 0) return;
        void import('../lightning-settle.ts').then((m) => m.settleLightning(at));
      }),
    [],
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Document the rest of Phase 4 in the README**

In `README.md`, insert these two sections right before `## Course text stays out`:

```markdown
## Configure and Simulate labs

Configure labs walk a short wizard of choices, and Check judges the finished setup against the goals.
Simulate labs set a few parameters, and `Enter` (or `Run and check`) runs the lab's fixed model before it checks the goals, so the goals always read the parameters on screen.
Every price in a lab is in lab credits, a made-up unit, never an AWS price.
The six Simulate labs take their constants, solutions and wrong paths from fixed tables, and `tests/content/sim-labs.test.ts` pins the outcome each lesson rests on.

## Incidents and lightning

Once every Module 2 lesson is read, Home offers one incident a day on the extras line, picked from the weakest read modules, and it stays the day's incident until 04:00.
An incident runs at `#/s/incident` as 2 to 4 practice steps with the bet buttons; solving it pays 15 XP when every step is right, else 8, once a day, and the day counts toward the streak.
Incidents live in `content/incidents/inc-NNN.yaml`; `pnpm --silent content:report --incident-targets` lists what each module still needs, and the check rounds run over their steps with `--pool incident`.
The lightning round at `#/s/lightning` opens once 20 concepts have settled (an FSRS stability of at least 3 days) and asks single practice items for 90 seconds, with a summary at the end.
Only the first round of a study day pays, 1 XP per right answer up to 10; lightning never moves a card or the streak, and every miss comes back in today's review.
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Deploy and confirm the labs are on the site**

The controller asks Dani to run `pnpm site:deploy` on Saturday, October 31.
Its last line reads `site:deploy: done, ` followed by the site's address.
Once it is up, export that address as `SITE` and run this; it prints ids and counts only:

```bash
node --input-type=module -e '
const site = process.env.SITE;
const html = await (await fetch(site)).text();
const name = html.match(/<meta name="ccp-content" content="([^"]+)"/)?.[1] ?? "";
const index = await (await fetch(new URL(name, site))).json();
const by = (k) => index.labs.filter((l) => l.engine === k).length;
console.log(`labs ${index.labs.length} sort ${by("sort")} configure ${by("configure")} simulate ${by("simulate")} incidents ${index.incidents.length}`);
'
```

Expected: `labs 19 sort 11 configure 2 simulate 6 incidents <n>`, where `<n>` is the number of live incidents (48 when every one passed its checks), so the labs, incidents and lightning work offline on Dani's phone from Sunday, November 1.

### Deliberate deviations

- The lab contract has no `schema` member; the zod spec and action schemas live in `schemas/lab.ts`, so zod stays out of the app bundle (Task 1).
- Every check goes through the engine registry's `settle` and `complete`, so a Simulate path is always judged on its run and a Sort wrong path fails for its mistake, not for cards left in the tray (Tasks 1, 12 and 13).
- Lab files gain `title`, `notes`, `status` and each wrong path's `mistake`, and lab ids are `lab-mNN-<file slug>` (Task 1).
- Sort has buckets only, no ordered slots (Task 1).
- Goals are checked on demand with `Enter`, not live (Task 7).
- Only a lab's first completion pays and qualifies the day (Task 5).
- Debrief answers use a new `debrief` mode, and every rerun of a lab runs its debrief again (Tasks 5 and 7).
- Lab working state is one kv key, `labDrafts`, holding actions only (Task 6).
- R7 and the retired Support plan check run on lab text, and R2 makes a committed lab's `rev` rise with any edit (Tasks 2 and 3).
- Hands On counts a module only with both its core labs done, and Lab Complete stays out of reach until Phase 5 (Tasks 5 and 20).
- `ENGINE_VERSION` moves to 5 once, in Task 5, and Tasks 16 and 18 extend only its doc comment (Tasks 5, 16 and 18).
- Simulate runs six fixed models with fixed lab constants, and every priced lab carries `credits` (Tasks 4 and 13).
- Incidents are files with a `status`, R2 keeps a shipped incident's id, and incident steps join the Phase 3 check pipeline as `QuestionEntry` items with `incident` set (Tasks 14 and 15).
- The incident runs at `#/s/incident` in its own lazy screen; lightning runs at `#/s/lightning`, linked from Home, with no bet and a summary at the end (Tasks 17 and 19).
- A lightning round left open is settled at the next app start, and the round takes `practicePoolOf` only, since `learnPool` drops the `form` field of taken-form items (Tasks 18 and 19).
- `review.now` is ignored for a concept already answered that study day, so a review can never loop on a concept whose one update of the day is spent; the lightning miss line counts only the misses now due (Tasks 18 and 19).
- The lightning end line counts the answers given, not the items drawn (Task 19).
- `Step` carries no `stale` member on a `'now'` step, against the brief, because nothing reads it (Task 18).
- `src/app/memory.ts` is not changed by Task 19, against the brief's file list: the lazy screen computes the settled concepts itself, which keeps `src/engine/lightning.ts` out of the initial bundle.
- The Help sheet becomes lazy, and the incident offer and the lightning settle load through `import()` (Tasks 6, 17 and 19).
- The visual screenshot test of a lab stays out, and the keyboard-only e2e runs on a Sort, a Configure and a Simulate lab instead of the Phase 5 Place lab (Tasks 9, 12 and 13).

- Task 7's `vite.config.ts` ships every module the entry imports statically as one initial chunk, because Rolldown otherwise splits the modules the lazy Lab and Help chunks share with the entry into small preloaded chunks and the initial JS measured 50.9 KB; with the grouping it measured 48.1 KB in 1 script (Task 7).
- The initial JS starts at 49.4 KB at HEAD 56f2c1a, not the 48.7 KB the brief assumed, so Tasks 8, 16, 17, 18 and 19 share about 1.9 KB of headroom and each stops and reports rather than raising the budget (Tasks 6 and 7).
- The Sort tray is a `<fieldset>` with a visually hidden `<legend>Cards</legend>`, not a `div` with `role="group"`, because Biome's `useSemanticElements` rule forbids the role (Task 7).
- Task 5 gives `extraLink` a minimal `lab` branch so the widened `Extra` stays exhaustive, and Task 8 replaces it with the `(extra, idx: ExtraContent)` form and its own tests (Tasks 5 and 8).
- `labRows` takes `Pick<ContentIndex, 'labs'>` and `extraLink` takes the structural `ExtraContent`, narrower than the brief's types, so the tests can pass bare modules; both still accept a whole `ContentIndex` (Tasks 8 and 17).
- The lab draft merge case in `backup.test.ts` passes before Task 6's code, since backup merges every kv key by the newer `updatedAt`; it stays as a regression guard (Task 6).
- The debrief's bet buttons, its Sure hint and the lab's XP flash come from the Runner and the top bar, so they are proven by Task 9's end-to-end test rather than by new unit tests (Tasks 7 and 9).
- Incident steps are kept out of every coverage count in `scripts/content/lib/coverage.ts`, so `r10-coverage.ts` needs no change (Task 14).
- The incident end-to-end test clicks Next on the last step after a `Shift+Enter` bet, because `Enter` did not reach the end line on chromium in the prototype; Task 17 reproduces that on a check run and fixes it as a Phase 2 regression if it is one (Task 17).
